FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/access/AccessFlow.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, BadgeCheck, Check, Crown, Fingerprint, HardDrive, KeyRound, LockKeyhole, LogIn, Mail, Plus, RotateCcw, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, UserPlus, Users, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api, errorMessage, isTauri } from "../../lib/tauri";
import type { AccessAccount, AccessStatus } from "../../lib/types";
import { Word3D } from "../fx/Word3D";
import { LogoMark } from "../shell/Logo";
import { LogoHero } from "./LogoHero";
import { PasswordInput, StrengthMeter, usePasswordCheck } from "./password";

export type AccessStep = "start" | "account" | "key" | "login" | "reset";

/** New here: 1. an account (email + password), 2. the key, 3. in. */
const STEPS: Array<{ id: AccessStep | "in"; label: string }> = [
  { id: "account", label: "Account" },
  { id: "key", label: "Your key" },
  { id: "in", label: "Play" },
];

const PLAN_STYLE: Record<string, { label: string; className: string; icon: typeof Crown }> = {
  owner: { label: "Owner", className: "border-amber-300/50 bg-amber-300/10 text-amber-200", icon: Crown },
  guest: { label: "Guest", className: "border-cyan-300/40 bg-cyan-300/10 text-cyan-200", icon: Users },
  lifetime: { label: "Lifetime", className: "border-accent/50 bg-accent/10 text-accent-2", icon: BadgeCheck },
  none: { label: "No key yet", className: "border-white/20 bg-white/5 text-muted", icon: KeyRound },
  blocked: { label: "Key stopped", className: "border-red-400/40 bg-red-400/10 text-red-200", icon: KeyRound },
};

/** Reads what a key says (email + kind) without checking it; the app checks the signature. */
export function readKey(key: string): { email: string; plan: string } | null {
  try {
    const part = key.trim().replace(/^SLNC-/, "").split(".")[1];
    if (!part) return null;
    const json = JSON.parse(atob(part.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/").padEnd(Math.ceil(part.length / 4) * 4, "=")));
    return typeof json.sub === "string" && typeof json.plan === "string" ? { email: json.sub, plan: json.plan } : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * The pages before the app (nobody gets in without access): Start (saved accounts on this PC, or
 * new) → make an account (email + password) → logged in, the "your key" page (paste the key, or
 * buy one for €4) → the app. Every account has its own data; with its key the app restarts on
 * that account's folder.
 */
export function AccessFlow({ status, onDone }: { status: AccessStatus; onDone: (s: AccessStatus) => void }) {
  // Browser preview only (screenshots): ?step=plan|account|login opens that page directly.
  const [step, setStep] = useState<AccessStep>(() => (!isTauri && (new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("step") as AccessStep)) || "start");
  const [loginEmail, setLoginEmail] = useState("");
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<AccessAccount[]>(status.accounts);
  // Logged in but no key yet: always the key page.
  const page: AccessStep = status.needs_key ? "key" : step;
  const index = STEPS.findIndex((s) => s.id === page);
  const done = (s: AccessStatus) => (s.restarting ? onDone(s) : onDone(s));

  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
      <BrandSide />
      <div className="relative z-10 flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col overflow-y-auto">
        <div className="mx-auto my-auto w-full max-w-[480px] px-10 py-12">
          {index >= 0 && (
            <ol className="mb-9 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-[0.16em] uppercase">
              {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
                <li key={s.id} className={clsx("space-y-2", i <= index ? "text-accent-2" : "text-muted/70")}>
                  <span className={clsx("block h-0.5 rounded-full transition", i < index ? "bg-accent" : i === index ? "bg-accent/70 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.7)]" : "bg-white/10")} />
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    {i < index ? <Check className="size-3" /> : <span>{String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>}
                    {s.label}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          )}
          <div key={page} className="access-in w-full">
            {page === "key" && status.needs_key && <KeyPage
                email={status.needs_key}
                stopped={status.accounts.some((a) => a.email === status.needs_key && a.plan === "blocked")}
                onDone={done}
                onLoggedOut={(s) => {
                  setStep("start");
                  setAccounts(s.accounts);
                  done(s);
                }}
              />}
            {page === "start" && (
              <Start
                accounts={accounts}
                onPick={(email) => {
                  setLoginEmail(email);
                  setStep("login");
                }}
                onForget={(email) => api.accessForgetAccount(email).then((s) => setAccounts(s.accounts)).catch(() => {})}
                onNew={() => {
                  setLoginEmail("");
                  setStep("account");
                }}
                onLogin={() => {
                  setLoginEmail("");
                  setStep("login");
                }}
              />
            )}
            {page === "account" && <CreateAccount email={loginEmail} onBack={() => setStep("start")} onLogin={() => setStep("login")} onDone={done} />}
            {page === "reset" && <CreateAccount reset email={loginEmail} onBack={() => setStep("login")} onLogin={() => setStep("login")} onDone={done} />}
            {page === "login" && (
              <LogIn_
                email={loginEmail}
                accounts={accounts}
                onRefresh={() => api.accessStatus().then((s) => setAccounts(s.accounts)).catch(() => {})}
                onBack={() => setStep("start")}
                onCreate={() => {
                  setLoginEmail("");
                  setStep("account");
                }}
                onForgot={(email) => {
                  setLoginEmail(email);
                  // No key yet: there's nothing to prove it with, and no data yet, so make it again.
                  setStep(accounts.find((a) => a.email === email)?.plan === "none" ? "account" : "reset");
                }}
                onDone={done}
              />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Full-screen "opening your account" while the app restarts onto that account's data. */
export function OpeningAccount({ email }: { email: string | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center overflow-hidden">
      <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center text-center">
        <LogoMark className="access-pulse size-28 drop-shadow-[0_0_40px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.55)]" />
        <p className="section-title mt-6 text-accent-2!">Opening your account</p>
        {email && <p className="mt-2 text-lg font-semibold">{email}</p>}
        <span className="relative mt-6 block h-0.5 w-48 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/10">
          <span className="access-loading absolute inset-y-0 w-1/3 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-r from-transparent via-accent-2 to-transparent" />
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function PlanBadge({ plan }: { plan: string }) {
  const p = PLAN_STYLE[plan] ?? PLAN_STYLE.lifetime!;
  return (
    <span className={clsx("inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-bold tracking-wider uppercase", p.className)}>
      <p.icon className="size-3" /> {p.label}
    </span>
  );
}

/** The left half of the start pages: the logo, the name, and what's true about your data. */
function BrandSide() {
  return (
    <aside className="access-brand relative z-10 flex w-[42%] max-w-[640px] min-w-[420px] shrink-0 flex-col justify-between overflow-hidden border-r border-line px-12 py-10">
      <p className="section-title relative">Macro studio · this PC</p>
      <div className="relative flex flex-col items-start">
        <div className="-ml-[3%]">
          <LogoHero />
        </div>
        <h1 className="access-title mt-1 text-[3.4rem] leading-none font-black tracking-tight">
          <span className="t3d" data-text="SLNC">SLNC</span> <Word3D text="MACRO" />
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-5 max-w-sm text-[15px] leading-relaxed text-muted">Record anything, let it play for you, and run ready-made routines for your games.</p>
      </div>
      <ul className="relative grid gap-2.5 font-mono text-[11px] tracking-wide text-muted">
        <li className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <ShieldCheck className="size-3.5 text-accent" /> Password kept as an Argon2id hash, never as text
        </li>
        <li className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <HardDrive className="size-3.5 text-accent" /> Every account's data is encrypted with its own key
        </li>
        <li className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
          <Fingerprint className="size-3.5 text-accent" /> Unlock with Windows Hello, if your PC has it
        </li>
      </ul>
    </aside>
  );
}

function Start({ accounts, onPick, onForget, onNew, onLogin }: { accounts: AccessAccount[]; onPick: (email: string) => void; onForget: (email: string) => void; onNew: () => void; onLogin: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="section-title">{accounts.length > 0 ? "Sign in" : "Start"}</p>
      <h2 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em]">{accounts.length > 0 ? "Who's playing?" : "Welcome."}</h2>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">{accounts.length > 0 ? "Pick your account on this PC." : "Make your account on this PC, then put in your key."}</p>

      {accounts.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-8 space-y-2">
          {accounts.map((a, i) => (
            <div key={a.email} className="access-card group relative" style={{ animationDelay: `${120 + i * 70}ms` }}>
              <button className="access-account flex w-full items-center gap-4 rounded-lg px-4 py-3.5 text-left" onClick={() => onPick(a.email)}>
                <Avatar email={a.email} plan={a.plan} />
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <span className="block truncate font-semibold">{a.email}</span>
                  <span className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
                    <PlanBadge plan={a.plan} />
                    {a.last_login && <span className="font-mono text-[11px]">Last login {new Date(a.last_login).toLocaleDateString("en-GB", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span>}
                  </span>
                </span>
                <ArrowRight className="size-4 text-muted transition group-hover:translate-x-1 group-hover:text-accent" />
              </button>
              <button className="absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 hidden rounded p-1 text-muted hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-text group-hover:block" title="Forget this account on this PC (its data stays)" onClick={() => onForget(a.email)}>
                <X className="size-3.5" />
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
          <button className="access-card flex w-full items-center gap-4 rounded-lg border border-dashed border-white/15 px-4 py-3.5 text-left text-muted transition hover:border-accent/60 hover:text-text" style={{ animationDelay: `${120 + accounts.length * 70}ms` }} onClick={onNew}>
            <span className="grid size-11 place-items-center rounded-md border border-white/10">
              <Plus className="size-5" />
            </span>
            <span className="font-medium">Add another account</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mt-9 flex flex-col gap-3">
          <button className="btn-primary access-cta justify-between px-5 py-3.5 text-base" onClick={onNew}>
            Get started <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
          </button>
          <button className="btn-secondary justify-between px-5 py-3.5 text-base" onClick={onLogin}>
            I already have an account <LogIn className="size-4" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Avatar({ email, plan }: { email: string; plan: string }) {
  const hue = useMemo(() => [...email].reduce((h, ch) => (h * 31 + ch.charCodeAt(0)) % 360, 7), [email]);
  return (
    <span className="relative grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md text-lg font-bold text-white uppercase" style={{ background: `linear-gradient(135deg, hsl(${hue} 70% 45%), hsl(${(hue + 60) % 360} 70% 30%))` }}>
      {email[0]}
      {plan === "owner" && <Crown className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 size-4 rotate-12 text-amber-300 drop-shadow" />}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Step 2, inside your account: paste the key from the email, or buy one. */
function KeyPage({ email, stopped, onDone, onLoggedOut }: { email: string; stopped?: boolean; onDone: (s: AccessStatus) => void; onLoggedOut: (s: AccessStatus) => void }) {
  const [key, setKey] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [opened, setOpened] = useState(false);
  const read = useMemo(() => (key.trim().length > 20 ? readKey(key) : null), [key]);
  const badKey = key.trim().length > 20 && !read;
  const otherEmail = !!read && read.email !== email;

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!read || otherEmail) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      onDone(await api.accessAddKey(key));
    } catch (err) {
      setProblem(errorMessage(err));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };
  const buy = () =>
    api
      .openCheckout("lifetime")
      .then(() => setOpened(true))
      .catch((e) => setProblem(errorMessage(e)));
  const logOut = () => api.accessLogOut().then(onLoggedOut).catch((e) => setProblem(errorMessage(e)));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-7">
      <form className="access-form space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
        <div>
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.18em] text-accent uppercase">
            <KeyRound className="size-3.5" /> Your account
          </p>
          <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em]">Put your key here</h2>
          <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-muted">
            You're in as <span className="font-mono text-text">{email}</span>. Your key unlocks everything — it's in the email you got after paying.
          </p>
          {stopped && (
            <p className="mt-3 rounded-md border border-red-400/30 bg-red-400/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-red-200">
              The owner stopped your old key. Ask the owner for a new one and put it here — your macros and settings are still in your account.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        <Field label="Your key">
          <textarea className={clsx("field min-h-24 resize-none font-mono text-[11px] break-all", (badKey || otherEmail) && "border-red-400/60")} value={key} onChange={(e) => setKey(e.target.value.replace(/\s+/g, ""))} placeholder="SLNC-…" spellCheck={false} autoFocus />
        </Field>
        {read && !otherEmail && (
          <div className="access-card flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-accent/35 bg-accent/[0.06] px-4 py-3">
            <Check className="size-4 text-accent" />
            <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-sm">This key is for your account</span>
            <PlanBadge plan={read.plan} />
          </div>
        )}
        {otherEmail && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">This key is for {read?.email}, not for {email}. Use the key that was sent to {email}.</p>}
        {badKey && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">That doesn't look like a whole key — copy all of it from the email.</p>}
        {problem && <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{problem}</p>}
        <button className="btn-primary access-cta w-full justify-center py-3.5 text-base" disabled={!read || otherEmail || busy}>
          <Sparkles className="size-4" /> {busy ? "Unlocking…" : "Unlock SLNC Macro"}
        </button>
      </form>

      <div className="panel relative p-5">
        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
          <div>
            <p className="section-title">No key yet?</p>
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted">Lifetime: every feature and every future update. Pay once.</p>
          </div>
          <p className="text-right">
            <span className="num text-4xl font-semibold text-accent">€4</span>
            <span className="block font-mono text-[10px] tracking-wider text-muted uppercase">one time</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" className="btn-secondary mt-4 w-full justify-center py-2.5 font-semibold" onClick={buy}>
          Buy a key for €4 <ArrowRight className="size-4" />
        </button>
        <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-muted">
          Pay with <span className="font-mono text-text">{email}</span>, so the key is made for this account.
        </p>
        {opened && (
          <p className="mt-3 flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md border border-accent/30 bg-accent/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-accent-2">
            <Mail className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" /> The payment page opened in your browser. After paying, your key arrives by email.
          </p>
        )}
      </div>

      <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted">
        Not you?{" "}
        <button type="button" className="text-accent-2 hover:underline" onClick={logOut}>
          Log out
        </button>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Windows Hello opt-in (only shown when it's set up on this PC). */
function useHello() {
  const [available, setAvailable] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    api
      .accessHelloAvailable()
      .then((v) => live && setAvailable(v))
      .catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, []);
  return available;
}

function HelloOptIn({ checked, onChange }: { checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-line-strong bg-panel px-3.5 py-3 text-sm transition hover:border-accent/50">
      <input type="checkbox" className="mt-0.5 size-4 accent-[rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]" checked={checked} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.checked)} />
      <span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 font-semibold">
          <Fingerprint className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> Unlock with Windows Hello next time
        </span>
        <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-muted">Your PIN, fingerprint or face instead of typing the password, on this PC only.</span>
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

function CreateAccount({ reset, email: knownEmail, onBack, onLogin, onDone }: { reset?: boolean; email?: string; onBack: () => void; onLogin: () => void; onDone: (s: AccessStatus) => void }) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(reset ? "" : (knownEmail ?? ""));
  const [key, setKey] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [again, setAgain] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const helloAvailable = useHello();
  const [hello, setHello] = useState(false);
  // reset: the key from the email proves it's you, and gives the email
  const read = useMemo(() => (reset && key.trim().length > 20 ? readKey(key) : null), [reset, key]);
  const badKey = !!reset && key.trim().length > 20 && !read;
  const otherEmail = !!reset && !!knownEmail && !!read && read.email !== knownEmail;
  const typed = email.trim().toLowerCase();
  const emailOk = typed.length <= 254 && /^[^\s@]+@[^\s@.][^\s@]*\.[^\s@]*[^\s@.]$/.test(typed);
  const who = reset ? (read?.email ?? "") : emailOk ? typed : "";
  const unlocked = reset ? !!read && !otherEmail : emailOk;
  const check = usePasswordCheck(unlocked ? password : "", who);
  const mismatch = again.length > 0 && again !== password;
  const ready = unlocked && !!check?.ok && again === password;

  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!ready) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      onDone(await api.accessCreateAccount(who, reset ? key : null, password, hello));
    } catch (err) {
      setProblem(errorMessage(err));
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
      <BackButton onClick={onBack} />
      <div className="access-form space-y-5">
        {reset ? (
          <Heading icon={RotateCcw} title="Reset your password" text={`Your key from the email proves it's you${knownEmail ? ` (${knownEmail})` : ""}. Then choose a new password.`} />
        ) : (
          <Heading icon={UserPlus} title="Make your account" text="Your email and a password. Your key comes on the next page." />
        )}
        {reset ? (
          <>
            <Field label="Key from the email">
              <textarea className={clsx("field min-h-24 resize-none font-mono text-[11px] break-all", (badKey || otherEmail) && "border-red-400/60")} value={key} onChange={(e) => setKey(e.target.value.replace(/\s+/g, ""))} placeholder="SLNC-…" spellCheck={false} autoFocus />
            </Field>
            {read && !otherEmail && (
              <div className="access-card flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-accent/35 bg-accent/[0.06] px-4 py-3">
                <Avatar email={read.email} plan={read.plan} />
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <span className="block truncate font-semibold">{read.email}</span>
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted">This key is for this email</span>
                </span>
                <PlanBadge plan={read.plan} />
              </div>
            )}
            {otherEmail && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">This key is for {read?.email}, not {knownEmail}.</p>}
            {badKey && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">That doesn't look like a whole key — copy all of it from the email.</p>}
            {!read && !badKey && <p className="-mt-1 text-xs text-muted">Paste your key first — then choose a password.</p>}
          </>
        ) : (
          <Field label="Email">
            <input className="field" type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} placeholder="you@example.com" autoFocus />
          </Field>
        )}
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label={reset ? "New password" : "Password"}>
            <PasswordInput autoComplete="new-password" value={password} onChange={setPassword} disabled={!unlocked} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Password again">
            <PasswordInput autoComplete="new-password" value={again} onChange={setAgain} disabled={!unlocked} bad={mismatch} />
          </Field>
        </div>
        {!reset && !emailOk && <p className="-mt-2 text-xs text-muted">Type your email first — then choose a password.</p>}
        {unlocked && <StrengthMeter check={check} length={[...password].length} />}
        {mismatch && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">The passwords are not the same.</p>}
        {unlocked && helloAvailable && <HelloOptIn checked={hello} onChange={setHello} />}
        {problem && <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{problem}</p>}
        <button className="btn-primary access-cta w-full justify-center py-3.5 text-base" disabled={!ready || busy}>
          <ShieldCheck className="size-4" /> {busy ? "Opening…" : reset ? "Save new password" : "Make my account"}
        </button>
        <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted">
          Your password never leaves this PC: only an Argon2id hash is kept, in a file encrypted for your Windows account.{" "}
          {!reset && (
            <button type="button" className="text-accent-2 hover:underline" onClick={onLogin}>
              Already have an account?
            </button>
          )}
        </p>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

function LogIn_({ email: initial, accounts, onRefresh, onBack, onCreate, onForgot, onDone }: { email: string; accounts: AccessAccount[]; onRefresh: () => void; onBack: () => void; onCreate: () => void; onForgot: (email: string) => void; onDone: (s: AccessStatus) => void }) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState(initial);
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const helloAvailable = useHello();
  const saved = accounts.find((a) => a.email === email.trim().toLowerCase());
  const [hello, setHello] = useState(false);
  // A running wait after too many wrong passwords: count it down here.
  const [waitUntil, setWaitUntil] = useState(0);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    setWaitUntil(saved?.wait_s ? Date.now() + saved.wait_s * 1000 : 0);
  }, [saved?.wait_s, saved?.email]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (waitUntil <= Date.now()) return;
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 250);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [waitUntil]);
  const waitLeft = Math.max(0, Math.ceil((waitUntil - now) / 1000));

  const run = async (go: () => Promise<AccessStatus>) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setProblem(null);
    try {
      onDone(await go());
    } catch (err) {
      setProblem(errorMessage(err));
      setBusy(false);
      setPassword("");
      onRefresh(); // picks up a new wait after too many tries
    }
  };
  const submit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    void run(() => api.accessLogIn(email, password, helloAvailable && !saved?.hello ? hello : undefined));
  };

  return (
    <form className="space-y-5" onSubmit={submit}>
      <BackButton onClick={onBack} />
      <div className="access-form space-y-5">
        {initial ? (
          <div>
            <p className="section-title">Log in</p>
            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-4">
              <Avatar email={initial} plan={saved?.plan ?? ""} />
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h2 className="text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em]">Welcome back</h2>
                <p className="truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted">{initial}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Heading icon={LogIn} title="Log in" text="With the email and password of your account." />
        )}
        {saved?.hello && helloAvailable && (
          <>
            <button type="button" className="btn-secondary w-full justify-center py-3.5 text-base font-semibold" disabled={busy} onClick={() => void run(() => api.accessLogInHello(saved.email))}>
              <Fingerprint className="size-5 text-accent-2" /> Unlock with Windows Hello
            </button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-widest text-muted uppercase">
              <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-white/10" /> or your password <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-white/10" />
            </div>
          </>
        )}
        {!initial && (
          <Field label="Email">
            <input className="field" type="email" autoComplete="username" value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} autoFocus />
          </Field>
        )}
        <Field label="Password">
          <PasswordInput autoComplete="current-password" value={password} onChange={setPassword} autoFocus={!!initial && !saved?.hello} disabled={waitLeft > 0} />
        </Field>
        {waitLeft > 0 ? (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-amber-300/30 bg-amber-300/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-amber-200" role="status">
            <LockKeyhole className="size-4 shrink-0" /> Too many wrong passwords. You can try again in <span className="num font-semibold">{waitLeft}s</span>.
          </p>
        ) : (
          problem && <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{problem}</p>
        )}
        {helloAvailable && saved && !saved.hello && <HelloOptIn checked={hello} onChange={setHello} />}
        <button className="btn-primary access-cta w-full justify-center py-3.5 text-base" disabled={!email || !password || busy || waitLeft > 0}>
          <LogIn className="size-4" /> {busy ? "Opening…" : "Log in"}
        </button>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs text-muted">
          <button type="button" className="text-accent-2 hover:underline" onClick={() => onForgot(email.trim().toLowerCase())}>
            Forgot your password?
          </button>
          <button type="button" className="hover:text-text hover:underline" onClick={onCreate}>
            New here? Make an account
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

function Heading({ icon: Icon, title, text }: { icon: typeof LogIn; title: string; text: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.18em] text-accent uppercase">
        <Icon className="size-3.5" /> Account
      </p>
      <h2 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em]">{title}</h2>
      <p className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-muted">{text}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block space-y-2">
      <span className="label tracking-wide">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

function BackButton({ onClick }: { onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" className="btn-ghost -ml-2 px-2 font-mono text-xs tracking-wider uppercase" onClick={onClick}>
      <ArrowLeft className="size-4" /> Back
    </button>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/access/AccountSecurity.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Fingerprint, KeyRound, ShieldCheck } from "lucide-react";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../../lib/tauri";
import type { AccessStatus } from "../../lib/types";
import { Toggle } from "../PlaybackPanel";
import { PasswordInput, StrengthMeter, usePasswordCheck } from "./password";

/**
 * Settings → Account security: change the password (the current one is needed; the new one
 * follows the same rules as a new account) and switch Windows Hello unlocking on or off.
 */
export function AccountSecurity() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AccessStatus | null>(null);
  const [helloAvailable, setHelloAvailable] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    api.accessStatus().then(setStatus).catch(() => {});
    api.accessHelloAvailable().then(setHelloAvailable).catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  const me = status?.accounts.find((a) => a.email === status.email);

  const [current, setCurrent] = useState("");
  const [next, setNext] = useState("");
  const [again, setAgain] = useState("");
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [note, setNote] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  const check = usePasswordCheck(next, status?.email ?? "");
  const mismatch = again.length > 0 && again !== next;
  const ready = !!current && !!check?.ok && again === next;

  const save = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!ready) return;
    setBusy(true);
    setNote(null);
    try {
      setStatus(await api.accessChangePassword(current, next));
      setCurrent("");
      setNext("");
      setAgain("");
      setNote({ ok: true, text: "Password changed." });
    } catch (err) {
      setNote({ ok: false, text: errorMessage(err) });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const setHello = (on: boolean) =>
    api
      .accessSetHello(on)
      .then(setStatus)
      .catch((err) => setNote({ ok: false, text: errorMessage(err) }));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      {me?.weak_password && (
        <p className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-xl border border-amber-300/30 bg-amber-300/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-amber-200">
          <ShieldCheck className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" /> Your password is from before the 15-character rule. Choose a longer one below for better protection.
        </p>
      )}
      <form className="space-y-3" onSubmit={save}>
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
          <KeyRound className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> Change password
        </p>
        <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">Current password</span>
            <PasswordInput autoComplete="current-password" value={current} onChange={setCurrent} />
          </label>
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">New password</span>
            <PasswordInput autoComplete="new-password" value={next} onChange={setNext} />
          </label>
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">New password again</span>
            <PasswordInput autoComplete="new-password" value={again} onChange={setAgain} bad={mismatch} />
          </label>
        </div>
        <StrengthMeter check={check} length={[...next].length} />
        {mismatch && <p className="text-xs text-red-300">The new passwords are not the same.</p>}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <button className="btn-primary" disabled={!ready || busy}>
            {busy ? "Saving…" : "Change password"}
          </button>
          {note && <span className={note.ok ? "text-sm text-emerald-300" : "text-sm text-red-300"}>{note.text}</span>}
        </div>
      </form>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-white/5 pt-4">
        <span>
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold">
            <Fingerprint className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> Unlock with Windows Hello
          </span>
          <span className="mt-0.5 block text-xs text-muted">
            {helloAvailable ? "Your PIN, fingerprint or face instead of the password, on this PC only." : "Set up Windows Hello first: Windows Settings → Accounts → Sign-in options."}
          </span>
        </span>
        <Toggle checked={!!me?.hello} onChange={setHello} disabled={!helloAvailable || !me} />
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">
        Your password never leaves this PC: only an Argon2id hash is kept, in a file encrypted for your Windows account. After 5 wrong passwords the app makes you wait, longer each time. Forgot it? Log out and use “Forgot your password?” with the key from your email.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/access/LogoHero.tsx

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { SM } from "../shell/sm-shape";

/**
 * The SM logo as a hero, fully vector: the letters (with a thin diagonal cut) are drawn from the original logo
 * shape (docs/logo/sm-logo-v5-shape.json, 1024 tile units), so they are razor sharp at any size.
 *
 * Opening: the dial ticks switch on one by one, the letters trace themselves in light and fill,
 * then a shockwave rolls out.
 * Loop (one 6 s clock, --ha in styles.css): a comet orbits with a glowing tail and lights the
 * ticks as it passes; when it reaches the top right, a shine sweeps over the letters, the rim flares and a shockwave rolls out. Sparks swirl around the ring
 * on a device-pixel canvas. The logo tilts slightly towards the mouse.
 */

const DEPTH = [9, 8, 7, 6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1];

function Sparks() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const c = ref.current;
    const x = c?.getContext("2d");
    if (!c || !x) return;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches || document.documentElement.dataset.motion === "reduced";
    if (reduced) return;
    const accent = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue("--accent-2-rgb").trim() || "134 239 172";
    const parts = Array.from({ length: 34 }, () => ({
      a: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
      r: 0.36 + Math.random() * 0.12,
      v: (0.25 + Math.random() * 0.55) * (Math.random() < 0.8 ? 1 : -1),
      s: 0.6 + Math.random() * 1.1,
      p: Math.random() * Math.PI * 2,
      cyan: Math.random() < 0.3,
    }));
    let W = 0, dpr = 1;
    const size = () => {
      dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2.5);
      W = c.clientWidth;
      c.width = c.height = Math.floor(W * dpr);
      x.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    };
    size();
    const ro = new ResizeObserver(size);
    ro.observe(c);
    let raf = 0, last = performance.now();
    const frame = (t: number) => {
      const dt = Math.min(0.05, (t - last) / 1000);
      last = t;
      x.clearRect(0, 0, W, W);
      x.globalCompositeOperation = "lighter";
      x.lineCap = "round";
      const cx = W / 2;
      for (const p of parts) {
        p.a += p.v * dt;
        const tw = 0.5 + 0.5 * Math.sin(t / 1000 * (1.5 + p.s) + p.p);
        const r = W * (p.r + Math.sin(t / 1700 + p.p) * 0.012);
        const col = p.cyan ? "103 232 249" : accent;
        // a short trail behind the spark
        x.strokeStyle = `rgb(${col} / ${0.32 * tw})`;
        x.lineWidth = p.s;
        x.beginPath();
        x.arc(cx, cx, r, p.a - 0.16 * Math.sign(p.v), p.a, p.v < 0);
        x.stroke();
        x.fillStyle = `rgb(255 255 255 / ${0.55 + 0.45 * tw})`;
        x.beginPath();
        x.arc(cx + Math.cos(p.a) * r, cx + Math.sin(p.a) * r, p.s * 0.9, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        x.fill();
      }
      if (!document.hidden) raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
    const onVis = () => {
      if (!document.hidden) {
        last = performance.now();
        raf = requestAnimationFrame(frame);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    return () => {
      cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
      ro.disconnect();
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVis);
    };
  }, []);
  return <canvas ref={ref} className="hero-sparks pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 size-full" aria-hidden />;
}

function SmLogo() {
  return (
    <svg className="size-full overflow-visible" viewBox="8 8 1008 1008" aria-hidden>
      <defs>
        <radialGradient id="sm-bg" cx="50%" cy="30%" r="80%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#163a31" />
          <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#081313" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#030607" />
        </radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="sm-glow" cx="50%" cy="50%" r="50%">
          <stop offset="0%" style={{ stopColor: "rgb(var(--accent-rgb))", stopOpacity: 0.32 }} />
          <stop offset="100%" style={{ stopColor: "rgb(var(--accent-rgb))", stopOpacity: 0 }} />
        </radialGradient>
        <radialGradient id="sm-cool" cx="50%" cy="120%" r="70%">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#22d3ee" stopOpacity="0.28" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#22d3ee" stopOpacity="0" />
        </radialGradient>
        <linearGradient id="sm-face" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#b4f8d4" />
          <stop offset="40%" stopColor="#6ee7b0" />
          <stop offset="78%" stopColor="#4fd8b4" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#6ee9e0" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id="sm-side" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#0f5c46" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#062a20" />
        </linearGradient>
        <linearGradient id="sm-shine" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0">
          <stop offset="0%" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity="0" />
          <stop offset="50%" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity="0.75" />
          <stop offset="100%" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity="0" />
        </linearGradient>
        <clipPath id="sm-circle">
          <circle cx="512" cy="512" r="504" />
        </clipPath>
        <clipPath id="sm-letters">
          <path d={SM} />
        </clipPath>
        {/* a thin diagonal cut through the letters */}
        <mask id="sm-cut" maskUnits="userSpaceOnUse" x="0" y="0" width="1024" height="1024">
          <rect width="1024" height="1024" fill="#fff" />
          <line x1="118" y1="892" x2="930" y2="118" stroke="#000" strokeWidth="18" />
        </mask>
        <filter id="sm-blur" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%">
          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="14" />
        </filter>
        <filter id="sm-soft" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%">
          <feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="5" />
        </filter>
      </defs>

      <g clipPath="url(#sm-circle)">
        {/* the disc: dark green-black, light behind the letters, cool light from below, fine grid */}
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="1024" height="1024" fill="url(#sm-bg)" />
        <circle cx="512" cy="500" r="430" fill="url(#sm-glow)" className="sm-disc-glow" />
        <rect x="0" y="0" width="1024" height="1024" fill="url(#sm-cool)" />
        <g opacity="0.05" stroke="rgb(134 239 172)" strokeWidth="1.5">
          {Array.from({ length: 15 }, (_, i) => (i + 1) * 64).map((v) => (
            <g key={v}>
              <line x1={v} y1="0" x2={v} y2="1024" />
              <line x1="0" y1={v} x2="1024" y2={v} />
            </g>
          ))}
        </g>

        {/* letters */}
        <g mask="url(#sm-cut)">
          <g className="sm-fill">
            {/* glow under the letters */}
            <path d={SM} fill="rgb(45 212 150)" opacity="0.45" filter="url(#sm-blur)" />
            {/* depth: the side of the letters */}
            {DEPTH.map((i) => (
              <path key={i} d={SM} fill="url(#sm-side)" transform={`translate(${i * 1.4} ${i * 2.2})`} />
            ))}
            {/* face, its dark edge and a bevel light on the top-left edges */}
            <path d={SM} fill="url(#sm-face)" stroke="#0d4f3c" strokeWidth="5" strokeLinejoin="round" />
            <g clipPath="url(#sm-letters)">
              <path d={SM} fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" strokeOpacity="0.55" strokeWidth="7" strokeLinejoin="round" transform="translate(3 4)" />
              <path d={SM} fill="none" stroke="#0b3f30" strokeOpacity="0.35" strokeWidth="10" strokeLinejoin="round" transform="translate(-4 -5)" />
              {/* shine sweeping over the letters */}
              <g transform="rotate(22 512 512)">
                <rect className="sm-shine" x="-260" y="-200" width="150" height="1424" fill="url(#sm-shine)" />
              </g>
            </g>
          </g>
          {/* light tracing the letters on opening */}
          <path className="sm-trace" d={SM} pathLength={1} fill="none" stroke="#d9fff0" strokeWidth="6" strokeLinejoin="round" />
        </g>

      </g>
    </svg>
  );
}

export function LogoHero() {
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [tilt, setTilt] = useState({ x: 0, y: 0 });
  const onMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const r = box.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dx = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5;
    const dy = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5;
    setTilt({ x: -dy * 12, y: dx * 12 });
  };
  const ticks = Array.from({ length: 72 }, (_, i) => i);
  const dial = (lit: boolean) => (
    <svg className="absolute inset-0 size-full overflow-visible" viewBox="0 0 400 400" aria-hidden>
      <g className="hero-dial">
        {ticks.map((i) => {
          const major = i % 6 === 0;
          return (
            <line
              key={i}
              className={lit ? undefined : "hero-tick"}
              x1="200"
              y1={major ? 12 : 16}
              x2="200"
              y2="22"
              style={{
                stroke: lit ? (major ? "#ffffff" : "rgb(var(--accent-2-rgb))") : major ? "rgb(var(--accent-2-rgb) / 0.55)" : "rgb(255 255 255 / 0.12)",
                animationDelay: lit ? undefined : `${100 + i * 8}ms`,
              }}
              strokeWidth={major ? 1.8 : 1.2}
              strokeLinecap="round"
              transform={`rotate(${i * 5} 200 200)`}
            />
          );
        })}
      </g>
    </svg>
  );

  return (
    <div ref={box} className="hero-stage relative grid place-items-center" style={{ width: "min(380px, 40vh)", height: "min(380px, 40vh)" }} onMouseMove={onMove} onMouseLeave={() => setTilt({ x: 0, y: 0 })}>
      {/* soft glow behind everything, flaring when the comet reaches the top right */}
      <div className="hero-glow absolute inset-[20%] rounded-full bg-accent/25 blur-3xl" aria-hidden />

      {/* dial: dim ticks, and a lit copy that only shows just behind the comet */}
      {dial(false)}
      <div className="hero-lit absolute inset-0" aria-hidden>
        {dial(true)}
      </div>

      {/* HUD: a dotted ring outside, three turning segments inside */}
      <svg className="absolute inset-0 size-full overflow-visible" viewBox="0 0 400 400" aria-hidden>
        <circle className="hero-dots" cx="200" cy="200" r="166" fill="none" stroke="rgb(255 255 255 / 0.18)" strokeWidth="1.3" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray="0.01 6.95" />
        <circle className="hero-hud" cx="200" cy="200" r="140" fill="none" style={{ stroke: "rgb(var(--accent-2-rgb) / 0.4)" }} strokeWidth="1.4" strokeDasharray="46 247.2" />
        {/* shockwaves: one on opening, one each time the comet reaches the top right */}
        <circle className="hero-shock hero-shock-open" cx="200" cy="200" r="130" fill="none" stroke="#ffffff" strokeWidth="2" />
        <circle className="hero-shock hero-shock-loop" cx="200" cy="200" r="130" fill="none" style={{ stroke: "rgb(var(--accent-2-rgb))" }} strokeWidth="1.6" />
      </svg>

      {/* the track, the comet's tail (blurred glow + sharp line) and its head */}
      <div className="hero-track absolute inset-0 rounded-full" aria-hidden />
      <div className="hero-tail hero-tail-glow absolute inset-0 rounded-full" aria-hidden />
      <div className="hero-tail absolute inset-0 rounded-full" aria-hidden />
      <div className="hero-head-orbit absolute inset-0" aria-hidden>
        <span className="hero-head" />
      </div>

      <Sparks />

      {/* the vector logo filling the inner circle */}
      <div className="hero-float relative size-[64%]">
        <div className="hero-logo size-full" style={{ transform: `perspective(900px) rotateX(${tilt.x}deg) rotateY(${tilt.y}deg)` }}>
          <div className="hero-pop size-full rounded-full">
            <SmLogo />
          </div>
          <div className="hero-rim pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 rounded-full" aria-hidden />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/access/password.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Eye, EyeOff, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../../lib/tauri";
import type { PasswordCheck } from "../../lib/types";

/**
 * Password pieces shared by the start pages and Settings → Account security.
 * The rules themselves live in the app (src-tauri/src/secure.rs: NIST SP 800-63B-4 + zxcvbn);
 * these only show them.
 */

/** Password field with a show/hide eye and a Caps Lock warning. */
export function PasswordInput({ value, onChange, autoComplete, disabled, autoFocus, bad, id }: { value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void; autoComplete: string; disabled?: boolean; autoFocus?: boolean; bad?: boolean; id?: string }) {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false);
  const [caps, setCaps] = useState(false);
  const watchCaps = (e: React.KeyboardEvent | React.MouseEvent) => setCaps(e.getModifierState("CapsLock"));
  return (
    <span className="block">
      <span className="relative block">
        <input
          id={id}
          className={clsx("field pr-10", bad && "border-red-400/60")}
          type={show ? "text" : "password"}
          autoComplete={autoComplete}
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)}
          onKeyDown={watchCaps}
          onKeyUp={watchCaps}
          onMouseDown={watchCaps}
          onBlur={() => setCaps(false)}
          disabled={disabled}
          autoFocus={autoFocus}
          spellCheck={false}
          maxLength={128}
        />
        {!disabled && (
          <button type="button" tabIndex={-1} className="absolute inset-y-0 right-2 my-auto grid size-7 place-items-center rounded-md text-muted transition hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-text" onClick={() => setShow((v) => !v)} aria-label={show ? "Hide password" : "Show password"}>
            {show ? <EyeOff className="size-4" /> : <Eye className="size-4" />}
          </button>
        )}
      </span>
      {caps && (
        <span className="mt-1.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-amber-300" role="status">
          <TriangleAlert className="size-3.5" /> Caps Lock is on
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

/** Asks the app how good a new password is (debounced; nothing leaves the PC). */
export function usePasswordCheck(password: string, email: string): PasswordCheck | null {
  const [check, setCheck] = useState<PasswordCheck | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!password) {
      setCheck(null);
      return;
    }
    let live = true;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => {
      api
        .accessPasswordCheck(password, email)
        .then((c) => live && setCheck(c))
        .catch(() => live && setCheck(null));
    }, 120);
    return () => {
      live = false;
      window.clearTimeout(t);
    };
  }, [password, email]);
  return check;
}

const LEVELS = [
  { label: "Very weak", bar: "bg-red-500" },
  { label: "Weak", bar: "bg-orange-500" },
  { label: "Fair", bar: "bg-amber-400" },
  { label: "Strong", bar: "bg-emerald-400" },
  { label: "Very strong", bar: "bg-accent" },
];

/** Four-part strength bar + what the app says about the password. */
export function StrengthMeter({ check, length }: { check: PasswordCheck | null; length: number }) {
  if (!length) {
    return <p className="text-xs text-muted">At least 15 characters. A few random words is easiest to remember — any characters, spaces and emoji are fine.</p>;
  }
  const score = check?.score ?? 0;
  const level = LEVELS[score]!;
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="flex gap-1" aria-hidden>
        {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => (
          <span key={i} className={clsx("h-1.5 flex-1 rounded-full transition-colors duration-300", score >= i ? level.bar : "bg-white/10")} />
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3 text-xs">
        <span className={clsx("font-semibold", check?.ok ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-amber-300")}>
          {check ? (check.ok ? level.label : length < 15 ? `${length} / 15 characters` : level.label) : "Checking…"}
        </span>
        {check?.ok && <span className="text-muted">Would take {check.crack_time} to crack</span>}
      </div>
      {check?.problem && length >= 15 && <p className="text-xs text-amber-200">{check.problem}</p>}
      {check && !check.ok && check.tips[0] && length >= 15 && <p className="text-xs text-muted">{check.tips[0]}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/fx/Scramble.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

const GLYPHS = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789#%&*+=/<>";

/**
 * Text that decodes into place: random glyphs that settle letter by letter, left to right.
 * Re-runs when `text` changes. With reduced motion it simply shows the text.
 */
export function Scramble({ text, delay = 0, speed = 28, className }: { text: string; delay?: number; speed?: number; className?: string }) {
  const [out, setOut] = useState(() => text.replace(/\S/g, " "));

  useEffect(() => {
    const root = document.documentElement;
    if (root.dataset.motion === "reduced") {
      setOut(text);
      return;
    }
    let timer = 0;
    const start = window.setTimeout(() => {
      // Letters settle by elapsed time, not by ticks: when Windows slows timers down (window in
      // the tray or minimised) the text still finishes instead of staying scrambled.
      const began = performance.now();
      timer = window.setInterval(() => {
        const settled = Math.floor((performance.now() - began) / (speed * 2.2));
        setOut(
          text
            .split("")
            .map((c, i) => (c === " " || i < settled ? c : GLYPHS[Math.floor(Math.random() * GLYPHS.length)]!))
            .join(""),
        );
        if (settled >= text.length) window.clearInterval(timer);
      }, speed);
    }, delay);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(start);
      window.clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, [text, delay, speed]);

  return (
    <span className={className} aria-label={text}>
      {out}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/fx/Word3D.tsx
====================================================================================================
import clsx from "clsx";

/**
 * A word as 3D outline lettering (used for MACRO): a bright green front outline with a faint glass
 * face, outline copies stepping back and darkening behind it (a wireframe extrusion), a dark core
 * at the back for body, and a light glint gliding over the front every few seconds.
 * The front layer is the real text (layout, selection, screen readers); the rest is decoration.
 * `size="sm"` (title bar): a solid face with a 2-step extrusion, since an outline fills in at 13 px.
 */
const DEPTH = [6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1];

export function Word3D({ text, size = "lg", className }: { text: string; size?: "lg" | "sm"; className?: string }) {
  const steps = size === "sm" ? [2, 1] : DEPTH;
  return (
    <span className={clsx("w3d", size === "sm" && "w3d-sm", className)}>
      <span className="w3d-core" aria-hidden>
        {text}
      </span>
      {steps.map((i) => (
        <span key={i} className="w3d-layer" style={{ "--i": i } as React.CSSProperties} aria-hidden>
          {text}
        </span>
      ))}
      <span className="w3d-front">{text}</span>
      {size === "lg" && (
        <span className="w3d-glint" aria-hidden>
          {text}
        </span>
      )}
    </span>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/shell/Dock.tsx

import clsx from "clsx";
import { NAV, type View } from "../../lib/nav";

/**
 * The dock: every section as an icon, floating at the bottom like a game HUD. Hovering (or
 * focusing) an icon shows its name above it; the active one is lit. A dot shows while a macro is
 * playing or recording. The owner sees the Owner icon too.
 */
export function Dock({ view, onNavigate, owner, busy }: { view: View; onNavigate: (v: View) => void; owner: boolean; busy: "recording" | "playing" | null }) {
  const items = NAV.filter((n) => n.id !== "owner" || owner);
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Sections" className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 bottom-4 z-30 flex justify-center px-4">
      <div className="pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-2xl border border-line-strong bg-panel/95 p-2 shadow-[0_18px_50px_-12px_rgb(0_0_0/0.85)] backdrop-blur">
        {items.map((n) => {
          const active = view === n.id;
          const live = n.id === "record" && busy;
          return (
            <button
              key={n.id}
              onClick={() => onNavigate(n.id)}
              aria-label={n.label}
              aria-current={active ? "page" : undefined}
              className={clsx(
                "group relative grid size-12 place-items-center rounded-xl transition duration-200",
                active ? "bg-accent/15 text-accent-2" : "text-muted hover:-translate-y-1 hover:bg-white/[0.07] hover:text-text focus-visible:-translate-y-1",
                n.id === "owner" && !active && "text-amber-300/80 hover:text-amber-200",
              )}
            >
              <n.icon className={clsx("size-[26px] transition duration-200", active ? "scale-110 drop-shadow-[0_0_8px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.7)]" : "group-hover:scale-105")} />
              {/* The name, shown on hover / keyboard focus */}
              <span
                role="tooltip"
                className="pointer-events-none absolute bottom-full mb-3 translate-y-1 rounded-md border border-line-strong bg-panel-2 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold whitespace-nowrap text-text opacity-0 shadow-lg transition duration-150 group-hover:translate-y-0 group-hover:opacity-100 group-focus-visible:translate-y-0 group-focus-visible:opacity-100"
              >
                {n.label}
                <span className="absolute top-full left-1/2 -ml-1 size-2 -translate-y-1 rotate-45 border-r border-b border-line-strong bg-panel-2" />
              </span>
              {active && <span className="absolute -bottom-1 h-0.5 w-5 rounded-full bg-accent shadow-[0_0_10px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]" />}
              {live && <span className={clsx("absolute top-1.5 right-1.5 size-2 rounded-full animate-pulse", busy === "recording" ? "bg-red-500" : "bg-accent")} />}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}

====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/shell/LoadingScreen.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Word3D } from "../fx/Word3D";
import { LogoMark } from "./Logo";
import { SM, SM_CUT } from "./sm-shape";

/**
 * Startup screen, "the axis": one precise vertical line on the left third carries the identity and
 * the real startup milestones; a huge cropped SM outline gives the screen depth. When everything
 * is ready the line fills, the outlined MACRO lights up, and the axis sweeps across to open the app.
 *
 * Progress is real: a mark lights only when its milestone is done (fonts/interface, engine reply,
 * settings, account status) — no percentages, no timers pretending to load. Purely presentation:
 * the app decides readiness, this only shows it.
 * - Stuck for 10 s → "Still loading" with Reload / Open app. An error before ready → the same
 *   actions plus the message. Never traps the user.
 * - Reduced motion: no ambient motion or sweep, short fades only (CSS).
 */

const STEPS = [
  { label: "Interface", busy: "Preparing the interface" },
  { label: "Engine", busy: "Waking the engine" },
  { label: "Your settings", busy: "Loading your settings" },
  { label: "Your account", busy: "Checking your account" },
] as const;

const ENTRY_MS = 750; // the entrance plays out before the ready state can show
const HOLD_MS = 380; // the ready state is seen for a moment
const EXIT_MS = 650; // the axis sweep
const STALL_MS = 10_000;

type Phase = "loading" | "stalled" | "failed" | "ready" | "leaving";

export function LoadingScreen({
  engine,
  settings,
  account,
  error,
  version,
  onDone,
}: {
  engine: boolean;
  settings: boolean;
  account: boolean;
  error: string | null;
  version: string;
  onDone: () => void;
}) {
  // Milestone 1: the interface can draw its final text (fonts loaded).
  const [fonts, setFonts] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    const done = () => live && setFonts(true);
    if (document.fonts?.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(done, done);
    else done();
    return () => {
      live = false;
    };
  }, []);
  const real = [fonts, engine, settings, account];
  const realCount = real.filter(Boolean).length;
  const allDone = realCount === real.length;

  // Each mark lights when its own milestone is done; the trace points at the first one still busy.
  const busy = real.findIndex((d) => !d);

  const [entered, setEntered] = useState(false);
  const [stalled, setStalled] = useState(false);
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>("loading");
  useEffect(() => {
    const a = window.setTimeout(() => setEntered(true), ENTRY_MS);
    const b = window.setTimeout(() => setStalled(true), STALL_MS);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(a);
      window.clearTimeout(b);
    };
  }, []);

  // The latest onDone, read when the exit ends (a re-render must not restart the exit).
  const finish = useRef(onDone);
  finish.current = onDone;

  const leave = () => setPhase((p) => (p === "leaving" ? p : "leaving"));

  // loading → ready once every milestone is lit and the entrance has played.
  const visualDone = allDone && entered;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase === "leaving" || phase === "ready") return;
    if (visualDone) setPhase("ready");
    else if (error && entered) setPhase("failed");
    else if (stalled) setPhase("stalled");
  }, [visualDone, error, entered, stalled, phase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== "ready") return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(leave, HOLD_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [phase]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== "leaving") return;
    const reduced = window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches || document.documentElement.dataset.motion === "reduced";
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => finish.current(), reduced ? 160 : EXIT_MS);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [phase]);

  // The action gets focus when it appears (nothing else on this screen is focusable).
  const reload = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const blocked = phase === "stalled" || phase === "failed";
  useEffect(() => {
    if (blocked) reload.current?.focus();
  }, [blocked]);

  const status =
    phase === "ready" || phase === "leaving"
      ? "Ready"
      : phase === "failed"
        ? "Couldn't finish starting"
        : phase === "stalled"
          ? "Still loading"
          : `${STEPS[Math.max(0, busy)]!.busy}…`;

  return (
    <div className="ld" data-phase={phase} data-entered={entered || undefined}>
      {/* depth: the SM letters as huge line art, cropped by the right edge */}
      <svg className="ld-shape" viewBox="130 250 780 520" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet" aria-hidden>
        <path className="ld-shape-line" d={SM} pathLength={1} />
        <line className="ld-shape-cut" {...SM_CUT} pathLength={1} />
      </svg>

      {/* the axis: draws in from the middle, sweeps across on exit */}
      <div className="ld-axis" aria-hidden>
        <span className="ld-axis-fill" />
      </div>

      <div className="ld-block">
        <LogoMark className="ld-mark" />
        <h1 className="ld-word">
          <span className="t3d" data-text="SLNC">
            SLNC
          </span>
          <Word3D text="MACRO" className="ld-word-macro" />
        </h1>
        <p className="ld-tag">Built for the grind.</p>

        {/* real milestones; the dots sit on the axis */}
        <div className="ld-steps" role="progressbar" aria-label="Starting SLNC MACRO" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={STEPS.length} aria-valuenow={realCount} aria-valuetext={`${realCount} of ${STEPS.length} steps done`}>
          {STEPS.map((s, i) => (
            <div key={s.label} className="ld-step" data-state={real[i] ? "done" : i === busy && !blocked ? "busy" : "todo"}>
              <span className="ld-dot" aria-hidden />
              <span className="ld-step-label">{s.label}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
          {phase === "loading" && busy >= 0 && <span className="ld-trace" style={{ "--ld-at": busy } as React.CSSProperties} aria-hidden />}
        </div>

        <div className="ld-status">
          <span role="status" aria-live="polite">
            <span className="sr-only">SLNC MACRO: </span>
            {status}
          </span>
          {phase === "failed" && error && <span className="ld-error">{error}</span>}
          {blocked && (
            <div className="ld-actions">
              <button ref={reload} type="button" className="ld-btn ld-btn-main" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
                Reload
              </button>
              <button type="button" className="ld-btn" onClick={leave}>
                Open app
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="ld-version num" aria-hidden>
        {version ? `v${version}` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import clsx from "clsx";
import logo from "../../assets/logo-sm.png";
import { Word3D } from "../fx/Word3D";

/**
 * SLNC Macro mark: the SM app icon itself (S = SLNC, M = Macro; made in Illustrator with

export function Logo({ size = "md" }: { size?: "md" | "lg" }) {

        <span className={clsx("t3d", size === "md" && "t3d-sm")} data-text="SLNC">SLNC</span>{" "}
        <Word3D text="MACRO" size={size === "md" ? "sm" : "lg"} />
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/shell/sm-shape.ts
====================================================================================================
/**
 * The SM letters as one vector shape (the S runs into the M), in logo tile units (1024 x 1024),
 * from docs/logo/sm-logo-v5-shape.json. The logo has a thin diagonal cut from (118, 892) to
 * (930, 118). Used by the start-screen hero and the loading screen.
 */
export const SM =
