FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/shell/Splash.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Word3D } from "../fx/Word3D";
import { LogoMark } from "./Logo";

/**
 * Short startup animation (≈1.1 s): the crystal mark tilts up out of the dark and the word mark
 * resolves. Not shown when reduced motion is on or the user turned it off; it never blocks input
 * for longer than the animation itself.
 */
export function Splash({ onDone }: { onDone: () => void }) {
  const [leaving, setLeaving] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const a = window.setTimeout(() => setLeaving(true), 900);
    const b = window.setTimeout(onDone, 1250);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(a);
      window.clearTimeout(b);
    };
  }, [onDone]);

  return (
    <div
      className="fixed inset-0 z-[100] grid place-items-center bg-[radial-gradient(600px_400px_at_50%_45%,rgb(var(--accent-deep-rgb)/0.35),transparent_70%),#050408]"
      style={leaving ? { animation: "splash-out 350ms ease forwards" } : undefined}
      onClick={onDone}
      aria-hidden
    >
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-6">
        <div className="relative grid size-36 place-items-center" style={{ animation: "splash-core 850ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1) both" }}>
          <div className="core-ring absolute inset-0 rounded-full" />
          <div className="absolute inset-4 rounded-full bg-[radial-gradient(circle,rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.35),transparent_70%)] blur-md" />
          <LogoMark className="relative size-20" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-lg font-semibold" style={{ animation: "splash-text 800ms 150ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1) both" }}>
          <span className="t3d" data-text="SLNC">SLNC</span>{" "}
          <Word3D text="MACRO" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/shell/TitleBar.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Copy, LogOut, Minus, Square, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { isTauri } from "../../lib/tauri";
import { navItem, type View } from "../../lib/nav";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../../lib/use-app-state";
import { Logo } from "./Logo";

/**
 * Custom title bar (the window has no Windows frame): drag area, logo, the page you're on, what is
 * running live, your access and log out, and the window buttons. The sections are in the dock at
 * the bottom. Close hides to the tray (lib.rs).
 */
/** `locked`: before access (start pages) only the logo and the window buttons show. */
export function TitleBar({ app, view, onNavigate, locked, onLogOut, plan }: { app: AppStateHook; view: View; onNavigate: (v: View) => void; locked?: boolean; onLogOut?: () => void; plan?: string | null }) {
  const [maximized, setMaximized] = useState(false);
  const win = useMemo(() => (isTauri ? import("@tauri-apps/api/window").then((m) => m.getCurrentWindow()) : null), []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!win) return;
    let unlisten: (() => void) | undefined;
    let alive = true;
    win.then(async (w) => {
      setMaximized(await w.isMaximized());
      const off = await w.onResized(async () => setMaximized(await w.isMaximized()));
      if (alive) unlisten = off;
      else off();
    });
    return () => {
      alive = false;
      unlisten?.();
    };
  }, [win]);

  const current = navItem(view);
  const live = [app.routine.running && `Fishing · ${app.routine.gold} perfect`, app.rebirth.running && `Rebirth · ${app.rebirth.rebirths}`].filter(Boolean) as string[];

  return (
    <header data-tauri-drag-region className="relative z-30 flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-3 border-b border-line bg-panel/90 pl-4">
      <button className="flex items-center" onClick={() => !locked && onNavigate("record")} title={locked ? undefined : "Record Macro"}>
        <Logo />
      </button>

      {!locked && (
        <>
          <span className="h-5 w-px bg-line" />
          <span data-tauri-drag-region className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2 text-sm">
            <current.icon className="size-4 text-accent" />
            <span className="truncate font-medium">{current.label}</span>
          </span>
        </>
      )}

      <div data-tauri-drag-region className="flex-1 self-stretch" />

      {!locked && live.map((l) => (
        <button key={l} onClick={() => onNavigate("record")} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-accent/40 bg-accent/10 px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold text-accent-2">
          <span className="size-1.5 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-accent" /> {l}
        </button>
      ))}

      {!locked && (
        <>
          {plan && (
            <span className={clsx("rounded-md border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide uppercase", plan === "owner" ? "border-amber-400/50 bg-amber-400/10 text-amber-200" : plan === "guest" ? "border-cyan-400/40 bg-cyan-400/10 text-cyan-200" : "border-accent/40 bg-accent/10 text-accent-2")} title="Your access">
              {plan}
            </span>
          )}
          {onLogOut && (
            <IconButton label="Log out" active={false} onClick={onLogOut}>
              <LogOut className="size-4" />
            </IconButton>
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {win ? (
        <div className="ml-1 flex h-full items-stretch">
          <WindowButton label="Minimize" onClick={() => win.then((w) => w.minimize())}>
            <Minus className="size-4" />
          </WindowButton>
          <WindowButton label={maximized ? "Restore" : "Maximize"} onClick={() => win.then((w) => w.toggleMaximize())}>
            {maximized ? <Copy className="size-3.5 -scale-x-100" /> : <Square className="size-3.5" />}
          </WindowButton>
          <WindowButton label="Close to tray" danger onClick={() => win.then((w) => w.close())}>
            <X className="size-4" />
          </WindowButton>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <span className="pr-3" />
      )}
    </header>
  );
}

function IconButton({ label, active, onClick, children }: { label: string; active: boolean; onClick: () => void; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button aria-label={label} title={label} onClick={onClick} className={clsx("grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-md transition", active ? "bg-white/[0.08] text-text" : "text-muted hover:bg-white/[0.06] hover:text-text")}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

function WindowButton({ label, onClick, danger, children }: { label: string; onClick: () => void; danger?: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button
      aria-label={label}
      title={label}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={clsx("grid w-12 place-items-center text-muted transition", danger ? "hover:bg-red-600 hover:text-white" : "hover:bg-white/8 hover:text-text")}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/ActionTable.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import type { MacroStep } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { flatten, samePath, totalDuration, type StepPath } from "../lib/step-tree";
import { stepMeta, type StepKind } from "../lib/step-meta";

interface ActionTableProps {
  steps: MacroStep[];
  filter: StepKind | "all";
  selected: StepPath | null;
  onSelect: (path: StepPath) => void;
  /** Step that is currently playing (from engine://progress), or null. */
  activePath: StepPath | null;
  /** Playback speed (delays animate at duration / speed). */
  speed: number;
  paused: boolean;
}

const ROW_H = 38;
const GRID = "grid-cols-[minmax(340px,1fr)_minmax(180px,0.8fr)]";

/**
 * Action list (left) with a time-aligned track per row (right), like a DAW timeline.
 * Bars sit at each step's start time; delays and loops get a width proportional to their duration.
 * During playback a violet playhead follows the running step (it glides through delays).
 */
export function ActionTable({ steps, filter, selected, onSelect, activePath, speed, paused }: ActionTableProps) {
  const rows = useMemo(() => flatten(steps), [steps]);
  const total = Math.max(1000, totalDuration(steps));
  const visible = rows.filter((r) => filter === "all" || stepMeta(r.step).kind === filter);
  const ticks = useMemo(() => axisTicks(total), [total]);
  const rowRefs = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLButtonElement>());

  const active = activePath ? rows.find((r) => samePath(r.path, activePath)) : undefined;
  const activeKey = active?.path.join(".");

  // Keep the running step visible.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeKey) rowRefs.current.get(activeKey)?.scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" });
  }, [activeKey]);

  if (steps.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center text-sm text-muted">
        <p className="text-base text-text">No actions yet</p>
        <p>Press Start Recording, or add steps manually with “+ Add step”.</p>
      </div>
    );
  }

  // Playhead: at the step's start, or gliding to the end of a delay while it runs.
  const gliding = !!active && active.step.type === "delay" && !paused;
  const playheadMs = active ? active.startMs + (gliding ? active.durationMs : 0) : 0;
  const playheadStyle = {
    left: `${Math.min(100, (playheadMs / total) * 100)}%`,
    transition: gliding ? `left ${Math.max(0, active!.durationMs / speed)}ms linear` : "none",
  };

  return (
    <div className="relative flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className={clsx("grid shrink-0 border-b border-line text-[11px] tracking-wide text-muted uppercase", GRID)}>
        <div className="grid grid-cols-[44px_1fr_1fr_64px] items-center px-3 py-2">
          <span>#</span>
          <span>Action</span>
          <span>Details</span>
          <span className="text-right">Time</span>
        </div>
        <div
          className="relative cursor-pointer border-l border-line"
          title="Click to select the action at this time"
          onClick={(e) => {
            // Select the last visible action that starts at or before the clicked time.
            const rect = e.currentTarget.getBoundingClientRect();
            const t = ((e.clientX - rect.left) / rect.width) * total;
            const hit = [...visible].reverse().find((r) => r.startMs <= t) ?? visible[0];
            if (hit) onSelect(hit.path);
          }}
        >
          {ticks.map((t) => (
            <span key={t} className="absolute top-2 -translate-x-1/2 text-[10px] normal-case" style={{ left: `${(t / total) * 100}%` }}>
              {t === 0 ? "0s" : `${+(t / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`}
            </span>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Rows */}
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-x-hidden overflow-y-auto">
        {visible.map((row, i) => {
          const meta = stepMeta(row.step);
          const Icon = meta.icon;
          const key = row.path.join(".");
          const isSel = samePath(selected, row.path);
          const isActive = key === activeKey;
          const left = (row.startMs / total) * 100;
          const width = Math.max(1.6, (row.durationMs / total) * 100);
          return (
            <button
              key={key}
              ref={(el) => {
                if (el) rowRefs.current.set(key, el);
                else rowRefs.current.delete(key);
              }}
              onClick={() => onSelect(row.path)}
              style={{ height: ROW_H }}
              className={clsx(
                "grid w-full text-left text-sm transition",
                GRID,
                isActive ? "bg-accent/25" : isSel ? "bg-accent/15 shadow-[inset_3px_0_0_var(--accent)]" : "hover:bg-panel-3/60",
              )}
            >
              <div className="grid grid-cols-[44px_1fr_1fr_64px] items-center px-3">
                <span className={clsx("font-mono text-xs", isActive ? "text-white" : "text-muted")}>{isActive ? "▶" : String(i + 1).padStart(2, "0")}</span>
                <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2" style={{ paddingLeft: row.depth * 16 }}>
                  <Icon className={clsx("size-4 shrink-0", meta.kind === "mouse" ? "text-sky-300" : meta.kind === "key" ? "text-accent-2" : "text-amber-300")} />
                  <span className="truncate">{meta.label}</span>
                </span>
                <span className="truncate pr-2 text-muted">{meta.details}</span>
                <span className="text-right font-mono text-xs text-accent-2">{(row.startMs / 1000).toFixed(2)}s</span>
              </div>
              <div className="relative border-l border-line">
                {ticks.map((t) => (
                  <span key={t} className="absolute inset-y-0 w-px bg-line/60" style={{ left: `${(t / total) * 100}%` }} />
                ))}
                <span
                  className={clsx(
                    "absolute top-1/2 h-4 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-sm",
                    row.step.type === "delay" ? "bg-accent/30" : row.step.type === "loop" ? "border border-accent/60 bg-accent/10" : "bg-gradient-to-r from-accent-deep to-accent shadow-[0_0_10px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.6*var(--fx)))]",
                    (isSel || isActive) && "ring-2 ring-accent-2",
                  )}
                  style={{ left: `${Math.min(left, 98.4)}%`, width: `${Math.min(width, 100 - Math.min(left, 98.4))}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
        {visible.length === 0 && <p className="p-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">No actions of this type.</p>}
      </div>

      {/* Playhead overlay (spans the visible timeline column) */}
      {active && (
        <div className={clsx("pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 grid", GRID)} aria-hidden>
          <div />
          <div className="relative">
            <div className="absolute inset-y-0 w-0.5 -translate-x-1/2 bg-fuchsia-400 shadow-[0_0_10px_rgb(232_121_249/0.9)]" style={playheadStyle}>
              <span className="absolute -top-0.5 left-1/2 size-2.5 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-45 bg-fuchsia-300" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function axisTicks(total: number): number[] {
  const step = [250, 500, 1000, 2000, 5000, 10_000, 30_000, 60_000].find((s) => total / s <= 6) ?? 120_000;
  const out: number[] = [];
  for (let t = 0; t <= total; t += step) out.push(t);
  return out;
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/AutoClickerPanel.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { MousePointerClick, Play, Square } from "lucide-react";
import { HotkeyInput } from "./HotkeyInput";
import { Badge, Segmented } from "./ui";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AutoClickerSettings } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

/**
 * Auto clicker controls (Automation page). Settings save with the app settings; start/stop via
 * these buttons, the toggle hotkey or the emergency stop.
 */
export function AutoClickerPanel({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { settings, autoclicker, status, reportError } = app;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<AutoClickerSettings | null>(settings?.autoclicker ?? null);
  useEffect(() => setDraft(settings?.autoclicker ?? null), [settings?.autoclicker]);
  if (!settings || !draft) return null;

  const cps = 1000 / draft.interval_ms;
  const problem =
    draft.interval_ms < 10 ? "Interval must be at least 10 ms (100 clicks per second)." : draft.hold_ms >= draft.interval_ms ? "Hold time must be shorter than the interval." : null;
  const dirty = JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(settings.autoclicker);
  const busy = status.state !== "idle";

  const save = () => (problem ? Promise.resolve(false) : app.saveSettings({ ...settings, autoclicker: draft }));
  const start = async () => {
    if (dirty && !(await save())) return;
    api.autoclickerStart().catch(reportError);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Badge tone={autoclicker.running ? "accent" : "muted"}>{autoclicker.running ? "Running" : "Stopped"}</Badge>
        <span className="text-sm tabular-nums text-muted">{autoclicker.clicks.toLocaleString()} clicks</span>
        <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
          {autoclicker.running ? (
            <button className="btn-danger" onClick={() => api.autoclickerStop().catch(reportError)}>
              <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={start} disabled={!!problem || busy} title={busy ? "Stop recording/playback first" : undefined}>
              <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Start
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Mouse button</span>
          <Segmented
            value={draft.button}
            onChange={(button) => setDraft({ ...draft, button })}
            options={[
              { value: "left", label: "Left" },
              { value: "right", label: "Right" },
              { value: "middle", label: "Middle" },
            ]}
          />
        </label>
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Interval (ms) · {cps >= 10 ? cps.toFixed(0) : cps.toFixed(1)} clicks/s</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            className="field font-mono"
            min={10}
            max={3600000}
            value={draft.interval_ms}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, interval_ms: Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 0)) })}
          />
        </label>
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Hold each click (ms)</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            className="field font-mono"
            min={0}
            value={draft.hold_ms}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, hold_ms: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 0)) })}
          />
        </label>
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Stop after (clicks, 0 = until stopped)</span>
          <input
            type="number"
            className="field font-mono"
            min={0}
            value={draft.click_limit}
            onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, click_limit: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 0)) })}
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <span className="label">Start / stop hotkey</span>
        <HotkeyInput value={draft.hotkey} placeholder="No hotkey — click to set" onChange={(hotkey) => void app.saveSettings({ ...settings, autoclicker: problem ? { ...settings.autoclicker, hotkey } : { ...draft, hotkey } })} />
      </div>

      {problem && <p className="text-xs text-amber-300">{problem}</p>}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => void save()} disabled={!dirty || !!problem}>
          Save settings
        </button>
        <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted">
          <MousePointerClick className="size-3.5 text-accent-2" />
          Clicks where the mouse is. Emergency stop ({settings.stop_playback_hotkey}) always stops it.
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/AutoRebirthPanel.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { Play, RotateCw, Square } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "./ui";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

/**
 * Auto rebirth (rebirth.rs): watches the game for "100%" at the Rebirth button, opens it and
 * clicks the green REBIRTH button as soon as it is green.
 */
export function AutoRebirthPanel({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { settings, rebirth, status, reportError } = app;
  if (!settings) return null;
  const busy = status.state !== "idle" || app.autoclicker.running || app.routine.running;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Badge tone={rebirth.running ? "accent" : "muted"}>{rebirth.running ? rebirth.phase || "Running" : "Stopped"}</Badge>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm tabular-nums text-muted">
          <RotateCw className="size-3.5" /> {rebirth.rebirths} rebirths
        </span>
        <div className="ml-auto">
          {rebirth.running ? (
            <button className="btn-danger" onClick={() => api.rebirthStop().catch(reportError)}>
              <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => api.rebirthStart().catch(reportError)} disabled={busy} title={busy ? "Stop what is running first" : undefined}>
              <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Start
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      {!rebirth.running && rebirth.note && <p className="text-sm text-amber-300">{rebirth.note}</p>}
      <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-muted">
        <li>Brings Roblox to the front and clicks Rebirth at the left of the game.</li>
        <li>Keeps the window open while the REBIRTH button is grey and clicks it the moment it turns green.</li>
        <li>The window closes; it clicks Rebirth again. Repeats until you stop it.</li>
        <li>Built for AFK: the PC stays awake, the mouse moves a little every few minutes so Roblox doesn't kick you for being idle, and the game comes back to the front when you're away.</li>
      </ol>
      <p className="text-xs text-muted">
        Hotkey {settings.rebirth.start_hotkey || "none (set one under Hotkeys)"}. Clicks only go to the game while it is the active window; otherwise it pauses until you have been away for a minute, then brings the game back. Stops with {settings.stop_playback_hotkey}, the top-left corner or Stop.
        Check that the game's rules allow automation.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/Banner.tsx

import { X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";

const TONES = {
  error: "bg-red-950/80 text-red-200 border-red-500/30",
  info: "bg-accent-deep/40 text-text border-accent/30",
  recording: "bg-red-600/90 text-white border-red-400/40 font-medium",
} as const;

export function Banner({ tone, children, onClose }: { tone: keyof typeof TONES; children: React.ReactNode; onClose?: () => void }) {
  return (
    <div role={tone === "error" ? "alert" : "status"} className={clsx("flex items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b px-4 py-2 text-sm", TONES[tone])}>
      <div className="flex flex-1 items-center gap-3">{children}</div>
      {onClose && (
        <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Dismiss" className="opacity-60 hover:opacity-100">
          <X className="size-4" />
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/DiscordCards.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Send } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { RoutineSettings } from "../lib/types";

export interface Embed {
  title: string;
  color: number;
  fields: { name: string; value: string }[];
  footer?: { text: string };
}

const CHANNELS = [
  { key: "discord_stats" as const, name: "your-stats" },
  { key: "discord_history" as const, name: "fishing-history" },
  { key: "discord_streaks" as const, name: "perfect-streak" },
];

/**
 * Fishing → Discord: the three live messages (one per channel) exactly as Discord shows them,
 * with sample numbers. While fishing, each message is edited in place every ~20 s.
 */
export function DiscordCards({ cfg }: { cfg: RoutineSettings }) {
  const [running, setRunning] = useState(true);
  const [embeds, setEmbeds] = useState<Embed[]>([]);
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ kind: "idle" | "busy" | "ok" | "err"; text?: string }>({ kind: "idle" });

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .discordPreviewEmbeds(cfg.account, cfg.daily_goal, running)
      .then((e) => setEmbeds(e as Embed[]))
      .catch(() => setEmbeds([]));
  }, [cfg.account, cfg.daily_goal, running]);

  const send = async () => {
    setState({ kind: "busy" });
    try {
      const n = await api.discordSendPreview();
      setState({ kind: "ok", text: `${n} preview message${n === 1 ? "" : "s"} sent — have a look in Discord` });
    } catch (e) {
      setState({ kind: "err", text: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="panel flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 p-5">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">One live message per channel</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">While you fish, each message updates itself in place (about every 20 s). Times like “in 6 minutes” keep counting in Discord on their own.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex rounded-lg border border-line p-0.5 text-sm">
          {[true, false].map((r) => (
            <button key={String(r)} onClick={() => setRunning(r)} className={clsx("rounded-md px-3 py-1 font-semibold transition", running === r ? "bg-accent/15 text-accent-2" : "text-muted hover:text-text")}>
              {r ? "While fishing" : "After stopping"}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <button className="btn-primary px-5" disabled={state.kind === "busy"} onClick={send}>
          <Send className="size-4" /> {state.kind === "busy" ? "Sending…" : "Send preview"}
        </button>
        {state.text && <p className={clsx("w-full text-xs", state.kind === "ok" ? "text-accent-2" : "text-red-300")}>{state.text}</p>}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 2xl:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-2">
        {embeds.map((e, i) => {
          const ch = CHANNELS[i]!;
          return (
            <section key={ch.name} className="space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
                <span className="font-bold text-text/90"># {ch.name}</span>
                {!cfg[ch.key] && !cfg.discord_webhook && <span className="chip text-amber-300">no webhook link</span>}
              </div>
              <DiscordEmbed embed={e} />
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** A Discord embed as Discord draws it: dark card, coloured bar, bold title, fields. */
export function DiscordEmbed({ embed }: { embed: Embed }) {
  const color = `#${embed.color.toString(16).padStart(6, "0")}`;
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg bg-[#2b2d31] p-4 font-['gg_sans','Segoe_UI',sans-serif] text-[15px] leading-[1.4] text-[#dbdee1]">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pb-2">
        <span className="grid size-8 place-items-center rounded-full bg-[#1e1f22] text-[11px] font-black text-accent">SM</span>
        <span className="font-semibold text-white">SLNC Macro</span>
        <span className="rounded bg-[#5865f2] px-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-white">APP</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex overflow-hidden rounded bg-[#2b2d31] shadow-[0_0_0_1px_rgb(255_255_255/0.04)]">
        <div className="w-1 shrink-0" style={{ background: color }} />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-3 bg-[#2b2d31] px-4 py-3">
          <p className="font-semibold text-white">{embed.title}</p>
          {embed.fields.map((f) => (
            <div key={f.name}>
              <p className="text-[14px] font-semibold text-white">{f.name}</p>
              <div className="space-y-0.5 text-[14px]">
                {f.value.split("\n").map((line, i) => (
                  <p key={i}>
                    <Markdown text={line} />
                  </p>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
          {embed.footer && <p className="pt-1 text-[12px] text-[#b5bac1]">{embed.footer.text}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** **bold** and Discord timestamps (<t:…:R> relative, <t:…:f> date and time). */
function Markdown({ text }: { text: string }) {
  const parts = text.split(/(\*\*[^*]+\*\*|<t:\d+:[Rf]>)/g).filter(Boolean);
  return (
    <>
      {parts.map((p, i) => {
        if (p.startsWith("**")) return <b key={i} className="font-semibold text-white">{p.slice(2, -2)}</b>;
        const t = /^<t:(\d+):([Rf])>$/.exec(p);
        if (t) {
          const ms = Number(t[1]) * 1000;
          return (
            <span key={i} className="rounded bg-white/[0.06] px-1">
              {t[2] === "R" ? relative(ms) : new Date(ms).toLocaleString([], { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}
            </span>
          );
        }
        return <span key={i}>{p}</span>;
      })}
    </>
  );
}

function relative(ms: number): string {
  const d = Math.round((ms - Date.now()) / 60000);
  const a = Math.abs(d);
  const text = a < 1 ? "a few seconds" : a < 60 ? `${a} minute${a === 1 ? "" : "s"}` : `${Math.round(a / 60)} hour${Math.round(a / 60) === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  return d >= 0 ? `in ${text}` : `${text} ago`;
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Bot, Check, KeyRound, Smartphone } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { RoutineSettings } from "../lib/types";

/**
 * Discord remote: start/stop fishing and see stats by DM'ing your own bot from your phone.
 * The bot token goes to the Windows Credential Manager; only `remote_owner_id` is obeyed.
 */
export function DiscordRemote({ cfg, onSave }: { cfg: RoutineSettings; onSave: (next: RoutineSettings) => void }) {
  const [token, setToken] = useState("");
  const [owner, setOwner] = useState(cfg.remote_owner_id);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<{ has_token: boolean; text: string }>({ has_token: false, text: "Off" });
  const [note, setNote] = useState<{ ok: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const load = () => api.discordRemoteStatus().then(setStatus).catch(() => {});
    load();
    const id = window.setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => setOwner(cfg.remote_owner_id), [cfg.remote_owner_id]);

  const saveToken = async () => {
    try {
      await api.discordRemoteSetToken(token);
      setToken("");
      const name = await api.discordRemoteTest();
      setNote({ ok: true, text: `Connected to ${name}.` });
      setStatus(await api.discordRemoteStatus());
    } catch (e) {
      setNote({ ok: false, text: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  };
  const ownerOk = /^\d{15,21}$/.test(owner.trim());

  return (
    <section className="panel space-y-4 p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <span className="raised grid size-10 place-items-center text-accent-2">
          <Smartphone className="size-5" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0">
          <h3 className="font-semibold">Remote control</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">DM your bot from your phone: start · stop · status · wrapped · help. Only your Discord account is obeyed.</p>
        </div>
        <label className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            className="size-4 accent-[rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]"
            checked={cfg.remote_enabled}
            disabled={!status.has_token || !ownerOk}
            onChange={(e) => onSave({ ...cfg, remote_enabled: e.target.checked, remote_owner_id: owner.trim() })}
          />
          On
        </label>
      </div>

      <p className={clsx("flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-3 py-2 text-xs", cfg.remote_enabled && status.text.startsWith("Listening") ? "border-accent/40 bg-accent/10 text-accent-2" : "border-line text-muted")}>
        <Bot className="size-4" /> {cfg.remote_enabled ? status.text : "Off"}
      </p>

      <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-2">
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <KeyRound className="size-3.5" /> Bot token {status.has_token && <Check className="size-3.5 text-accent-2" />}
          </span>
          <span className="flex gap-2">
            <input className="field font-mono" type="password" placeholder={status.has_token ? "Saved — paste a new one to replace" : "Paste your bot token"} value={token} onChange={(e) => setToken(e.target.value)} />
            <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0" onClick={saveToken} disabled={!token.trim()}>
              Save
            </button>
          </span>
        </label>
        <label className="space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Your Discord user ID</span>
          <span className="flex gap-2">
            <input className="field font-mono" placeholder="e.g. 123456789012345678" value={owner} onChange={(e) => setOwner(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))} />
            <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0" onClick={() => onSave({ ...cfg, remote_owner_id: owner.trim() })} disabled={!ownerOk || owner.trim() === cfg.remote_owner_id}>
              Save
            </button>
          </span>
        </label>
      </div>
      {note && <p className={clsx("text-xs", note.ok ? "text-accent-2" : "text-red-300")}>{note.text}</p>}

      <details className="text-xs text-muted">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer text-text">How to set it up (2 minutes)</summary>
        <ol className="mt-2 list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5">
          <li>Go to discord.com/developers/applications → New Application → give it a name.</li>
          <li>Bot → Reset Token → copy it and paste it above (it is kept in Windows' Credential Manager).</li>
          <li>OAuth2 → URL Generator → tick "bot" → open the link and add the bot to a server you're in.</li>
          <li>In Discord: Settings → Advanced → Developer Mode on → right-click your name → Copy User ID → paste it above.</li>
          <li>Switch it On. The bot DMs you "SLNC Macro is listening" — reply start, stop, status or wrapped.</li>
        </ol>
      </details>
    </section>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/FishingHistory.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ChevronDown, Flame, Fish, Package, RefreshCw, RotateCcw, Square, Sparkles, Trash2, TriangleAlert } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { FishingSession, HistoryEvent } from "../lib/types";
import { useDialogs } from "./ui";

/** How long, as "1 h 12 min" / "4 min" / "35 s". */
export function duration(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 1000));
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  if (h) return `${h} h ${m} min`;
  if (m) return `${m} min`;
  return `${s} s`;
}

const when = (at: number) => new Date(at).toLocaleString([], { weekday: "short", day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" });
const time = (at: number) => new Date(at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" });

/** Fishing history: every session, what it caught, its streaks and how it stopped. */
export function FishingHistory({ running, accountFilter = null, onWrapped }: { running: boolean; accountFilter?: string | null; onWrapped?: (start: number) => void }) {
  const [loaded, setSessions] = useState<FishingSession[] | null>(null);
  const sessions = loaded && (accountFilter === null ? loaded : loaded.filter((s) => (s.account ?? "") === accountFilter));
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const dialog = useDialogs();

  const load = useCallback(() => {
    api
      .getFishingHistory(100)
      .then(setSessions)
      .catch(() => setSessions([]));
  }, []);

  // Fresh numbers while it fishes, and once more when it stops.
  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    if (!running) return;
    const id = window.setInterval(load, 5000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [running, load]);

  const clear = async () => {
    const ok = await dialog.confirm({ title: "Clear fishing history?", message: "All sessions are removed from this PC. This can't be undone.", confirmText: "Clear", danger: true });
    if (!ok) return;
    await api.clearFishingHistory().catch(() => {});
    load();
  };

  if (!sessions) return <p className="p-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">Loading history…</p>;

  const all = sessions.reduce(
    (a, s) => ({
      perfect: a.perfect + s.perfect,
      casts: a.casts + s.casts,
      catches: a.catches + s.catches,
      best: Math.max(a.best, s.best_streak),
      broken: a.broken + s.streaks_broken,
      ms: a.ms + ((s.end ?? Date.now()) - s.start),
    }),
    { perfect: 0, casts: 0, catches: 0, best: 0, broken: 0, ms: 0 },
  );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-5">
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-px overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-line md:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6">
        <Total label="Sessions" value={sessions.length.toLocaleString()} />
        <Total label="Time fished" value={duration(all.ms)} />
        <Total label="PERFECT" value={all.perfect.toLocaleString()} tone="text-amber-300" />
        <Total label="Perfect rate" value={all.casts ? `${Math.round((all.perfect / all.casts) * 100)}%` : "—"} tone="text-amber-300" />
        <Total label="Best streak ever" value={`×${all.best}`} tone="text-orange-300" />
        <Total label="Caught" value={all.catches.toLocaleString()} />
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <h3 className="section-title flex-1">Sessions</h3>
        <button className="btn-ghost" onClick={load}>
          <RefreshCw className="size-4" /> Refresh
        </button>
        <button className="btn-ghost text-red-300!" onClick={clear} disabled={sessions.length === 0}>
          <Trash2 className="size-4" /> Clear
        </button>
      </div>

      {sessions.length === 0 && <p className="panel p-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">No fishing yet. Start a session and it shows up here.</p>}

      <ol className="space-y-3">
        {sessions.map((s, i) => {
          const live = s.end === null && i === 0 && running;
          const rate = s.casts ? Math.round((s.perfect / s.casts) * 100) : null;
          const expanded = open === s.start;
          return (
            <li key={s.start} className={clsx("panel feed-row overflow-hidden", live && "fx-running")} style={{ animationDelay: `${Math.min(i, 8) * 40}ms` }}>
              <button className="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-2 px-5 py-4 text-left" onClick={() => setOpen(expanded ? null : s.start)}>
                <div className="min-w-44">
                  <p className="font-semibold">
                    {when(s.start)}
                    {s.account && <span className="chip ml-2 align-middle">{s.account}</span>}
                  </p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                    {live ? (
                      <span className="text-accent-2">● fishing now · {duration(Date.now() - s.start)}</span>
                    ) : (
                      <>
                        {duration((s.end ?? s.start) - s.start)} · {s.stop_reason ?? "app closed"}
                      </>
                    )}
                  </p>
                </div>
                <Mini label="PERFECT" value={s.perfect} tone="text-amber-300" />
                <Mini label="Rate" value={rate === null ? "—" : `${rate}%`} tone="text-amber-300" />
                <Mini label="Best streak" value={`×${s.best_streak}`} tone="text-orange-300" />
                <Mini label="Streaks broken" value={s.streaks_broken} tone="text-red-300" />
                <Mini label="Casts" value={s.casts} />
                <Mini label="Caught" value={s.catches} />
                <ChevronDown className={clsx("ml-auto size-5 text-muted transition-transform", expanded && "rotate-180")} />
              </button>

              {expanded && (
                <div className="grid gap-5 border-t border-line px-5 py-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr]">
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    {onWrapped && s.end !== null && (
                      <button className="btn-secondary w-full" onClick={() => onWrapped(s.start)}>
                        <Sparkles className="size-4" /> Open Wrapped for this session
                      </button>
                    )}
                    <div>
                      <p className="label mb-2">What it caught</p>
                      {s.seen.length === 0 ? (
                        <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nothing readable yet.</p>
                      ) : (
                        <ul className="space-y-1">
                          {s.seen.slice(0, 12).map(([name, times, amount]) => (
                            <li key={name} className="flex justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
                              <span className="truncate">{name}</span>
                              <span className="num text-muted">
                                <b className="text-text">×{amount.toLocaleString()}</b>
                                {amount > times && ` · ${times} catches`}
                              </span>
                            </li>
                          ))}
                        </ul>
                      )}
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 text-xs">
                      <span className="chip">
                        <Package className="size-3" /> Rod taken from backpack ×{s.rod_fetches}
                      </span>
                      <span className="chip">
                        <RotateCcw className="size-3" /> Recovered ×{s.recoveries}
                      </span>
                      <span className="chip">
                        <Fish className="size-3" /> No PERFECT ×{s.missed}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="label mb-2">Timeline</p>
                    <ol className="max-h-72 space-y-px overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                      {s.events.filter((e) => e.kind !== "cast" || !e.perfect || e.streak % 5 === 0).slice(0, 150).map((e, k) => (
                        <EventRow key={`${e.at}-${k}`} e={e} />
                      ))}
                    </ol>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </div>
  );
}

function Total({ label, value, tone = "text-text" }: { label: string; value: string; tone?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-panel px-4 py-3">
      <p className="label">{label}</p>
      <p className={clsx("num mt-0.5 text-2xl font-black", tone)}>{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

function Mini({ label, value, tone = "text-text" }: { label: string; value: string | number; tone?: string }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-wider text-muted uppercase">{label}</p>
      <p className={clsx("num text-lg font-bold", tone)}>{typeof value === "number" ? value.toLocaleString() : value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

/** One line of the timeline. Single PERFECTs are left out (every 5th of a run is shown). */
function EventRow({ e }: { e: HistoryEvent }) {
  const [icon, text, tone] = ((): [React.ReactNode, string, string] => {
    switch (e.kind) {
      case "start":
        return [<Fish key="i" className="size-3.5" />, "Started fishing", "text-accent-2"];
      case "stop":
        return [<Square key="i" className="size-3" />, `Stopped — ${e.reason ?? "app closed"}`, "text-muted"];
      case "cast":
        return e.perfect ? [<Sparkles key="i" className="size-3.5" />, `PERFECT ×${e.streak}`, "text-amber-300"] : [<Fish key="i" className="size-3.5" />, "No PERFECT", "text-red-300/80"];
      case "streak_broken":
        return [<Flame key="i" className="size-3.5" />, `Streak of ${e.length} broken`, "text-red-300"];
      case "catch":
        return [<Fish key="i" className="size-3.5" />, e.seen ? `Caught — ${e.seen}` : "Caught one", "text-text"];
      case "rod_fetched":
        return [<Package key="i" className="size-3.5" />, "Took the rod from the backpack", "text-text/80"];
      case "recovered":
        return [<TriangleAlert key="i" className="size-3.5" />, `Hiccup, went on: ${e.problem}`, "text-amber-200/80"];
    }
  })();
  return (
    <li className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded px-2 py-1 text-sm hover:bg-white/[0.03]">
      <span className="num w-16 shrink-0 text-[11px] text-muted">{time(e.at)}</span>
      <span className={clsx("shrink-0", tone)}>{icon}</span>
      <span className={clsx("truncate", tone)}>{text}</span>
    </li>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Bell, Clock, Fish, Gauge, Keyboard, Monitor, RotateCcw, Save, Trophy, UserRound, Volume2, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { HotkeyInput } from "./HotkeyInput";
import { playPerfect, playStreak } from "../lib/sound";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";
import { fishNow, nextReward } from "../lib/rewards";
import type { OverlayStyle, RoutineSettings } from "../lib/types";

/** Fishing → Settings: everything about the fishing routine, in groups. Saves with one button. */
export function FishingSettings({ value, onSave }: { value: RoutineSettings; onSave: (v: RoutineSettings) => Promise<unknown> | void }) {
  const [d, setD] = useState(value);
  useEffect(() => setD(value), [value]);
  const dirty = JSON.stringify(d) !== JSON.stringify(value);
  const set = <K extends keyof RoutineSettings>(k: K, v: RoutineSettings[K]) => setD((x) => ({ ...x, [k]: v }));
  const num = (v: string, max: number) => Math.max(0, Math.min(max, Math.round(Number(v)) || 0));
  // Webhook links are saved the moment a valid one is pasted (or a field is emptied): nothing to
  // forget, and nothing lost when the app restarts.
  const saveLink = async (k: "discord_history" | "discord_streaks" | "discord_stats", v: string): Promise<LinkState> => {
    const next = { ...d, [k]: v };
    setD(next);
    if (v !== "" && !isWebhook(v)) return "invalid";
    try {
      if ((await onSave(next)) === false) return "error";
      return v === "" ? "cleared" : "saved";
    } catch {
      return "error";
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4 pb-20">
      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-2">
        <Group icon={Keyboard} title="Hotkeys" hint="A letter, number or Space works only while Roblox is in front, so you can still type it everywhere else. F-keys and Ctrl/Alt/Shift combos work everywhere.">
          <Row label="Start / stop fishing">
            <HotkeyInput value={d.hotkey} placeholder="None" onChange={(v) => set("hotkey", v)} />
          </Row>
          <Row label="Show / hide PERFECT counter">
            <HotkeyInput value={d.overlay_hotkey} placeholder="None" onChange={(v) => set("overlay_hotkey", v)} />
          </Row>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={Clock} title="Timer & goal" hint="0 = off.">
          <Row label="Stop after (minutes)">
            <input type="number" className="field num w-32" min={0} max={1440} value={d.stop_after_minutes} onChange={(e) => set("stop_after_minutes", num(e.target.value, 1440))} />
          </Row>
          <Row label="Stop after (PERFECT)">
            <input type="number" className="field num w-32" min={0} max={100000} value={d.stop_after_perfects} onChange={(e) => set("stop_after_perfects", num(e.target.value, 100000))} />
          </Row>
          <Row label="Daily goal (PERFECT)">
            <input type="number" className="field num w-32" min={0} max={100000} value={d.daily_goal} onChange={(e) => set("daily_goal", num(e.target.value, 100000))} />
          </Row>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={RotateCcw} title="Stay in the game" hint="For AFK sessions in Steal An Egg: back to the Lagoon pier by itself.">
          <Toggle label="Walk back to the Lagoon after an egg reset (corridor → Alien Ferryman → boat → pier)" checked={d.follow_route} onChange={(v) => set("follow_route", v)} />
          <Toggle label="Rejoin after a kick, disconnect or crash (clicks Reconnect, else restarts the game) and walk back" checked={d.auto_rejoin} onChange={(v) => set("auto_rejoin", v)} />
          <Row label="Game to rejoin (place ID)">
            <input className="field num w-48 font-mono" value={d.place_id} onChange={(e) => set("place_id", e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ""))} placeholder="107778070777162" />
          </Row>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={Volume2} title="Sound" hint="A chime on every PERFECT, a chord at streak 5, 10, 25…">
          <Toggle label="Play sounds" checked={d.sound} onChange={(v) => set("sound", v)} />
          <Row label={`Volume · ${d.sound_volume}%`}>
            <input type="range" className="w-48" min={0} max={100} step={5} value={d.sound_volume} disabled={!d.sound} onChange={(e) => set("sound_volume", Number(e.target.value))} />
          </Row>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!d.sound} onClick={() => playPerfect(d.sound_volume)}>
              Test PERFECT
            </button>
            <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!d.sound} onClick={() => playStreak(d.sound_volume, 10)}>
              Test streak
            </button>
          </div>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={Monitor} title="PERFECT counter" hint="The small window above the game. In OBS: Window Capture → “SLNC Perfect Counter”.">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
            {(["gold", "neon", "minimal"] as OverlayStyle[]).map((s) => (
              <button
                key={s}
                onClick={() => set("overlay_style", s)}
                className={clsx("rounded-lg border p-2 text-left transition", d.overlay_style === s ? "border-accent bg-accent/10" : "border-line hover:border-white/20")}
              >
                <span className={clsx("ov-swatch mb-2 block h-10 rounded-md", `ov-swatch-${s}`)} />
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold capitalize">{s}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <Row label={`Size · ${d.overlay_scale}%`}>
            <input type="range" className="w-48" min={60} max={200} step={10} value={d.overlay_scale} onChange={(e) => set("overlay_scale", Number(e.target.value))} />
          </Row>
          <Toggle label="Open it by itself when fishing starts" checked={d.overlay_auto} onChange={(v) => set("overlay_auto", v)} />
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">A new size shows the next time the counter opens.</p>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={Bell} title="Discord" hint="A webhook per channel: in Discord, the channel's ⚙ → Integrations → Webhooks → New Webhook → Copy Webhook URL. Kept on this PC; empty = that channel gets nothing.">
          <Webhook label="Fishing history" sub="Start/stop, what was caught, totals, hiccups" value={d.discord_history} onChange={(v) => saveLink("discord_history", v)} />
          <Webhook label="Perfect streak" sub="Streak milestones, new records, broken streaks, PERFECT milestones" value={d.discord_streaks} onChange={(v) => saveLink("discord_streaks", v)} />
          <Webhook label="Your stats" sub="Live stats, session summary + records, yesterday's overview" value={d.discord_stats} onChange={(v) => saveLink("discord_stats", v)} />
          <p className="rounded-lg border border-accent/30 bg-accent/[0.06] p-3 text-xs text-muted">
            Every message is a picture card. See them all, and send any of them, in the <b className="text-accent-2">Discord</b> tab.
          </p>
          <Row label="PERFECT milestone every (this session)">
            <input type="number" className="field num w-32" min={0} max={100000} value={d.notify_every} onChange={(e) => set("notify_every", num(e.target.value, 100000))} />
          </Row>

          </Row>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={UserRound} title="Account" hint="Which Roblox account you fish on. Sessions in History and Stats carry it, so you can look at one account.">
          <Row label="Account name">
            <input className="field" maxLength={40} placeholder="e.g. main" value={d.account} onChange={(e) => set("account", e.target.value)} />

          <FishCount cfg={value} />
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">

          </label>
        </Group>

        <Group icon={Fish} title="Your rods" hint="When the rod isn't in hand (e.g. after fishing an egg), it takes it out of the backpack. It knows the purple and the green rod; teach it any new one here.">
          <LearnRod />

        <Group icon={Gauge} title="Fishing" hint="How it plays.">
          <Row label="Clicks per second (Click Fast! bar)">
            <input type="number" className="field num w-32" min={1} max={100} value={d.tap_clicks_per_second} onChange={(e) => set("tap_clicks_per_second", Math.max(1, num(e.target.value, 100)))} />
          </Row>
          <Row label={`Let go at gold — starting point · ${d.release_early_ms} ms`}>
            <input type="range" className="w-48" min={0} max={300} step={10} value={d.release_early_ms} onChange={(e) => set("release_early_ms", Number(e.target.value))} />
          </Row>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">It tunes the release itself after every cast; only change this if the first casts are far off.</p>
        </Group>
      </div>

      {/* Save bar: sticks to the bottom while there are changes. */}
      <div className={clsx("save-bar", dirty && "save-bar-on")}>
        <span className="text-sm text-muted">You have unsaved changes</span>
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => setD(value)}>
          Undo
        </button>
        <button className="btn-primary px-6" onClick={() => onSave(d)}>
          <Save className="size-4" /> Save
        </button>
      </div>

  );
}

function Group({ icon: Icon, title, hint, children }: { icon: LucideIcon; title: string; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="panel fx-panel space-y-4 p-5">
      <header className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <span className="grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg border border-line bg-white/[0.03] text-accent">
          <Icon className="size-4.5" />
        </span>
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-semibold">{title}</h3>
          {hint && <p className="text-xs text-muted">{hint}</p>}
        </div>
      </header>
      <div className="space-y-3">{children}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

function Row({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-1.5">
      <span className="text-sm text-text/85">{label}</span>
      <span className="min-w-0">{children}</span>
    </label>
  );
}

function Toggle({ label, checked, onChange }: { label: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" role="switch" aria-checked={checked} onClick={() => onChange(!checked)} className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 text-left">
      <span className="text-sm text-text/85">{label}</span>
      <span className={clsx("relative h-5 w-9 shrink-0 rounded-full transition", checked ? "bg-accent" : "bg-white/15")}>
        <span className={clsx("absolute top-0.5 size-4 rounded-full bg-white shadow transition-all", checked ? "left-[1.1rem]" : "left-0.5")} />
      </span>
    </button>
  );
}

/** One channel's webhook, with a Test button that sends a message there and says if it arrived. */
type LinkState = "saved" | "cleared" | "invalid" | "error";

/** A Discord webhook link as Discord hands it out. */
const isWebhook = (u: string) => /^https:\/\/(ptb\.|canary\.)?discord(app)?\.com\/api\/webhooks\/\d+\/[\w-]+$/.test(u.trim());

function Webhook({ label, sub, value, onChange }: { label: string; sub: string; value: string; onChange: (v: string) => Promise<LinkState> }) {
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ kind: "idle" | "busy" | "ok" | "err"; text?: string }>({ kind: "idle" });
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState<LinkState | null>(value ? "saved" : null);
  const test = async () => {
    setState({ kind: "busy" });
    try {
      await api.discordTest(value, label);
      setState({ kind: "ok", text: "Sent — check the channel" });
    } catch (e) {
      setState({ kind: "err", text: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-1.5 rounded-lg border border-line bg-white/[0.02] p-3">
      <div>
        <p className="text-sm font-semibold">{label}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">{sub}</p>
      </div>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <input
          type="password"
          autoComplete="off"
          className="field font-mono text-xs"
          placeholder="https://discord.com/api/webhooks/…"
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => {
            setState({ kind: "idle" });
            void onChange(e.target.value.trim()).then(setSaved);
          }}
        />
        <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0" disabled={!value || state.kind === "busy"} onClick={test}>
          {state.kind === "busy" ? "Sending…" : "Test"}
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 text-xs">
        {saved === "saved" && <span className="text-accent-2">✓ Saved</span>}
        {saved === "cleared" && <span className="text-muted">Removed</span>}
        {saved === "invalid" && <span className="text-amber-300">Not a Discord webhook link yet — paste the whole link</span>}
        {saved === "error" && <span className="text-red-300">Could not save — see the message at the top</span>}
        {state.text && <span className={state.kind === "ok" ? "text-accent-2" : "text-red-300"}>{state.text}</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** "Learn my rod": the app takes a picture of the rod in hand in the open Gear tab. */
function LearnRod() {
  const [count, setCount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ kind: "idle" | "busy" | "ok" | "err"; text?: string }>({ kind: "idle" });
  useEffect(() => {
    api.learnedRods().then(setCount).catch(() => setCount(0));
  }, []);
  const learn = async () => {
    setState({ kind: "busy" });
    try {
      const n = await api.learnRod();
      setCount(n);
      setState({ kind: "ok", text: "Got it — this rod is recognised from now on." });
    } catch (e) {
      setState({ kind: "err", text: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  };
  const forget = async () => {
    setCount(await api.forgetRods().catch(() => 0));
    setState({ kind: "ok", text: "Learned rods removed (the purple and green rod stay)." });
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <ol className="list-decimal space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-text/85">
        <li>In Roblox, hold your new rod.</li>
        <li>Open the backpack and go to the <b>Gear</b> tab — the rod has a white border.</li>
        <li>Click <b>Learn my rod</b> here (Roblox can stay behind this window).</li>
      </ol>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <button className="btn-primary" disabled={state.kind === "busy"} onClick={learn}>
          <Fish className="size-4" /> {state.kind === "busy" ? "Looking…" : "Learn my rod"}
        </button>
        {!!count && (
          <button className="btn-ghost text-xs" onClick={forget}>
            Forget learned rods
          </button>
        )}
        <span className="text-xs text-muted">{count === null ? "" : `${count} learned`}</span>
      </div>
      {state.text && <p className={clsx("text-xs", state.kind === "ok" ? "text-accent-2" : "text-red-300")}>{state.text}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

/** "Fish caught now": what the game says; the app counts on from it. */
function FishCount({ cfg }: { cfg: RoutineSettings }) {
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");
  const [ours, setOurs] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ kind: "idle" | "ok" | "err"; text?: string }>({ kind: "idle" });
  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .getFishingHistory(1000)
      .then((list) => setOurs(list.reduce((a, s) => a + s.catches, 0)))
      .catch(() => setOurs(0));
  }, [cfg.fish_count, cfg.fish_count_ours]);
  const now = ours === null ? null : fishNow(cfg.fish_count, cfg.fish_count_ours, ours);
  const next = nextReward(now, cfg.rewards);
  const save = async () => {
    const n = Number(input.replace(/[.,\s]/g, ""));
    if (!Number.isFinite(n) || n <= 0) return setState({ kind: "err", text: "Type the number from the game, e.g. 132" });
    try {
      await api.setFishCount(n);
      setInput("");
      setState({ kind: "ok", text: "Saved — counting on from here." });
    } catch (e) {
      setState({ kind: "err", text: errorMessage(e) });
    }
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <input className="field num w-36" inputMode="numeric" placeholder={now === null ? "e.g. 132" : String(now)} value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} />
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={save} disabled={!input}>
          Set fish caught
        </button>
        {now !== null && (
          <span className="text-sm">
            Now <b className="num text-accent-2">{now.toLocaleString()}</b>
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      {next && (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">
          Next reward <b className="text-text">{next.name}</b> at <span className="num">{next.at.toLocaleString()}</span> · <b className="num text-amber-300">{next.left.toLocaleString()}</b> fish to go
        </p>
      )}
      {state.text && <p className={clsx("text-xs", state.kind === "ok" ? "text-accent-2" : "text-red-300")}>{state.text}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/FishingStats.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Download, Flame, Target, Timer, Trophy, Zap, type LucideIcon } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { FishingSession } from "../lib/types";
import { duration } from "./FishingHistory";

/**
 * Fishing → Stats: records, today against the daily goal, PERFECT per day and the perfect rate per
 * session, for all accounts or one. Export writes the history as CSV.
 */
export function FishingStats({ running, goal, accountFilter }: { running: boolean; goal: number; accountFilter: string | null }) {
  const [all, setAll] = useState<FishingSession[]>([]);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const load = () => api.getFishingHistory(500).then(setAll).catch(() => {});
    load();
    if (!running) return;
    const id = window.setInterval(load, 8000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [running]);

  const sessions = useMemo(() => (accountFilter === null ? all : all.filter((s) => (s.account ?? "") === accountFilter)), [all, accountFilter]);

  const days = useMemo(() => perDay(sessions, 14), [sessions]);
  const recent = useMemo(() => sessions.filter((s) => s.casts >= 5).slice(0, 24).reverse(), [sessions]);
  const today = days[days.length - 1]?.perfect ?? 0;

  const records = useMemo(() => {
    const best = (f: (s: FishingSession) => number) => sessions.reduce<FishingSession | null>((b, s) => (!b || f(s) > f(b) ? s : b), null);
    const rated = sessions.filter((s) => s.casts >= 20);
    return {
      streak: best((s) => s.best_streak),
      session: best((s) => s.perfect),
      rate: rated.length ? rated.reduce((b, s) => (s.perfect / s.casts > b.perfect / b.casts ? s : b)) : null,
      longest: best((s) => (s.end ?? Date.now()) - s.start),
    };
  }, [sessions]);

  const exportCsv = async () => {
    const path = await api.exportFishingHistory().catch(() => null);
    setSaved(path);
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-4">
        <Record icon={Flame} label="Best streak ever" value={records.streak ? `×${records.streak.best_streak}` : "—"} when={records.streak?.start} tone="text-orange-300" />
        <Record icon={Trophy} label="Most PERFECT in a session" value={records.session ? records.session.perfect.toLocaleString() : "—"} when={records.session?.start} tone="text-amber-300" />
        <Record icon={Zap} label="Best perfect rate" value={records.rate ? `${Math.round((records.rate.perfect / records.rate.casts) * 100)}%` : "—"} when={records.rate?.start} tone="text-accent-2" hint="sessions of 20+ casts" />
        <Record icon={Timer} label="Longest session" value={records.longest ? duration((records.longest.end ?? Date.now()) - records.longest.start) : "—"} when={records.longest?.start} />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[320px_1fr]">
        <section className="panel flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-6">
          <h3 className="section-title flex items-center gap-2 self-start">
            <Target className="size-3.5" /> Today
          </h3>
          <GoalRing value={today} goal={goal} />
          <p className="text-center text-xs text-muted">{goal > 0 ? (today >= goal ? "Daily goal reached. Nice." : `${(goal - today).toLocaleString()} PERFECT to go`) : "Set a daily goal in Settings → Timer & goal."}</p>
        </section>

        <section className="panel p-6">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-baseline justify-between">
            <h3 className="section-title">PERFECT per day · last 14 days</h3>
            <span className="num text-xs text-muted">{days.reduce((a, d) => a + d.perfect, 0).toLocaleString()} total</span>
          </div>
          <Bars days={days} goal={goal} />
        </section>
      </div>

      <section className="panel p-6">
        <div className="mb-4 flex items-baseline justify-between">
          <h3 className="section-title">Perfect rate per session · last {recent.length}</h3>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted">sessions of 5+ casts</span>
        </div>
        <RateLine sessions={recent} />
      </section>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={exportCsv} disabled={all.length === 0}>
          <Download className="size-4" /> Export history (CSV)
        </button>
        {saved && <span className="truncate text-xs text-muted">Saved to {saved}</span>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Record({ icon: Icon, label, value, when, tone = "text-text", hint }: { icon: LucideIcon; label: string; value: string; when?: number; tone?: string; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel fx-panel p-5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted">
        <Icon className="size-4" />
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold">{label}</span>
      </div>
      <p className={clsx("num mt-2 text-3xl font-black", tone)}>{value}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-muted">{when ? new Date(when).toLocaleDateString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" }) : hint ?? "no sessions yet"}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Today's PERFECT against the goal, as a ring. */
function GoalRing({ value, goal }: { value: number; goal: number }) {
  const R = 64;
  const C = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  const share = goal > 0 ? Math.min(1, value / goal) : 0;
  return (
    <div className="relative grid size-44 place-items-center">
      <svg viewBox="0 0 160 160" className="absolute inset-0 -rotate-90">
        <circle cx="80" cy="80" r={R} fill="none" stroke="rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06)" strokeWidth="10" />
        <circle cx="80" cy="80" r={R} fill="none" stroke="url(#goal-g)" strokeWidth="10" strokeLinecap="round" strokeDasharray={`${share * C} ${C}`} style={{ transition: "stroke-dasharray 700ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)", filter: "drop-shadow(0 0 6px rgb(252 211 77 / 0.5))" }} />
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="goal-g" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0" stopColor="#fde68a" />
            <stop offset="1" stopColor="#f59e0b" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
      </svg>
      <div className="text-center">
        <div className="num text-4xl font-black text-amber-300">{value.toLocaleString()}</div>
        <div className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] text-muted uppercase">{goal > 0 ? `of ${goal.toLocaleString()}` : "PERFECT"}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

interface Day {
  key: string;
  label: string;
  perfect: number;
}

/** PERFECT per local day for the last `n` days (a session counts on the day it started). */
function perDay(sessions: FishingSession[], n: number): Day[] {
  const days: Day[] = [];
  const start = new Date();
  start.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0);
  for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const d = new Date(start);
    d.setDate(d.getDate() - i);
    days.push({ key: d.toDateString(), label: d.toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "short", day: "numeric" }), perfect: 0 });
  }
  const at = new Map(days.map((d) => [d.key, d]));
  for (const s of sessions) {
    const d = at.get(new Date(s.start).toDateString());
    if (d) d.perfect += s.perfect;
  }
  return days;
}

function Bars({ days, goal }: { days: Day[]; goal: number }) {
  const max = Math.max(goal, ...days.map((d) => d.perfect), 1);
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      {goal > 0 && (
        <div className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 border-t border-dashed border-amber-300/40" style={{ bottom: `${(goal / max) * 100}%`, height: 0 }}>
          <span className="absolute -top-4 right-0 text-[10px] text-amber-300/70">goal</span>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="flex h-48 items-end gap-1.5">
        {days.map((d, i) => {
          const last = i === days.length - 1;
          return (
            <div key={d.key} className="group flex h-full flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
              <span className="num text-[10px] text-muted opacity-0 transition group-hover:opacity-100">{d.perfect.toLocaleString()}</span>
              <div
                className={clsx("chart-bar w-full rounded-t-md", last ? "chart-bar-today" : d.perfect > 0 ? "chart-bar-on" : "bg-white/[0.04]")}
                style={{ height: `${Math.max(2, (d.perfect / max) * 100)}%`, animationDelay: `${i * 35}ms` }}
              />
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-2 flex gap-1.5">
        {days.map((d) => (
          <span key={d.key} className="flex-1 truncate text-center text-[10px] text-muted">
            {d.label}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function RateLine({ sessions }: { sessions: FishingSession[] }) {
  if (sessions.length < 2) return <p className="py-10 text-center text-sm text-muted">After a couple of sessions the trend shows here.</p>;
  const W = 1000;
  const H = 200;
  const pts = sessions.map((s, i) => [(i / (sessions.length - 1)) * W, H - (s.perfect / s.casts) * H] as const);
  const line = pts.map(([x, y], i) => `${i ? "L" : "M"}${x.toFixed(1)},${y.toFixed(1)}`).join(" ");
  const area = `${line} L${W},${H} L0,${H} Z`;
  const avg = sessions.reduce((a, s) => a + s.perfect / s.casts, 0) / sessions.length;
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${W} ${H}`} preserveAspectRatio="none" className="h-48 w-full overflow-visible">
        <defs>
          <linearGradient id="rate-area" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <stop offset="0" stopColor="rgb(var(--accent-rgb))" stopOpacity="0.35" />
            <stop offset="1" stopColor="rgb(var(--accent-rgb))" stopOpacity="0" />
          </linearGradient>
        </defs>
        {[0.25, 0.5, 0.75].map((g) => (
          <line key={g} x1="0" x2={W} y1={H - g * H} y2={H - g * H} stroke="rgb(255 255 255 / 0.06)" vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" />
        ))}
        <line x1="0" x2={W} y1={H - avg * H} y2={H - avg * H} stroke="rgb(252 211 77 / 0.5)" strokeDasharray="6 6" vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" />
        <path d={area} fill="url(#rate-area)" />
        <path d={line} fill="none" stroke="rgb(var(--accent-rgb))" strokeWidth="2.5" vectorEffect="non-scaling-stroke" className="chart-line" />
      </svg>
      <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between text-[10px] text-muted">
        <span>{new Date(sessions[0]!.start).toLocaleDateString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span>
        <span className="text-amber-300/80">average {Math.round(avg * 100)}%</span>
        <span>{new Date(sessions[sessions.length - 1]!.start).toLocaleDateString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
