FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/HotkeyInput.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";

/** Shared by all fields: going straight from one field to the next keeps hotkeys off. */
let captureEnd: number | undefined;

/**
 * Press-to-capture hotkey field. Produces strings in the global-shortcut format ("Ctrl+Alt+1").
 * Only keys the engine can map (hotkeys.rs → hotkey_groups) are accepted.
 * Any single key works; a plain letter can't be typed anywhere else while it is a hotkey.
 * Backspace/Delete alone clears. Note: a combination that is already registered globally (e.g. the record
 * hotkey) is consumed by Windows before it reaches this field.
 */
export function HotkeyInput({
  value,
  onChange,
  placeholder = "Click, then press keys",
  className,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (hotkey: string) => void;
  placeholder?: string;
  className?: string;
}) {
  const [listening, setListening] = useState(false);

  // No hotkey is active while listening: pressing F7 here must not start what F7 is bound to.
  // The engine turns them back on after every key is let go.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!listening) return;
    window.clearTimeout(captureEnd);
    api.setHotkeyCapture(true).catch(() => {});
    return () => {
      captureEnd = window.setTimeout(() => void api.setHotkeyCapture(false).catch(() => {}), 100);
    };
  }, [listening]);

  const onKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLButtonElement>) => {
    if (e.key === "Tab") return; // keep keyboard navigation working
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();

    const mods = [e.ctrlKey && "Ctrl", e.altKey && "Alt", e.shiftKey && "Shift"].filter(Boolean) as string[];

    if (mods.length === 0 && (e.key === "Backspace" || e.key === "Delete")) {
      onChange("");
      setListening(false);
      return;
    }

    const key = keyName(e.code);
    if (!key) return; // modifier only (wait for the main key) or unsupported key

    onChange([...mods, key].join("+"));
    setListening(false);
    e.currentTarget.blur();
  };

  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      className={clsx("field text-left font-mono", listening && "ring-2 ring-indigo-500", className)}
      onFocus={() => setListening(true)}
      onBlur={() => setListening(false)}
      onKeyDown={onKeyDown}
      title="Click and press a key combination. Backspace clears."
    >
      {listening ? <span className="text-indigo-600">Press keys… (Backspace = clear)</span> : value || <span className="text-zinc-400">{placeholder}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}

/** KeyboardEvent.code → hotkey key name, limited to what hotkeys.rs can map. */
function keyName(code: string): string | null {
  if (/^Key[A-Z]$/.test(code)) return code.slice(3);
  if (/^Digit[0-9]$/.test(code)) return code.slice(5);
  if (/^F([1-9]|1[0-9]|2[0-4])$/.test(code)) return code;
  if (/^Numpad([0-9]|Add|Subtract|Multiply|Divide|Decimal|Enter)$/.test(code)) return code;
  if (/^Arrow(Up|Down|Left|Right)$/.test(code)) return code;
  const others = ["Escape", "Space", "Enter", "Pause", "Backquote", "Minus", "Equal", "BracketLeft", "BracketRight", "Backslash", "Semicolon", "Quote", "Comma", "Period", "Slash", "Backspace", "Delete", "Insert", "Home", "End", "PageUp", "PageDown", "CapsLock", "NumLock", "ScrollLock", "PrintScreen"];
  return others.includes(code) ? code : null;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/Inspector.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Copy, Repeat, ScanSearch, Trash2, Ungroup } from "lucide-react";
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import type { ClickTarget, MacroStep, Modifier, MouseButton, ScreenSettings } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { ScreenFields, type Teach } from "./ScreenStep";
import { KEY_OPTIONS, keyName } from "../lib/keys";
import { stepMeta } from "../lib/step-meta";
import type { Variable } from "../lib/types";
import { BUILT_IN_VARIABLES, numericVariables, unknownRefs } from "../lib/variables";

interface InspectorProps {
  step: MacroStep | null;
  canWrap: boolean;
  /** Delay before this action (ms); null when not applicable (the step is itself a delay). */
  waitBefore: number | null;
  onWaitBefore: (ms: number) => void;
  onChange: (step: MacroStep) => void;
  onMove: (dir: -1 | 1) => void;
  onWrap: () => void;
  onUnwrap: () => void;
  onDuplicate: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  /** Variables for {{name}} helpers in text and delay steps. */
  variables: Variable[];
  /** The macro's game window (screen steps). */
  screen: ScreenSettings | undefined;
  teach: Teach;
}

/** Edits the selected step. Only real, supported parameters are editable. */
export function Inspector({ step, canWrap, waitBefore, onWaitBefore, onChange, onMove, onWrap, onUnwrap, onDuplicate, onDelete, variables, screen, teach }: InspectorProps) {
  if (!step) {
    return (
      <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 py-6 text-center text-sm text-muted">
        <ScanSearch className="size-6" />
        Select an action in the list to edit it.
      </div>
    );
  }

  const meta = stepMeta(step);
  const Icon = meta.icon;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-line bg-panel-3/50 p-3">
        <div className="grid size-10 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-accent/20 text-accent-2">
          <Icon className="size-5" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <div className="font-medium">{meta.label}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-muted">{meta.description}</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {waitBefore !== null && (
        <Field label="Wait before this action (ms)">
          <Num value={waitBefore} min={0} max={86_400_000} step={50} onChange={onWaitBefore} />
        </Field>
      )}

      {step.type === "screen" ? <ScreenFields step={step} onChange={onChange} screen={screen} teach={teach} /> : <Fields step={step} onChange={onChange} variables={variables} />}

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-t border-line pt-3">
        <IconBtn label="Move up (Ctrl+↑)" onClick={() => onMove(-1)}>
          <ArrowUp className="size-4" />
        </IconBtn>
        <IconBtn label="Move down (Ctrl+↓)" onClick={() => onMove(1)}>
          <ArrowDown className="size-4" />
        </IconBtn>
        <IconBtn label="Duplicate (Ctrl+D)" onClick={onDuplicate}>
          <Copy className="size-4" />
        </IconBtn>
        {step.type === "loop" ? (
          <IconBtn label="Unwrap loop" onClick={onUnwrap}>
            <Ungroup className="size-4" />
          </IconBtn>
        ) : (
          canWrap && (
            <IconBtn label="Wrap in loop" onClick={onWrap}>
              <Repeat className="size-4" />
            </IconBtn>
          )
        )}
        <div className="flex-1" />
        <IconBtn label="Delete action (Del)" onClick={onDelete} danger>
          <Trash2 className="size-4" />
        </IconBtn>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Fields({ step, onChange, variables }: { step: MacroStep; onChange: (s: MacroStep) => void; variables: Variable[] }) {
  switch (step.type) {
    case "key_press":
      return (
        <>
          <KeyField vk={step.vk} onChange={(vk) => onChange({ ...step, vk, scan: 0 })} />
          <HoldField value={step.hold_ms ?? 0} onChange={(hold_ms) => onChange({ ...step, hold_ms })} />
          <Field label="Modifiers">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {(["ctrl", "alt", "shift"] as Modifier[]).map((m) => {
                const on = step.mods?.includes(m) ?? false;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={m}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => onChange({ ...step, mods: on ? (step.mods ?? []).filter((x) => x !== m) : [...(step.mods ?? []), m] })}
                    className={on ? "btn-primary px-2.5 py-1 text-xs" : "btn-secondary px-2.5 py-1 text-xs"}
                  >
                    {m === "ctrl" ? "Ctrl" : m === "alt" ? "Alt" : "Shift"}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </Field>
        </>
      );
    case "key_down":
    case "key_up":
      return <KeyField vk={step.vk} onChange={(vk) => onChange({ ...step, vk, scan: 0 })} />;
    case "text": {
      const unknown = unknownRefs(step.value, variables);
      return (
        <>
          <Field label="Text to type">
            <textarea className="field min-h-20 resize-y" value={step.value} maxLength={10_000} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...step, value: e.target.value })} />
          </Field>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="label shrink-0">Insert variable</span>
            <select
              className="field py-1!"
              value=""
              onChange={(e) => e.target.value && onChange({ ...step, value: `${step.value}{{${e.target.value}}}` })}
            >
              <option value="">Choose…</option>
              {variables.map((v) => (
                <option key={v.name} value={v.name}>
                  {v.name}
                </option>
              ))}
              {BUILT_IN_VARIABLES.map((b) => (
                <option key={b.name} value={b.name}>
                  {b.name} (built-in)
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          {unknown.length > 0 && (
            <p className="text-xs text-amber-300">
              Unknown variable{unknown.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}: {unknown.map((n) => `{{${n}}}`).join(", ")} — will be typed literally. Add it in the
              Variables tab.
            </p>
          )}
        </>
      );
    }
    case "delay": {
      const numeric = numericVariables(variables);
      const missing = step.var && !numeric.some((v) => v.name.toLowerCase() === step.var!.toLowerCase());
      return (
        <>
          <Field label={step.var ? "Fallback delay (ms) — used if the variable isn't a number" : "Delay (ms)"}>
            <Num value={step.ms} min={0} max={86_400_000} step={50} onChange={(ms) => onChange({ ...step, ms })} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Use a variable (number of ms)">
            <select
              className="field"
              value={step.var ?? ""}
              onChange={(e) => {
                const { var: _old, ...rest } = step;
                onChange(e.target.value ? { ...rest, var: e.target.value } : rest);
              }}
            >
              <option value="">— no, use the fixed delay —</option>
              {numeric.map((v) => (
                <option key={v.name} value={v.name}>
                  {v.name} ({v.value.trim()} ms)
                </option>
              ))}
              {missing && <option value={step.var}>{step.var} (missing / not a number)</option>}
            </select>
          </Field>
          {missing && <p className="text-xs text-amber-300">“{step.var}” isn't a numeric variable right now — the fallback delay is used.</p>}
        </>
      );
    }
    case "scroll":
      return (
        <Field label="Wheel amount (120 = one notch up, −120 = down)">
          <Num value={step.delta} min={-12_000} max={12_000} step={120} onChange={(delta) => onChange({ ...step, delta })} />
        </Field>
      );
    case "loop":
      return (
        <Field label="Repeat count">
          <Num value={step.count} min={1} max={1_000_000} onChange={(count) => onChange({ ...step, count })} />
        </Field>
      );
    case "mouse_click":
      return (
        <>
          <ButtonField value={step.button} onChange={(button) => onChange({ ...step, button })} />
          <HoldField value={step.hold_ms ?? 0} onChange={(hold_ms) => onChange({ ...step, hold_ms })} />
          {step.target ? (
            <SmartTarget
              target={step.target}
              onChange={(target) => onChange({ ...step, target })}
              onMakeFixed={() => {
                const { target: _drop, ...fixed } = step;
                onChange(fixed);
              }}
            />
          ) : (
            <Position x={step.x} y={step.y} onChange={(x, y) => onChange({ ...step, x, y })} />
          )}
        </>
      );
    case "mouse_move":
      return <Position x={step.x} y={step.y} onChange={(x, y) => onChange({ ...step, x, y })} />;
    case "mouse_move_by":
      return (
        <>
          <Field label="Sideways (mouse counts, − = left)">
            <Num value={step.dx} min={-100_000} max={100_000} onChange={(dx) => onChange({ ...step, dx })} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Up/down (mouse counts, − = up)">
            <Num value={step.dy} min={-100_000} max={100_000} onChange={(dy) => onChange({ ...step, dy })} />
          </Field>
        </>
      );
    case "mouse_down":
    case "mouse_up":
      return <ButtonField value={step.button} onChange={(button) => onChange({ ...step, button })} />;
    case "screen":
      return null; // ScreenFields
  }
}

function KeyField({ vk, onChange }: { vk: number; onChange: (vk: number) => void }) {
  const known = KEY_OPTIONS.some((k) => k.vk === vk);
  return (
    <Field label="Key">
      <select className="field" value={vk} onChange={(e) => onChange(Number(e.target.value))}>
        {!known && <option value={vk}>{keyName(vk)}</option>}
        {KEY_OPTIONS.map((k) => (
          <option key={k.vk} value={k.vk}>
            {k.name}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
    </Field>
  );
}

function HoldField({ value, onChange }: { value: number; onChange: (ms: number) => void }) {
  return (
    <Field label="Hold time (ms) — 0 = instant">
      <Num value={value} min={0} max={60_000} step={10} onChange={onChange} />
    </Field>
  );
}

function ButtonField({ value, onChange }: { value: MouseButton; onChange: (b: MouseButton) => void }) {
  return (
    <Field label="Button">
      <select className="field" value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as MouseButton)}>
        <option value="left">Left</option>
        <option value="right">Right</option>
        <option value="middle">Middle</option>
        <option value="back">Side: back</option>
        <option value="forward">Side: forward</option>
      </select>
    </Field>
  );
}

/** Positions are stored as 0..1 of the monitor; edited here as percentages. */
function Position({ x, y, onChange }: { x: number; y: number; onChange: (x: number, y: number) => void }) {
  const pct = (v: number) => Math.round(v * 1000) / 10;
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
      <Field label="X (% of screen)">
        <Num value={pct(x)} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} decimals onChange={(v) => onChange(v / 100, y)} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Y (% of screen)">
        <Num value={pct(y)} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} decimals onChange={(v) => onChange(x, v / 100)} />
      </Field>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block space-y-1.5">
      <span className="label">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

function Num({
  value,
  onChange,
  min,
  max,
  step = 1,
  decimals = false,
}: {
  value: number;
  onChange: (v: number) => void;
  min: number;
  max: number;
  step?: number;
  decimals?: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <input
      type="number"
      className="field font-mono"
      value={value}
      min={min}
      max={max}
      step={step}
      onChange={(e) => {
        const raw = Number(e.target.value);
        if (!Number.isFinite(raw)) return;
        const v = decimals ? raw : Math.round(raw);
        onChange(Math.min(max, Math.max(min, v)));
      }}
    />
  );
}

function IconBtn({ label, onClick, danger, children }: { label: string; onClick: () => void; danger?: boolean; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <button type="button" title={label} aria-label={label} onClick={onClick} className={danger ? "btn-ghost px-2 text-red-400 hover:text-red-300" : "btn-ghost px-2"}>
      {children}
    </button>
  );
}

/** Steps offered by "+ Add step". */
export const NEW_STEPS: Array<{ label: string; make: () => MacroStep }> = [
  { label: "Delay", make: () => ({ type: "delay", ms: 500 }) },
  { label: "Key press", make: () => ({ type: "key_press", vk: 0x41, scan: 0 }) },
  { label: "Type text", make: () => ({ type: "text", value: "" }) },
  { label: "Mouse click", make: () => ({ type: "mouse_click", button: "left", x: 0.5, y: 0.5, monitor: 0 }) },
  { label: "Scroll", make: () => ({ type: "scroll", delta: -120 }) },
  { label: "Loop", make: () => ({ type: "loop", count: 2, steps: [] }) },
];


/** What a smart click looks for: the remembered picture (grayscale), its text and how long to look. */
function SmartTarget({ target, onChange, onMakeFixed }: { target: ClickTarget; onChange: (t: ClickTarget) => void; onMakeFixed: () => void }) {
  const canvas = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const c = canvas.current;
    const ctx = c?.getContext("2d");
    if (!c || !ctx) return;
    let bytes: Uint8Array;
    try {
      bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(target.gray_b64), (ch) => ch.charCodeAt(0));
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    const img = ctx.createImageData(target.width, target.height);
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      const v = bytes[i]!;
      img.data.set([v, v, v, 255], i * 4);
    }
    ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  }, [target]);
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2.5">
      <Field label="Looks for this on screen">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="relative shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-line-strong">
            <canvas ref={canvas} width={target.width} height={target.height} className="block size-20 [image-rendering:pixelated]" />
            <span
              className="absolute size-2.5 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 rounded-full border-2 border-white bg-accent shadow"
              style={{ left: `${(target.click_x / target.width) * 100}%`, top: `${(target.click_y / target.height) * 100}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">
            {target.label ? <>Read as <span className="text-text">“{target.label}”</span>. </> : null}
            Clicked wherever it is when the step runs; if it isn't recognised, clicked where it was when recording.
          </p>
        </div>
      </Field>
      <Field label="Look for it up to (seconds)">
        <Num decimals value={Math.round((target.find_ms == null || target.find_ms === 3000 ? 400 : target.find_ms) / 100) / 10} min={0} max={120} step={0.5} onChange={(s) => onChange({ ...target, find_ms: Math.round(s * 1000) })} />
      </Field>
      <button className="btn-secondary w-full py-1.5 text-xs" onClick={onMakeFixed}>
        Make it a normal click (always the same spot)
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/LiveBar.tsx
====================================================================================================
import clsx from "clsx";
import type { BarSample } from "../lib/use-live";

/**
 * The charge bar as the routine sees it, drawn from its measurements (no screenshot leaves the
 * routine): a glowing fill with moving sheen, and the gold band at the top that flares when the
 * fill reaches it.
 */
export function LiveBar({ bar, active }: { bar: BarSample | null; active: boolean }) {
  // 0 = empty, 1 = touching the gold band.
  const level = bar && bar.bottom > bar.band ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (bar.bottom - bar.fill) / (bar.bottom - bar.band))) : 0;
  const gold = !!bar?.gold;
  const BAND = 16; // % of the height the gold band takes at the top

  return (
    <div className="flex items-end gap-5">
      <div className={clsx("lb-tube relative h-60 w-16 overflow-hidden rounded-xl", gold && "lb-tube-gold", active && "lb-tube-live")}>
        {/* gold band */}
        <div className={clsx("lb-band absolute inset-x-0 top-0", gold && "lb-band-lit")} style={{ height: `${BAND}%` }}>
          <span className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 h-px bg-amber-200/60" />
        </div>
        {/* fill */}
        <div className={clsx("lb-fill absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0", gold && "lb-fill-gold")} style={{ height: `${level * (100 - BAND)}%` }}>
          <span className="lb-top" />
        </div>
        {/* ticks */}
        {[25, 50, 75].map((p) => (
          <div key={p} className="absolute inset-x-2 h-px bg-white/10" style={{ bottom: `${(p / 100) * (100 - BAND)}%` }} />
        ))}
        <div className="lb-glass pointer-events-none absolute inset-0" />
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-1 pb-1">
        <p className="label">Charge</p>
        <p className={clsx("num text-4xl font-black transition-colors", gold ? "text-amber-300 [text-shadow:0_0_20px_rgb(252_211_77/0.7)]" : "text-text")}>{Math.round(level * 100)}%</p>
        <p className={clsx("text-xs font-bold tracking-[0.18em] uppercase", gold ? "text-amber-300" : active ? "text-accent-2" : "text-muted")}>
          {gold ? "Gold — letting go" : active && bar ? "Charging" : active ? "Waiting for a cast" : "Idle"}
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/MacroLibrary.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Bot, Copy, Pencil, Crosshair, Download, Gamepad2, Hammer, MoreHorizontal, Plus, RefreshCw, Repeat, Rocket, Search, Swords, Trash2, Upload, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { formatMs } from "../lib/step-meta";
import type { BrokenMacro, MacroSummary } from "../lib/types";

const ICONS = [Zap, Swords, Crosshair, Hammer, Gamepad2, Bot, Rocket, Repeat];

/** Stable icon per macro (derived from its id) so cards are easy to tell apart. */
export function macroIcon(id: string) {
  let h = 0;
  for (const c of id) h = (h * 31 + c.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0;
  return ICONS[h % ICONS.length]!;
}

export interface LibraryActions {
  onSelect: (id: string) => void;
  onNew: () => void;
  onImport: () => void;
  onDuplicate: (id: string) => void;
  onSaveAs: (id: string) => void;
  onRename: (id: string) => void;
  onToggleFavorite: (id: string) => void;
  onExport: (id: string) => void;
  onDelete: (id: string) => void;
  onRevealFile: (path: string) => void;
  onOpenFolder: () => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}

/**
 * Lists macro files that could not be read. Nothing is changed on disk — the user can open the
 * file location, fix or remove the file themselves, then refresh.
 */
export function BrokenFilesNotice({
  broken,
  compact = false,
  onReveal,
  onRefresh,
}: {
  broken: BrokenMacro[];
  compact?: boolean;
  onReveal: (path: string) => void;
  onRefresh: () => void;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(!compact);
  if (broken.length === 0) return null;
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 p-3 text-xs text-amber-100">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
        <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0 text-amber-300" />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="font-medium">
            {broken.length} macro file{broken.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} could not be read
          </p>
          <p className="text-amber-200/80">They are left untouched. Fix or remove them, then refresh.</p>
        </div>
        <button className="shrink-0 text-amber-300 underline" onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)}>
          {open ? "Hide" : "Details"}
        </button>
      </div>
      {open && (
        <ul className="mt-2 space-y-2">
          {broken.map((b) => (
            <li key={b.path} className="rounded border border-amber-500/20 bg-bg/40 p-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono" title={b.path}>
                  {b.file_name}
                </span>
                <button className="shrink-0 text-amber-300 underline" onClick={() => onReveal(b.path)}>
                  Show in folder
                </button>
              </div>
              <p className="mt-1 text-amber-200/80">{b.error}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
          <li>
            <button className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={onRefresh}>
              <RefreshCw className="size-3.5" /> Refresh
            </button>
          </li>
        </ul>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function useMacroSearch(macros: MacroSummary[]) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const filtered = q ? macros.filter((m) => m.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || m.hotkey?.toLowerCase().includes(q)) : macros;
  return { query, setQuery, filtered };
}

export function SearchBox({ value, onChange }: { value: string; onChange: (v: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-line-strong bg-bg/70 px-2.5 py-1.5 focus-within:border-accent">
      <Search className="size-4 text-muted" />
      <input className="w-full bg-transparent text-sm outline-none placeholder:text-muted/70" placeholder="Search macros…" value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value)} />
    </label>
  );
}

/** Compact list used in the recorder's right column. */
export function LibraryPanel({
  macros,
  broken,
  selectedId,
  canCreate,
  actions,
}: {
  macros: MacroSummary[];
  broken: BrokenMacro[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  canCreate: boolean;
  actions: LibraryActions;
}) {
  const { query, setQuery, filtered } = useMacroSearch(macros);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-3">
      <SearchBox value={query} onChange={setQuery} />
      <BrokenFilesNotice broken={broken} compact onReveal={actions.onRevealFile} onRefresh={actions.onRefresh} />
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
        {filtered.map((m) => (
          <MacroRow key={m.id} macro={m} selected={m.id === selectedId} actions={actions} canCreate={canCreate} />
        ))}
        {macros.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted">No macros yet.</p>}
        {macros.length > 0 && filtered.length === 0 && <p className="p-4 text-center text-sm text-muted">No macros match “{query}”.</p>}
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={actions.onNew} disabled={!canCreate} title={canCreate ? "Create an empty macro" : "Plan limit reached"}>
          <Plus className="size-4" /> New
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={actions.onImport} disabled={!canCreate}>
          <Upload className="size-4" /> Import
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MacroRow({ macro, selected, actions, canCreate }: { macro: MacroSummary; selected: boolean; actions: LibraryActions; canCreate: boolean }) {
  const Icon = macroIcon(macro.id);
  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(
        "group flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2 transition",
        selected ? "border-accent/60 bg-accent/15 shadow-[0_0_14px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.25*var(--fx)))]" : "border-transparent hover:border-line hover:bg-panel-3/60",
      )}
    >
      <button className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3 text-left" onClick={() => actions.onSelect(macro.id)}>
        <span className="grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg bg-accent/15 text-accent-2">
          <Icon className="size-[18px]" />
        </span>
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <span className="block truncate text-sm font-medium" title={macro.name}>
            {macro.name}
          </span>
          <span className="block truncate text-[11px] text-muted">
            <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{macro.hotkey ?? "—"}</span> · {macro.step_count} actions · {formatMs(macro.duration_ms)}
          </span>
        </span>
      </button>
      <RowMenu id={macro.id} actions={actions} canCreate={canCreate} />
    </div>
  );
}

export function RowMenu({ id, actions, canCreate }: { id: string; actions: LibraryActions; canCreate: boolean }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    const close = (e: MouseEvent) => {
      if (!ref.current?.contains(e.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    };
    window.addEventListener("mousedown", close);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("mousedown", close);
  }, [open]);

  const item = (label: string, Icon: typeof Copy, fn: () => void, opts: { danger?: boolean; disabled?: boolean } = {}) => (
    <button
      disabled={opts.disabled}
      onClick={() => {
        setOpen(false);
        fn();
      }}
      className={clsx("flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-panel-3 disabled:opacity-40", opts.danger && "text-red-400")}
    >
      <Icon className="size-4" /> {label}
    </button>
  );

  return (
    <div className="relative" ref={ref}>
      <button className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-1" onClick={() => setOpen((o) => !o)} aria-label="Macro actions">
        <MoreHorizontal className="size-4" />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <div className="absolute top-full right-0 z-20 mt-1 w-40 rounded-lg border border-line-strong bg-panel-2 p-1 shadow-xl">
          {item("Rename", Pencil, () => actions.onRename(id))}
          {item("Duplicate", Copy, () => actions.onDuplicate(id), { disabled: !canCreate })}
          {item("Export", Download, () => actions.onExport(id))}
          {item("Delete", Trash2, () => actions.onDelete(id), { danger: true })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/Overlay.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Flame, Square, Timer, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { RoutineSettings } from "../lib/types";
import { useLive } from "../lib/use-live";

/**
 * The PERFECT counter: a small always-on-top window above the game (index.html#overlay; also fine
 * to capture in OBS as the window "SLNC Perfect Counter"). Counts every PERFECT the game shows,
 * pops on each one, keeps the streak and the pace, today's and all-time totals, the daily goal
 * and the timer, and has Stop. Three looks (Fishing → Settings). Drag it by its top line.
 */
export function Overlay() {
  const { status } = useLive(1);
  const [cfg, setCfg] = useState<RoutineSettings | null>(null);
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());

  // Settings can change in the app while this window is open: read them again now and then.
  useEffect(() => {
    const load = () =>
      api
        .getSettings()
        .then((s) => setCfg(s.fishing))
        .catch(() => {});
    load();
    const id = window.setInterval(load, 4000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.style.background = "#0c0d0f";
    document.body.style.background = "#0c0d0f";
  }, []);

  const perfect = status.gold;
  const rate = status.casts > 0 ? Math.round((perfect / status.casts) * 100) : 0;
  const today = status.today_before + perfect;
  const ever = status.all_before + perfect;
  const hours = status.running && status.started_at ? Math.max(1 / 60, (now - status.started_at) / 3_600_000) : 0;
  const perHour = hours ? Math.round(perfect / hours) : 0;
  const goal = cfg?.daily_goal ?? 0;
  const left = status.running && cfg?.stop_after_minutes ? Math.max(0, status.started_at + cfg.stop_after_minutes * 60_000 - now) : null;
  const style = cfg?.overlay_style ?? "gold";

  // A new PERFECT: replay the pop (a key change restarts the CSS animations).
  const [hit, setHit] = useState(0);
  const last = useRef(perfect);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (perfect > last.current) setHit((h) => h + 1);
    last.current = perfect;
  }, [perfect]);
  const [miss, setMiss] = useState(0);
  const lastMiss = useRef(status.missed);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (status.missed > lastMiss.current) setMiss((m) => m + 1);
    lastMiss.current = status.missed;
  }, [status.missed]);

  const hot = status.streak >= 3;
  const R = 52;
  const C = 2 * Math.PI * R;
  const zoom = (cfg?.overlay_scale ?? 100) / 100;

  return (
    <div className={clsx("pc-root h-screen overflow-hidden", `pc-${style}`)} style={{ zoom }}>
      <div className={clsx("pc-card relative flex h-full flex-col overflow-hidden", hot && "pc-hot")}>
        {hit > 0 && style !== "minimal" && <div key={`f${hit}`} className="pc-flash pointer-events-none absolute inset-0" />}

        <div data-tauri-drag-region className="relative z-10 flex cursor-move items-center gap-2 px-3.5 pt-2.5">
          <span className={clsx("size-2 rounded-full", status.running ? "pc-live bg-accent" : "bg-white/25")} />
          <span data-tauri-drag-region className="flex-1 truncate text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.28em] text-white/50 uppercase">
            {status.running ? (left !== null ? `Live · ${clock(left)} left` : "Live · fishing") : "Stopped"}
          </span>
          {left !== null && <Timer className="size-3 text-white/40" />}
          <button className="text-white/40 transition hover:text-white" aria-label="Close" onClick={() => api.overlayHide().catch(() => {})}>
            <X className="size-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="relative z-10 flex flex-1 items-center gap-4 px-4">
          <div className="relative grid size-[7.25rem] shrink-0 place-items-center">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" className="absolute inset-0 -rotate-90">
              <circle cx="60" cy="60" r={R} fill="none" stroke="rgb(255 255 255 / 0.07)" strokeWidth="5" />
              <circle
                cx="60"
                cy="60"
                r={R}
                fill="none"
                className="pc-ring-fill"
                strokeWidth="5"
                strokeLinecap="round"
                strokeDasharray={`${(rate / 100) * C} ${C}`}
                style={{ transition: "stroke-dasharray 500ms cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)" }}
              />
            </svg>
            {hit > 0 && style !== "minimal" && <span key={`r${hit}`} className="pc-ring absolute inset-2 rounded-full" />}
            <div className="text-center leading-none">
              <div key={`n${hit}`} className={clsx("pc-num num text-[2.5rem] font-black", hit > 0 && "pc-pop")}>
                {perfect.toLocaleString()}
              </div>
              <div className="pc-label mt-1 text-[9px] font-bold tracking-[0.3em] uppercase">Perfect</div>
            </div>
            {hit > 0 && (
              <span key={`p${hit}`} className="pc-plus num absolute -top-1 right-0 text-sm font-black">
                +1
              </span>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1.5">
            <div className={clsx("flex items-center gap-1.5", hot ? "pc-streak-hot" : "text-white/70")}>
              <Flame className={clsx("size-4", hot && "pc-flame")} />
              <span className="num text-xl font-black">×{status.streak}</span>
              <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-[0.2em] text-white/40 uppercase">streak</span>
              <span className="num ml-auto text-[10px] text-white/40">best ×{status.best_streak}</span>
            </div>
            <div key={`m${miss}`} className={clsx("grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 text-center", miss > 0 && "pc-shake")}>
              <Fig label="rate" value={status.casts ? `${rate}%` : "—"} />
              <Fig label="per hour" value={hours ? perHour.toLocaleString() : "—"} />
              <Fig label="missed" value={status.missed.toLocaleString()} />
            </div>
            <div className="flex justify-between text-[10px] text-white/45">
              <span>
                today <b className="num text-white/80">{today.toLocaleString()}</b>
              </span>
              <span>
                ever <b className="num text-white/80">{ever.toLocaleString()}</b>
              </span>
            </div>
            {goal > 0 && (
              <div className="space-y-0.5">
                <div className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/[0.08]">
                  <div className="pc-goal h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (today / goal) * 100)}%` }} />
                </div>
                <div className="num text-right text-[9px] text-white/40">
                  {today >= goal ? "daily goal reached ✓" : `${today.toLocaleString()} / ${goal.toLocaleString()} today`}
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {status.running && (
              <button className="pc-stop flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-md py-0.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide text-white uppercase" onClick={() => api.routineStop().catch(() => {})}>
                <Square className="size-2.5 fill-current" /> Stop
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Fig({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-md bg-white/[0.04] py-1">
      <div className="pc-fig num text-sm font-bold">{value}</div>
      <div className="text-[8px] font-bold tracking-[0.18em] text-white/40 uppercase">{label}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** 1:05:09 / 4:09 */
function clock(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  const sec = String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  return h ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${sec}` : `${m}:${sec}`;
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/PlaybackPanel.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { Pause, Play, Square } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import type { Entitlements } from "@macroapp/shared";
import type { Macro } from "../lib/types";

interface PlaybackPanelProps {
  settings: Macro["settings"];
  entitlements: Entitlements | undefined;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  isPaused: boolean;
  disabled: boolean;
  onChange: (patch: Partial<Macro["settings"]>) => void;
  onPlay: () => void;
  onPause: () => void;
  onStop: () => void;
}

export function PlaybackPanel({ settings, entitlements, isPlaying, isPaused, disabled, onChange, onPlay, onPause, onStop }: PlaybackPanelProps) {
  const loop = settings.repeat === 0;
  const canLoop = entitlements?.max_repeat === null;
  const canSpeed = entitlements?.speed_control ?? false;
  const overLimit = entitlements?.max_repeat != null && settings.repeat > entitlements.max_repeat;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="label">Repeat count</span>
        <input
          type="number"
          min={1}
          className="field w-24 font-mono"
          disabled={loop}
          value={loop ? 1 : settings.repeat}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ repeat: Math.max(1, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 1)) })}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="label">
          Loop until stopped{!canLoop && <span className="ml-1 rounded bg-accent/20 px-1 text-[10px] text-accent-2">NORMAL+</span>}
        </span>
        <Toggle checked={loop} disabled={!canLoop} onChange={(on) => onChange({ repeat: on ? 0 : 1 })} />
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <span className="label">
            Speed{!canSpeed && <span className="ml-1 rounded bg-accent/20 px-1 text-[10px] text-accent-2">NORMAL+</span>}
          </span>
          <span className="font-mono text-xs text-accent-2">{settings.speed.toFixed(2)}×</span>
        </div>
        <input
          type="range"
          min={0.25}
          max={4}
          step={0.25}
          disabled={!canSpeed}
          value={settings.speed}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ speed: Number(e.target.value) })}
          className="w-full accent-[var(--accent)] disabled:opacity-40"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <span className="label">Pause between runs (ms)</span>
        <input
          type="number"
          min={0}
          step={100}
          className="field w-24 font-mono"
          value={settings.delay_between_runs_ms}
          onChange={(e) => onChange({ delay_between_runs_ms: Math.max(0, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 0)) })}
        />
      </div>

      {overLimit && (
        <p className="text-xs text-amber-300">Your edition allows up to {entitlements?.max_repeat} repeats.</p>
      )}

      {isPlaying ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
          <button className="btn-secondary py-2.5" onClick={onPause}>
            {isPaused ? <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> : <Pause className="size-4 fill-current" />}
            {isPaused ? "Resume" : "Pause"}
          </button>
          <button className="btn-danger py-2.5" onClick={onStop}>
            <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <button className="btn-primary w-full py-2.5" onClick={onPlay} disabled={disabled}>
          <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Play
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Toggle({ checked, onChange, disabled }: { checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      role="switch"
      aria-checked={checked}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
      className={clsx(
        "relative h-6 w-11 shrink-0 rounded-full border transition disabled:opacity-40",
        checked ? "border-accent bg-accent shadow-[0_0_12px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.6*var(--fx)))]" : "border-line-strong bg-panel-3",
      )}
    >
      <span className={clsx("absolute top-0.5 size-[18px] rounded-full bg-white transition-all", checked ? "left-[22px]" : "left-0.5")} />
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/ProfileApps.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AppWindow, Plus, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Toggle } from "./PlaybackPanel";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { Profile } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

/**
 * Programs linked to a profile. With auto switching on, bringing one of them to the front
 * activates the profile (never while recording or playing). Only exe names are read.
 */
export function ProfileApps({ app, profile }: { app: AppStateHook; profile: Profile }) {
  const { settings, reportError, reloadProfiles } = app;
  const [recent, setRecent] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [input, setInput] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const load = () => api.recentApps().then(setRecent).catch(() => {});
    load();
    const t = window.setInterval(load, 3000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, []);

  const save = (apps: string[]) =>
    api
      .setProfileApps(profile.id, apps)
      .then(reloadProfiles)
      .catch(reportError);

  const add = (name: string) => {
    const n = name.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!n || profile.apps.includes(n)) return;
    void save([...profile.apps, n.endsWith(".exe") ? n : `${n}.exe`]);
  };

  const suggestions = recent.filter((r) => !profile.apps.includes(r)).slice(0, 8);

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 border-b border-line pb-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <AppWindow className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
        <h3 className="flex-1 text-sm font-medium">Programs that activate “{profile.name}”</h3>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
          Switch automatically
          <Toggle
            checked={settings?.auto_switch_profiles ?? false}
            onChange={(v) => settings && void app.saveSettings({ ...settings, auto_switch_profiles: v })}
          />
        </label>
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        {profile.apps.map((a) => (
          <span key={a} className="chip pr-1 font-mono">
            {a}
            <button className="rounded p-0.5 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-text" aria-label={`Remove ${a}`} onClick={() => void save(profile.apps.filter((x) => x !== a))}>
              <X className="size-3" />
            </button>
          </span>
        ))}
        {profile.apps.length === 0 && <span className="text-xs text-muted">No programs linked.</span>}
      </div>

      {suggestions.length > 0 && (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">Recently used:</span>
          {suggestions.map((s) => (
            <button key={s} className="chip font-mono hover:border-line-strong hover:text-text" onClick={() => add(s)}>
              <Plus className="size-3" /> {s}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      <form
        className="flex gap-2"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          add(input);
          setInput("");
        }}
      >
        <input className="field font-mono" placeholder="program.exe" maxLength={100} value={input} onChange={(e) => setInput(e.target.value)} />
        <button className="btn-secondary" type="submit" disabled={!input.trim()}>
          Add
        </button>
      </form>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">
        {settings?.auto_switch_profiles
          ? "On: when a linked program comes to the front, this profile becomes active (not while recording or playing)."
          : "Off: profiles only change when you pick one. Turn on “Switch automatically” to use these links."}{" "}
        A program can be linked to one profile. Only the program's file name is read.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/RecordCore.tsx
