import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Circle, Pause, Play, Square } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";

export type CoreState = "idle" | "countdown" | "recording" | "playing" | "paused";

/**
 * The Dashboard's recording module: a machined metal bezel around a lit glass dome, slightly tilted
 * in 3D. Pure CSS (no WebGL): it costs no GPU time when idle and follows the accent colour and the
 * effects slider. The dome itself is the main button (record / stop), so the primary action is
 * always the biggest target. The tilt follows the mouse a little; reduced motion turns that off
 * through the global transition rules.
 */
export function RecordCore({
  state,
  label,
  sublabel,
  progress,
  onPrimary,
  disabled,
}: {
  state: CoreState;
  label: string;
  sublabel?: string;
  /** 0–1 playback progress ring (optional). */
  progress?: number | null;
  onPrimary: () => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}) {
  const [tilt, setTilt] = useState({ x: 6, y: 0 });
  const stage = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const onMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const r = stage.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    const dx = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width - 0.5;
    const dy = (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height - 0.5;
    setTilt({ x: 6 - dy * 4, y: dx * 5 });
  };

  const recording = state === "recording";
  const active = recording || state === "playing";
  const Icon = state === "idle" ? Circle : state === "countdown" ? Circle : state === "paused" ? Play : recording ? Square : Pause;

  return (
    <div ref={stage} className="core-stage grid place-items-center py-4" onMouseMove={onMove} onMouseLeave={() => setTilt({ x: 6, y: 0 })}>
      <div className="core-device relative size-64" style={{ transform: `rotateX(${tilt.x}deg) rotateY(${tilt.y}deg)` }}>
        {/* floor shadow */}
        <div className="absolute inset-x-6 -bottom-8 h-10 rounded-[50%] bg-black/70 blur-xl" style={{ transform: "translateZ(-40px)" }} />
        {/* bezel */}
        <div className="core-bezel absolute inset-0 rounded-full" />
        {/* tick marks */}
        <svg className="absolute inset-0 size-full" viewBox="0 0 100 100" aria-hidden>
          {Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => (
            <line
              key={i}
              x1="50"
              y1="3.2"
              x2="50"
              y2={i % 5 === 0 ? "6.6" : "5"}
              transform={`rotate(${i * 6} 50 50)`}
              stroke={i % 5 === 0 ? "rgb(255 255 255 / 0.35)" : "rgb(255 255 255 / 0.12)"}
              strokeWidth="0.5"
            />
          ))}
          {progress != null && (
            <circle
              cx="50"
              cy="50"
              r="46.5"
              fill="none"
              stroke="rgb(var(--accent-2-rgb))"
              strokeWidth="1.2"
              strokeLinecap="round"
              strokeDasharray={`${Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progress)) * 292} 292`}
              transform="rotate(-90 50 50)"
              style={{ filter: "drop-shadow(0 0 3px rgb(var(--accent-rgb)))", transition: "stroke-dasharray 300ms" }}
            />
          )}
        </svg>
        {/* rotating light ring */}
        <div className={clsx("core-ring absolute inset-[9%] rounded-full", !active && "[animation-play-state:paused]")} />
        {/* pulse when recording */}
        {recording && <div className="core-pulse absolute inset-[16%] rounded-full border-2 border-red-500/70" />}
        {/* glass dome = primary button */}
        <button
          onClick={onPrimary}
          disabled={disabled}
          data-state={recording ? "recording" : undefined}
          aria-label={label}
          className="core-glass group absolute inset-[16%] grid place-items-center rounded-full transition hover:brightness-125 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-60"
          style={{ transform: "translateZ(18px)" }}
        >
          <span className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
            <span
              className={clsx(
                "grid size-14 place-items-center rounded-full border transition group-active:scale-95",
                recording ? "border-red-300/40 bg-red-500/80 shadow-[0_0_30px_rgb(239_68_68/0.8)]" : "border-white/15 bg-white/5 shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgb(255_255_255/0.2)]",
              )}
            >
              <Icon className={clsx("size-6", recording ? "fill-white text-white" : state === "idle" || state === "countdown" ? "fill-red-500 text-red-500" : "fill-accent-2 text-accent-2")} />
            </span>
            <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-[0.2em] text-text/90 uppercase">{label}</span>
            {sublabel && <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted tabular-nums">{sublabel}</span>}
          </span>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/RegionPicker.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { Region, ScreenPreview } from "../lib/types";

export type PickResult =
  | { kind: "region"; region: Region }
  | { kind: "point"; x: number; y: number; color: { r: number; g: number; b: number } };

interface Props {
  /** "region": drag a rectangle. "point": click a pixel (position + colour). */
  mode: "region" | "point";
  title: string;
  onPick: (result: PickResult) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
}

/**
 * Full-window picker over a one-off screenshot of all monitors. SLNC Macro hides itself while the
 * screenshot is taken. The image only lives in memory while this dialog is open.
 */
export function RegionPicker({ mode, title, onPick, onClose }: Props) {
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<ScreenPreview | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<{ x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number } | null>(null);
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const img = useRef<HTMLImageElement>(null);
  const canvas = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.captureScreenPreview().then(setPreview).catch((e) => setError(errorMessage(e)));
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  // Position in preview pixels.
  const toPreview = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    const r = img.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    const x = Math.max(0, Math.min(preview!.width - 1, Math.floor(((e.clientX - r.left) * preview!.width) / r.width)));
    const y = Math.max(0, Math.min(preview!.height - 1, Math.floor(((e.clientY - r.top) * preview!.height) / r.height)));
    return { x, y };
  };
  const toPhysical = (x: number, y: number) => ({ x: preview!.origin_x + x * preview!.scale, y: preview!.origin_y + y * preview!.scale });

  const pixelColor = (x: number, y: number) => {
    if (!canvas.current) {
      const c = document.createElement("canvas");
      c.width = preview!.width;
      c.height = preview!.height;
      c.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true })!.drawImage(img.current!, 0, 0);
      canvas.current = c;
    }
    const [r, g, b] = canvas.current.getContext("2d")!.getImageData(x, y, 1, 1).data;
    return { r: r!, g: g!, b: b! };
  };

  const onDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!preview) return;
    const p = toPreview(e);
    if (mode === "point") {
      const phys = toPhysical(p.x, p.y);
      onPick({ kind: "point", x: phys.x, y: phys.y, color: pixelColor(p.x, p.y) });
      return;
    }
    setDrag({ x0: p.x, y0: p.y, x1: p.x, y1: p.y });
  };
  const onMove = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (!preview) return;
    const p = toPreview(e);
    setHover(p);
    if (drag) setDrag({ ...drag, x1: p.x, y1: p.y });
  };
  const onUp = () => {
    if (!drag || !preview) return;
    const x = Math.min(drag.x0, drag.x1);
    const y = Math.min(drag.y0, drag.y1);
    const w = Math.abs(drag.x1 - drag.x0) + 1;
    const h = Math.abs(drag.y1 - drag.y0) + 1;
    setDrag(null);
    if (w < 3 || h < 3) return; // a click, not a drag
    const p = toPhysical(x, y);
    onPick({ kind: "region", region: { x: p.x, y: p.y, width: w * preview.scale, height: h * preview.scale } });
  };

  const box = drag && preview && {
    left: `${(Math.min(drag.x0, drag.x1) / preview.width) * 100}%`,
    top: `${(Math.min(drag.y0, drag.y1) / preview.height) * 100}%`,
    width: `${((Math.abs(drag.x1 - drag.x0) + 1) / preview.width) * 100}%`,
    height: `${((Math.abs(drag.y1 - drag.y0) + 1) / preview.height) * 100}%`,
  };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col bg-black/90" role="dialog" aria-label={title}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b border-line px-4 py-2 text-sm">
        <span className="font-medium">{title}</span>
        <span className="text-muted">
          {mode === "region" ? "Drag a rectangle around the area to watch." : "Click the exact spot."} Esc cancels. The screenshot is not saved.
        </span>
        {hover && preview && (
          <span className="ml-auto font-mono text-xs text-muted">
            {toPhysical(hover.x, hover.y).x}, {toPhysical(hover.x, hover.y).y}
          </span>
        )}
        <button className={hover ? "btn-ghost px-2" : "btn-ghost ml-auto px-2"} onClick={onClose} aria-label="Cancel">
          <X className="size-5" />
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 items-center justify-center p-3">
        {error ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{error}</p>
        ) : !preview ? (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted">
            <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin" /> Taking a screenshot…
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="relative max-h-full max-w-full cursor-crosshair select-none" onMouseDown={onDown} onMouseMove={onMove} onMouseUp={onUp} onMouseLeave={() => setHover(null)}>
            <img ref={img} src={preview.data_url} alt="Screenshot of your screens" draggable={false} className="block max-h-[calc(100vh-5rem)] max-w-full object-contain" />
            {box && <div className="pointer-events-none absolute border-2 border-accent-2 bg-accent/20" style={box} />}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/ReplayPicker.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Check, Loader2, Video, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api, errorMessage, isTauri } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { ReplayStatus } from "../lib/types";

const CHOICES = [
  { secs: 30, label: "30 seconds" },
  { secs: 60, label: "1 minute" },
  { secs: 300, label: "5 minutes" },
  { secs: 600, label: "10 minutes" },
  { secs: 1800, label: "30 minutes" },
  { secs: 3600, label: "1 hour" },
];

const fmt = (s: number) => (s >= 60 ? `${Math.floor(s / 60)} min${s % 60 ? ` ${s % 60} s` : ""}` : `${s} s`);

async function closeWindow() {
  if (!isTauri) return;
  const { getCurrentWindow } = await import("@tauri-apps/api/window");
  await getCurrentWindow().close();
}

/**
 * The small always-on-top window opened by the "Save replay" hotkey (window label "replay",
 * URL index.html#replay). Keys 1–6 pick a length, Esc closes.
 */
export function ReplayPicker() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<ReplayStatus | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getReplayStatus().then(setStatus).catch((e) => setError(errorMessage(e)));
  }, []);

  const save = (secs: number) => {
    if (saving !== null || done) return;
    setSaving(secs);
    setError(null);
    api
      .saveReplay(secs)
      .then((r) => {
        setDone(`Saved ${Math.round(r.seconds)} s to ${r.path}`);
        window.setTimeout(() => void closeWindow(), 1800);
      })
      .catch((e) => setError(errorMessage(e)))
      .finally(() => setSaving(null));
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape") void closeWindow();
      const n = Number(e.key);
      if (n >= 1 && n <= CHOICES.length) {
        const c = CHOICES[n - 1]!;
        if (!status || c.secs <= status.max_secs) save(c.secs);
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  });

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen flex-col gap-3 border border-accent/40 bg-[radial-gradient(500px_200px_at_50%_-20%,rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.25),transparent),#09080d] p-4">
      <div data-tauri-drag-region className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Video className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
        <h1 data-tauri-drag-region className="flex-1 text-sm font-semibold">
          Save replay — how far back?
        </h1>
        {status && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">{fmt(status.buffered_secs)} buffered</span>}
        <button className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-1" onClick={() => void closeWindow()} aria-label="Close">
          <X className="size-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid flex-1 grid-cols-3 gap-2">
        {CHOICES.map((c, i) => {
          const beyond = !!status && c.secs > status.max_secs;
          const partial = !!status && !beyond && c.secs > status.buffered_secs;
          return (
            <button
              key={c.secs}
              disabled={beyond || saving !== null || !!done}
              onClick={() => save(c.secs)}
              title={beyond ? "Longer than your buffer setting" : partial ? `Only ${fmt(status!.buffered_secs)} recorded so far — saves what there is` : undefined}
              className={clsx(
                "raised relative flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 px-2 text-sm transition hover:border-accent/50 disabled:opacity-35",
                saving === c.secs && "border-accent/70",
              )}
            >
              <span className="absolute top-1.5 left-2 kbd text-[10px]">{i + 1}</span>
              {saving === c.secs ? <Loader2 className="size-4 animate-spin text-accent-2" /> : <span className="font-medium">{c.label}</span>}
              {partial && <span className="text-[10px] text-amber-300">only {fmt(status!.buffered_secs)}</span>}
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {done ? (
        <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-xs text-emerald-300">
          <Check className="size-3.5 shrink-0" /> {done}
        </p>
      ) : error ? (
        <p className="text-xs text-red-300">{error}</p>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Saves the time before you pressed the hotkey. Press 1–6, or Esc to cancel.</p>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/RulesPanel.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Crosshair, FlaskConical, Plus, ScanEye, ShieldCheck, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import { KEY_OPTIONS } from "../lib/keys";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import type { Rule, RuleAction, RuleCondition, RuleStatus, RuleTestResult } from "../lib/types";
import { Toggle } from "./PlaybackPanel";
import { RegionPicker, type PickResult } from "./RegionPicker";

const MODS = ["ctrl", "alt", "shift", "win"] as const;

const CONDITION_DEFAULTS: Record<RuleCondition["type"], RuleCondition> = {
  color: { type: "color", r: 255, g: 0, b: 0, tolerance: 30, min_ratio: 0.05 },
  image: { type: "image", template: { width: 0, height: 0, gray_b64: "" }, threshold: 0.9 },
  text: { type: "text", contains: "", case_sensitive: false },
  change: { type: "change", sensitivity: 0.1 },
};

const CONDITION_LABELS: Record<RuleCondition["type"], string> = {
  color: "Colour is visible",
  image: "Image is visible",
  text: "Text is visible (OCR)",
  change: "Area changes",
};

const ACTION_LABELS: Record<RuleAction["type"], string> = {
  run_macro: "Run a macro",
  click: "Click",
  key_press: "Press a key",
  type_text: "Type text",
  stop_playback: "Stop playback",
  notify: "Only notify (safe test)",
};

function newRule(n: number): Rule {
  return {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    name: `Rule ${n}`,
    enabled: true,
    region: { x: 0, y: 0, width: 200, height: 100 },
    condition: CONDITION_DEFAULTS.color,
    trigger: "appears",
    action: { type: "notify" },
    delay_ms: 0,
    cooldown_ms: 2000,
    interval_ms: 500,
    allow_while_playing: false,
  };
}

/** What's wrong with a rule before saving (the backend validates again). */
function problemOf(r: Rule, macroIds: Set<string>): string | null {
  if (!r.name.trim() || r.name.length > 60) return "Give the rule a name (max 60 characters).";
  if (r.region.width < 1 || r.region.height < 1) return "Select an area on the screen.";
  if (r.condition.type === "image" && !r.condition.template.width) return "Capture a reference image first.";
  if (r.condition.type === "image" && (r.region.width < r.condition.template.width || r.region.height < r.condition.template.height))
    return "The area must be at least as large as the reference image.";
  if (r.condition.type === "text" && !r.condition.contains.trim()) return "Enter the text to look for.";
  if (r.action.type === "run_macro" && !macroIds.has(r.action.macro_id)) return "Choose the macro to run.";
  if (r.interval_ms < 100) return "Check interval must be at least 100 ms.";
  return null;
}

function conditionSummary(c: RuleCondition): string {
  switch (c.type) {
    case "color":
      return `Colour rgb(${c.r}, ${c.g}, ${c.b})`;
    case "image":
      return c.template.width ? `Image ${c.template.width}×${c.template.height}` : "Image (not captured)";
    case "text":
      return `Text “${c.contains}”`;
    case "change":
      return "Area changes";
  }
}

const pct = (v: number | null | undefined) => (v == null ? "–" : `${Math.round(v * 100)}%`);

/**
 * Conditions tab: screen rules that watch an area of the screen and run an action.
 * Everything is checked locally and in memory; screenshots are never saved or uploaded.
 */
export function RulesPanel({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { macros, license, monitor, reportError } = app;
  const [rules, setRules] = useState<Rule[]>([]);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Rule | null>(null);
  const [picker, setPicker] = useState<null | "region" | "color" | "click">(null);
  const [test, setTest] = useState<RuleTestResult | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<null | "save" | "test" | "template" | "monitor">(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .getRules()
      .then((r) => {
        setRules(r);
        if (r[0]) select(r[0]);
      })
      .catch(reportError);
  }, []);

  // null = unlimited; missing (old license) = the Free limit of 1.
  const maxActive = license?.entitlements.max_active_rules === undefined ? 1 : license.entitlements.max_active_rules;
  const limitText = `Your edition allows ${maxActive} active screen rule${maxActive === 1 ? "" : "s"}. Switch another rule off first.`;
  const saved = rules.find((r) => r.id === draft?.id) ?? null;
  const dirty = !!draft && JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(saved);
  const problem = draft ? problemOf(draft, new Set(macros.map((m) => m.id))) : null;
  const running = !!monitor?.running;
  const statusOf = (id: string): RuleStatus | undefined => (running ? monitor?.rules.find((s) => s.rule_id === id) : undefined);
  const enabledCount = rules.filter((r) => r.enabled).length;

  function select(r: Rule) {
    setSelected(r.id);
    setDraft(structuredClone(r));
    setTest(null);
  }

  const persist = async (next: Rule[]) => {
    const result = await api.saveRules(next);
    setRules(result);
    return result;
  };

  const save = async () => {
    if (!draft || problem) return;
    if (maxActive !== null && draft.enabled && rules.filter((r) => r.enabled && r.id !== draft.id).length >= maxActive) return app.setError(limitText);
    setBusy("save");
    try {
      await persist(saved ? rules.map((r) => (r.id === draft.id ? draft : r)) : [...rules, draft]);
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const toggleEnabled = async (rule: Rule, enabled: boolean) => {
    if (maxActive !== null && enabled && enabledCount >= maxActive) return app.setError(limitText);
    try {
      await persist(rules.map((r) => (r.id === rule.id ? { ...r, enabled } : r)));
      if (draft?.id === rule.id) setDraft((d) => d && { ...d, enabled });
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    }
  };

  const remove = async () => {
    if (!draft) return;
    if (saved && !window.confirm(`Delete the rule “${saved.name}”?`)) return;
    try {
      const next = saved ? await persist(rules.filter((r) => r.id !== draft.id)) : rules;
      if (next[0]) select(next[0]);
      else {
        setSelected(null);
        setDraft(null);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    }
  };

  const addRule = () => {
    const r = newRule(rules.length + 1);
    if (maxActive !== null && enabledCount >= maxActive) r.enabled = false;
    setSelected(r.id);
    setDraft(r);
    setTest(null);
  };

  const runTest = async () => {
    if (!draft) return;
    setBusy("test");
    setTest(null);
    try {
      setTest(await api.testRule(draft));
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const captureTemplate = async () => {
    if (!draft) return;
    setBusy("template");
    try {
      const template = await api.captureTemplate(draft.region);
      setDraft((d) => d && { ...d, condition: { type: "image", template, threshold: d.condition.type === "image" ? d.condition.threshold : 0.9 } });
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const setMonitoring = async (mode: "off" | "test" | "on") => {
    if (dirty) return app.setError("Save or discard your rule changes first.");
    setBusy("monitor");
    try {
      if (mode === "off") {
        await api.stopMonitoring();
        app.setMonitor(null);
      } else {
        await api.startMonitoring(mode === "test");
        app.setMonitor({ running: true, test_mode: mode === "test", rules: [], stopped_reason: null });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(null);
    }
  };

  const onPick = (p: PickResult) => {
    const target = picker;
    setPicker(null);
    setDraft((d) => {
      if (!d) return d;
      if (p.kind === "region") return { ...d, region: p.region };
      if (target === "color") return { ...d, condition: { ...(d.condition.type === "color" ? d.condition : CONDITION_DEFAULTS.color), type: "color", ...p.color } as RuleCondition };
      return { ...d, action: { type: "click", button: d.action.type === "click" ? d.action.button : "left", x: p.x, y: p.y } };
    });
  };

  const patch = (p: Partial<Rule>) => setDraft((d) => d && { ...d, ...p });
  const patchCondition = (p: Partial<RuleCondition>) => setDraft((d) => d && { ...d, condition: { ...d.condition, ...p } as RuleCondition });
  const patchAction = (p: Partial<RuleAction>) => setDraft((d) => d && { ...d, action: { ...d.action, ...p } as RuleAction });

  const mode = !running ? "off" : monitor?.test_mode ? "test" : "on";
  const live = draft ? statusOf(draft.id) : undefined;

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 grid-cols-[260px_1fr]">
      {/* ----- Rule list ----- */}
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col border-r border-line">
        <div className="space-y-2 border-b border-line p-3">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <ScanEye className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
            <h3 className="font-medium">Screen rules</h3>
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 rounded-lg bg-panel-2 p-1 text-xs" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Screen monitoring">
            {(["off", "test", "on"] as const).map((m) => (
              <button
                key={m}
                role="radio"
                aria-checked={mode === m}
                disabled={busy === "monitor"}
                onClick={() => mode !== m && setMonitoring(m)}
                className={clsx(
                  "rounded-md px-2 py-1 transition",
                  mode === m ? (m === "on" ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-accent-deep to-accent text-white" : m === "test" ? "bg-amber-500/25 text-amber-200" : "bg-panel-3 text-text") : "text-muted hover:text-text",
                )}
              >
                {m === "off" ? "Off" : m === "test" ? "Test mode" : "On"}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <p className="text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted">
            {mode === "test" ? "Test mode: rules are checked and shown live, but nothing is clicked or typed." : mode === "on" ? "Active: rules run their actions." : "Monitoring is off. Nothing on your screen is checked."}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 space-y-1 overflow-y-auto p-2">
          {rules.length === 0 && !draft && <p className="p-3 text-center text-xs text-muted">No rules yet.</p>}
          {[...rules, ...(draft && !saved ? [draft] : [])].map((r) => {
            const s = statusOf(r.id);
            return (
              <div
                key={r.id}
                onClick={() => r.id !== selected && (dirty ? window.confirm("Discard unsaved changes to this rule?") : true) && select(r)}
                className={clsx("flex cursor-pointer items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-2", r.id === selected ? "border-accent/50 bg-accent/10" : "border-transparent hover:bg-panel-3")}
              >
                <span
                  className={clsx("size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full", !s ? "bg-panel-3" : s.error ? "bg-red-500" : s.matched ? "bg-emerald-400 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgb(52_211_153/0.8)]" : "bg-muted/50")}
                  title={!s ? "Not monitored" : s.error ?? (s.matched ? "Matches now" : "No match")}
                />
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <p className="truncate text-sm">{r.name || "Untitled"}</p>
                  <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted">
                    {conditionSummary(r.condition)}
                    {s && s.fired_count > 0 && ` · fired ${s.fired_count}×`}
                    {!rules.some((x) => x.id === r.id) && " · not saved"}
                  </p>
                </div>
                {rules.some((x) => x.id === r.id) && (
                  <span onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                    <Toggle checked={r.enabled} onChange={(v) => toggleEnabled(r, v)} />
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-2 border-t border-line p-3">
          <button className="btn-secondary w-full py-1.5" onClick={addRule} disabled={dirty || rules.length >= 50}>
            <Plus className="size-4" /> New rule
          </button>
          <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">{maxActive === null ? "Unlimited active rules on your edition." : `Active rules on your edition: ${enabledCount} of ${maxActive}.`}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* ----- Editor ----- */}
      <div className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto p-4">
        {!draft ? (
          <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 text-center">
            <ScanEye className="size-10 text-accent-2" />
            <p className="text-base font-medium">React to what's on your screen</p>
            <p className="max-w-md text-sm text-muted">
              A rule watches an area of your screen for a colour, an image, text or any change — and then runs a macro, clicks, presses a key or types text.
            </p>
            <button className="btn-primary mt-2" onClick={addRule}>
              <Plus className="size-4" /> New rule
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-4">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <input className="field max-w-sm font-medium" value={draft.name} maxLength={60} onChange={(e) => patch({ name: e.target.value })} aria-label="Rule name" />
              <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
                <button className="btn-ghost py-1 text-red-300" onClick={remove} title="Delete rule">
                  <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                </button>
                {dirty && saved && (
                  <button className="btn-ghost py-1" onClick={() => select(saved)}>
                    Discard
                  </button>
                )}
                <button className="btn-primary py-1" onClick={save} disabled={!dirty || !!problem || busy === "save"} title={problem ?? undefined}>
                  {busy === "save" ? "Saving…" : "Save rule"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            {problem && dirty && <p className="text-xs text-amber-300">{problem}</p>}

            {/* 1. Where */}
            <Section n={1} title="Where to look">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <button className="btn-secondary py-1.5" onClick={() => setPicker("region")}>
                  <Crosshair className="size-4" /> Select on screen
                </button>
                <span className="font-mono text-xs text-muted">
                  x {draft.region.x}, y {draft.region.y} · {draft.region.width}×{draft.region.height} px
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Smaller areas are faster and more reliable. SLNC Macro's own window counts as screen content — minimise it while monitoring.</p>
            </Section>

            {/* 2. What */}
            <Section n={2} title="What to look for">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                <label className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="label">Condition</span>
                  <select
                    className="field w-56"
                    value={draft.condition.type}
                    onChange={(e) => patch({ condition: CONDITION_DEFAULTS[e.target.value as RuleCondition["type"]] })}
                  >
                    {Object.entries(CONDITION_LABELS).map(([k, v]) => (
                      <option key={k} value={k}>
                        {v}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>
                {draft.condition.type !== "change" && (
                  <label className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="label">Fire when it</span>
                    <select className="field w-40" value={draft.trigger} onChange={(e) => patch({ trigger: e.target.value as Rule["trigger"] })}>
                      <option value="appears">appears</option>
                      <option value="disappears">disappears</option>
                    </select>
                  </label>
                )}
              </div>

              {draft.condition.type === "color" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                  <span className="size-9 rounded-lg border border-line-strong" style={{ background: `rgb(${draft.condition.r}, ${draft.condition.g}, ${draft.condition.b})` }} />
                  <label className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="label">Colour</span>
                    <input
                      type="color"
                      className="h-9 w-16 cursor-pointer rounded border border-line bg-transparent"
                      value={`#${[draft.condition.r, draft.condition.g, draft.condition.b].map((v) => v.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("")}`}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const hex = e.target.value;
                        patchCondition({ r: parseInt(hex.slice(1, 3), 16), g: parseInt(hex.slice(3, 5), 16), b: parseInt(hex.slice(5, 7), 16) });
                      }}
                    />
                  </label>
                  <button className="btn-secondary py-1.5" onClick={() => setPicker("color")}>
                    <Crosshair className="size-4" /> Pick from screen
                  </button>
                  <NumField label="Tolerance" value={draft.condition.tolerance} min={0} max={255} onChange={(v) => patchCondition({ tolerance: v })} hint="0 = exact colour" />
                  <NumField label="Min. amount of area" value={Math.round(draft.condition.min_ratio * 100)} min={0} max={100} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => patchCondition({ min_ratio: v / 100 })} />
                </div>
              )}

              {draft.condition.type === "image" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                  {draft.condition.template.width ? (
                    <TemplatePreview template={draft.condition.template} />
                  ) : (
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted">Select the area around the image first (max 400×400), then capture it as reference.</p>
                  )}
                  <button className="btn-secondary py-1.5" onClick={captureTemplate} disabled={busy === "template"}>
                    {busy === "template" ? "Capturing…" : draft.condition.template.width ? "Recapture reference" : "Capture reference image"}
                  </button>
                  <NumField label="Similarity" value={Math.round(draft.condition.threshold * 100)} min={0} max={100} suffix="%" onChange={(v) => patchCondition({ threshold: v / 100 })} hint="Higher = stricter" />
                </div>
              )}

              {draft.condition.type === "text" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                  <label className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                    <span className="label">Text contains</span>
                    <input className="field" value={draft.condition.contains} maxLength={200} onChange={(e) => patchCondition({ contains: e.target.value })} placeholder="e.g. Ready" />
                  </label>
                  <label className="flex items-center gap-2 pb-2 text-sm">
                    <input type="checkbox" checked={draft.condition.case_sensitive} onChange={(e) => patchCondition({ case_sensitive: e.target.checked })} /> Match case
                  </label>
                  <p className="w-full text-[11px] text-muted">Uses Windows' built-in text recognition, on this PC. Clear, horizontal text works best.</p>
                </div>
              )}

              {draft.condition.type === "change" && (
                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                  <NumField label="Fire when at least" value={Math.round(draft.condition.sensitivity * 100)} min={1} max={100} suffix="% changed" onChange={(v) => patchCondition({ sensitivity: v / 100 })} />
                  <p className="pb-2 text-[11px] text-muted">After firing, the new picture becomes the reference.</p>
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 rounded-lg bg-panel-2/60 px-3 py-2">
                <button
                  className="btn-secondary py-1"
                  onClick={runTest}
                  disabled={busy === "test" || (draft.condition.type === "image" && !draft.condition.template.width) || (draft.condition.type === "text" && !draft.condition.contains.trim())}
                >
                  <FlaskConical className="size-4" /> {busy === "test" ? "Checking…" : "Test now"}
                </button>
                {test ? (
                  <span className="text-sm">
                    <span className={test.matched ? "text-emerald-300" : "text-muted"}>{test.matched ? "Match" : "No match"}</span>
                    {test.score != null && <span className="text-muted"> · score {pct(test.score)}</span>}
                    {test.text != null && <span className="text-muted"> · read: “{test.text.slice(0, 80) || "(nothing)"}”</span>}
                    {draft.condition.type === "change" && <span className="text-muted"> · change needs monitoring to compare</span>}
                  </span>
                ) : live ? (
                  <span className="text-sm text-muted">
                    Live: {live.error ? <span className="text-red-300">{live.error}</span> : live.matched ? <span className="text-emerald-300">match</span> : "no match"}
                    {live.score != null && ` · ${pct(live.score)}`}
                    {live.text != null && ` · “${live.text.slice(0, 60)}”`}
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="text-xs text-muted">Checks once, right now. Nothing is executed.</span>
                )}
              </div>
            </Section>

            {/* 3. Then */}
            <Section n={3} title="Then do">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <label className="space-y-1">
                  <span className="label">Action</span>
                  <select className="field w-56" value={draft.action.type} onChange={(e) => patch({ action: defaultAction(e.target.value as RuleAction["type"], macros[0]?.id) })}>
                    {Object.entries(ACTION_LABELS).map(([k, v]) => (
                      <option key={k} value={k}>
                        {v}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </label>

                {draft.action.type === "run_macro" && (
                  <label className="space-y-1">
                    <span className="label">Macro</span>
                    <select className="field w-60" value={draft.action.macro_id} onChange={(e) => patchAction({ macro_id: e.target.value })}>
                      <option value="">Choose…</option>
                      {macros.map((m) => (
                        <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                          {m.name}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </label>
                )}

                {draft.action.type === "click" && (
                  <>
                    <label className="space-y-1">
                      <span className="label">Button</span>
                      <select className="field w-28" value={draft.action.button} onChange={(e) => patchAction({ button: e.target.value as "left" })}>
                        <option value="left">Left</option>
                        <option value="right">Right</option>
                        <option value="middle">Middle</option>
                      </select>
                    </label>
                    <NumField label="X" value={draft.action.x} min={-32768} max={32767} onChange={(v) => patchAction({ x: v })} />
                    <NumField label="Y" value={draft.action.y} min={-32768} max={32767} onChange={(v) => patchAction({ y: v })} />
                    <button className="btn-secondary py-1.5" onClick={() => setPicker("click")}>
                      <Crosshair className="size-4" /> Pick on screen
                    </button>
                  </>
                )}

                {draft.action.type === "key_press" && (
                  <>
                    <label className="space-y-1">
                      <span className="label">Key</span>
                      <select className="field w-36" value={draft.action.vk} onChange={(e) => patchAction({ vk: Number(e.target.value) })}>
                        {KEY_OPTIONS.map((k) => (
                          <option key={k.vk} value={k.vk}>
                            {k.name}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </label>
                    <div className="flex gap-3 pb-2">
                      {MODS.map((m) => {
                        const mods = draft.action.type === "key_press" ? draft.action.mods : [];
                        return (
                          <label key={m} className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-sm capitalize">
                            <input type="checkbox" checked={mods.includes(m)} onChange={(e) => patchAction({ mods: e.target.checked ? [...mods, m] : mods.filter((x) => x !== m) })} />
                            {m}
                          </label>
                        );
                      })}
                    </div>
                  </>
                )}

                {draft.action.type === "type_text" && (
                  <label className="flex-1 space-y-1">
                    <span className="label">Text</span>
                    <input className="field" value={draft.action.value} maxLength={10000} onChange={(e) => patchAction({ value: e.target.value })} placeholder="Supports {{variables}}" />
                  </label>
                )}
              </div>
              {draft.action.type === "click" && <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Screen pixel coordinates (all monitors).</p>}
            </Section>

            {/* 4. Timing & safety */}
            <Section n={4} title="Timing & safety">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-3">
                <NumField label="Check every" value={draft.interval_ms} min={100} max={60000} suffix="ms" onChange={(v) => patch({ interval_ms: v })} />
                <NumField label="Wait before action" value={draft.delay_ms} min={0} max={600000} suffix="ms" onChange={(v) => patch({ delay_ms: v })} />
                <NumField label="Cooldown" value={draft.cooldown_ms} min={0} max={3600000} suffix="ms" onChange={(v) => patch({ cooldown_ms: v })} hint="Min. time between two firings" />
              </div>
              <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                <Toggle checked={draft.allow_while_playing} onChange={(v) => patch({ allow_while_playing: v })} />
                Also fire while a macro is playing
              </label>
              <p className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted">
                <ShieldCheck className="mt-px size-3.5 shrink-0 text-accent-2" />
                Rules never fire while you record. A rule fires once when its condition starts (or stops) matching, not on every check. If rules fire more than 30 times a minute,
                monitoring stops itself. Emergency stop <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{app.settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span> also stops monitoring.
              </p>
            </Section>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {picker && (
        <RegionPicker
          mode={picker === "region" ? "region" : "point"}
          title={picker === "region" ? "Select the area to watch" : picker === "color" ? "Pick a colour" : "Pick the spot to click"}
          onPick={onPick}
          onClose={() => setPicker(null)}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function defaultAction(type: RuleAction["type"], macroId: string | undefined): RuleAction {
  switch (type) {
    case "run_macro":
      return { type, macro_id: macroId ?? "" };
    case "click":
      return { type, button: "left", x: 0, y: 0 };
    case "key_press":
      return { type, vk: 0x0d, mods: [] };
    case "type_text":
      return { type, value: "" };
    default:
      return { type };
  }
}

function Section({ n, title, children }: { n: number; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="card space-y-3 p-4">
      <h4 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
        <span className="grid size-5 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent/20 text-[11px] text-accent-2">{n}</span>
        {title}
      </h4>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

function NumField({ label, value, onChange, min, max, suffix, hint }: { label: string; value: number; onChange: (v: number) => void; min: number; max: number; suffix?: string; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <label className="space-y-1" title={hint}>
      <span className="label">{label}</span>
      <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <input
          type="number"
          className="field w-24"
          value={value}
          min={min}
          max={max}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const v = Math.round(Number(e.target.value));
            if (Number.isFinite(v)) onChange(Math.max(min, Math.min(max, v)));
          }}
        />
        {suffix && <span className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap text-muted">{suffix}</span>}
      </span>
    </label>
  );
}

/** Shows the stored grayscale reference image. */
function TemplatePreview({ template }: { template: { width: number; height: number; gray_b64: string } }) {
  const [url, setUrl] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const gray = Uint8Array.from(atob(template.gray_b64), (c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
    const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    canvas.width = template.width;
    canvas.height = template.height;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d")!;
    const data = ctx.createImageData(template.width, template.height);
    gray.forEach((v, i) => data.data.set([v, v, v, 255], i * 4));
    ctx.putImageData(data, 0, 0);
    setUrl(canvas.toDataURL());
  }, [template]);
  return url ? <img src={url} alt="Reference" className="max-h-24 max-w-48 rounded border border-line object-contain [image-rendering:pixelated]" /> : null;
}
