FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/ScreenStep.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Crosshair, Gamepad2, Loader2, ScanEye } from "lucide-react";
import { DEFAULT_SCREEN_LIMITS, type MacroStep, type MouseButton, type ScreenAction, type ScreenExpect, type ScreenInput, type ScreenLimits, type ScreenSettings, type SeenTarget } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { KEY_OPTIONS, keyName } from "../lib/keys";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";

type ScreenStep = Extract<MacroStep, { type: "screen" }>;
/** Opens the box picker over the screen and teaches a target in the game window (null = cancelled). */
export type Teach = (kind: SeenTarget["kind"], hint: string) => Promise<SeenTarget | null>;

/** Screen steps offered by "+ Add step" (the ones with a target are taught right away). */
export const NEW_SCREEN_STEPS: Array<{ label: string; teach?: { kind: SeenTarget["kind"]; hint: string }; make: (t: SeenTarget | null) => MacroStep }> = [
  { label: "See & click a button", teach: { kind: "button", hint: "Draw a box around the button to click" }, make: (t) => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "click_target", target: t!, button: "left", clicks: 1 } }) },
  { label: "Hold while a bar shows", teach: { kind: "indicator", hint: "Draw a box around the hold bar or prompt" }, make: (t) => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "hold", input: { type: "key", vk: 0x45 }, until: { type: "while_visible", target: t! } } }) },
  { label: "Hold + click", make: () => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "hold_and_click", hold: { type: "key", vk: 0x10 }, click: { type: "input", input: { type: "mouse", button: "left" } }, delay_ms: 900, click_ms: 100, release: "click_first" } }) },
  { label: "Hold for a time", make: () => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "hold", input: { type: "key", vk: 0x45 }, until: { type: "time", ms: 1000 } } }) },
  { label: "Wait until something shows", teach: { kind: "button", hint: "Draw a box around what to wait for" }, make: (t) => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "wait_for", target: t!, gone: false } }) },
  { label: "Click a spot of the game window", make: () => ({ type: "screen", action: { kind: "click_point", x: 0.5, y: 0.5, button: "left" } }) },
];

/** The macro's game window and limits (Recorder side panel). */
export function ScreenSettingsPanel({ value, onChange }: { value: ScreenSettings | undefined; onChange: (v: ScreenSettings | undefined) => void }) {
  const [recent, setRecent] = useState<string[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    api.recentApps().then(setRecent).catch(() => {});
  }, []);
  const limits = value?.limits ?? DEFAULT_SCREEN_LIMITS;
  const set = (patch: Partial<ScreenLimits>) => value && onChange({ ...value, limits: { ...limits, ...patch } });
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <label className="block space-y-1.5">
        <span className="label">Game window (screen steps act only here)</span>
        <select className="field" value={value?.exe ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value ? { exe: e.target.value, limits } : undefined)}>
          <option value="">— none —</option>
          {value?.exe && !recent.includes(value.exe) && <option value={value.exe}>{value.exe}</option>}
          {recent.map((a) => (
            <option key={a} value={a}>
              {a}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </label>
      {value && (
        <>
          <p className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">The whole macro pauses and lets go of every key while this window isn't in front.</p>
          <details className="rounded-md border border-line p-2.5">
            <summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs font-medium">Limits (safe defaults)</summary>
            <div className="mt-2.5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <Lim label="Timeout (s)" value={limits.timeout_ms / 1000} min={0.1} max={600} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => set({ timeout_ms: Math.round(v * 1000) })} />
              <Lim label="Retries" value={limits.retries} min={0} max={10} onChange={(v) => set({ retries: v })} />
              <Lim label="Look again when unsure" value={limits.unsure_retries} min={0} max={10} onChange={(v) => set({ unsure_retries: v })} />
              <Lim label="Clicks per second" value={limits.max_clicks_per_second} min={1} max={20} onChange={(v) => set({ max_clicks_per_second: v })} />
              <Lim label="Sure from (%)" value={Math.round(limits.sure * 100)} min={50} max={99} onChange={(v) => set({ sure: v / 100 })} />
              <Lim label="Longest hold (s)" value={limits.max_hold_ms / 1000} min={0.1} max={600} step={1} onChange={(v) => set({ max_hold_ms: Math.round(v * 1000) })} />
            </div>
            <button className="btn-ghost mt-2 px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => onChange({ ...value, limits: DEFAULT_SCREEN_LIMITS })}>
              Back to defaults
            </button>
          </details>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Lim({ label, value, min, max, step = 1, onChange }: { label: string; value: number; min: number; max: number; step?: number; onChange: (v: number) => void }) {
  return (
    <label className="block space-y-1">
      <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">{label}</span>
      <input
        type="number"
        className="field py-1! font-mono text-xs"
        value={value}
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const v = Number(e.target.value);
          if (Number.isFinite(v)) onChange(Math.min(max, Math.max(min, step < 1 ? v : Math.round(v / step) * step)));
        }}
      />
    </label>
  );
}

/** Editor for one screen step (Inspector). */
export function ScreenFields({ step, onChange, screen, teach }: { step: ScreenStep; onChange: (s: MacroStep) => void; screen: ScreenSettings | undefined; teach: Teach }) {
  const a = step.action;
  const setA = (action: ScreenAction) => onChange({ ...step, action });
  const mainTarget = a.kind === "click_target" || a.kind === "wait_for" ? a.target : a.kind === "hold" && a.until.type === "while_visible" ? a.until.target : a.kind === "hold_and_click" && a.click.type === "target" ? a.click.target : null;
  const reteach = async (t: SeenTarget, apply: (t: SeenTarget) => void) => {
    const n = await teach(t.kind, `Draw a box around “${t.name}” again`);
    if (n) apply({ ...n, name: t.name });
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {!screen && <p className="rounded-md border border-amber-400/30 bg-amber-400/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-100">Pick the game window under “Game window” first: screen steps only act there.</p>}

      {a.kind === "click_target" && (
        <>
          <TargetBox target={a.target} screen={screen} onChange={(target) => setA({ ...a, target })} onReteach={() => reteach(a.target, (target) => setA({ ...a, target }))} />
          <Button value={a.button} onChange={(button) => setA({ ...a, button })} />
          <Field label="Clicks (once unless you ask for more)">
            <Num value={a.clicks ?? 1} min={1} max={10} onChange={(clicks) => setA({ ...a, clicks })} />
          </Field>
        </>
      )}

      {a.kind === "hold" && (
        <>
          <InputField label="Hold" value={a.input} onChange={(input) => setA({ ...a, input })} />
          <Field label="Until">
            <select
              className="field"
              value={a.until.type}
              onChange={async (e) => {
                if (e.target.value === "time") return setA({ ...a, until: { type: "time", ms: 1000 } });
                const t = await teach("indicator", "Draw a box around the hold bar or prompt");
                if (t) setA({ ...a, until: { type: "while_visible", target: t } }); // max_ms only for recorded holds
              }}
            >
              <option value="time">a set time</option>
              <option value="while_visible">a bar / prompt is gone</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
          {a.until.type === "time" ? (
            <Field label="Hold time (s)">
              <Num decimals value={a.until.ms / 1000} min={0.04} max={600} step={0.1} onChange={(s) => setA({ ...a, until: { type: "time", ms: Math.round(s * 1000) } })} />
            </Field>
          ) : (
            <TargetBox target={a.until.target} screen={screen} onChange={(target) => setA({ ...a, until: { type: "while_visible", target } })} onReteach={() => a.until.type === "while_visible" && reteach(a.until.target, (target) => setA({ ...a, until: { type: "while_visible", target } }))} />
          )}
        </>
      )}

      {a.kind === "hold_and_click" && (
        <>
          <InputField label="Hold" value={a.hold} onChange={(hold) => setA({ ...a, hold })} />
          <Field label="Wait after pressing (s)">
            <Num decimals value={a.delay_ms / 1000} min={0} max={600} step={0.05} onChange={(s) => setA({ ...a, delay_ms: Math.round(s * 1000) })} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Then click">
            <select
              className="field"
              value={a.click.type}
              onChange={async (e) => {
                if (e.target.value === "input") return setA({ ...a, click: { type: "input", input: { type: "mouse", button: "left" } } });
                const t = await teach("button", "Draw a box around what to click while holding");
                if (t) setA({ ...a, click: { type: "target", target: t, button: "left" } });
              }}
            >
              <option value="input">a key / mouse button</option>
              <option value="target">a button on screen</option>
              {a.click.type === "point" && <option value="point">a spot of the game window</option>}
            </select>
          </Field>
          {a.click.type === "input" ? (
            <InputField label="Click" value={a.click.input} onChange={(input) => setA({ ...a, click: { type: "input", input } })} />
          ) : a.click.type === "point" ? (
            <>
              <Button value={a.click.button} onChange={(button) => a.click.type === "point" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, button } })} />
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <Field label="X (% of window)">
                  <Num decimals value={Math.round(a.click.x * 1000) / 10} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => a.click.type === "point" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, x: v / 100 } })} />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Y (% of window)">
                  <Num decimals value={Math.round(a.click.y * 1000) / 10} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => a.click.type === "point" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, y: v / 100 } })} />
                </Field>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <TargetBox target={a.click.target} screen={screen} onChange={(target) => a.click.type === "target" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, target } })} onReteach={() => a.click.type === "target" && reteach(a.click.target, (target) => a.click.type === "target" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, target } }))} />
              <Button value={a.click.button} onChange={(button) => a.click.type === "target" && setA({ ...a, click: { ...a.click, button } })} />
            </>
          )}
          <Field label="Click held for (s)">
            <Num decimals value={a.click_ms / 1000} min={0.04} max={600} step={0.05} onChange={(s) => setA({ ...a, click_ms: Math.round(s * 1000) })} />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Let go of">
            <select className="field" value={a.release ?? "click_first"} onChange={(e) => setA({ ...a, release: e.target.value as "click_first" | "hold_first" })}>
              <option value="click_first">the click first, then the hold</option>
              <option value="hold_first">the hold first, then the click</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
        </>
      )}

      {a.kind === "click_point" && (
        <>
          <Button value={a.button} onChange={(button) => setA({ ...a, button })} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <Field label="X (% of window)">
              <Num decimals value={Math.round(a.x * 1000) / 10} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => setA({ ...a, x: v / 100 })} />
            </Field>
            <Field label="Y (% of window)">
              <Num decimals value={Math.round(a.y * 1000) / 10} min={0} max={100} step={0.5} onChange={(v) => setA({ ...a, y: v / 100 })} />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {a.kind === "wait_for" && (
        <>
          <TargetBox target={a.target} screen={screen} onChange={(target) => setA({ ...a, target })} onReteach={() => reteach(a.target, (target) => setA({ ...a, target }))} />
          <Field label="Wait until it">
            <select className="field" value={a.gone ? "gone" : "shows"} onChange={(e) => setA({ ...a, gone: e.target.value === "gone" })}>
              <option value="shows">shows</option>
              <option value="gone">is gone</option>
            </select>
          </Field>
        </>
      )}

      <Field label="Timeout for this step (s) — empty = the macro's">
        <input
          type="number"
          className="field font-mono"
          placeholder={String((screen?.limits.timeout_ms ?? DEFAULT_SCREEN_LIMITS.timeout_ms) / 1000)}
          value={step.timeout_ms ? step.timeout_ms / 1000 : ""}
          min={0.1}
          max={600}
          step={0.5}
          onChange={(e) => {
            const { timeout_ms: _drop, ...rest } = step;
            const v = Number(e.target.value);
            onChange(e.target.value && Number.isFinite(v) ? { ...rest, timeout_ms: Math.round(Math.min(600, Math.max(0.1, v)) * 1000) } : rest);
          }}
        />
      </Field>

      {a.kind !== "wait_for" && (
        <ExpectField
          expect={step.expect}
          main={mainTarget}
          teach={teach}
          onChange={(expect) => {
            const { expect: _drop, ...rest } = step;
            onChange(expect ? { ...rest, expect } : rest);
          }}
        />
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function ExpectField({ expect, main, teach, onChange }: { expect: ScreenExpect | undefined; main: SeenTarget | null; teach: Teach; onChange: (e: ScreenExpect | undefined) => void }) {
  const value = !expect ? "none" : main && expect.target.gray_b64 === main.gray_b64 ? `${expect.type}-main` : `${expect.type}-other`;
  return (
    <Field label="Afterwards check (tried again if it doesn't happen)">
      <select
        className="field"
        value={value}
        onChange={async (e) => {
          const v = e.target.value;
          if (v === "none") return onChange(undefined);
          const [type, which] = v.split("-") as ["gone" | "visible", "main" | "other"];
          if (which === "main" && main) return onChange({ type, target: main });
          const t = await teach("button", type === "visible" ? "Draw a box around what should show afterwards" : "Draw a box around what should be gone afterwards");
          if (t) onChange({ type, target: t });
        }}
      >
        <option value="none">nothing</option>
        {main && <option value="gone-main">“{main.name}” is gone</option>}
        {main && <option value="visible-main">“{main.name}” still shows</option>}
        <option value="visible-other">{expect?.type === "visible" && value === "visible-other" ? `“${expect.target.name}” shows` : "something else shows… (teach it)"}</option>
        <option value="gone-other">{expect?.type === "gone" && value === "gone-other" ? `“${expect.target.name}” is gone` : "something else is gone… (teach it)"}</option>
      </select>
    </Field>
  );
}

/** The taught picture, its name, how sure it must be, and a "test now" button. */
function TargetBox({ target, screen, onChange, onReteach }: { target: SeenTarget; screen: ScreenSettings | undefined; onChange: (t: SeenTarget) => void; onReteach: () => void }) {
  const [test, setTest] = useState<{ busy: boolean; text?: string; tone?: "ok" | "warn" | "bad" }>({ busy: false });
  const run = () => {
    if (!screen) return;
    setTest({ busy: true });
    api
      .screenTest(screen.exe, target, screen.limits)
      .then((r) => setTest({ busy: false, text: r.text, tone: r.certainty === "sure" ? "ok" : r.certainty === "unsure" ? "warn" : "bad" }))
      .catch((e) => setTest({ busy: false, text: errorMessage(e), tone: "bad" }));
  };
  return (
    <div className="space-y-2 rounded-md border border-line p-2.5">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Picture target={target} />
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1.5">
          <input className="field py-1! text-sm" value={target.name} maxLength={60} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...target, name: e.target.value })} />
          <span className="block text-[11px] text-muted">
            {target.kind === "indicator" ? "Hold bar / indicator" : "Button"} · {target.width}×{target.height} px{target.color ? " · colour cue" : ""}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={target.search_everywhere !== false} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...target, search_everywhere: e.target.checked })} />
        Look in the whole window (off: only near where it was taught)
      </label>
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
        <span className="shrink-0 text-muted">Sure from</span>
        <input type="number" className="field w-16! py-0.5! font-mono text-xs" placeholder={String(Math.round((screen?.limits.sure ?? 0.85) * 100))} value={target.sure ? Math.round(target.sure * 100) : ""} min={50} max={99} onChange={(e) => {
          const { sure: _drop, ...rest } = target;
          const v = Number(e.target.value);
          onChange(e.target.value && Number.isFinite(v) ? { ...rest, sure: Math.min(99, Math.max(50, v)) / 100 } : rest);
        }} />
        <span className="text-muted">% (empty = the macro's)</span>
      </label>
      <div className="flex gap-1.5">
        <button className="btn-secondary flex-1 py-1 text-xs" disabled={!screen || test.busy} onClick={run} title="Looks in the game window now; nothing is clicked">
          {test.busy ? <Loader2 className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> : <ScanEye className="size-3.5" />} Test now
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary flex-1 py-1 text-xs" disabled={!screen} onClick={onReteach}>
          <Crosshair className="size-3.5" /> Teach again
        </button>
      </div>
      {test.text && <p className={test.tone === "ok" ? "text-xs text-emerald-300" : test.tone === "warn" ? "text-xs text-amber-300" : "text-xs text-red-300"}>{test.text}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Picture({ target }: { target: SeenTarget }) {
  const canvas = useRef<HTMLCanvasElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const ctx = canvas.current?.getContext("2d");
    if (!ctx) return;
    let bytes: Uint8Array;
    try {
      bytes = Uint8Array.from(atob(target.gray_b64), (ch) => ch.charCodeAt(0));
    } catch {
      return;
    }
    const img = ctx.createImageData(target.width, target.height);
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) {
      const v = bytes[i]!;
      img.data.set([v, v, v, 255], i * 4);
    }
    ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  }, [target]);
  return <canvas ref={canvas} width={target.width} height={target.height} className="block h-14 w-20 shrink-0 rounded border border-line-strong object-contain [image-rendering:pixelated]" />;
}

function InputField({ label, value, onChange }: { label: string; value: ScreenInput; onChange: (i: ScreenInput) => void }) {
  const known = value.type === "key" && KEY_OPTIONS.some((k) => k.vk === value.vk);
  const code = value.type === "key" ? `k${value.vk}` : `m${value.button}`;
  return (
    <Field label={label}>
      <select
        className="field"
        value={code}
        onChange={(e) => {
          const v = e.target.value;
          onChange(v.startsWith("m") ? { type: "mouse", button: v.slice(1) as MouseButton } : { type: "key", vk: Number(v.slice(1)) });
        }}
      >
        <optgroup label="Mouse">
          <option value="mleft">Left mouse</option>
          <option value="mright">Right mouse</option>
          <option value="mmiddle">Middle mouse</option>
        </optgroup>
        <optgroup label="Keys">
          {value.type === "key" && !known && <option value={code}>{keyName(value.vk)}</option>}
          {KEY_OPTIONS.map((k) => (
            <option key={k.vk} value={`k${k.vk}`}>
              {k.name}
            </option>
          ))}
        </optgroup>
      </select>
    </Field>
  );
}

function Button({ value, onChange }: { value: MouseButton; onChange: (b: MouseButton) => void }) {
  return (
    <Field label="Mouse button">
      <select className="field" value={value} onChange={(e) => onChange(e.target.value as MouseButton)}>
        <option value="left">Left</option>
        <option value="right">Right</option>
        <option value="middle">Middle</option>
      </select>
    </Field>
  );
}

function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block space-y-1.5">
      <span className="label">{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  );
}

function Num({ value, onChange, min, max, step = 1, decimals = false }: { value: number; onChange: (v: number) => void; min: number; max: number; step?: number; decimals?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <input
      type="number"
      className="field font-mono"
      value={value}
      min={min}
      max={max}
      step={step}
      onChange={(e) => {
        const raw = Number(e.target.value);
        if (!Number.isFinite(raw)) return;
        onChange(Math.min(max, Math.max(min, decimals ? raw : Math.round(raw))));
      }}
    />
  );
}

export const GameIcon = Gamepad2;


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/Trackers.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Eye, Hash, Plus, ScanText, Send, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import type { Area, LiveMessage, MacroDocument, SeenTarget, Tracker } from "@macroapp/shared";
import type { AppSettings } from "../lib/types";
import { api, errorMessage, onEvent } from "../lib/tauri";
import { DiscordEmbed, type Embed } from "./DiscordCards";
import { Picture, type Teach } from "./ScreenStep";

/** Draws a box in the game window and gives it back as an area (null = cancelled). */
export type PickArea = (hint: string) => Promise<Area | null>;

const newId = () => crypto.randomUUID();

/**
 * What a macro keeps an eye on while it plays: counts (PERFECT, Missed…), names it reads (what you
 * caught, its rarity) and numbers (kick power, speed). All names are yours; everything is read on
 * this PC from the game window.
 */
export function TrackersPanel({ macro, onChange, teach, pickArea }: { macro: MacroDocument; onChange: (trackers: Tracker[]) => void; teach: Teach; pickArea: PickArea }) {
  const list = macro.trackers ?? [];
  const exe = macro.screen?.exe;
  const set = (t: Tracker) => onChange(list.map((x) => (x.id === t.id ? t : x)));
  const counters = list.filter((t) => t.kind.type === "count");
  const [read, setRead] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});

  const addCount = async () => {
    const target = await teach("indicator", "Draw a box around what shows when it counts (e.g. PERFECT)");
    if (target) onChange([...list, { id: newId(), name: target.name, kind: { type: "count", target, role: "hit" }, show: true }]);
  };
  const addText = async () => {
    const area = await pickArea("Draw a box around where the name shows (e.g. what you caught)");
    if (area) onChange([...list, { id: newId(), name: "Caught", kind: { type: "read_text", area, after: counters[0]?.id }, show: true }]);
  };
  const addNumber = async () => {
    const area = await pickArea("Draw a box around the number (e.g. kick power)");
    if (area) onChange([...list, { id: newId(), name: "Number", kind: { type: "read_number", area, unit: "" }, show: true }]);
  };
  const test = (t: Tracker, area: Area) => {
    if (!exe) return;
    setRead((r) => ({ ...r, [t.id]: "Reading…" }));
    api
      .screenRead(exe, area)
      .then((text) => setRead((r) => ({ ...r, [t.id]: text ? `Reads: “${text}”` : "Nothing readable there right now" })))
      .catch((e) => setRead((r) => ({ ...r, [t.id]: errorMessage(e) })));
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {!exe && <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-200">Pick the game window first: trackers read from it.</p>}
      {list.length === 0 && <p className="text-xs text-muted">Nothing tracked yet. Add what you want counted or read — it shows up live in Discord with your names.</p>}
      {list.map((t) => (
        <div key={t.id} className="space-y-2 rounded-md border border-line p-2.5">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {t.kind.type === "count" ? <Eye className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> : t.kind.type === "read_text" ? <ScanText className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> : <Hash className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" />}
            <input className="field py-1! text-sm" value={t.name} maxLength={40} onChange={(e) => set({ ...t, name: e.target.value })} aria-label="Name" />
            <button className="btn-ghost px-1.5 text-red-400" title="Remove" onClick={() => onChange(list.filter((x) => x.id !== t.id).map((x) => (x.kind.type === "read_text" && x.kind.after === t.id ? { ...x, kind: { ...x.kind, after: undefined } } : x)))}>
              <Trash2 className="size-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
          {t.kind.type === "count" && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Picture target={t.kind.target as SeenTarget} />
              <select className="field py-1! text-xs" value={t.kind.role ?? "hit"} onChange={(e) => t.kind.type === "count" && set({ ...t, kind: { ...t.kind, role: e.target.value as "hit" | "miss" | "plain" } })}>
                <option value="hit">a hit (streak, rate)</option>
                <option value="miss">a miss (ends the streak)</option>
                <option value="plain">just count it</option>
              </select>
            </div>
          )}
          {t.kind.type === "read_text" && (
            <>
              <label className="block space-y-1 text-xs">
                <span className="text-muted">Read it</span>
                <select className="field py-1! text-xs" value={t.kind.after ?? ""} onChange={(e) => t.kind.type === "read_text" && set({ ...t, kind: { ...t.kind, after: e.target.value || undefined } })}>
                  <option value="">whenever it changes</option>
                  {counters.map((c) => (
                    <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>
                      each time “{c.name}” counts
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              <div className="flex gap-1.5">
                <button className="btn-secondary flex-1 py-1 text-xs" disabled={!exe} onClick={() => t.kind.type === "read_text" && test(t, t.kind.area)}>
                  Test read
                </button>
                <button
                  className="btn-secondary flex-1 py-1 text-xs"
                  disabled={!exe}
                  onClick={async () => {
                    if (t.kind.type !== "read_text") return;
                    if (t.kind.rarity) return set({ ...t, kind: { ...t.kind, rarity: undefined } });
                    const rarity = await pickArea("Draw a box around where the rarity shows");
                    if (rarity && t.kind.type === "read_text") set({ ...t, kind: { ...t.kind, rarity } });
                  }}
                >
                  {t.kind.rarity ? "Remove rarity" : "+ Rarity"}
                </button>
              </div>
            </>
          )}
          {t.kind.type === "read_number" && (
            <div className="flex gap-1.5">
              <input className="field py-1! text-xs" placeholder="unit (optional)" maxLength={12} value={t.kind.unit ?? ""} onChange={(e) => t.kind.type === "read_number" && set({ ...t, kind: { ...t.kind, unit: e.target.value } })} />
              <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0 py-1 text-xs" disabled={!exe} onClick={() => t.kind.type === "read_number" && test(t, t.kind.area)}>
                Test read
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
          {read[t.id] && <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">{read[t.id]}</p>}
          <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-muted">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={t.show !== false} onChange={(e) => set({ ...t, show: e.target.checked })} /> Show in Discord
          </label>
        </div>
      ))}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-1.5">
        <button className="btn-secondary flex-col gap-0.5 py-1.5 text-[11px]" disabled={!exe} onClick={addCount} title="+1 each time something shows (PERFECT, Missed, Goal…)">
          <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Count
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary flex-col gap-0.5 py-1.5 text-[11px]" disabled={!exe} onClick={addText} title="Read a name (what you caught, which item)">
          <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Read name
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary flex-col gap-0.5 py-1.5 text-[11px]" disabled={!exe} onClick={addNumber} title="Read a number (kick power, speed)">
          <Plus className="size-3.5" /> Read number
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The macro's live Discord message: your title and footer, what it shows, and a real preview. */
export function LivePanel({ macro, onChange, settings, onSaveSettings }: { macro: MacroDocument; onChange: (live: LiveMessage) => void; settings: AppSettings | null; onSaveSettings: (s: AppSettings) => void }) {
  const live: LiveMessage = macro.live ?? { enabled: false, title: macro.name, footer: "", predictions: true, streaks: true, totals: true };
  const [embed, setEmbed] = useState<Embed | null>(null);
  // While this macro plays: the real message, updated every 2 s (also without Discord).
  const [now, setNow] = useState<Embed | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setNow(null);
    const off = onEvent<{ macro_id: string; message: Embed }>("macro://live", (e) => e.macro_id === macro.id && setNow(e.message));
    return () => void off.then((f) => f());
  }, [macro.id]);
  const [hook, setHook] = useState(settings?.live_webhook ?? "");
  const [state, setState] = useState<{ ok?: boolean; text: string } | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => setHook(settings?.live_webhook ?? ""), [settings?.live_webhook]);
  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .livePreview(macro)
      .then((e) => setEmbed(e as Embed))
      .catch(() => setEmbed(null));
  }, [macro]);
  const hookOk = /^https:\/\/(ptb\.|canary\.)?discord(app)?\.com\/api\/webhooks\/.{10,}/.test(hook.trim());

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        <input type="checkbox" checked={live.enabled} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, enabled: e.target.checked })} />
        Live message while it plays (updates every ~20 s)
      </label>
      <label className="block space-y-1">
        <span className="label">Title</span>
        <input className="field" maxLength={60} value={live.title} placeholder={macro.name} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, title: e.target.value })} />
      </label>
      <label className="block space-y-1">
        <span className="label">Footer (empty = SLNC Macro · macro name)</span>
        <input className="field" maxLength={100} value={live.footer ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, footer: e.target.value })} />
      </label>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-4 gap-y-1 text-xs">
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={live.predictions !== false} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, predictions: e.target.checked })} /> Predictions
        </label>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={live.streaks !== false} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, streaks: e.target.checked })} /> Ended streaks
        </label>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
          <input type="checkbox" checked={live.totals !== false} onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...live, totals: e.target.checked })} /> Today & all time
        </label>
      </div>
      <label className="block space-y-1">
        <span className="label">Discord webhook (all your macros)</span>
        <span className="flex gap-1.5">
          <input className="field font-mono text-xs" type="password" autoComplete="off" placeholder="https://discord.com/api/webhooks/…" value={hook} onChange={(e) => setHook(e.target.value)} />
          <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0 py-1 text-xs" disabled={!settings || hook === (settings?.live_webhook ?? "") || (!!hook.trim() && !hookOk)} onClick={() => settings && onSaveSettings({ ...settings, live_webhook: hook.trim() })}>
            Save
          </button>
        </span>
      </label>
      {(now ?? embed) && (
        <div className="space-y-1">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">{now ? "Live now (from the macro that is playing)" : "How it looks"}</p>
          <DiscordEmbed embed={(now ?? embed)!} />
        </div>
      )}
      <button
        className="btn-primary w-full"
        disabled={!settings?.live_webhook}
        onClick={() => {
          setState({ text: "Sending…" });
          api
            .liveSendPreview(macro)
            .then(() => setState({ ok: true, text: "Preview sent — look in Discord" }))
            .catch((e) => setState({ ok: false, text: errorMessage(e) }));
        }}
      >
        <Send className="size-4" /> Send preview to Discord
      </button>
      {state && <p className={state.ok === false ? "text-xs text-red-300" : "text-xs text-accent-2"}>{state.text}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Page layout                                                         */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

export function Page({ children, className, wide }: { children: React.ReactNode; className?: string; wide?: boolean }) {
  return <div className={clsx("page-enter mx-auto space-y-5 p-6", wide ? "max-w-[1400px]" : "max-w-6xl", className)}>{children}</div>;
}

export function PageHeader({ icon: Icon, title, subtitle, actions }: { icon: typeof X; title: string; subtitle?: string; actions?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-5 border-b border-line pb-5">
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-mono text-[11px] tracking-[0.22em] text-accent uppercase">
          <Icon className="size-3.5" /> SLNC <span className="text-muted/60">/</span> <span className="text-muted">{title}</span>
        </p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-[2.1rem] leading-tight font-semibold tracking-[-0.02em] text-text">{title}</h1>
        {subtitle && <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-muted">{subtitle}</p>}
      </div>
      {actions && <div className="ml-auto flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">{actions}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Section({ title, description, children, actions, className }: { title: string; description?: string; children: React.ReactNode; actions?: React.ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <section className={clsx("panel space-y-4 p-5", className)}>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start gap-3">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h2 className="section-title">{title}</h2>
          {description && <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">{description}</p>}
        </div>
        {actions}
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

export function StatTile({ icon: Icon, label, value, hint }: { icon: typeof X; label: string; value: string; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel flex items-center gap-4 p-4" title={hint}>
      <div className="grid size-10 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-md border border-accent/30 bg-accent/[0.07] text-accent">
        <Icon className="size-[18px]" />
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <div className="truncate font-mono text-[10px] tracking-[0.14em] text-muted uppercase">{label}</div>
        <div className="num truncate text-xl font-semibold text-text">{value}</div>
        {hint && <div className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted/80">{hint}</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({ icon: Icon, title, text, action }: { icon: typeof X; title: string; text?: string; action?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-10 text-center">
      <div className="mb-2 grid size-14 place-items-center rounded-lg border border-dashed border-line-strong text-accent">
        <Icon className="size-6" />
      </div>
      <p className="font-medium">{title}</p>
      {text && <p className="max-w-md text-sm text-muted">{text}</p>}
      {action && <div className="mt-2">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Segmented<T extends string>({ value, options, onChange, disabled }: { value: T; options: Array<{ value: T; label: string }>; onChange: (v: T) => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="well inline-flex gap-1 p-1" role="radiogroup">
      {options.map((o) => (
        <button
          key={o.value}
          role="radio"
          aria-checked={value === o.value}
          disabled={disabled}
          onClick={() => onChange(o.value)}
          className={clsx(
            "rounded px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium transition disabled:opacity-40",
            value === o.value ? "bg-accent/15 text-accent-2 shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.45)]" : "text-muted hover:text-text",
          )}
        >
          {o.label}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Badge({ children, tone = "accent" }: { children: React.ReactNode; tone?: "accent" | "success" | "warning" | "danger" | "muted" }) {
  const tones = {
    accent: "bg-accent/15 text-accent-2 border-accent/25",
    success: "bg-emerald-500/12 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/25",
    warning: "bg-amber-500/12 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/25",
    danger: "bg-red-500/12 text-red-300 border-red-500/25",
    muted: "bg-white/5 text-muted border-line",
  };
  return <span className={clsx("inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-wider uppercase", tones[tone])}>{children}</span>;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
/* Dialogs (confirm / prompt) — replaces window.confirm / prompt        */
/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

interface ConfirmOptions {
  title: string;
  message?: React.ReactNode;
  confirmText?: string;
  danger?: boolean;
  /** User must type this word to enable the confirm button. */
  typeToConfirm?: string;
}
interface PromptOptions {
  title: string;
  label?: string;
  initial?: string;
  confirmText?: string;
  maxLength?: number;
}

type DialogRequest =
  | ({ kind: "confirm"; resolve: (ok: boolean) => void } & ConfirmOptions)
  | ({ kind: "prompt"; resolve: (value: string | null) => void } & PromptOptions);

const DialogContext = createContext<{ confirm: (o: ConfirmOptions) => Promise<boolean>; prompt: (o: PromptOptions) => Promise<string | null> } | null>(null);

export function DialogProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [request, setRequest] = useState<DialogRequest | null>(null);
  const confirm = useCallback((o: ConfirmOptions) => new Promise<boolean>((resolve) => setRequest({ kind: "confirm", resolve, ...o })), []);
  const prompt = useCallback((o: PromptOptions) => new Promise<string | null>((resolve) => setRequest({ kind: "prompt", resolve, ...o })), []);
  const ctx = useRef({ confirm, prompt });

  return (
    <DialogContext.Provider value={ctx.current}>
      {children}
      {request && <DialogView request={request} onDone={() => setRequest(null)} />}
    </DialogContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useDialogs() {
  const ctx = useContext(DialogContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useDialogs outside DialogProvider");
  return ctx;
}

function DialogView({ request, onDone }: { request: DialogRequest; onDone: () => void }) {
  const [text, setText] = useState(request.kind === "prompt" ? (request.initial ?? "") : "");
  const input = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const finish = (ok: boolean) => {
    if (request.kind === "confirm") request.resolve(ok);
    else request.resolve(ok && text.trim() ? text.trim() : null);
    onDone();
  };
  useEffect(() => {
    input.current?.focus();
    input.current?.select();
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && finish(false);
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  const needsWord = request.kind === "confirm" && request.typeToConfirm;
  const canConfirm = request.kind === "prompt" ? Boolean(text.trim()) : !needsWord || text === request.typeToConfirm;
  const danger = request.kind === "confirm" && request.danger;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] grid place-items-center bg-black/75 p-6" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={request.title} onMouseDown={() => finish(false)}>
      <form
        className="panel page-enter w-full max-w-md space-y-4 p-5"
        onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault();
          if (canConfirm) finish(true);
        }}
      >
        <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
          {danger && <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-5 shrink-0 text-red-400" />}
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">{request.title}</h2>
            {request.kind === "confirm" && request.message && <div className="mt-1.5 text-sm text-muted">{request.message}</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
        {request.kind === "prompt" && (
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">
            {request.label && <span className="label">{request.label}</span>}
            <input ref={input} className="field" value={text} maxLength={request.maxLength ?? 100} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
          </label>
        )}
        {needsWord && (
          <label className="block space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">
              Type <span className="font-mono text-text">{request.typeToConfirm}</span> to confirm
            </span>
            <input ref={input} className="field font-mono" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} />
          </label>
        )}
        <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
          <button type="button" className="btn-ghost" onClick={() => finish(false)}>
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button type="submit" className={danger ? "btn-danger" : "btn-primary"} disabled={!canConfirm}>
            {request.confirmText ?? (request.kind === "prompt" ? "Save" : "Confirm")}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/VariablesPanel.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Braces, Plus, Trash2 } from "lucide-react";
import type { MacroStep } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { Variable } from "../lib/types";
import { BUILT_IN_VARIABLES, countUses, VARIABLE_NAME } from "../lib/variables";

/**
 * Variables tab: reusable values shared by all macros.
 * Use `{{name}}` in a Type Text step, or pick a variable as a Delay's length.
 */
export function VariablesPanel({
  variables,
  steps,
  onSaved,
  onError,
}: {
  variables: Variable[];
  steps: MacroStep[];
  onSaved: (vars: Variable[]) => void;
  onError: (e: unknown) => void;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<Variable[]>(variables);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => setDraft(variables), [variables]);

  const dirty = JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(variables);
  const names = draft.map((v) => v.name.trim().toLowerCase());
  const problem = (v: Variable, i: number): string | null => {
    const name = v.name.trim();
    if (!VARIABLE_NAME.test(name)) return "Letters, digits and _ only; can't start with a digit (max 32)";
    if (BUILT_IN_VARIABLES.some((b) => b.name === name.toLowerCase())) return "Built-in name";
    if (names.indexOf(name.toLowerCase()) !== i) return "Duplicate name";
    return null;
  };
  const invalid = draft.some((v, i) => problem(v, i));

  const update = (i: number, patch: Partial<Variable>) => setDraft((d) => d.map((v, j) => (j === i ? { ...v, ...patch } : v)));
  const save = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      onSaved(await api.saveVariables(draft));
    } catch (e) {
      onError(e);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 gap-4 overflow-y-auto p-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_260px]">
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Braces className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
          <h3 className="font-medium">Variables</h3>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted">shared by all macros</span>
          <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
            {dirty && (
              <button className="btn-ghost py-1" onClick={() => setDraft(variables)}>
                Discard
              </button>
            )}
            <button className="btn-primary py-1" onClick={save} disabled={!dirty || invalid || saving}>
              {saving ? "Saving…" : "Save variables"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-line">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr_70px_36px] gap-2 border-b border-line bg-panel-2/60 px-3 py-2 text-[11px] tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">
            <span>Name</span>
            <span>Value</span>
            <span className="text-right">Used here</span>
            <span />
          </div>
          {draft.map((v, i) => {
            const err = problem(v, i);
            return (
              <div key={i} className="grid grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr_70px_36px] items-start gap-2 border-b border-line/60 px-3 py-2 last:border-0">
                <div>
                  <input
                    className="field font-mono"
                    value={v.name}
                    placeholder="email"
                    maxLength={32}
                    onChange={(e) => update(i, { name: e.target.value })}
                    aria-invalid={!!err}
                  />
                  {err && <p className="mt-1 text-[11px] text-red-400">{err}</p>}
                </div>
                <input className="field" value={v.value} placeholder="value" maxLength={2000} onChange={(e) => update(i, { value: e.target.value })} />
                <span className="pt-1.5 text-right font-mono text-xs text-accent-2">{v.name.trim() ? countUses(steps, v.name.trim()) : 0}</span>
                <button className="btn-ghost px-2 py-1.5 text-red-400" title="Delete variable" onClick={() => setDraft((d) => d.filter((_, j) => j !== i))}>
                  <Trash2 className="size-4" />
                </button>
              </div>
            );
          })}
          {draft.length === 0 && <p className="px-3 py-4 text-sm text-muted">No variables yet.</p>}
        </div>
        <button className="btn-secondary py-1" onClick={() => setDraft((d) => [...d, { name: "", value: "" }])}>
          <Plus className="size-4" /> Add variable
        </button>
      </div>

      <aside className="space-y-3 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
        <div className="card space-y-2 p-3">
          <p className="font-medium text-text">How it works</p>
          <p>
            Type <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{"{{name}}"}</span> in a <b>Type Text</b> action — it is replaced by the value when the macro
            plays.
          </p>
          <p>
            A <b>Delay</b> can use a variable that holds a number (milliseconds). Change the variable once to change every delay that uses it.
          </p>
          <p>Values are read when you press Play. Unknown names are typed literally, and the editor warns you about them.</p>
        </div>
        <div className="card space-y-1.5 p-3">
          <p className="font-medium text-text">Built-in</p>
          {BUILT_IN_VARIABLES.map((b) => (
            <p key={b.name}>
              <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{`{{${b.name}}}`}</span> — {b.description}
            </p>
          ))}
        </div>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/components/WrappedCard.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Award, Crown, Fish, Flame, Lock, Sparkles, Target, TrendingDown, TrendingUp, X, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { FishingAchievement, FishingWrapped } from "../lib/types";
import { duration } from "./FishingHistory";

/**
 * Fishing "Wrapped": a big summary card of one session, made from the real history on this PC.
 * `start` = that session, or null for the newest finished one. Shows nothing until it has loaded,
 * and closes itself when there is no finished session yet.
 */
export function WrappedCard({ start, onClose }: { start: number | null; onClose: () => void }) {
  const [card, setCard] = useState<FishingWrapped | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const [achievements, setAchievements] = useState<FishingAchievement[]>([]);
  const [showAll, setShowAll] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getFishingWrapped(start).then(setCard).catch(() => setCard(null));
    api.getFishingAchievements().then(setAchievements).catch(() => {});
  }, [start]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (card === null) onClose();
  }, [card, onClose]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === "Escape" && onClose();
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [onClose]);

  if (!card) return null;
  const ms = card.end - card.start;
  const date = new Date(card.start).toLocaleDateString([], { weekday: "long", day: "numeric", month: "long" });

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 grid place-items-center bg-black/70 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm" onClick={onClose}>
      <div
        className="wrapped-card relative max-h-[92vh] w-full max-w-2xl overflow-y-auto rounded-3xl border border-accent/30 p-7 shadow-[0_0_80px_-20px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.6)]"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        role="dialog"
        aria-label="Session Wrapped"
      >
        <button className="btn-ghost absolute top-4 right-4 px-1.5" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">
          <X className="size-5" />
        </button>

        <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-[0.3em] text-accent-2 uppercase">Session Wrapped</p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">{date}</h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">
          {duration(ms)} of fishing{card.account ? ` · ${card.account}` : ""}
        </p>

        {/* the big numbers */}
        <div className="mt-6 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-4">
          <Big icon={Fish} label="Catches" value={card.catches.toLocaleString()} hint={card.catches_per_hour != null ? `${card.catches_per_hour}/hour` : undefined} />
          <Big icon={Target} label="PERFECT" value={card.perfect_rate != null ? `${card.perfect_rate}%` : "–"} hint={`${card.perfect} of ${card.casts} casts`} />
          <Big icon={Flame} label="Best streak" value={String(card.best_streak)} hint="PERFECTs in a row" />
          <Big icon={Zap} label="Best minute" value={String(card.best_minute)} hint={card.best_minute_at ? `at ${new Date(card.best_minute_at).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}` : "catches in 60 s"} />
        </div>

        {card.vs_average != null && (
          <p className={clsx("mt-4 flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border px-4 py-2.5 text-sm", card.vs_average >= 0 ? "border-accent/30 bg-accent/10 text-accent-2" : "border-line bg-panel-2 text-muted")}>
            {card.vs_average >= 0 ? <TrendingUp className="size-4" /> : <TrendingDown className="size-4" />}
            {card.vs_average >= 0 ? `${card.vs_average}% more catches per hour than your average` : `${-card.vs_average}% fewer catches per hour than your average`}
          </p>
        )}

        {/* records */}
        {card.records.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-5 space-y-2">
            {card.records.map((r) => (
              <p key={r} className="wrapped-record flex items-center gap-2.5 rounded-xl border border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-400/10 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-semibold text-amber-200">
                <Crown className="size-4 shrink-0" /> New record — {r}
              </p>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* top catches */}
        {card.top.length > 0 && (
          <div className="mt-6">
            <p className="label mb-2">Top catches</p>
            <ol className="space-y-1.5">
              {card.top.map(([name, times, amount], i) => (
                <li key={name} className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg bg-panel-2/70 px-3 py-2 text-sm">
                  <span className={clsx("grid size-6 place-items-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold", i === 0 ? "bg-accent text-black" : "bg-panel-3 text-muted")}>{i + 1}</span>
                  <span className="font-medium">{name}</span>
                  <span className="ml-auto font-mono text-xs text-muted">
                    {times}× {amount !== times ? `· ${amount.toLocaleString()} total` : ""}
                  </span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* achievements */}
        <div className="mt-6">
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <p className="label">Achievements</p>
            <span className="text-xs text-muted">
              {card.achievements_done} / {card.achievements_total}
            </span>
            <button className="ml-auto text-xs text-accent-2 hover:underline" onClick={() => setShowAll((v) => !v)}>
              {showAll ? "Hide all" : "Show all"}
            </button>
          </div>
          {card.new_achievements.length > 0 ? (
            <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {card.new_achievements.map((a) => (
                <AchievementTile key={a.id} a={a} fresh />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (
            !showAll && <p className="text-sm text-muted">No new achievements this session.</p>
          )}
          {showAll && (
            <div className="mt-2 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {achievements.map((a) => (
                <AchievementTile key={a.id} a={a} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Big({ icon: Icon, label, value, hint }: { icon: typeof Fish; label: string; value: string; hint?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-line bg-panel-2/70 p-4">
      <Icon className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
      <p className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tabular-nums">{value}</p>
      <p className="text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">{label}</p>
      {hint && <p className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-muted">{hint}</p>}
    </div>
  );
}

function AchievementTile({ a, fresh }: { a: FishingAchievement; fresh?: boolean }) {
  const done = a.earned_at != null;
  const [have, goal] = a.progress;
  return (
    <div className={clsx("flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border px-3 py-2.5", fresh ? "wrapped-new border-accent/50 bg-accent/10" : done ? "border-line bg-panel-2/70" : "border-line/60 bg-panel/40 opacity-70")}>
      <span className={clsx("grid size-9 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full", done ? "bg-accent/20 text-accent-2" : "bg-panel-3 text-muted")}>
        {fresh ? <Sparkles className="size-4" /> : done ? <Award className="size-4" /> : <Lock className="size-4" />}
      </span>
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold">{a.title}</p>
        <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">{a.detail}</p>
        {!done && (
          <div className="mt-1 h-1 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-panel-3">
            <div className="h-full rounded-full bg-accent/70" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (have / Math.max(1, goal)) * 100)}%` }} />
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      {!done && <span className="font-mono text-[10px] text-muted">{have}/{goal}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

import type { LicenseState } from "./types";

const NAMES: Record<string, string> = { lifetime: "Lifetime", guest: "Guest", owner: "Owner", free: "Free" };

/** "Premium", "Normal", "Free" … from the signed license (Free when not active). */
export function editionLabel(license: LicenseState | null): string {
  if (!license || license.status === "signed_out" || license.status === "expired") return "Free";
  const base = license.plan.split(" ")[0]!.toLowerCase();
  const name = NAMES[base] ?? license.plan;
  return license.plan.includes("(dev)") ? `${name} (dev)` : name;
}

export function isPaid(license: LicenseState | null): boolean {
  return editionLabel(license) !== "Free";
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/effects.ts

import { isTauri } from "./tauri";

/**
 * The endless background animations stop while another window (the game) is active, so they don't
 * take GPU time from it. The picture stays where it is; see :root[data-idle] in styles.css.
 */
export async function installIdlePause() {
  // The PERFECT counter never has focus but must keep its pop animations.
  if (!isTauri || window.location.hash === "#overlay") return;
  const { getCurrentWindow } = await import("@tauri-apps/api/window");
  await getCurrentWindow().onFocusChanged(({ payload: focused }) => {
    document.documentElement.toggleAttribute("data-idle", !focused);
  });
}

/**
 * Pointer light: the panel under the mouse gets CSS variables --mx / --my (pointer position in
 * px, relative to the panel) which styles.css uses for a soft spotlight and a brighter edge near
 * the cursor. One listener for the whole app, throttled to animation frames; no work when the
 * mouse doesn't move. Turned off with reduced motion / effects at 0 via CSS.
 */
export function installPointerLight() {
  const SELECTOR = ".panel, .raised, .card";
  let lit: HTMLElement | null = null;
  let frame = 0;
  let last: PointerEvent | null = null;

  const root = document.documentElement;
  const apply = () => {
    frame = 0;
    const e = last;
    if (!e) return;
    // Background parallax (-1…1 from the window centre).
    root.style.setProperty("--bgx", ((e.clientX / window.innerWidth - 0.5) * 2).toFixed(3));
    root.style.setProperty("--bgy", ((e.clientY / window.innerHeight - 0.5) * 2).toFixed(3));
    const el = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest?.(SELECTOR) as HTMLElement | null;
    if (lit && lit !== el) {
      lit.style.removeProperty("--mx");
      lit.style.removeProperty("--my");
    }
    lit = el;
    if (!el) return;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    el.style.setProperty("--mx", `${e.clientX - r.left}px`);
    el.style.setProperty("--my", `${e.clientY - r.top}px`);
  };

  window.addEventListener(
    "pointermove",
    (e) => {
      last = e;
      if (!frame) frame = requestAnimationFrame(apply);
    },
    { passive: true },
  );
  document.addEventListener("pointerleave", () => {
    if (lit) {
      lit.style.removeProperty("--mx");
      lit.style.removeProperty("--my");
      lit = null;
    }
  });
}

/** Virtual-key codes ↔ display names (used by the step inspector and the action table). */

const NAMED: Array<[number, string]> = [
  [0x08, "Backspace"],
  [0x09, "Tab"],
  [0x0d, "Enter"],
  [0x10, "Shift"],
  [0x11, "Ctrl"],
  [0x12, "Alt"],
  [0x13, "Pause"],
  [0x14, "Caps Lock"],
  [0x1b, "Esc"],
  [0x20, "Space"],
  [0x21, "Page Up"],
  [0x22, "Page Down"],
  [0x23, "End"],
  [0x24, "Home"],
  [0x25, "←"],
  [0x26, "↑"],
  [0x27, "→"],
  [0x28, "↓"],
  [0x2c, "Print Screen"],
  [0x2d, "Insert"],
  [0x2e, "Delete"],
  [0x5b, "Win"],
  [0xa0, "Left Shift"],
  [0xa1, "Right Shift"],
  [0xa2, "Left Ctrl"],
  [0xa3, "Right Ctrl"],
  [0xa4, "Left Alt"],
  [0xa5, "Right Alt"],
];

const NAME_BY_VK = new Map(NAMED);

export function keyName(vk: number): string {
  const named = NAME_BY_VK.get(vk);
  if (named) return named;
  if ((vk >= 0x30 && vk <= 0x39) || (vk >= 0x41 && vk <= 0x5a)) return String.fromCharCode(vk);
  if (vk >= 0x60 && vk <= 0x69) return `Num ${vk - 0x60}`;
  if (vk >= 0x70 && vk <= 0x87) return `F${vk - 0x6f}`;
  return `Key ${vk}`;
}

/** Keys offered in the inspector's key picker. */
export const KEY_OPTIONS: Array<{ vk: number; name: string }> = [
  ...Array.from({ length: 26 }, (_, i) => ({ vk: 0x41 + i, name: String.fromCharCode(0x41 + i) })),
  ...Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => ({ vk: 0x30 + i, name: String(i) })),
  ...Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => ({ vk: 0x70 + i, name: `F${i + 1}` })),
  ...NAMED.filter(([vk]) => vk < 0xa0).map(([vk, name]) => ({ vk, name })),
];

import type { ComponentType } from "react";

/**
 * The app's sections, each its own full page, switched from the dock at the bottom
 * (components/shell/Dock.tsx): record macros, the chosen macro's settings, its history, your
 * buttons (hotkeys), app settings, support / report, and the owner tools for the owner.

  label: string;

  { id: "record", label: "Record Macro", icon: RecordIcon, subtitle: "Record, edit and play your macros" },
  { id: "macro", label: "Macro settings", icon: SlidersIcon, subtitle: "Game window, trackers, Discord, hotkey" },

  profiles: "record",
  store: "record",
  analytics: "history",
};

}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/rewards.ts

/** The game's fishing milestones ("750 Lunar Rod" per line), as in routine/mod.rs parse_rewards. */
export function parseRewards(text: string): { at: number; name: string }[] {
  return text
    .split("\n")
    .map((l) => l.trim())
    .map((l) => {
      const m = /^([\d.,\s]+?)\s+(.*)$/.exec(l) ?? /^([\d.,]+)$/.exec(l);
      if (!m) return null;
      const at = Number(m[1]!.replace(/[.,\s]/g, ""));
      return Number.isFinite(at) && at > 0 ? { at, name: (m[2] ?? "").trim() || "reward" } : null;
    })
    .filter((r): r is { at: number; name: string } => r !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => a.at - b.at);
}

/** The game's fish count now: what was entered, plus the catches the app counted since. */
export function fishNow(entered: number, oursAtEntry: number, oursNow: number): number | null {
  return entered > 0 ? entered + Math.max(0, oursNow - oursAtEntry) : null;
}

export function nextReward(fish: number | null, rewards: string) {
  if (fish === null) return null;
  const r = parseRewards(rewards).find((x) => x.at > fish);
  return r ? { ...r, left: r.at - fish } : null;
}
