FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/sound.ts
====================================================================================================
/**
 * Short synthesized sounds for fishing (no audio files): a bright two-note chime on every PERFECT
 * and a rising chord when a streak hits a milestone. Played by the main window.
 */

let ctx: AudioContext | null = null;

function audio(): AudioContext | null {
  try {
    ctx ??= new AudioContext();
    if (ctx.state === "suspended") void ctx.resume();
    return ctx;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** One soft bell-like note: a sine with a quick attack and a long fall, plus a faint octave. */
function bell(a: AudioContext, out: AudioNode, freq: number, at: number, length: number, level: number) {
  for (const [mult, share] of [
    [1, 1],
    [2, 0.25],
    [3.01, 0.08],
  ] as const) {
    const osc = a.createOscillator();
    const gain = a.createGain();
    osc.type = "sine";
    osc.frequency.value = freq * mult;
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, at);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(level * share, at + 0.008);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, at + length);
    osc.connect(gain).connect(out);
    osc.start(at);
    osc.stop(at + length + 0.05);
  }
}

function master(a: AudioContext, volume: number): AudioNode {
  const g = a.createGain();
  g.gain.value = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, volume / 100)) * 0.5;
  g.connect(a.destination);
  // Unhook it once the notes have faded: left connected, every PERFECT added one more node the
  // audio engine kept processing, so a long session slowly made everything heavier.
  setTimeout(() => g.disconnect(), 3000);
  return g;
}

/** Every PERFECT: two quick notes going up. */
export function playPerfect(volume: number) {
  const a = audio();
  if (!a || volume <= 0) return;
  const out = master(a, volume);
  const t = a.currentTime + 0.01;
  bell(a, out, 1046.5, t, 0.35, 0.6); // C6
  bell(a, out, 1568, t + 0.07, 0.5, 0.5); // G6
}

/** A streak milestone: a rising major chord, bigger for bigger streaks. */
export function playStreak(volume: number, streak: number) {
  const a = audio();
  if (!a || volume <= 0) return;
  const out = master(a, volume);
  const t = a.currentTime + 0.01;
  const notes = streak >= 25 ? [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5, 1318.5] : [523.25, 659.25, 783.99, 1046.5];
  notes.forEach((f, i) => bell(a, out, f, t + i * 0.075, 0.9, 0.45));
}

/** The streaks that get the chord. */
export const isMilestone = (streak: number) => [5, 10, 25, 50, 100].includes(streak) || (streak > 100 && streak % 50 === 0);


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/step-meta.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { ArrowDownToLine, ArrowUpFromLine, Eye, Hand, Keyboard, MousePointer2, MousePointerClick, Repeat, ScanEye, ScanSearch, ScrollText, Timer, Type } from "lucide-react";
import type { MacroStep, ScreenAction, ScreenInput } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { keyName } from "./keys";

export type StepKind = "key" | "mouse" | "other";

export interface StepMeta {
  label: string;
  details: string;
  icon: typeof Keyboard;
  kind: StepKind;
  description: string;
}

/** Approximate physical pixels on the primary screen (for display only; macros store 0..1). */
function px(x: number, y: number) {
  const dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
  return `X: ${Math.round(x * window.screen.width * dpr)}  Y: ${Math.round(y * window.screen.height * dpr)}`;
}

const cap = (s: string) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);

export function stepMeta(step: MacroStep): StepMeta {
  switch (step.type) {
    case "key_press":
      return {
        label: "Key Press",
        details: [...(step.mods ?? []).map(cap), keyName(step.vk)].join(" + ") + (step.hold_ms ? ` · hold ${step.hold_ms} ms` : ""),
        icon: Keyboard,
        kind: "key",
        description: "Presses and releases a key.",
      };
    case "key_down":
      return { label: "Key Hold", details: keyName(step.vk), icon: ArrowDownToLine, kind: "key", description: "Holds a key down until a Key Release." };
    case "key_up":
      return { label: "Key Release", details: keyName(step.vk), icon: ArrowUpFromLine, kind: "key", description: "Releases a held key." };
    case "text":
      return {
        label: "Type Text",
        details: step.value.length > 28 ? `“${step.value.slice(0, 28)}…”` : `“${step.value}”`,
        icon: Type,
        kind: "key",
        description: "Types text exactly, independent of keyboard layout.",
      };
    case "mouse_move":
      return { label: "Mouse Move", details: px(step.x, step.y), icon: MousePointer2, kind: "mouse", description: "Moves the pointer." };
    case "mouse_move_by":
      return {
        label: "Camera Turn",
        details: `${step.dx > 0 ? "→" : step.dx < 0 ? "←" : ""} ${Math.abs(step.dx)}${step.dy ? ` · ${step.dy > 0 ? "↓" : "↑"} ${Math.abs(step.dy)}` : ""}`,
        icon: MousePointer2,
        kind: "mouse",
        description: "Moves the mouse by a distance (how games turn the camera while the right button is held).",
      };
    case "mouse_click":
      if (step.target)
        return {
          label: "Smart Click",
          details: `${cap(step.button)} · ${step.target.label ? `“${step.target.label}”` : "picture"} · finds it anywhere`,
          icon: ScanSearch,
          kind: "mouse",
          description: "Looks for what was clicked while recording, anywhere on the screen, and clicks it there. When it isn't recognised it clicks where it was when recording.",
        };
      return {
        label: "Mouse Click",
        details: `${cap(step.button)} · ${px(step.x, step.y)}${step.hold_ms ? ` · ${step.hold_ms} ms` : ""}`,
        icon: MousePointerClick,
        kind: "mouse",
        description: "Moves to a point and clicks.",
      };
    case "mouse_down":
      return { label: "Mouse Down", details: `${cap(step.button)} button`, icon: MousePointerClick, kind: "mouse", description: "Holds a mouse button (drag start)." };
    case "mouse_up":
      return { label: "Mouse Up", details: `${cap(step.button)} button`, icon: MousePointerClick, kind: "mouse", description: "Releases a mouse button (drag end)." };
    case "scroll":
      return {
        label: "Scroll",
        details: `${step.delta > 0 ? "Up" : "Down"} ${Math.abs(step.delta / 120).toFixed(step.delta % 120 ? 1 : 0)}`,
        icon: ScrollText,
        kind: "mouse",
        description: "Turns the mouse wheel (120 = one notch).",
      };
    case "delay":
      return {
        label: "Delay",
        details: step.var ? `{{${step.var}}} (fallback ${formatMs(step.ms)})` : formatMs(step.ms),
        icon: Timer,
        kind: "other",
        description: "Waits before the next step.",
      };
    case "loop":
      return {
        label: "Loop",
        details: `${step.count}× · ${step.steps.length} step${step.steps.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`,
        icon: Repeat,
        kind: "other",
        description: "Repeats the steps inside it.",
      };
    case "screen":
      return screenMeta(step.action);
  }
}

export function inputName(i: ScreenInput): string {
  return i.type === "key" ? keyName(i.vk) : `${cap(i.button)} mouse`;
}

const sec = (ms: number) => `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(2).replace(/\.?0+$/, "")} s`;

function screenMeta(a: ScreenAction): StepMeta {
  switch (a.kind) {
    case "click_target":
      return { label: "See & Click", details: `“${a.target.name}”${(a.clicks ?? 1) > 1 ? ` · ${a.clicks}×` : ""} · ${cap(a.button)}`, icon: ScanEye, kind: "mouse", description: "Finds the taught button in the game window and clicks it. Never clicks a guess: when unsure it looks again, then asks you." };
    case "hold":
      return {
        label: "Hold",
        details: a.until.type === "time" ? `${inputName(a.input)} · ${sec(a.until.ms)}` : `${inputName(a.input)} while “${a.until.target.name}” shows`,
        icon: Hand,
        kind: "key",
        description: a.until.type === "time" ? "Holds a key or mouse button for a set time." : "Waits for the bar or prompt, holds the input while it shows and lets go the moment it's gone.",
      };
    case "hold_and_click":
      return {
        label: "Hold + Click",
        details: `${inputName(a.hold)} → ${sec(a.delay_ms)} → ${a.click.type === "input" ? inputName(a.click.input) : a.click.type === "point" ? `${cap(a.click.button)} at ${Math.round(a.click.x * 100)}%, ${Math.round(a.click.y * 100)}%` : `“${a.click.target.name}”`}`,
        icon: Hand,
        kind: "key",
        description: "Holds one input, waits the delay, clicks another while still holding, then releases both.",
      };
    case "click_point":
      return { label: "Window Click", details: `${cap(a.button)} · ${Math.round(a.x * 100)}%, ${Math.round(a.y * 100)}% of the game window`, icon: MousePointerClick, kind: "mouse", description: "Clicks a spot of the game window (it moves with the window)." };
    case "wait_for":
      return { label: a.gone ? "Wait Until Gone" : "Wait Until Seen", details: `“${a.target.name}”`, icon: Eye, kind: "other", description: a.gone ? "Waits until the target is no longer in the game window." : "Waits until the target shows in the game window." };
  }
}

export function formatMs(ms: number): string {
  if (ms < 1000) return `${ms} ms`;
  if (ms < 60_000) return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(2)}s`;
  return `${Math.floor(ms / 60_000)}m ${Math.round((ms % 60_000) / 1000)}s`;
}

export function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
  const h = Math.floor(ms / 3_600_000);
  const m = Math.floor((ms % 3_600_000) / 60_000);
  if (h > 0) return `${h}h ${m}m`;
  if (m > 0) return `${m}m ${Math.round((ms % 60_000) / 1000)}s`;
  return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/step-tree.ts
====================================================================================================
import type { MacroStep } from "@macroapp/shared";

/** Address of a step in the (possibly nested) step tree: indices from the root, e.g. [3, 0]. */
export type StepPath = number[];

export interface StepRow {
  path: StepPath;
  depth: number;
  step: MacroStep;
  /** Start time (ms) within one run; loop bodies show their first iteration. */
  startMs: number;
  /** Duration (ms) this row contributes (delays, whole loops). */
  durationMs: number;
}

export function stepDuration(step: MacroStep): number {
  if (step.type === "delay") return step.ms;
  if (step.type === "key_press" || step.type === "mouse_click") return step.hold_ms ?? 0;
  if (step.type === "loop") return step.count * totalDuration(step.steps);
  if (step.type === "screen") {
    const a = step.action;
    if (a.kind === "hold" && a.until.type === "time") return a.until.ms;
    if (a.kind === "hold_and_click") return a.delay_ms + a.click_ms;
  }
  return 0;
}

export function totalDuration(steps: MacroStep[]): number {
  return steps.reduce((ms, s) => ms + stepDuration(s), 0);
}

/** Flattens the tree into table rows (loop children directly under their loop). */
export function flatten(steps: MacroStep[], base: StepPath = [], depth = 0, start = 0): StepRow[] {
  const rows: StepRow[] = [];
  let t = start;
  steps.forEach((step, i) => {
    const path = [...base, i];
    const durationMs = stepDuration(step);
    rows.push({ path, depth, step, startMs: t, durationMs });
    if (step.type === "loop") rows.push(...flatten(step.steps, path, depth + 1, t));
    t += durationMs;
  });
  return rows;
}

export const samePath = (a: StepPath | null, b: StepPath | null) =>
  !!a && !!b && a.length === b.length && a.every((v, i) => v === b[i]);

export function getAt(steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath): MacroStep | undefined {
  let list = steps;
  let node: MacroStep | undefined;
  for (const i of path) {
    node = list[i];
    if (!node) return undefined;
    list = node.type === "loop" ? node.steps : [];
  }
  return node;
}

/** Immutable edit of the list that contains `path`. */
function editParent(steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath, fn: (list: MacroStep[], index: number) => MacroStep[]): MacroStep[] {
  const [head, ...rest] = path;
  if (head === undefined) return steps;
  if (rest.length === 0) return fn(steps, head);
  return steps.map((s, i) => (i === head && s.type === "loop" ? { ...s, steps: editParent(s.steps, rest, fn) } : s));
}

export const updateAt = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath, step: MacroStep) =>
  editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => list.map((s, j) => (j === i ? step : s)));

export const removeAt = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath) => editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => list.filter((_, j) => j !== i));

export const insertAfter = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath | null, step: MacroStep) =>
  path ? editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => [...list.slice(0, i + 1), step, ...list.slice(i + 1)]) : [...steps, step];

export const moveAt = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath, dir: -1 | 1) =>
  editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => {
    const j = i + dir;
    if (j < 0 || j >= list.length) return list;
    const next = [...list];
    [next[i], next[j]] = [next[j]!, next[i]!];
    return next;
  });

export const wrapInLoop = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath) =>
  editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => list.map((s, j) => (j === i ? { type: "loop" as const, count: 2, steps: [s] } : s)));

export const unwrapLoop = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath) =>
  editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => {
    const s = list[i];
    return s?.type === "loop" ? [...list.slice(0, i), ...s.steps, ...list.slice(i + 1)] : list;
  });

/** Inserts a deep copy of the step right after it. */
export const duplicateAt = (steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath) =>
  editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => [...list.slice(0, i + 1), structuredClone(list[i]!), ...list.slice(i + 1)]);

/** Path of the previous sibling, or null for the first step in its list. */
export function prevSibling(path: StepPath): StepPath | null {
  const last = path[path.length - 1]!;
  return last > 0 ? [...path.slice(0, -1), last - 1] : null;
}

/**
 * "Wait before this action" = the Delay step directly before it (same list).
 * Setting it updates that delay, inserts one if there is none, or removes it when set to 0.
 * Returns the new steps and the (possibly shifted) path of the edited action.
 */
export function setWaitBefore(steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath, ms: number): { steps: MacroStep[]; path: StepPath } {
  const prev = prevSibling(path);
  const prevStep = prev ? getAt(steps, prev) : undefined;
  if (prev && prevStep?.type === "delay") {
    if (ms <= 0) return { steps: removeAt(steps, prev), path: prev };
    return { steps: updateAt(steps, prev, { type: "delay", ms }), path };
  }
  if (ms <= 0) return { steps, path };
  // Insert a new delay before the action: insert after the previous sibling, or at the start of the list.
  const inserted = prev
    ? insertAfter(steps, prev, { type: "delay", ms })
    : editParent(steps, path, (list, i) => [...list.slice(0, i), { type: "delay", ms }, ...list.slice(i)]);
  return { steps: inserted, path: movedPath(path, 1) };
}

export function waitBefore(steps: MacroStep[], path: StepPath): number {
  const prev = prevSibling(path);
  const s = prev ? getAt(steps, prev) : undefined;
  return s?.type === "delay" ? s.ms : 0;
}

/** Path of the sibling after moving (so the selection follows the step). */
export function movedPath(path: StepPath, dir: -1 | 1): StepPath {
  const next = [...path];
  next[next.length - 1] = Math.max(0, next[next.length - 1]! + dir);
  return next;
}

/** Backend events emitted by Rust (`app.emit(...)`). */
export type AppEvent =
  | "engine://status"
  | "engine://action"
  | "engine://progress"
  | "engine://error"
  | "macros://changed"
  | "stats://changed"
  | "profiles://changed"
  | "variables://changed"
  | "library://changed"
  | "autoclicker://status"
  | "rebirth://status"
  | "routine://status"
  | "routine://live"
  | "scan://status"
  | "replay://status"
  | "replay://saved"
  | "rules://status"
  | "rules://fired"
  | "license://changed"
  | "auth://signed-in"
  | "auth://error"
  | "updater://available"
  | "updater://progress"
  | "hotkeys://problems"
  | "owner://changed"
  | "screen://ask"
  | "screen://answered"
  | "macro://live";

export function onEvent<T>(event: AppEvent, handler: (payload: T) => void): Promise<UnlistenFn> {
  if (!isTauri) return Promise.resolve(() => {});
  return listen<T>(event, (e) => handler(e.payload));
}

export function errorMessage(err: unknown): string {
  if (typeof err === "object" && err && "message" in err) return String((err as CommandError).message);
  return String(err);
}

async function call<T>(cmd: string, args?: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {
  if (isTauri) return invoke<T>(cmd, args);
  return mock(cmd, args) as Promise<T>;
}

    fishing: { hotkey: "F8", pumpkin_hotkey: "", scan_hotkey: "", dimension: "", pumpkin_taps_per_second: 10, fishing_name: "Fishing", pumpkin_name: "Pumpkin Collector", game_exe: "RobloxPlayerBeta.exe", tap_clicks_per_second: 100, walk_key: "W", jump_key: "Space", follow_route: false, auto_rejoin: false, place_id: "107778070777162", release_early_ms: 60, stop_after_minutes: 0, stop_after_perfects: 0, daily_goal: 0, discord_webhook: "", discord_history: "", discord_streaks: "", discord_stats: "", remote_enabled: false, remote_owner_id: "", notify_every: 100, notify_streak: 10, notify_stop: true, sound: true, sound_volume: 60, overlay_style: "gold", overlay_scale: 100, overlay_hotkey: "F10", overlay_auto: true, account: "", fish_count: 0, fish_count_ours: 0, rewards: ["750 Lunar Rod", "2250 Void Rod", "5000 Kraken Rod + Abyssjaw"].join(String.fromCharCode(10)) },
    routine: { game_exe: "RobloxPlayerBeta.exe", scan_with_macro: true },
  replay: { start_with_app: false, hotkey: "Alt+F10", output_dir: "", max_minutes: 5, fps: 30, bitrate_kbps: 8000 },
  auto_switch_profiles: false,
  live_webhook: "",
};

const mockLibrary: LibraryMeta = {};
const mockTrainer: TrainerResult[] = [];

let mockVariables: Variable[] = [];
let mockRules: Rule[] = [];

const mockProfiles: Profiles = {
  active: "p-default",
  profiles: [{ id: "p-default", name: "Default", macro_ids: mockMacros.map((m) => m.id), apps: [] }],
};

const summary = (m: Macro): MacroSummary => ({
  id: m.id,
  name: m.name,
  hotkey: m.hotkey,
  step_count: m.steps.length,
  duration_ms: totalDuration(m.steps),
  updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),
});

async function mock(cmd: string, args: Record<string, unknown> = {}): Promise<unknown> {
  const find = () => mockMacros.find((m) => m.id === args.id);
  switch (cmd) {
    case "get_app_info":
      return { version: __APP_VERSION__, api_base: "http://localhost:3000" };
    case "list_macros":
      return mockMacros.map(summary);
    case "get_macro":
      return structuredClone(find());
    case "save_macro": {
      const data = args.data as Macro;
      const i = mockMacros.findIndex((m) => m.id === data.id);
      if (i >= 0) mockMacros[i] = data;
      else mockMacros.push(data);
      return;
    }
    case "duplicate_macro": {
      const copy = { ...structuredClone(find()!), id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: `${find()!.name} (copy)`, hotkey: null };
      mockMacros.push(copy);
      return copy;
    }
    case "rename_macro":
      if (find()) find()!.name = String(args.name);
      return;
    case "delete_macro":
      mockMacros.splice(mockMacros.findIndex((m) => m.id === args.id), 1);
      return;
    case "set_macro_hotkey":
      if (find()) find()!.hotkey = (args.hotkey as string | null) ?? null;
      return [];
    case "get_engine_status":
      return { state: "idle" };
    case "get_settings":
      return mockSettings;
    case "save_settings":
      mockSettings = args.settings as AppSettings;
      return [];
    case "get_license":
    case "refresh_license":
      return { status: "signed_out", plan: "free", email: null, entitlements: FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS.free, expires_at: null };
    case "check_for_update":
      return null;
    case "list_broken_macros":
    case "get_hotkey_conflicts":
      return [];
    case "set_current_macro":
    case "emergency_stop":
    case "rebirth_start":
    case "rebirth_stop":
    case "routine_start":
    case "routine_stop":
    case "clear_fishing_history":
    case "discord_test":
    case "discord_send_preview":
    case "routine_learn_rod":
    case "routine_set_fish_count":
    case "routine_learned_rods":
    case "routine_forget_rods":
    case "overlay_show":
    case "overlay_hide":
    case "request_dev":
    case "autoclicker_start":
    case "autoclicker_stop":
    case "save_scan_labels":
    case "delete_scan":
    case "open_scan_folder":
      return;
    case "get_scan_status":
      return { running: false, session: null, frames: 0, seconds: 0, note: null };
    case "list_scans":
    case "get_scan_labels":
      return [];
    case "get_fishing_history":
    case "discord_preview_embeds":
    case "get_fishing_achievements":
      return [];
    case "get_fishing_wrapped":
      return null;
    case "access_log_out":
      return { has_access: false, plan: null, has_account: true, email: null, accounts: [], restarting: false, needs_key: null };
    case "access_status":
    case "access_forget_account":
      return { has_access: new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("access") !== "none", plan: "owner", has_account: false, email: null, accounts: new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("accounts") ? [{ email: "rayan@example.com", plan: "owner", last_login: "2026-11-17T18:00:00Z", hello: true, wait_s: 0, weak_password: false }, { email: "friend@example.com", plan: "guest", last_login: null, hello: false, wait_s: new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("wait") ? 42 : 0, weak_password: true }, { email: "new@example.com", plan: "none", last_login: null, hello: false, wait_s: 0, weak_password: false }] : [], restarting: new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("opening"), needs_key: new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("needkey") ? "new@example.com" : null };
    case "access_password_check": {
      // rough browser-preview stand-in for the real check in secure.rs
      const pw = String((args as { password?: string })?.password ?? "");
      const words = pw.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;
      const score = pw.length < 8 ? 0 : pw.length < 15 ? 1 : words >= 3 ? 4 : 3;
      const problem = pw.length < 15 ? "Use at least 15 characters — three or four random words work well." : null;
      return { ok: !problem, score, problem, tips: problem ? ["Add another word or two. Uncommon words are better."] : [], crack_time: score >= 4 ? "centuries" : score >= 3 ? "3 years" : "2 hours" };
    }
    case "access_hello_available":
      return true;
    case "screen_teach": {
      const a = args as { region: Region; kind: SeenTarget["kind"]; name: string };
      const w = Math.min(60, a.region.width), h = Math.min(30, a.region.height);
      const px = Array.from({ length: w * h }, (_, i) => ((i % w) * 4 + Math.floor(i / w) * 3) % 256);
      return { name: a.name, kind: a.kind, width: w, height: h, gray_b64: btoa(String.fromCharCode(...px)), taught_width: 1920, taught_height: 1080, taught_x: 800, taught_y: 500, search_everywhere: true } satisfies SeenTarget;
    }
    case "screen_test":
      return { certainty: "sure", score: 0.93, ambiguous: false, text: "saw it (93% sure) — browser preview" };
    case "screen_answer":
    case "live_send_preview":
      return null;
    case "macro_history":
      return { sessions: [] };
    case "screen_area":
      return { x: 0.4, y: 0.7, w: 0.2, h: 0.05 };
    case "screen_read":
      return "browser preview: no game to read";
    case "live_preview": {
      const m = (args as { macro_: MacroDocument }).macro_;
      const counts = (m.trackers ?? []).filter((t) => t.kind.type === "count");
      const texts = (m.trackers ?? []).filter((t) => t.kind.type === "read_text");
      return {
        title: `${m.live?.title || m.name} · Live`,
        color: 0x2dd496,
        fields: [
          { name: "Session", value: [`Running since <t:${Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)}:R> · 0 min`, ...counts.map((t) => `**${t.name}** ×0 · 0 per hour`)].join("\n") },
          ...texts.map((t) => ({ name: t.name, value: "Nothing read yet." })),
        ],
        footer: { text: `PREVIEW · ${m.live?.footer || `SLNC Macro · ${m.name}`}` },
      };
    }
    case "owner_notice":
      return new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("notice") ? { id: "1", text: "New version out — update from Help → Check for updates." } : null;
    case "owner_get":
    case "owner_publish":
      return { list: { iss: "slnc", aud: "slnc-owner-list", iat: 1795000000, keys_from: 1795000000, blocked: ["friend@example.com"], notice: null }, can_sign: true, can_publish: true, my_email: "preview@example.com" };
    case "owner_make_key":
      return "SLNC-preview.key.not-real";
    case "access_set_hello":
    case "access_change_password":
      return { has_access: true, plan: "owner", has_account: true, email: "preview@example.com", accounts: [], restarting: false, needs_key: null };
    case "access_create_account":
      return { has_access: false, plan: null, has_account: true, email: null, accounts: [], restarting: false, needs_key: "preview@example.com" };
    case "access_add_key":
    case "access_log_in":
    case "access_log_in_hello":
      return { has_access: true, plan: "guest", has_account: true, email: "preview@example.com", accounts: [], restarting: false, needs_key: null };
    case "discord_remote_status":
      return { has_token: false, text: "Off" };
    case "export_fishing_history":
      return null;
    case "get_routine_status":
      return { running: false, kind: null, phase: "", catches: 0, note: null, casts: 0, gold: 0, missed: 0, streak: 0, best_streak: 0, started_at: 0, today_before: 0, all_before: 0 };
    case "get_rebirth_status":
      return { running: false, rebirths: 0, phase: "", note: null };
    case "get_autoclicker_status":
      return { running: false, clicks: 0 };
    case "get_trainer_results":
      return structuredClone(mockTrainer);
    case "save_trainer_result":
      mockTrainer.push({ ...(args.result as TrainerResult), at: new Date().toISOString() });
      return structuredClone(mockTrainer);
    case "recent_apps":
      return ["notepad.exe", "chrome.exe"];
    case "set_profile_apps":
      mockProfiles.profiles.find((p) => p.id === args.id)!.apps = (args.apps as string[]).map((a) => a.toLowerCase());
      return structuredClone(mockProfiles);
    case "get_replay_status":
      return { running: false, buffered_secs: 0, max_secs: 300, disk_bytes: 0, width: 0, height: 0, fps: 30, error: null };
    case "get_rules":
      return mockRules;
    case "save_rules":
      mockRules = args.rules as Rule[];
      return mockRules;
    case "get_monitoring":
      return { running: false, test_mode: false, rule_count: 0 };
    case "test_rule":
      return { matched: false, score: 0.12, text: null };
    case "get_variables":
      return mockVariables;
    case "save_variables":
      mockVariables = args.variables as Variable[];
      return mockVariables;
    case "get_profiles":
      return mockProfiles;
    case "create_profile":
      mockProfiles.profiles.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: String(args.name), macro_ids: [], apps: [] });
      return mockProfiles;
    case "rename_profile":
      mockProfiles.profiles.find((p) => p.id === args.id)!.name = String(args.name);
      return mockProfiles;
    case "delete_profile":
      mockProfiles.profiles = mockProfiles.profiles.filter((p) => p.id !== args.id);
      if (mockProfiles.active === args.id) mockProfiles.active = mockProfiles.profiles[0]!.id;
      return mockProfiles;
    case "set_active_profile":
      mockProfiles.active = String(args.id);
      return [];
    case "set_profile_member": {
      const p = mockProfiles.profiles.find((x) => x.id === args.profileId)!;
      p.macro_ids = args.member ? [...p.macro_ids, String(args.macroId)] : p.macro_ids.filter((m) => m !== args.macroId);
      return [];
    }
    case "get_stats":
      return { runs: 0, actions_executed: 0, played_ms: 0, recordings: 0, recorded_ms: 0, daily: {}, since: null };
    case "get_library":
      return structuredClone(mockLibrary);
    case "set_macro_meta": {
      const prev = mockLibrary[String(args.id)] ?? { favorite: false, tags: [], category: null, last_played_at: null, play_count: 0 };
      mockLibrary[String(args.id)] = { ...prev, favorite: Boolean(args.favorite), tags: args.tags as string[], category: (args.category as string | null) ?? null };
      return mockLibrary[String(args.id)];
    }
    case "duplicate_profile": {
      const src = mockProfiles.profiles.find((p) => p.id === args.id)!;
      mockProfiles.profiles.push({ id: crypto.randomUUID(), name: `${src.name} (copy)`, macro_ids: [...src.macro_ids], apps: [] });
      return mockProfiles;
    }
    case "get_data_info":
      return { data_dir: "%APPDATA%\\com.macroapp.desktop", macros_dir: "%APPDATA%\\com.macroapp.desktop\\macros", logs_dir: null, macro_count: mockMacros.length, total_bytes: 4096 };
    case "get_support_links":
      return { discord: false, discord_bugs: false, email: false };
    default:
      throw { code: "browser_mock", message: `“${cmd}” needs the desktop app (run: pnpm tauri dev)` } satisfies CommandError;
  }
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/time.ts
====================================================================================================
/** "3 min ago", "yesterday", or a date. */
export function relative(iso: string): string {
  const diff = Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime();
  const min = Math.round(diff / 60000);
  if (min < 1) return "just now";
  if (min < 60) return `${min} min ago`;
  const h = Math.round(min / 60);
  if (h < 24) return `${h} h ago`;
  const d = Math.round(h / 24);
  if (d === 1) return "yesterday";
  if (d < 7) return `${d} days ago`;
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString();
}

import type { Entitlements, MacroDocument } from "@macroapp/shared";

/** Types returned by Rust commands (src-tauri/src/model.rs). */

export type Macro = MacroDocument;

  id: string;
  name: string;
  hotkey: string | null;
  step_count: number;
  duration_ms: number;
  updated_at: string | null;
}

// ---------- Screen rules (vision/rules.rs, vision/monitor.rs) ----------

/** Physical virtual-desktop pixels. */
export interface Region {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  width: number;
  height: number;
}

export interface RuleTemplate {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  gray_b64: string;
}

export type RuleCondition =
  | { type: "color"; r: number; g: number; b: number; tolerance: number; min_ratio: number }
  | { type: "image"; template: RuleTemplate; threshold: number }
  | { type: "text"; contains: string; case_sensitive: boolean }
  | { type: "change"; sensitivity: number };

export type RuleAction =
  | { type: "run_macro"; macro_id: string }
  | { type: "click"; button: "left" | "right" | "middle"; x: number; y: number }
  | { type: "key_press"; vk: number; mods: Array<"ctrl" | "alt" | "shift" | "win"> }
  | { type: "type_text"; value: string }
  | { type: "stop_playback" }
  | { type: "notify" };

export interface Rule {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  enabled: boolean;
  region: Region;
  condition: RuleCondition;
  trigger: "appears" | "disappears";
  action: RuleAction;
  delay_ms: number;
  cooldown_ms: number;
  interval_ms: number;
  allow_while_playing: boolean;
}

  rule_id: string;

/** routine/history.rs — one thing a fishing session did (`at` = ms since 1970). */
export type HistoryEvent =
  | { kind: "start"; at: number; account?: string }
  | { kind: "cast"; at: number; perfect: boolean; streak: number }
  | { kind: "streak_broken"; at: number; length: number }
  | { kind: "catch"; at: number; seen: string | null }
  | { kind: "rod_fetched"; at: number }
  | { kind: "recovered"; at: number; problem: string }
  | { kind: "stop"; at: number; reason: string | null };

/** One fishing session summed up (newest first in the list). */
export interface FishingSession {
  start: number;
  account: string | null;
  end: number | null;
  stop_reason: string | null;
  casts: number;
  perfect: number;
  missed: number;
  catches: number;
  best_streak: number;
  streaks_broken: number;
  rod_fetches: number;
  recoveries: number;
  /** What was caught (cleaned up), most first: [name, times caught, amount in total]. */
  seen: [string, number, number][];
  /** Newest first. */
  events: HistoryEvent[];
}

  | { kind: "bar"; fill: number; band: number; bottom: number; gold: boolean };

import type { AccentName, UiPrefs } from "./types";

export const ACCENTS: Array<{ id: AccentName; label: string; rgb: string }> = [
  { id: "emerald", label: "Emerald", rgb: "45 212 150" },
  { id: "purple", label: "Violet", rgb: "139 92 246" },
  { id: "indigo", label: "Indigo", rgb: "99 102 241" },
  { id: "magenta", label: "Magenta", rgb: "217 70 239" },
  { id: "cyan", label: "Cyan", rgb: "34 211 238" },
  { id: "amber", label: "Amber", rgb: "245 158 11" },
];

/** Applies the appearance preferences to <html> (see styles.css). */
export function applyUiPrefs(p: UiPrefs) {
  const root = document.documentElement;
  root.dataset.accent = p.accent;
  root.dataset.density = p.density;
  root.dataset.motion = p.motion;
  root.style.setProperty("--fx", String(Math.max(0, Math.min(100, p.effects)) / 100));
}

/** True when animations should be skipped (explicit setting, or Windows animation effects off). */
export function reducedMotion(p: UiPrefs | undefined): boolean {
  if (p?.motion === "reduced") return true;
  if (p?.motion === "full") return false;
  return window.matchMedia("(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)").matches;
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-app-state.ts
====================================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, errorMessage, onEvent } from "./tauri";
import type { AppSettings, AutoClickerStatus, RebirthStatus, RoutineStatus, ScanStatus, ReplayStatus, SavedReplay, BrokenMacro, EngineStatus, HotkeyConflict, LibraryMeta, MonitorStatus, Profiles, RuleFired, Variable, LicenseState, MacroSummary, PlayProgress, UpdateInfo, UsageStats } from "./types";

/**
 * Central UI state: engine status, license, settings, macro list, usage stats, update banner, errors.
 * Kept as a single hook on purpose — add Zustand only when this grows.
 */
export function useAppState() {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<EngineStatus>({ state: "idle" });
  const [license, setLicense] = useState<LicenseState | null>(null);
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState<AppSettings | null>(null);
  const [macros, setMacros] = useState<MacroSummary[]>([]);
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<UsageStats>({ runs: 0, actions_executed: 0, played_ms: 0, recordings: 0, recorded_ms: 0, daily: {}, since: null });
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState<PlayProgress | null>(null);
  const [update, setUpdate] = useState<UpdateInfo | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const reportError = useCallback((err: unknown) => setError(errorMessage(err)), []);

  const [broken, setBroken] = useState<BrokenMacro[]>([]);
  const [profiles, setProfiles] = useState<Profiles | null>(null);
  const [conflicts, setConflicts] = useState<HotkeyConflict[]>([]);
  const [variables, setVariables] = useState<Variable[]>([]);
  const [library, setLibrary] = useState<LibraryMeta>({});
  const [autoclicker, setAutoclicker] = useState<AutoClickerStatus>({ running: false, clicks: 0 });
  const [rebirth, setRebirth] = useState<RebirthStatus>({ running: false, rebirths: 0, phase: "", note: null });
  const [routine, setRoutine] = useState<RoutineStatus>({ running: false, kind: null, phase: "", catches: 0, note: null, casts: 0, gold: 0, missed: 0, streak: 0, best_streak: 0, started_at: 0, today_before: 0, all_before: 0 });
  const [scan, setScan] = useState<ScanStatus>({ running: false, session: null, frames: 0, seconds: 0, note: null });
  const [replay, setReplay] = useState<ReplayStatus | null>(null);
  const [savedReplay, setSavedReplay] = useState<SavedReplay | null>(null);
  // Screen monitoring: live status from the monitor thread, and the last rule that fired.
  const [monitor, setMonitor] = useState<MonitorStatus | null>(null);
  const [fired, setFired] = useState<(RuleFired & { at: number }) | null>(null);

  // The "rule fired" notice disappears by itself.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!fired) return;
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setFired(null), 5000);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [fired]);

  // Profiles + hotkey conflicts (conflicts depend on macros, profiles and settings).
  const reloadProfiles = useCallback(() => {
    api.getProfiles().then(setProfiles).catch(reportError);
    api.getHotkeyConflicts().then(setConflicts).catch(reportError);
  }, [reportError]);

  const reloadLibrary = useCallback(() => {
    api.getLibrary().then(setLibrary).catch(reportError);
  }, [reportError]);

  const reloadMacros = useCallback(() => {
    api.listMacros().then(setMacros).catch(reportError);
    api.listBrokenMacros().then(setBroken).catch(reportError);
    reloadProfiles();
  }, [reportError, reloadProfiles]);

  useEffect(() => {
    reloadMacros();
    api.engineStatus().then(setStatus).catch(reportError);
    api.getLicense().then(setLicense).catch(reportError);
    api.getSettings().then(setSettings).catch(reportError);
    api.getStats().then(setStats).catch(reportError);
    api.getVariables().then(setVariables).catch(reportError);
    reloadLibrary();
    api.getAutoclickerStatus().then(setAutoclicker).catch(reportError);
    api.getScanStatus().then(setScan).catch(reportError);
    api.getReplayStatus().then(setReplay).catch(reportError);
    api
      .getMonitoring()
      .then((m) => m.running && setMonitor((cur) => cur ?? { running: true, test_mode: m.test_mode, rules: [], stopped_reason: null }))
      .catch(reportError);

    const subs = [
      onEvent<EngineStatus>("engine://status", (s) => {
        setStatus(s);
        if (s.state !== "playing") setProgress(null);
      }),
      onEvent<PlayProgress>("engine://progress", setProgress),
      onEvent<string>("engine://error", setError),
      onEvent("macros://changed", reloadMacros),
      onEvent<UsageStats>("stats://changed", setStats),
      onEvent("profiles://changed", reloadProfiles),
      onEvent<Variable[]>("variables://changed", setVariables),
      onEvent("library://changed", reloadLibrary),
      onEvent<AutoClickerStatus>("autoclicker://status", setAutoclicker),
      onEvent<RebirthStatus>("rebirth://status", setRebirth),
      onEvent<RoutineStatus>("routine://status", setRoutine),
      onEvent<ScanStatus>("scan://status", setScan),
      onEvent<ReplayStatus>("replay://status", (s) => {
        setReplay(s);
        if (!s.running && s.error) setError(`Replay buffer stopped: ${s.error}`);
      }),
      onEvent<SavedReplay>("replay://saved", setSavedReplay),
      onEvent<MonitorStatus>("rules://status", (s) => {
        setMonitor(s);
        if (!s.running && s.stopped_reason) setError(`Screen monitoring stopped: ${s.stopped_reason}`);
      }),
      onEvent<RuleFired>("rules://fired", (f) => {
        if (f.error) setError(`Rule “${f.name}” failed: ${f.error}`);
        else setFired({ ...f, at: Date.now() });
      }),
      onEvent<LicenseState>("license://changed", setLicense),
      onEvent<LicenseState>("auth://signed-in", setLicense),
      onEvent<{ message: string }>("auth://error", (e) => setError(e.message)),
      onEvent<UpdateInfo>("updater://available", setUpdate),
      onEvent<string[]>("hotkeys://problems", (p) => setError(`Some hotkeys could not be registered: ${p.join("; ")}`)),
    ];
    return () => {
      subs.forEach((p) => p.then((unlisten) => unlisten()));
    };
  }, [reloadMacros, reloadProfiles, reloadLibrary, reportError]);

  /** Saves settings (the engine normalises hotkeys), updates state, reports problems. */
  const saveSettings = useCallback(
    async (next: AppSettings) => {
      setSettings(next);
      try {
        const problems = await api.saveSettings(next);
        if (problems.length) setError(`Saved, but: ${problems.join("; ")}`);
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        reportError(e);
        api.getSettings().then(setSettings).catch(() => {});
        return false;
      }
    },
    [reportError],
  );

  return {
    status,
    saveSettings,
    license,
    setLicense,
    settings,
    setSettings,
    macros,
    broken,
    reloadMacros,
    profiles,
    conflicts,
    reloadProfiles,
    variables,
    setVariables,
    library,
    setLibrary,
    autoclicker,
    rebirth,
    routine,
    scan,
    setAutoclicker,
    replay,
    setReplay,
    savedReplay,
    setSavedReplay,
    reloadLibrary,
    monitor,
    setMonitor,
    fired,
    setFired,
    stats,
    progress,
    update,
    setUpdate,
    error,
    setError,
    reportError,
  };
}

export type AppStateHook = ReturnType<typeof useAppState>;


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-library-actions.ts
====================================================================================================
import { useMemo } from "react";
import { canCreateMacro } from "@macroapp/shared";
import type { LibraryActions } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import { api } from "./tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "./use-app-state";
import type { Macro } from "./types";

/** Library operations shared by Macro Studio, the Macro Library page and the Dashboard. */
export function useLibraryActions(app: AppStateHook, select: (id: string | null) => void, openStudio: () => void) {
  const { macros, license, library, settings, reportError, reloadMacros, reloadLibrary } = app;
  const dialogs = useDialogs();
  const canCreate = license ? canCreateMacro(license.entitlements, macros.length) : true;
  const confirmDelete = settings?.ui.confirm_delete ?? true;

  const actions: LibraryActions = useMemo(() => {
    const run = (fn: () => Promise<unknown>) => fn().catch(reportError);
    const nameOf = (id: string) => macros.find((m) => m.id === id)?.name ?? "this macro";
    return {
      onSelect: (id) => {
        select(id);
        openStudio();
      },
      onNew: () =>
        run(async () => {
          const m: Macro = {
            schema_version: 1,
            id: crypto.randomUUID(),
            name: "Untitled macro",
            hotkey: null,
            settings: { repeat: 1, speed: 1, delay_between_runs_ms: 0 },
            steps: [],
          };
          await api.saveMacro(m);
          reloadMacros();
          select(m.id);
          openStudio();
        }),
      onImport: () =>
        run(async () => {
          const m = await api.importMacro();
          reloadMacros();
          if (m) select(m.id);
        }),
      onDuplicate: (id) =>
        run(async () => {
          const m = await api.duplicateMacro(id);
          reloadMacros();
          select(m.id);
        }),
      onSaveAs: (id) =>
        run(async () => {
          const name = await dialogs.prompt({ title: "Save as new macro", label: "Name of the copy", initial: `${nameOf(id)} (copy)`, confirmText: "Save copy" });
          if (!name) return;
          const m = await api.duplicateMacro(id);
          await api.renameMacro(m.id, name);
          reloadMacros();
          select(m.id);
        }),
      onRename: (id) =>
        run(async () => {
          const name = await dialogs.prompt({ title: "Rename macro", label: "Name", initial: nameOf(id), confirmText: "Rename" });
          if (!name || name === nameOf(id)) return;
          await api.renameMacro(id, name);
          reloadMacros();
        }),
      onToggleFavorite: (id) =>
        run(async () => {
          const meta = library[id];
          await api.setMacroMeta(id, !meta?.favorite, meta?.tags ?? [], meta?.category ?? null);
          reloadLibrary();
        }),
      onExport: (id) => run(() => api.exportMacro(id)),
      onRevealFile: (path) => run(() => api.revealMacroFile(path)),
      onOpenFolder: () => run(() => api.openMacrosFolder()),
      onRefresh: () => reloadMacros(),
      onDelete: (id) =>
        run(async () => {
          if (
            confirmDelete &&
            !(await dialogs.confirm({
              title: `Delete “${nameOf(id)}”?`,
              message: "The macro file is removed from this PC. Export it first if you want to keep a copy.",
              confirmText: "Delete macro",
              danger: true,
            }))
          )
            return;
          await api.deleteMacro(id);
          reloadMacros();
          select(null);
        }),
    };
  }, [macros, library, confirmDelete, dialogs, reportError, reloadMacros, reloadLibrary, select, openStudio]);

  return { actions, canCreate };
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-live.ts
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { api, onEvent } from "./tauri";
import type { LiveEvent, RoutineStatus } from "./types";

export interface FeedLine {
  id: number;
  at: Date;
  text: string;
  tone: "info" | "gold" | "miss";
}

export type BarSample = Extract<LiveEvent, { kind: "bar" }>;

const EMPTY: RoutineStatus = { running: false, kind: null, phase: "", catches: 0, note: null, casts: 0, gold: 0, missed: 0, streak: 0, best_streak: 0, started_at: 0, today_before: 0, all_before: 0 };

/**
 * What the fishing routine is doing right now: its status (counters), the latest charge-bar
 * position and a short feed of what happened. Shared by the SLNC Macros page and the overlay.
 */
export function useLive(feedSize = 40) {
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<RoutineStatus>(EMPTY);
  const [bar, setBar] = useState<BarSample | null>(null);
  const [feed, setFeed] = useState<FeedLine[]>([]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let next = 0;
    // The bar is only measured while a cast is charging: once no new position comes in for a
    // moment, the cast is over and the view goes back to rest (not stuck on the last "gold").
    let stale = 0;
    let lastBar = 0;
    api.getRoutineStatus().then(setStatus).catch(() => {});
    const offs = [
      onEvent<RoutineStatus>("routine://status", setStatus),
      onEvent<LiveEvent>("routine://live", (e) => {
        if (e.kind === "bar") {
          // Behind the game nobody watches the bar move: a few updates a second are plenty
          // (30 a second made the window redraw all the time while fishing).
          const idle = document.documentElement.hasAttribute("data-idle") || document.hidden;
          const at = performance.now();
          if (idle && !e.gold && at - lastBar < 250) return;
          lastBar = at;
          setBar(e);
          window.clearTimeout(stale);
          stale = window.setTimeout(() => setBar(null), 1500);
          return;
        }
        setFeed((f) => {
          // The same phase twice in a row is one line.
          if (f[0] && f[0].text === e.text && e.tone === "info") return f;
          return [{ id: next++, at: new Date(), text: e.text, tone: e.tone }, ...f].slice(0, feedSize);
        });
      }),
    ];
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(stale);
      offs.forEach((p) => p.then((off) => off()));
    };
  }, [feedSize]);

  const goldRate = status.casts > 0 ? Math.round((status.gold / status.casts) * 100) : null;
  return { status, bar, feed, goldRate };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-macro-draft.ts
====================================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { macroDocumentSchema } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { api, errorMessage } from "./tauri";
import type { Macro } from "./types";

export type SaveState = "saved" | "saving" | "dirty" | "invalid";

const AUTOSAVE_MS = 600;

/**
 * Loads one macro and keeps an editable draft that autosaves (debounced) after every change.
 * `flush()` saves immediately — call it before playing so the engine runs the latest version.
 */
export function useMacroDraft(id: string | null, onError: (e: unknown) => void, onSaved: () => void) {
  const [draft, setDraftState] = useState<Macro | null>(null);
  const [saveState, setSaveState] = useState<SaveState>("saved");
  const [problem, setProblem] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const pending = useRef<Macro | null>(null);

  const save = useCallback(
    async (m: Macro) => {
      const parsed = macroDocumentSchema.safeParse(m);
      if (!parsed.success) {
        const issue = parsed.error.issues[0];
        setProblem(`${issue?.path.join(".") || "macro"}: ${issue?.message}`);
        setSaveState("invalid");
        return false;
      }
      setProblem(null);
      setSaveState("saving");
      try {
        await api.saveMacro(m);
        if (pending.current === m) pending.current = null;
        setSaveState(pending.current ? "dirty" : "saved");
        onSaved();
        return true;
      } catch (e) {
        setProblem(errorMessage(e));
        setSaveState("invalid");
        onError(e);
        return false;
      }
    },
    [onError, onSaved],
  );

  const flush = useCallback(async () => {
    if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = null;
    if (!pending.current) return saveState !== "invalid";
    return save(pending.current);
  }, [save, saveState]);

  // (Re)load when the selection changes; save anything pending for the previous macro first.
  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    void (async () => {
      if (pending.current) await save(pending.current);
      if (!id) {
        setDraftState(null);
        return;
      }
      try {
        const m = await api.getMacro(id);
        if (!cancelled) {
          setDraftState(m);
          setSaveState("saved");
          setProblem(null);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (!cancelled) onError(e);
      }
    })();
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [id]);

  const setDraft = useCallback(
    (update: (m: Macro) => Macro) => {
      setDraftState((current) => {
        if (!current) return current;
        const next = update(current);
        pending.current = next;
        setSaveState("dirty");
        if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
        timer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
          timer.current = null;
          if (pending.current) void save(pending.current);
        }, AUTOSAVE_MS);
        return next;
      });
    },
    [save],
  );

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
    },
    [],
  );

  return { draft, setDraft, saveState, problem, flush };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-recording.ts
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { api } from "./tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "./use-app-state";

/**
 * Start (with the countdown from Settings) / stop recording, shared by the Dashboard and Macro
 * Studio. `elapsedMs` is the recording time shown in the UI, without paused time.
 */
export function useRecording(app: AppStateHook, onRecorded: (id: string) => void) {
  const { status, settings, reportError, reloadMacros } = app;
  const [countdown, setCountdown] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => () => void (timer.current && window.clearInterval(timer.current)), []);

  const start = () => {
    const secs = settings?.countdown_secs ?? 3;
    if (secs <= 0) return void api.startRecording().catch(reportError);
    setCountdown(secs);
    timer.current = window.setInterval(() => {
      setCountdown((n) => {
        if (n === null || n <= 1) {
          if (timer.current) window.clearInterval(timer.current);
          timer.current = null;
          api.startRecording().catch(reportError);
          return null;
        }
        return n - 1;
      });
    }, 1000);
  };

  const cancel = () => {
    if (timer.current) window.clearInterval(timer.current);
    timer.current = null;
    setCountdown(null);
  };

  const stop = () =>
    api
      .stopRecording()
      .then((m) => {
        reloadMacros();
        onRecorded(m.id);
      })
      .catch(reportError);

  const recording = status.state === "recording";
  const paused = recording && status.paused;
  const elapsedMs = useElapsed(recording, paused);

  return { start, stop, cancel, countdown, recording, paused, events: status.state === "recording" ? status.events : 0, elapsedMs };
}

/** Milliseconds `active` has been true, not counting time while `paused`. */
export function useElapsed(active: boolean, paused: boolean): number {
  const [ms, setMs] = useState(0);
  const acc = useRef(0);
  const since = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) {
      acc.current = 0;
      since.current = null;
      setMs(0);
      return;
    }
    if (paused) {
      if (since.current !== null) acc.current += performance.now() - since.current;
      since.current = null;
      setMs(acc.current);
      return;
    }
    since.current = performance.now();
    const t = window.setInterval(() => setMs(acc.current + performance.now() - (since.current ?? performance.now())), 250);
    return () => window.clearInterval(t);
  }, [active, paused]);

  return Math.round(ms);
}

export function clock(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.floor(ms / 1000);
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60);
  const sec = String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0");
  return h ? `${h}:${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${sec}` : `${String(m).padStart(2, "0")}:${sec}`;
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/use-teach.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import type { Area, MacroDocument, SeenTarget } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { RegionPicker } from "../components/RegionPicker";
import type { Teach } from "../components/ScreenStep";
import type { PickArea } from "../components/Trackers";
import { useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import { api } from "./tauri";

/**
 * Teaching things in the macro's game window: a target (name it, draw a box around it) or an area
 * to read from. Returns the two functions and the box picker to render while one is open.
 */
export function useTeach(draft: MacroDocument | null, setError: (e: string) => void, reportError: (e: unknown) => void) {
  const { prompt } = useDialogs();
  const [teaching, setTeaching] = useState<{ kind: SeenTarget["kind"]; hint: string; name: string; resolve: (t: SeenTarget | null) => void } | null>(null);
  const [picking, setPicking] = useState<{ hint: string; resolve: (a: Area | null) => void } | null>(null);

  const teach: Teach = async (kind, hint) => {
    if (!draft?.screen) {
      setError("Pick the game window first (Macro settings → Game window).");
      return null;
    }
    const again = hint.includes(" again");
    const name = again ? "x" : await prompt({ title: "Name what you teach", label: "Name (e.g. Start, Hold bar, PERFECT)", initial: kind === "indicator" ? "Hold bar" : "Button", confirmText: "Next: draw the box", maxLength: 60 });
    if (!name?.trim()) return null;
    return new Promise((resolve) => setTeaching({ kind, hint, name: name.trim(), resolve }));
  };

  const pickArea: PickArea = async (hint) => {
    if (!draft?.screen) {
      setError("Pick the game window first (Macro settings → Game window).");
      return null;
    }
    return new Promise((resolve) => setPicking({ hint, resolve }));
  };

  const overlay = (
    <>
      {teaching && (
        <RegionPicker
          mode="region"
          title={teaching.hint}
          onClose={() => {
            teaching.resolve(null);
            setTeaching(null);
          }}
          onPick={(r) => {
            const t = teaching;
            setTeaching(null);
            if (r.kind !== "region" || !draft?.screen) return t.resolve(null);
            api
              .screenTeach(draft.screen.exe, r.region, t.kind, t.name)
              .then(t.resolve)
              .catch((e) => {
                reportError(e);
                t.resolve(null);
              });
          }}
        />
      )}
      {picking && (
        <RegionPicker
          mode="region"
          title={picking.hint}
          onClose={() => {
            picking.resolve(null);
            setPicking(null);
          }}
          onPick={(r) => {
            const p = picking;
            setPicking(null);
            if (r.kind !== "region" || !draft?.screen) return p.resolve(null);
            api
              .screenArea(draft.screen.exe, r.region)
              .then(p.resolve)
              .catch((e) => {
                reportError(e);
                p.resolve(null);
              });
          }}
        />
      )}
    </>
  );

  return { teach, pickArea, overlay };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/lib/variables.ts
====================================================================================================
import type { MacroStep } from "@macroapp/shared";
import type { Variable } from "./types";

/** Built-in variables (computed during playback by variables.rs). */
export const BUILT_IN_VARIABLES: Array<{ name: string; description: string }> = [
  { name: "run", description: "Current repeat number: 1, 2, 3…" },
  { name: "date", description: "Today, YYYY-MM-DD" },
  { name: "time", description: "Current time, HH:MM:SS" },
];

export const VARIABLE_NAME = /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,31}$/;

/** Names used as {{name}} in a text (trimmed, as written). */
export function variableRefs(text: string): string[] {
  return [...text.matchAll(/\{\{\s*([^{}]*?)\s*\}\}/g)].map((m) => m[1]!).filter(Boolean);
}

const known = (name: string, vars: Variable[]) =>
  BUILT_IN_VARIABLES.some((b) => b.name === name.toLowerCase()) || vars.some((v) => v.name.toLowerCase() === name.toLowerCase());

/** {{refs}} that don't match a variable — they would be typed literally. */
export function unknownRefs(text: string, vars: Variable[]): string[] {
  return [...new Set(variableRefs(text).filter((n) => !known(n, vars)))];
}

/** How many steps of a macro use this variable (text refs + delay vars, loops included). */
export function countUses(steps: MacroStep[], name: string): number {
  const n = name.toLowerCase();
  return steps.reduce((sum, s) => {
    if (s.type === "text") return sum + variableRefs(s.value).filter((r) => r.toLowerCase() === n).length;
    if (s.type === "delay") return sum + (s.var?.toLowerCase() === n ? 1 : 0);
    if (s.type === "loop") return sum + countUses(s.steps, name);
    return sum;
  }, 0);
}

/** Variables whose value is a whole number (usable as a delay in ms). */
export const numericVariables = (vars: Variable[]) => vars.filter((v) => /^\s*\d+\s*$/.test(v.value));

import { useState } from "react";
import { Activity, BarChart3, Clock3, Disc3, Hourglass, Layers, Lock, Repeat, Table2 } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { macroIcon } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { Page, PageHeader, Section, Segmented, StatTile } from "../components/ui";
import { formatDuration } from "../lib/step-meta";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { relative } from "../lib/time";

type Metric = "runs" | "played_ms" | "recordings";

/**
 * Local statistics from stats.json and library.json. Nothing here is sent anywhere; deleting local
 * data in Settings resets it.
 */
export function Analytics({ app, onOpenMacro }: { app: AppStateHook; onOpenMacro: (id: string) => void }) {
  const { stats, macros, library } = app;
  const [metric, setMetric] = useState<Metric>("runs");
  const [asTable, setAsTable] = useState(false);

  const days = lastDays(30).map((key) => ({ key, ...(stats.daily[key] ?? { runs: 0, played_ms: 0, recordings: 0 }) }));
  const value = (d: (typeof days)[number]) => d[metric];
  const max = Math.max(1, ...days.map(value));
  const fmt = (v: number) => (metric === "played_ms" ? formatDuration(v) : v.toLocaleString());
  const total30 = days.reduce((s, d) => s + value(d), 0);

  const played = macros.filter((m) => library[m.id]?.last_played_at);
  const recent = [...played].sort((a, b) => library[b.id]!.last_played_at!.localeCompare(library[a.id]!.last_played_at!)).slice(0, 6);
  const top = [...played].sort((a, b) => (library[b.id]?.play_count ?? 0) - (library[a.id]?.play_count ?? 0)).slice(0, 6);
  const topMax = Math.max(1, ...top.map((m) => library[m.id]?.play_count ?? 0));
  const totalDuration = macros.reduce((s, m) => s + m.duration_ms, 0);

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader icon={BarChart3} title="Analytics" subtitle={stats.since ? `Counted on this PC since ${new Date(stats.since).toLocaleDateString()}` : "Counted on this PC"} />

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-6">
        <StatTile icon={Layers} label="Saved macros" value={String(macros.length)} hint={`${formatDuration(totalDuration)} of actions in total`} />
        <StatTile icon={Disc3} label="Recording sessions" value={stats.recordings.toLocaleString()} hint="Recordings saved as a macro" />
        <StatTile icon={Hourglass} label="Total recorded" value={formatDuration(stats.recorded_ms)} hint="Length of those recordings" />
        <StatTile icon={Repeat} label="Completed runs" value={stats.runs.toLocaleString()} hint="One repeat = one run" />
        <StatTile icon={Activity} label="Actions executed" value={stats.actions_executed.toLocaleString()} hint="Keys, clicks, moves, text…" />
        <StatTile icon={Clock3} label="Time automated" value={formatDuration(stats.played_ms)} hint="Total playback time" />
      </div>

      <Section
        title="Last 30 days"
        description={`${fmt(total30)} ${metric === "runs" ? "runs" : metric === "recordings" ? "recording sessions" : "of playback"} in the last 30 days. Days are local dates on this PC.`}
        actions={
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Segmented<Metric>
              value={metric}
              onChange={setMetric}
              options={[
                { value: "runs", label: "Runs" },
                { value: "played_ms", label: "Playback time" },
                { value: "recordings", label: "Recordings" },
              ]}
            />
            <button className={clsx("btn-ghost px-2 py-1", asTable && "text-accent-2")} onClick={() => setAsTable((v) => !v)} title={asTable ? "Show chart" : "Show as table"} aria-pressed={asTable}>
              <Table2 className="size-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        }
      >
        {asTable ? (
          <div className="max-h-72 overflow-y-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-1 font-medium">Date</th>
                  <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Runs</th>
                  <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Playback time</th>
                  <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Recordings</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody className="divide-y divide-line">
                {[...days].reverse().map((d) => (
                  <tr key={d.key}>
                    <td className="py-1.5">{longDate(d.key)}</td>
                    <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{d.runs}</td>
                    <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{formatDuration(d.played_ms)}</td>
                    <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{d.recordings}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <BarChart days={days.map((d) => ({ key: d.key, value: value(d) }))} max={max} format={fmt} />
        )}
      </Section>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Section title="Recently used" description="Macros by the last time playback was started.">
          {recent.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nothing played yet.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
              {recent.map((m) => {
                const Icon = macroIcon(m.id);
                return (
                  <li key={m.id}>
                    <button className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:text-accent-2" onClick={() => onOpenMacro(m.id)}>
                      <Icon className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
                      <span className="flex-1 truncate">{m.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted">{relative(library[m.id]!.last_played_at!)}</span>
                    </button>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </Section>

        <Section title="Most played" description="Times playback was started (a run of 10 repeats counts once).">
          {top.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nothing played yet.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="space-y-2.5">
              {top.map((m) => {
                const n = library[m.id]?.play_count ?? 0;
                return (
                  <li key={m.id} className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,10rem)_1fr_3rem] items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                    <button className="truncate text-left hover:text-accent-2" onClick={() => onOpenMacro(m.id)} title={m.name}>
                      {m.name}
                    </button>
                    <span className="well h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full p-0">
                      <span className="block h-full rounded-full bg-accent" style={{ width: `${(n / topMax) * 100}%` }} />
                    </span>
                    <span className="text-right tabular-nums text-muted">{n}</span>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </Section>
      </div>

      <p className="flex items-start gap-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
        <Lock className="mt-0.5 size-3.5 shrink-0 text-accent-2" />
        These statistics are calculated and stored only on this PC (stats.json and library.json in your data folder). SLNC Macro sends no usage data, keystrokes or macro contents
        anywhere. You can reset them with “Delete local data” in Settings → Privacy & data.
      </p>
    </Page>
  );
}

function BarChart({ days, max, format }: { days: Array<{ key: string; value: number }>; max: number; format: (v: number) => string }) {
  const [hover, setHover] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const h = hover !== null ? days[hover] : null;
  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <div className="flex h-48 items-end gap-[2px] border-b border-line" onMouseLeave={() => setHover(null)} role="img" aria-label="Bar chart of the last 30 days; use the table button for exact values">
        {days.map((d, i) => (
          <div key={d.key} className="group flex h-full flex-1 cursor-default items-end" onMouseEnter={() => setHover(i)}>
            <div
              className={clsx("w-full rounded-t-[4px] transition-colors", d.value === 0 ? "bg-white/[0.06]" : hover === i ? "bg-accent-2" : "bg-accent/80")}
              style={{ height: d.value === 0 ? 2 : `${Math.max(3, (d.value / max) * 100)}%` }}
            />
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-1.5 flex justify-between text-[11px] text-muted">
        <span>{shortDate(days[0]!.key)}</span>
        <span>{shortDate(days[Math.floor(days.length / 2)]!.key)}</span>
        <span>Today</span>
      </div>
      {h && hover !== null && (
        <div
          className="panel pointer-events-none absolute -top-2 z-10 -translate-x-1/2 -translate-y-full px-2.5 py-1.5 text-xs whitespace-nowrap"
          style={{ left: `${((hover + 0.5) / days.length) * 100}%` }}
        >
          <div className="text-muted">{longDate(h.key)}</div>
          <div className="font-semibold tabular-nums">{format(h.value)}</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Local "YYYY-MM-DD" keys for the last `n` days, oldest first (same format as stats.json). */
function lastDays(n: number): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  const d = new Date();
  for (let i = n - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const x = new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate() - i);
    out.push(x.toLocaleDateString("sv-SE"));
  }
  return out;
}
const toDate = (key: string) => new Date(`${key}T00:00:00`);
const shortDate = (key: string) => toDate(key).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { day: "numeric", month: "short" });
const longDate = (key: string) => toDate(key).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "short", day: "numeric", month: "short" });
