import { useEffect } from "react";
import { Keyboard, OctagonX, Play, ScanEye, ShieldCheck, Sparkles, Square } from "lucide-react";
import { AutoClickerPanel } from "../components/AutoClickerPanel";
import { HotkeyInput } from "../components/HotkeyInput";
import { PlaybackPanel, Toggle } from "../components/PlaybackPanel";
import { RulesPanel } from "../components/RulesPanel";
import { Badge, Page, PageHeader, Section } from "../components/ui";
import type { View } from "../lib/nav";
import { formatMs } from "../lib/step-meta";
import { totalDuration } from "../lib/step-tree";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { useMacroDraft } from "../lib/use-macro-draft";

/**
 * Automation: how a macro repeats, how it is started and stopped, safety limits and screen rules.
 * Scheduled (time-based) runs are intentionally not offered yet — see docs/known limitations.
 */
export function Automation({
  app,
  selectedId,
  onSelect,
  onOpenSettings,
}: {
  app: AppStateHook;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onSelect: (id: string | null) => void;
  onNavigate: (v: string) => void;
  onOpenSettings: (section: string) => void;
}) {
  const { status, settings, macros, license, monitor, reportError, reloadMacros } = app;
  const { draft, setDraft, saveState, flush } = useMacroDraft(selectedId, reportError, reloadMacros);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId && macros[0]) onSelect(macros[0].id);
  }, [selectedId, macros, onSelect]);

  const playing = status.state === "playing";
  const isPlayingThis = playing && status.macro_id === draft?.id;
  const paused = status.state !== "idle" && status.paused;

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader icon={Sparkles} title="Automation" subtitle="Repeats, start/stop hotkeys, safety limits and screen rules" />

      {/* What is running right now */}
      <div className="panel flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 p-4">
        <span className="section-title">Status</span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <Badge tone={playing ? "accent" : "muted"}>{playing ? (paused ? "Playback paused" : "Playing") : "No playback"}</Badge>
          {playing && <span className="text-muted">“{macros.find((m) => m.id === status.macro_id)?.name ?? "macro"}” · run {status.run}{status.total_runs ? ` of ${status.total_runs}` : " (until stopped)"}</span>}
        </span>
        <span className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <Badge tone={monitor?.running ? (monitor.test_mode ? "warning" : "success") : "muted"}>
            {monitor?.running ? (monitor.test_mode ? "Rules: test mode" : "Rules: active") : "Rules: off"}
          </Badge>
        </span>
        <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
          <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.stopPlayback().catch(reportError)} disabled={!playing}>
            <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop playback
          </button>
          <button
            className="btn-danger"
            onClick={() => {
              api.emergencyStop().catch(reportError);
              app.setMonitor(null);
            }}
          >
            <OctagonX className="size-4" /> Stop everything
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_0.9fr]">
        <Section title="Repeat & loop" description="How the selected macro runs when it is started — from the app, its hotkey or a screen rule.">
          <select className="field" value={selectedId ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onSelect(e.target.value || null)} disabled={status.state !== "idle"} aria-label="Macro">
            {macros.length === 0 && <option value="">No macros yet</option>}
            {macros.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                {m.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
          {draft ? (
            <>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                One run takes {formatMs(totalDuration(draft.steps))} at 1× · {draft.steps.length} actions ·{" "}
                <span className={saveState === "invalid" ? "text-red-300" : ""}>{saveState === "saved" ? "saved" : saveState === "invalid" ? "not saved" : "saving…"}</span>
              </p>
              <PlaybackPanel
                settings={draft.settings}
                entitlements={license?.entitlements}
                isPlaying={isPlayingThis}
                isPaused={isPlayingThis && paused}
                disabled={status.state !== "idle" || draft.steps.length === 0 || saveState === "invalid"}
                onChange={(patch) => setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, settings: { ...m.settings, ...patch } }))}
                onPlay={async () => {
                  if (await flush()) api.playMacro(draft.id).catch(reportError);
                }}
                onPause={() => api.togglePause().catch(reportError)}
                onStop={() => api.stopPlayback().catch(reportError)}
              />
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">Create a macro first.</p>
          )}
        </Section>

        <Section
          title="Start & stop"
          description="Global hotkeys work while SLNC Macro is minimised or in the tray. They are registered with Windows; no special permissions are needed. Apps running as administrator can't receive input from SLNC Macro unless SLNC Macro also runs as administrator."
          actions={
            <button className="btn-ghost px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => onOpenSettings("hotkeys")}>
              <Keyboard className="size-3.5" /> All hotkeys
            </button>
          }
        >
          {draft && (
            <div className="space-y-1.5">
              <span className="label">Hotkey for “{draft.name}”</span>
              <HotkeyInput
                value={draft.hotkey ?? ""}
                placeholder="No hotkey — click to set"
                onChange={(next) =>
                  api
                    .setMacroHotkey(draft.id, next || null)
                    .then((problems) => {
                      setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, hotkey: next || null }));
                      if (problems.length) app.setError(problems.join("; "));
                    })
                    .catch(reportError)
                }
              />
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Only active while the macro is in the active profile.</p>
            </div>
          )}
          <dl className="space-y-2 text-sm">
            <HotkeyRow label="Play selected macro" value={settings?.play_hotkey} />
            <HotkeyRow label="Pause / resume" value={settings?.pause_hotkey} />
            <HotkeyRow label="Start / stop recording" value={settings?.record_hotkey} />
            <HotkeyRow label="Emergency stop" value={settings?.stop_playback_hotkey} danger />
          </dl>
          <button className="btn-primary w-full" disabled={!draft || status.state !== "idle" || !draft.steps.length} onClick={() => draft && flush().then((ok) => void (ok && api.playMacro(draft.id).catch(reportError)))}>
            <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Run “{draft?.name ?? "macro"}” now
          </button>
        </Section>

        <Section title="Safety limits" description="Endless loops always stay stoppable.">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
            <span>Stop when the mouse hits the top-left corner</span>
            <Toggle checked={settings?.corner_failsafe ?? true} onChange={(v) => settings && app.saveSettings({ ...settings, corner_failsafe: v })} />
          </div>
          <ul className="space-y-2 text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
            <li className="flex gap-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> The emergency stop releases every held key and mouse button, also mid-loop.
            </li>
            <li className="flex gap-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> “Loop until stopped” shows a permanent banner while it runs.
            </li>
            <li className="flex gap-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> Screen rules stop themselves if they fire more than 30 times a minute, never fire while you record, and don't fire during playback unless you allow it per rule.
            </li>
            <li className="flex gap-2">
              <ShieldCheck className="size-4 shrink-0 text-accent-2" /> Pausing releases held keys; resuming presses nothing by itself.
            </li>
          </ul>
        </Section>
      </div>

      <Section title="Auto clicker" description="Clicks a mouse button at a fixed rate until you stop it or the click limit is reached.">
        <AutoClickerPanel app={app} />
      </Section>

      <section className="panel overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-line px-5 py-3">
          <ScanEye className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
          <h2 className="section-title">Screen rules</h2>
          <span className="text-xs text-muted">· react to a colour, image, text or change on screen. Checked locally; screenshots are never saved.</span>
        </div>
        <div className="h-[640px]">
          <RulesPanel app={app} />
        </div>
      </section>
    </Page>
  );
}

function HotkeyRow({ label, value, danger }: { label: string; value: string | undefined; danger?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
      <dt className={danger ? "text-red-300" : "text-muted"}>{label}</dt>
      <dd>
        <span className="kbd">{value ?? "—"}</span>
      </dd>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Dashboard.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { Activity, Clapperboard, Clock3, Download, Gauge, Layers, Library, OctagonX, Pause, Play, Plus, Repeat, Sparkles, Square, Star, Upload } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { macroIcon, type LibraryActions } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { RecordCore, type CoreState } from "../components/RecordCore";
import { Badge, Page, PageHeader, StatTile } from "../components/ui";
import { formatDuration, formatMs } from "../lib/step-meta";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { View } from "../lib/nav";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { relative } from "../lib/time";
import { clock, useRecording } from "../lib/use-recording";

interface Props {
  app: AppStateHook;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onSelect: (id: string | null) => void;
  onNavigate: (v: string) => void;
  onOpenMacro: (id: string) => void;
  actions: LibraryActions;
  canCreate: boolean;
}

/** Home screen. Every number comes from local data (macro files, stats.json, library.json). */
export function Dashboard({ app, selectedId, onSelect, onNavigate, onOpenMacro, actions, canCreate }: Props) {
  const { status, macros, stats, library, settings, profiles, reportError } = app;
  const rec = useRecording(app, (id) => onSelect(id));
  const selected = macros.find((m) => m.id === selectedId) ?? null;
  const playing = status.state === "playing";
  const paused = status.state !== "idle" && status.paused;
  const playingMacro = playing ? macros.find((m) => m.id === status.macro_id) : null;

  const coreState: CoreState = rec.countdown !== null ? "countdown" : paused ? "paused" : rec.recording ? "recording" : playing ? "playing" : "idle";
  const coreLabel = { idle: "Record", countdown: `Starting in ${rec.countdown}`, recording: "Stop", playing: "Pause", paused: "Resume" }[coreState];
  const coreSub =
    coreState === "recording" || (rec.recording && paused)
      ? `${clock(rec.elapsedMs)} · ${rec.events} events`
      : playing
        ? `Run ${status.run}${status.total_runs ? ` of ${status.total_runs}` : " · looping"}`
        : (settings?.record_hotkey ?? undefined);

  const primary = () => {
    if (rec.countdown !== null) return rec.cancel();
    if (rec.recording) return paused ? api.togglePause().catch(reportError) : rec.stop();
    if (playing) return api.togglePause().catch(reportError);
    rec.start();
  };

  const recent = [...macros]
    .sort((a, b) => (library[b.id]?.last_played_at ?? b.updated_at ?? "").localeCompare(library[a.id]?.last_played_at ?? a.updated_at ?? ""))
    .slice(0, 6);
  const favorites = macros.filter((m) => library[m.id]?.favorite).slice(0, 6);
  const today = stats.daily[new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")];

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader icon={Gauge} title="Dashboard" subtitle="Your macro environment at a glance" />

      <div className="grid gap-5 xl:grid-cols-[1.35fr_1fr]">
        {/* ---------- Control module ---------- */}
        <section className="panel relative overflow-hidden p-6">
          <div className="pointer-events-none absolute -top-32 left-1/2 size-96 -translate-x-1/2 rounded-full bg-accent/10 blur-3xl" />
          <div className="relative grid items-center gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[auto_1fr]">
            <RecordCore
              state={coreState}
              label={coreLabel}
              sublabel={coreSub}
              progress={playing && status.total_runs ? (status.run - 1) / status.total_runs : null}
              onPrimary={primary}
              disabled={status.state === "idle" && rec.countdown === null && !canCreate}
            />

            <div className="min-w-0 space-y-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Badge tone={rec.recording ? "danger" : playing ? "accent" : paused ? "warning" : "success"}>
                  {paused ? "Paused" : rec.recording ? "Recording" : playing ? "Playing" : rec.countdown !== null ? "Get ready" : "Ready"}
                </Badge>
                {playingMacro && <span className="truncate text-sm text-muted">“{playingMacro.name}”</span>}
              </div>

              <label className="block space-y-1.5">
                <span className="label">Selected macro</span>
                <select className="field" value={selectedId ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onSelect(e.target.value || null)} disabled={status.state !== "idle"}>
                  <option value="">Choose a macro…</option>
                  {macros.map((m) => (
                    <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                      {m.name}
                    </option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </label>
              {selected && (
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
                  <Metric label="Actions" value={String(selected.step_count)} />
                  <Metric label="Duration" value={formatMs(selected.duration_ms)} />
                  <Metric label="Hotkey" value={selected.hotkey ?? "—"} />
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 sm:grid-cols-4">
                {rec.recording ? (
                  <button className="btn-danger py-2.5" onClick={rec.stop}>
                    <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop rec.
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <button className="btn-secondary py-2.5" onClick={rec.countdown !== null ? rec.cancel : rec.start} disabled={playing || !canCreate}>
                    <span className="size-2.5 rounded-full bg-red-500 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgb(239_68_68)]" /> {rec.countdown !== null ? "Cancel" : "Record"}
                  </button>
                )}
                <button className="btn-primary py-2.5" onClick={() => selected && api.playMacro(selected.id).catch(reportError)} disabled={!selected || status.state !== "idle" || selected.step_count === 0}>
                  <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Play
                </button>
                <button className="btn-secondary py-2.5" onClick={() => api.togglePause().catch(reportError)} disabled={status.state === "idle"}>
                  {paused ? <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> : <Pause className="size-4 fill-current" />} {paused ? "Resume" : "Pause"}
                </button>
                <button className="btn-secondary py-2.5" onClick={() => api.stopPlayback().catch(reportError)} disabled={!playing}>
                  <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
                </button>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
                Record <span className="kbd">{settings?.record_hotkey}</span> · Pause <span className="kbd">{settings?.pause_hotkey}</span> · Play selected{" "}
                <span className="kbd">{settings?.play_hotkey}</span> — hotkeys work while SLNC Macro is in the background.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </section>

        {/* ---------- Right column ---------- */}
        <div className="grid gap-5">
          <section className="panel flex items-center gap-4 border-red-500/25 p-5">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                api.emergencyStop().catch(reportError);
                app.setMonitor(null);
              }}
              className="grid size-16 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-2xl border border-red-400/40 bg-gradient-to-b from-red-500 to-red-800 text-white shadow-[inset_0_1px_0_rgb(255_255_255/0.3),0_10px_30px_-8px_rgb(239_68_68/0.8)] transition hover:brightness-110 active:translate-y-px"
              aria-label="Emergency stop"
            >
              <OctagonX className="size-8" />
            </button>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="font-semibold">Emergency stop</p>
              <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
                Stops playback, recording and screen rules and releases every key. Also: <span className="kbd">{settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span>
                {settings?.corner_failsafe && " or move the mouse into the top-left corner"}.
              </p>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="panel p-5">
            <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Quick actions</h2>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <Quick icon={Plus} label="New macro" onClick={actions.onNew} disabled={!canCreate} />
              <Quick icon={Upload} label="Import" onClick={actions.onImport} disabled={!canCreate} />
              <Quick icon={Clapperboard} label="Macro Studio" onClick={() => onNavigate("studio")} />
              <Quick icon={Library} label="Library" onClick={() => onNavigate("library")} />
              <Quick icon={Sparkles} label="Automation" onClick={() => onNavigate("automation")} />
              <Quick icon={Download} label="Export selected" onClick={() => selected && actions.onExport(selected.id)} disabled={!selected} />
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StatTile icon={Layers} label="Saved macros" value={String(macros.length)} hint={app.license?.entitlements.max_macros != null ? `of ${app.license.entitlements.max_macros} on your edition` : "Unlimited on your edition"} />
        <StatTile icon={Repeat} label="Runs today" value={String(today?.runs ?? 0)} hint={`${stats.runs.toLocaleString()} in total`} />
        <StatTile icon={Activity} label="Recording sessions" value={stats.recordings.toLocaleString()} hint={`${formatDuration(stats.recorded_ms)} recorded`} />
        <StatTile icon={Clock3} label="Time automated" value={formatDuration(stats.played_ms)} hint="Total playback time" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        <section className="panel p-5 lg:col-span-2">
          <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Recent macros</h2>
          {recent.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">No macros yet — press Record to create your first one.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
              {recent.map((m) => {
                const Icon = macroIcon(m.id);
                const meta = library[m.id];
                return (
                  <li key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2">
                    <button className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-3 text-left hover:text-accent-2" onClick={() => onOpenMacro(m.id)}>
                      <span className="raised grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-lg text-accent-2">
                        <Icon className="size-4" />
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <span className="block truncate text-sm">{m.name}</span>
                        <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">{meta?.last_played_at ? `Played ${relative(meta.last_played_at)}` : m.updated_at ? `Edited ${relative(m.updated_at)}` : ""}</span>
                      </span>
                    </button>
                    {meta?.favorite && <Star className="size-3.5 fill-amber-300 text-amber-300" />}
                    <span className="w-24 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted">{m.hotkey ?? "—"}</span>
                    <button className="btn-ghost px-2 py-1" title="Play" onClick={() => api.playMacro(m.id).catch(reportError)} disabled={status.state !== "idle" || m.step_count === 0}>
                      <Play className="size-3.5 fill-current" />
                    </button>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="panel space-y-4 p-5">
          <div>
            <h2 className="section-title mb-2">Profiles</h2>
            <ul className="space-y-1">
              {profiles?.profiles.map((p) => {
                const on = p.id === profiles.active;
                return (
                  <li key={p.id}>
                    <button
                      disabled={status.state !== "idle"}
                      onClick={() =>
                        !on &&
                        api
                          .setActiveProfile(p.id)
                          .then((problems) => {
                            app.reloadProfiles();
                            if (problems.length) app.setError(`Some hotkeys could not be registered: ${problems.join("; ")}`);
                          })
                          .catch(reportError)
                      }
                      className={clsx("flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-sm transition", on ? "bg-accent/12 text-accent-2" : "text-muted hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-text")}
                    >
                      <span className={clsx("size-1.5 rounded-full", on ? "bg-accent-2 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]" : "bg-white/15")} />
                      <span className="flex-1 truncate">{p.name}</span>
                      <span className="text-[11px]">{on ? "active" : `${p.macro_ids.length} macros`}</span>
                    </button>
                  </li>
                );
              })}
            </ul>
          </div>
          {favorites.length > 0 && (
            <div>
              <h2 className="section-title mb-2">Favorites</h2>
              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                {favorites.map((m) => (
                  <button key={m.id} className="chip hover:border-line-strong hover:text-text" onClick={() => onOpenMacro(m.id)}>
                    <Star className="size-3 fill-amber-300 text-amber-300" /> {m.name}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </Page>
  );
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="well py-2">
      <div className="text-muted">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-accent-2">{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Quick({ icon: Icon, label, onClick, disabled }: { icon: typeof Plus; label: string; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <button className="btn-secondary justify-start py-2.5" onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled}>
      <Icon className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> {label}
    </button>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Help.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Bug, ChevronDown, ClipboardCopy, ExternalLink, FolderOpen, LifeBuoy, Mail, MessagesSquare, RefreshCw } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { Page, PageHeader, Section } from "../components/ui";
import { editionLabel } from "../lib/edition";
import type { View } from "../lib/nav";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { SupportLinks } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

const GUIDES: Array<{ title: string; steps: string[] }> = [
  {
    title: "Getting started",
    steps: [
      "Open Macro Studio (or press Record on the Dashboard).",
      "After the countdown, do your actions in any window — SLNC Macro minimises itself.",
      "Press the record hotkey again (default Ctrl+Shift+R) to stop. The macro is saved automatically.",
      "Press Play. SLNC Macro minimises and plays in the window you used last.",
      "Give it a hotkey in the Studio inspector to play it from anywhere.",
    ],
  },
  {
    title: "Recording tips",
    steps: [
      "Start and end in the same state (same window, same scroll position) so repeats line up.",
      "Turn on “Record mouse movement” in Settings → Recording for drawing or games; leave it off for forms (smaller, more reliable macros).",
      "Pause recording with Ctrl+Alt+P to skip a part; the paused time is cut out.",
      "Fine-tune afterwards on the timeline: change waits, hold times, or insert steps.",
      "Keys held together (e.g. W and A) stay held together.",
    ],
  },
  {
    title: "Playback troubleshooting",
    steps: [
      "Nothing happens in the target window → click into that window once before pressing the macro hotkey, or raise “Wait before the first action” in Settings → Playback.",
      "Clicks land in the wrong place → the window moved or the display scaling changed. Keep windows in the same place, or re-record.",
      "A game or program ignores the input → it may run as administrator; Windows blocks input from normal apps to it. Run SLNC Macro as administrator only if you trust it.",
      "Too fast for the program → lower the playback speed or add waits on the timeline.",
      "Something goes wrong → emergency stop (Ctrl+Alt+Esc) or move the mouse into the top-left corner.",
    ],
  },
];

const FAQ: Array<{ q: string; a: string }> = [
  { q: "Does SLNC Macro record everything I type?", a: "No. Keyboard and mouse are only captured while a recording runs, with a red banner on screen the whole time. Nothing is sent anywhere." },
  { q: "Where are my macros stored?", a: "In your account's own data folder, encrypted with your account's key (Settings → Privacy & data → Open). Back them up with “Back up everything”." },
  { q: "What does recording in a game recognise?", a: "A click on a button is saved as “see & click”: playing waits for that button, finds it again (also when it moved) and clicks it. Holding a key while a bar or prompt shows becomes “hold while it shows”. A click while holding a key becomes “hold + click” with your timing. When it isn't sure what it sees, it looks again and then asks you — it never clicks a guess." },
  { q: "How do I count things and see them in Discord?", a: "In a macro, pick its game window and add Trackers: Count (e.g. PERFECT or Missed), Read name (what you caught, plus its rarity) and Read number (e.g. kick power). Paste a Discord webhook under Discord live message and switch it on: while the macro plays, one message keeps itself up to date with your names, today and all time. Everything is read on this PC; only that message goes to your channel." },
  { q: "The macro paused by itself", a: "Macros with a game window pause and let go of every key while that window isn't in front, and go on when it's back. Your stop key always stops everything." },
  { q: "Will it work in games?", a: "It sends normal Windows input, like a keyboard and mouse would. Many games accept that; some block it, and some don't allow automation in their rules. SLNC Macro does not bypass anti-cheat or protections — check the game's rules." },
  { q: "My key stopped working", a: "The owner can stop keys for every copy of the app. Log in with your password, then put in the new key you get from the owner — your macros and settings stay in your account." },
  { q: "What do I get?", a: "Everything. There is one plan — SLNC Macro Lifetime, €4 once — and every key (bought, guest or owner) unlocks all of it." },
  { q: "I reinstalled Windows — how do I get back in?", a: "Install SLNC Macro, make your account again with the same email, then put in the key from your email." },
  { q: "Why does Windows warn when I install it?", a: "Until the installer is code-signed, SmartScreen shows a warning for new apps. Choose “More info → Run anyway” only for the installer from the official download channel, and compare its checksum." },
];

const KNOWN_ISSUES = [
  "Mouse side buttons (X1/X2) are not recorded yet.",
  "Text typing steps use Unicode input; a few programs (some games, remote desktops) ignore that — record the keys instead.",
  "Programs running as administrator can't be controlled unless SLNC Macro also runs as administrator (a Windows rule).",
  "Screen rules see SLNC Macro's own window too — minimise it while monitoring if it covers the watched area.",
  "Time-based scheduling is not available yet.",
];

export function Help({ app, version }: { app: AppStateHook; version: string; onNavigate: (v: string) => void }) {
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<SupportLinks | null>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<number | null>(0);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const [update, setUpdate] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getSupportLinks().then(setLinks).catch(() => setLinks(null));
  }, []);

  const diagnostics = [
    `SLNC Macro ${version}`,
    `Edition: ${editionLabel(app.license)} (${app.license?.status ?? "unknown"})`,
    `Platform: ${navigator.userAgent.match(/Windows NT [\d.]+/)?.[0] ?? navigator.platform}`,
    `Display: ${window.screen.width}×${window.screen.height} @ ${Math.round(window.devicePixelRatio * 100)}% scaling`,
    `Macros: ${app.macros.length} · broken files: ${app.broken.length} · profiles: ${app.profiles?.profiles.length ?? "?"}`,
    `Engine: ${app.status.state}`,
    "",
    "What happened:",
    "What I expected:",
    "Steps to reproduce:",
  ].join("\n");

  const copy = () =>
    navigator.clipboard
      .writeText(diagnostics)
      .then(() => {
        setCopied(true);
        window.setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2500);
      })
      .catch(app.reportError);

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader icon={LifeBuoy} title="Help & Support" subtitle="Guides, answers and how to reach us" />

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {GUIDES.map((g) => (
          <Section key={g.title} title={g.title}>
            <ol className="space-y-2 text-sm">
              {g.steps.map((s, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="flex gap-2.5">
                  <span className="grid size-5 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent/15 text-[11px] text-accent-2">{i + 1}</span>
                  <span className="text-muted">{s}</span>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ol>
          </Section>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[1.4fr_1fr]">
        <Section title="Frequently asked questions">
          <div className="divide-y divide-line">
            {FAQ.map((f, i) => (
              <div key={f.q}>
                <button className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 py-3 text-left text-sm font-medium" onClick={() => setOpen(open === i ? null : i)} aria-expanded={open === i}>
                  <span className="flex-1">{f.q}</span>
                  <ChevronDown className={clsx("size-4 text-muted transition", open === i && "rotate-180")} />
                </button>
                {open === i && <p className="page-enter pb-3 text-sm text-muted">{f.a}</p>}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </Section>

        <div className="space-y-5">
          <Section title="Report a bug" description="Copy the diagnostic summary (no macro contents or keystrokes) and paste it into the bug channel together with what happened. Attach the log file if you can.">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={copy}>
                <ClipboardCopy className="size-4" /> {copied ? "Copied" : "Copy diagnostics"}
              </button>
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.openFolder("logs").catch(app.reportError)}>
                <FolderOpen className="size-4" /> Open logs
              </button>
              <button className="btn-primary" disabled={!links?.discord_bugs} onClick={() => api.openSupportLink("discord_bugs").catch(app.reportError)} title={links?.discord_bugs ? undefined : "Not configured in this build"}>
                <Bug className="size-4" /> Open bug channel
              </button>
            </div>
            {links && !links.discord_bugs && <p className="text-xs text-muted">No bug-report channel is configured in this build yet.</p>}
          </Section>

          <Section title="Community & contact">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
              <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!links?.discord} onClick={() => api.openSupportLink("discord").catch(app.reportError)} title={links?.discord ? undefined : "Not configured in this build"}>
                <MessagesSquare className="size-4" /> Open Discord
              </button>
              <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!links?.email} onClick={() => api.openSupportLink("email").catch(app.reportError)} title={links?.email ? undefined : "Not configured in this build"}>
                <Mail className="size-4" /> E-mail support
              </button>
              <button className="btn-ghost" onClick={() => api.openWebsite("docs").catch(app.reportError)}>
                <ExternalLink className="size-4" /> Online docs
              </button>
            </div>
            {links && !links.discord && !links.email && <p className="text-xs text-muted">Support links haven't been configured in this build yet.</p>}
          </Section>

          <Section title="Updates">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button
                className="btn-secondary"
                onClick={() => {
                  setUpdate("Checking…");
                  api
                    .checkForUpdate()
                    .then((u) => {
                      if (u) app.setUpdate(u);
                      setUpdate(u ? `Version ${u.version} is available — see the banner at the top.` : `You have the latest version (${version}).`);
                    })
                    .catch(() => setUpdate("Couldn't reach the update server."));
                }}
              >
                <RefreshCw className="size-4" /> Check for updates
              </button>
              {update && <span className="text-xs text-muted">{update}</span>}
            </div>
          </Section>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Section title="Known issues">
        <ul className="list-disc space-y-1.5 pl-5 text-sm text-muted">
          {KNOWN_ISSUES.map((k) => (
            <li key={k}>{k}</li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </Section>
    </Page>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Hotkeys.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Keyboard, Layers, ShieldAlert } from "lucide-react";
import { HotkeyInput } from "../components/HotkeyInput";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import type { AppSettings, HotkeyConflict } from "../lib/types";

/**
 * All global hotkeys in one place: app hotkeys (always active) and macro hotkeys (active only for
 * macros in the active profile). Conflicts come from the engine (hotkeys.rs), which also refuses to
 * save a conflicting hotkey.
 */
export function Hotkeys({ app, embedded = false }: { app: AppStateHook; embedded?: boolean }) {
  const { settings, setSettings, macros, profiles, conflicts, reportError, reloadMacros } = app;
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  if (!settings) return null;

  const active = profiles?.profiles.find((p) => p.id === profiles.active);
  const inActive = new Set(active?.macro_ids ?? []);
  const appConflict = (label: string) => conflicts.find((c) => c.macro_ids.length < 2 && (c.first === label || c.second === label));
  const macroConflict = (id: string) => conflicts.find((c) => c.macro_ids.includes(id));

  const saveAppHotkey = async (patch: Partial<AppSettings>) => {
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const problems = await api.saveSettings({ ...settings, ...patch });
      setSettings(await api.getSettings()); // saved values are normalised by the engine
      app.reloadProfiles();
      if (problems.length) app.setError(`Some hotkeys could not be registered: ${problems.join("; ")}`);
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const ordered = [...macros].sort((a, b) => Number(inActive.has(b.id)) - Number(inActive.has(a.id)));

  return (
    <div className={embedded ? "space-y-5" : "mx-auto max-w-4xl space-y-6 p-6"}>
      {!embedded && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Keyboard className="size-6 text-accent-2" />
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Hotkeys</h1>
        </div>
      )}
      {saving && <span className="text-xs text-muted">Saving…</span>}

      {conflicts.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 p-3 text-sm text-amber-100">
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 font-medium">
            <AlertTriangle className="size-4 text-amber-300" /> {conflicts.length} hotkey conflict{conflicts.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}
          </p>
          <ul className="mt-1 list-disc pl-6 text-xs text-amber-200/90">
            {conflicts.map((c, i) => (
              <li key={i}>
                <span className="font-mono">{c.hotkey}</span>: “{c.first}” and “{c.second}”{c.profile ? ` in profile “${c.profile}”` : ""} — the second one is not
                registered.
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="panel space-y-4 p-5">
        <h2 className="section-title">App hotkeys · always active</h2>
        <HotkeyRow label="Start / stop recording" conflict={appConflict("Start / stop recording")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.record_hotkey} onChange={(hk) => hk && saveAppHotkey({ record_hotkey: hk })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Play selected macro" conflict={appConflict("Play selected macro")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.play_hotkey} onChange={(hk) => hk && saveAppHotkey({ play_hotkey: hk })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Pause / resume (recording and playback)" conflict={appConflict("Pause / resume")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.pause_hotkey} onChange={(hk) => hk && saveAppHotkey({ pause_hotkey: hk })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Emergency stop (ends playback, releases all keys)" conflict={appConflict("Emergency stop")} icon={ShieldAlert}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.stop_playback_hotkey} onChange={(hk) => hk && saveAppHotkey({ stop_playback_hotkey: hk })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Save replay (opens the pop-up)" conflict={appConflict("Save replay")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.replay.hotkey} placeholder="No hotkey" onChange={(hk) => saveAppHotkey({ replay: { ...settings.replay, hotkey: hk } })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Auto rebirth start / stop" conflict={appConflict("Auto rebirth")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.rebirth.start_hotkey} placeholder="No hotkey" onChange={(hk) => saveAppHotkey({ rebirth: { ...settings.rebirth, start_hotkey: hk } })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Fishing routine start / stop" conflict={appConflict("Fishing")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.fishing.hotkey} placeholder="No hotkey" onChange={(hk) => saveAppHotkey({ fishing: { ...settings.fishing, hotkey: hk } })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <HotkeyRow label="Auto clicker start / stop" conflict={appConflict("Auto clicker")}>
          <HotkeyInput value={settings.autoclicker.hotkey} placeholder="No hotkey" onChange={(hk) => saveAppHotkey({ autoclicker: { ...settings.autoclicker, hotkey: hk } })} />
        </HotkeyRow>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">
          Work while SLNC Macro is in the background or in the tray. “Play selected macro” plays the macro selected in the recorder. Moving the mouse to the
          top-left corner also stops playback{settings.corner_failsafe ? "" : " (currently off in Settings)"}. Any key works, but a key without Ctrl, Alt or Shift can't be used for anything else, such as typing, while SLNC Macro runs.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="panel space-y-4 p-5">
        <h2 className="section-title flex items-center gap-2">
          Macro hotkeys · active profile <Layers className="size-3.5 text-accent-2" />
          <span className="text-accent-2 normal-case">{active?.name ?? "—"}</span>
        </h2>
        {macros.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted">No macros yet.</p>}
        {ordered.map((m) => {
          const on = inActive.has(m.id);
          return (
            <HotkeyRow
              key={m.id}
              label={m.name}
              conflict={macroConflict(m.id)}
              note={on ? undefined : "not in active profile — inactive"}
            >
              <HotkeyInput
                value={m.hotkey ?? ""}
                placeholder="No hotkey — click to set"
                onChange={(hk) =>
                  api
                    .setMacroHotkey(m.id, hk || null)
                    .then((problems) => {
                      reloadMacros();
                      if (problems.length) app.setError(problems.join("; "));
                    })
                    .catch(reportError)
                }
              />
            </HotkeyRow>
          );
        })}
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">Choose which macros belong to which profile on the Profiles page.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function HotkeyRow({
  label,
  conflict,
  note,
  icon: Icon,
  children,
}: {
  label: string;
  conflict?: HotkeyConflict;
  note?: string;
  icon?: typeof Keyboard;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
      <span className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-2 text-sm">
        {Icon && <Icon className="size-4 text-red-400" />}
        <span className={note ? "truncate text-muted" : "truncate"}>{label}</span>
        {note && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted/80">({note})</span>}
        {conflict && (
          <span
            className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-amber-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-amber-300"
            title={`${conflict.hotkey} is also used by “${conflict.first === label ? conflict.second : conflict.first}”`}
          >
            <AlertTriangle className="size-3" /> Conflict
          </span>
        )}
      </span>
      <div className="w-60">{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/LibraryPage.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Clapperboard, Copy, Download, FolderOpen, Library, Pencil, Play, Plus, Star, Tag, Trash2, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { BrokenFilesNotice, macroIcon, SearchBox, type LibraryActions } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { Badge, EmptyState, Page, PageHeader, Segmented } from "../components/ui";
import { formatMs } from "../lib/step-meta";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { MacroMeta, MacroSummary } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { relative } from "../lib/time";

type Sort = "edited" | "played" | "popular" | "name" | "duration";
type Filter = "all" | "favorites" | "recent";

const EMPTY_META: MacroMeta = { favorite: false, tags: [], category: null, last_played_at: null, play_count: 0 };

export function LibraryPage({ app, actions, canCreate }: { app: AppStateHook; actions: LibraryActions; canCreate: boolean }) {
  const { macros, license, library, status, reportError } = app;
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<Sort>("edited");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>("all");
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [tag, setTag] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [detailId, setDetailId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const max = license?.entitlements.max_macros;
  const meta = (id: string) => library[id] ?? EMPTY_META;

  const categories = useMemo(() => [...new Set(Object.values(library).map((m) => m.category).filter((c): c is string => !!c))].sort(), [library]);
  const tags = useMemo(() => [...new Set(Object.values(library).flatMap((m) => m.tags))].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b)), [library]);

  const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const list = macros
    .filter((m) => !q || m.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || m.hotkey?.toLowerCase().includes(q) || meta(m.id).tags.some((t) => t.toLowerCase().includes(q)))
    .filter((m) => filter !== "favorites" || meta(m.id).favorite)
    .filter((m) => filter !== "recent" || meta(m.id).last_played_at)
    .filter((m) => !category || meta(m.id).category === category)
    .filter((m) => !tag || meta(m.id).tags.includes(tag))
    .sort((a, b) => compare(a, b, filter === "recent" ? "played" : sort, meta));

  const detail = macros.find((m) => m.id === detailId) ?? null;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (detailId && !detail) setDetailId(null);
  }, [detailId, detail]);

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader
        icon={Library}
        title="Macro Library"
        subtitle={`${macros.length}${max != null ? ` of ${max}` : ""} macros · stored as files on this PC`}
        actions={
          <>
            <button className="btn-ghost" onClick={actions.onOpenFolder} title="Open the macros folder in Explorer">
              <FolderOpen className="size-4" /> Folder
            </button>
            <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={actions.onImport} disabled={!canCreate} title="Import a .macro.json file (validated before it is added)">
              <Upload className="size-4" /> Import
            </button>
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={actions.onNew} disabled={!canCreate}>
              <Plus className="size-4" /> New macro
            </button>
          </>
        }
      />

      <BrokenFilesNotice broken={app.broken} onReveal={actions.onRevealFile} onRefresh={actions.onRefresh} />

      <div className="panel flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-3">
        <div className="w-72">
          <SearchBox value={query} onChange={setQuery} />
        </div>
        <Segmented<Filter>
          value={filter}
          onChange={setFilter}
          options={[
            { value: "all", label: "All" },
            { value: "favorites", label: "Favorites" },
            { value: "recent", label: "Recently used" },
          ]}
        />
        {categories.length > 0 && (
          <select className="field w-44" value={category ?? ""} onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value || null)} aria-label="Category">
            <option value="">All categories</option>
            {categories.map((c) => (
              <option key={c}>{c}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        )}
        <label className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-muted">
          Sort
          <select className="field w-44" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value as Sort)} disabled={filter === "recent"}>
            <option value="edited">Recently edited</option>
            <option value="played">Recently played</option>
            <option value="popular">Most played</option>
            <option value="name">Name (A–Z)</option>
            <option value="duration">Duration</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        {tags.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex w-full flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 border-t border-line pt-3">
            <Tag className="size-3.5 text-muted" />
            {tags.map((t) => (
              <button key={t} className={clsx("chip transition", tag === t ? "border-accent/50 bg-accent/15 text-accent-2" : "hover:text-text")} onClick={() => setTag(tag === t ? null : t)}>
                {t}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className={clsx("grid gap-5", detail && "lg:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]")}>
        <div>
          {macros.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="panel">
              <EmptyState
                icon={Library}
                title="Your library is empty"
                text="Record a macro in Macro Studio, start an empty one, or import a .macro.json file."
                action={
                  <button className="btn-primary" onClick={actions.onNew} disabled={!canCreate}>
                    <Plus className="size-4" /> New macro
                  </button>
                }
              />
            </div>
          ) : list.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="panel p-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">No macros match these filters.</div>
          ) : (
            <div className={clsx("grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2", detail ? "xl:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-3" : "lg:grid-cols-3 2xl:grid-cols-4")}>
              {list.map((m) => (
                <MacroCard
                  key={m.id}
                  macro={m}
                  meta={meta(m.id)}
                  selected={m.id === detailId}
                  onOpen={() => setDetailId(m.id === detailId ? null : m.id)}
                  onFavorite={() => actions.onToggleFavorite(m.id)}
                  onPlay={() => api.playMacro(m.id).catch(reportError)}
                  canPlay={status.state === "idle" && m.step_count > 0}
                />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {detail && (
          <MacroDetails
            key={detail.id}
            macro={detail}
            meta={meta(detail.id)}
            categories={categories}
            canCreate={canCreate}
            busy={status.state !== "idle"}
            actions={actions}
            onClose={() => setDetailId(null)}
            onSaved={app.reloadLibrary}
            onError={reportError}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </Page>
  );
}

function compare(a: MacroSummary, b: MacroSummary, sort: Sort, meta: (id: string) => MacroMeta): number {
  switch (sort) {
    case "name":
      return a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    case "duration":
      return b.duration_ms - a.duration_ms;
    case "popular":
      return meta(b.id).play_count - meta(a.id).play_count || a.name.localeCompare(b.name);
    case "played":
      return (meta(b.id).last_played_at ?? "").localeCompare(meta(a.id).last_played_at ?? "");
    default:
      return (b.updated_at ?? "").localeCompare(a.updated_at ?? "");
  }
}

function MacroCard({ macro: m, meta, selected, onOpen, onFavorite, onPlay, canPlay }: { macro: MacroSummary; meta: MacroMeta; selected: boolean; onOpen: () => void; onFavorite: () => void; onPlay: () => void; canPlay: boolean }) {
  const Icon = macroIcon(m.id);
  return (
    <div
      className={clsx(
        "panel group flex cursor-pointer flex-col gap-3 p-4 transition duration-200 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-line-strong",
        selected && "border-accent/50 shadow-[0_0_24px_-6px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.6*var(--fx)))]",
      )}
      onClick={onOpen}
    >
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <span className="raised grid size-11 shrink-0 place-items-center text-accent-2">
          <Icon className="size-5" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="truncate font-medium" title={m.name}>
            {m.name}
          </p>
          <p className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted">
            {meta.category ? `${meta.category} · ` : ""}
            {meta.last_played_at ? `played ${relative(meta.last_played_at)}` : m.updated_at ? `edited ${relative(m.updated_at)}` : "—"}
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          className="btn-ghost -mt-1 -mr-1 px-1.5 py-1"
          aria-label={meta.favorite ? "Remove from favorites" : "Add to favorites"}
          title={meta.favorite ? "Remove from favorites" : "Add to favorites"}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onFavorite();
          }}
        >
          <Star className={clsx("size-4", meta.favorite ? "fill-amber-300 text-amber-300" : "")} />
        </button>
      </div>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 text-center text-xs">
        <Metric label="Events" value={String(m.step_count)} />
        <Metric label="Duration" value={formatMs(m.duration_ms)} />
        <Metric label="Hotkey" value={m.hotkey ?? "—"} />
      </div>
      <div className="flex min-h-5 items-center gap-1.5">
        {meta.tags.slice(0, 3).map((t) => (
          <span key={t} className="chip">
            {t}
          </span>
        ))}
        {meta.tags.length > 3 && <span className="text-[11px] text-muted">+{meta.tags.length - 3}</span>}
        <button
          className="btn-secondary ml-auto px-2 py-1 text-xs opacity-0 transition group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100"
          disabled={!canPlay}
          onClick={(e) => {
            e.stopPropagation();
            onPlay();
          }}
        >
          <Play className="size-3 fill-current" /> Play
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function MacroDetails({
  macro: m,
  meta,
  categories,
  canCreate,
  busy,
  actions,
  onClose,
  onSaved,
  onError,
}: {
  macro: MacroSummary;
  meta: MacroMeta;
  categories: string[];
  canCreate: boolean;
  busy: boolean;
  actions: LibraryActions;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaved: () => void;
  onError: (e: unknown) => void;
}) {
  const [category, setCategory] = useState(meta.category ?? "");
  const [tagInput, setTagInput] = useState("");
  const Icon = macroIcon(m.id);

  const save = (patch: Partial<Pick<MacroMeta, "favorite" | "tags" | "category">>) =>
    api
      .setMacroMeta(m.id, patch.favorite ?? meta.favorite, patch.tags ?? meta.tags, patch.category !== undefined ? patch.category : meta.category)
      .then(onSaved)
      .catch(onError);

  const addTag = () => {
    const t = tagInput.trim();
    if (!t) return;
    setTagInput("");
    save({ tags: [...meta.tags, t] });
  };

  return (
    <aside className="panel page-enter sticky top-6 h-fit space-y-4 p-5">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
        <span className="raised grid size-12 shrink-0 place-items-center text-accent-2">
          <Icon className="size-6" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h2 className="truncate font-semibold" title={m.name}>
            {m.name}
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">{meta.favorite ? <Badge tone="warning">Favorite</Badge> : "Macro details"}</p>
        </div>
        <button className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-1" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close details">
          <X className="size-4" />
        </button>
      </div>

      <dl className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-x-3 gap-y-2 text-sm">
        <dt className="text-muted">Events</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-mono">{m.step_count}</dd>
        <dt className="text-muted">Duration (1 run)</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-mono">{formatMs(m.duration_ms)}</dd>
        <dt className="text-muted">Hotkey</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-mono text-accent-2">{m.hotkey ?? "—"}</dd>
        <dt className="text-muted">Times played</dt>
        <dd className="text-right font-mono">{meta.play_count}</dd>
        <dt className="text-muted">Last played</dt>
        <dd className="text-right">{meta.last_played_at ? relative(meta.last_played_at) : "never"}</dd>
        <dt className="text-muted">Last edited</dt>
        <dd className="text-right">{m.updated_at ? new Date(m.updated_at).toLocaleString() : "—"}</dd>
      </dl>

      <label className="block space-y-1.5">
        <span className="label">Category</span>
        <input
          className="field"
          list="macro-categories"
          maxLength={32}
          placeholder="e.g. Work, Gaming"
          value={category}
          onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}
          onBlur={() => category.trim() !== (meta.category ?? "") && save({ category: category.trim() || null })}
          onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur()}
        />
        <datalist id="macro-categories">
          {categories.map((c) => (
            <option key={c} value={c} />
          ))}
        </datalist>
      </label>

      <div className="space-y-1.5">
        <span className="label">Tags</span>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
          {meta.tags.map((t) => (
            <span key={t} className="chip pr-1">
              {t}
              <button className="rounded p-0.5 hover:bg-white/10 hover:text-text" aria-label={`Remove tag ${t}`} onClick={() => save({ tags: meta.tags.filter((x) => x !== t) })}>
                <X className="size-3" />
              </button>
            </span>
          ))}
          {meta.tags.length === 0 && <span className="text-xs text-muted">No tags yet</span>}
        </div>
        <form
          className="flex gap-2"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            addTag();
          }}
        >
          <input className="field" placeholder="Add a tag…" maxLength={24} value={tagInput} onChange={(e) => setTagInput(e.target.value)} disabled={meta.tags.length >= 10} />
          <button className="btn-secondary" type="submit" disabled={!tagInput.trim()}>
            Add
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 border-t border-line pt-4">
        <button className="btn-primary col-span-2" onClick={() => actions.onSelect(m.id)}>
          <Clapperboard className="size-4" /> Open in Macro Studio
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => actions.onRename(m.id)}>
          <Pencil className="size-4" /> Rename
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => actions.onDuplicate(m.id)} disabled={!canCreate}>
          <Copy className="size-4" /> Duplicate
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => actions.onExport(m.id)}>
          <Download className="size-4" /> Export
        </button>
        <button className="btn-secondary hover:text-red-300" onClick={() => actions.onDelete(m.id)} disabled={busy}>
          <Trash2 className="size-4" /> Delete
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="text-[11px] text-muted">Favorites, tags and categories are stored next to your macros (library.json); the macro files themselves are not changed.</p>
    </aside>
  );
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="well py-1.5">
      <div className="text-muted">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-0.5 truncate font-mono text-accent-2">{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/MacroHistory.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import type { Tracker } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { HistoryIcon } from "../components/shell/DockIcons";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

type Session = { start: number; end: number; counts: Record<string, number>; best_streak: number; reason: string };

const day = (ms: number) => {
  const d = new Date(ms);
  return new Date(d.getFullYear(), d.getMonth(), d.getDate()).getTime();
};
const fmt = (n: number) => n.toLocaleString();
const dur = (ms: number) => {
  const m = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 60000));
  return m >= 60 ? `${Math.floor(m / 60)} h ${String(m % 60).padStart(2, "0")} min` : `${m} min`;
};

/**
 * A macro's history: what its counters counted per session, today, yesterday and all time, and its
 * records. Only real sessions (saved while the macro played with trackers) — nothing made up.
 */
export function MacroHistory({ app, selectedId, onSelect }: { app: AppStateHook; selectedId: string | null; onSelect: (id: string | null) => void }) {
  const { macros } = app;
  const [trackers, setTrackers] = useState<Tracker[]>([]);
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<Session[] | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId && macros[0]) onSelect(macros[0].id);
  }, [selectedId, macros, onSelect]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId) return;
    setSessions(null);
    api
      .getMacro(selectedId)
      .then((m) => setTrackers(((m as { trackers?: Tracker[] }).trackers ?? []).filter((t) => t.kind.type === "count")))
      .catch(() => setTrackers([]));
    api
      .macroHistory(selectedId)
      .then((h) => setSessions([...h.sessions].reverse()))
      .catch(() => setSessions([]));
  }, [selectedId]);

  const totals = useMemo(() => {
    const today = day(Date.now());
    const yesterday = today - 86_400_000;
    const sum = (filter: (s: Session) => boolean) => {
      const out: Record<string, number> = {};
      for (const s of (sessions ?? []).filter(filter)) for (const [id, c] of Object.entries(s.counts)) out[id] = (out[id] ?? 0) + c;
      return out;
    };
    const best: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (const s of sessions ?? []) for (const [id, c] of Object.entries(s.counts)) best[id] = Math.max(best[id] ?? 0, c);
    return {
      today: sum((s) => day(s.start) === today),
      yesterday: sum((s) => day(s.start) === yesterday),
      all: sum(() => true),
      best,
      bestStreak: Math.max(0, ...(sessions ?? []).map((s) => s.best_streak)),
      time: (sessions ?? []).reduce((t, s) => t + (s.end - s.start), 0),
    };
  }, [sessions]);

  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-6 p-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="section-title">History</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">What your macro did</h1>
        </div>
        {macros.length > 0 && (
          <label className="space-y-1">
            <span className="label">Macro</span>
            <select className="field w-72" value={selectedId ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onSelect(e.target.value || null)}>
              {macros.map((m) => (
                <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                  {m.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        )}
      </div>

      {sessions === null ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">Loading…</p>
      ) : sessions.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="panel flex flex-col items-center gap-3 p-10 text-center">
          <HistoryIcon className="size-10 text-accent-2" />
          <p className="font-semibold">No sessions yet</p>
          <p className="max-w-md text-sm text-muted">Give this macro a game window and a tracker (Macro settings → Trackers), then play it. Every session shows up here with what it counted.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-4">
            <Card label="Sessions" value={fmt(sessions.length)} />
            <Card label="Time played" value={dur(totals.time)} />
            <Card label="Best streak" value={totals.bestStreak ? `×${fmt(totals.bestStreak)}` : "—"} />
            <Card label="Last played" value={new Date(sessions[0]!.start).toLocaleString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })} />
          </div>

          {trackers.length > 0 && (
            <section className="panel overflow-x-auto p-5">
              <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Totals</h2>
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Counter</th>
                    <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Today</th>
                    <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Yesterday</th>
                    <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">All time</th>
                    <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Most in a session</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {trackers.map((t) => (
                    <tr key={t.id} className="border-t border-line">
                      <td className="py-2 font-semibold">{t.name}</td>
                      <td className="py-2 font-mono">{fmt(totals.today[t.id] ?? 0)}</td>
                      <td className="py-2 font-mono">{fmt(totals.yesterday[t.id] ?? 0)}</td>
                      <td className="py-2 font-mono">{fmt(totals.all[t.id] ?? 0)}</td>
                      <td className="py-2 font-mono">{fmt(totals.best[t.id] ?? 0)}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </section>
          )}

          <section className="panel overflow-x-auto p-5">
            <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Sessions</h2>
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted">
                <tr>
                  <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Started</th>
                  <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Played</th>
                  {trackers.map((t) => (
                    <th key={t.id} className="py-1.5 font-medium">
                      {t.name}
                    </th>
                  ))}
                  <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Best streak</th>
                  <th className="py-1.5 font-medium">Ended</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {sessions.slice(0, 100).map((s) => (
                  <tr key={s.start} className="border-t border-line">
                    <td className="py-2">{new Date(s.start).toLocaleString([], { day: "numeric", month: "short", hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit" })}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 font-mono">{dur(s.end - s.start)}</td>
                    {trackers.map((t) => (
                      <td key={t.id} className="py-2 font-mono">
                        {fmt(s.counts[t.id] ?? 0)}
                      </td>
                    ))}
                    <td className="py-2 font-mono">{s.best_streak ? `×${s.best_streak}` : "—"}</td>
                    <td className="py-2 text-muted">{s.reason || "app closed"}</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Card({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="panel p-4">
      <p className="text-[11px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">{label}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/MacroSettings.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { SlidersHorizontal } from "lucide-react";
import { HotkeyInput } from "../components/HotkeyInput";
import { ScreenSettingsPanel } from "../components/ScreenStep";
import { LivePanel, TrackersPanel } from "../components/Trackers";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { useMacroDraft } from "../lib/use-macro-draft";
import { useTeach } from "../lib/use-teach";

/**
 * One macro's settings on their own page: name and hotkey, the game window it plays in (and its
 * limits), what it tracks, and its live Discord message. Saves by itself, like the editor.
 */
export function MacroSettings({ app, selectedId, onSelect, onNavigate }: { app: AppStateHook; selectedId: string | null; onSelect: (id: string | null) => void; onNavigate: (v: string) => void }) {
  const { macros, reportError, reloadMacros, settings } = app;
  const { draft, setDraft, saveState, problem } = useMacroDraft(selectedId, reportError, reloadMacros);
  const { teach, pickArea, overlay } = useTeach(draft, (e) => app.setError(e), reportError);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId && macros[0]) onSelect(macros[0].id);
  }, [selectedId, macros, onSelect]);

  if (!macros.length) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-10 text-center">
        <SlidersHorizontal className="size-10 text-accent-2" />
        <p className="text-lg font-semibold">No macros yet</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">Record one first, then set it up here.</p>
        <button className="btn-primary" onClick={() => onNavigate("record")}>
          Record a macro
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-6xl space-y-6 p-8">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-end gap-4">
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <p className="section-title">Macro settings</p>
          <h1 className="mt-2 truncate text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">{draft?.name ?? "…"}</h1>
        </div>
        <label className="space-y-1">
          <span className="label">Macro</span>
          <select className="field w-72" value={selectedId ?? ""} onChange={(e) => onSelect(e.target.value || null)}>
            {macros.map((m) => (
              <option key={m.id} value={m.id}>
                {m.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </label>
        <span className={saveState === "invalid" ? "text-xs text-red-400" : "text-xs text-muted"} title={problem ?? undefined}>
          {saveState === "saved" ? "Saved" : saveState === "saving" ? "Saving…" : saveState === "dirty" ? "Unsaved" : (problem ?? "Not saved")}
        </span>
      </div>

      {draft && (
        <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
          <div className="space-y-5">
            <section className="panel space-y-3 p-5">
              <h2 className="section-title">Name & hotkey</h2>
              <label className="block space-y-1.5">
                <span className="label">Name</span>
                <input className="field" value={draft.name} maxLength={100} onChange={(e) => setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, name: e.target.value }))} />
              </label>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <span className="label">Hotkey (plays in the active window)</span>
                <HotkeyInput
                  value={draft.hotkey ?? ""}
                  placeholder="No hotkey — click to set"
                  onChange={(next) =>
                    api
                      .setMacroHotkey(draft.id, next || null)
                      .then((problems) => {
                        setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, hotkey: next || null }));
                        if (problems.length) app.setError(problems.join("; "));
                      })
                      .catch(reportError)
                  }
                />
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="panel p-5">
              <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Game window</h2>
              <ScreenSettingsPanel
                value={draft.screen}
                onChange={(screen) =>
                  setDraft((m) => {
                    const { screen: _drop, ...rest } = m;
                    return screen ? { ...rest, screen } : rest;
                  })
                }
              />
            </section>

            <section className="panel p-5">
              <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Trackers</h2>
              <TrackersPanel macro={draft} teach={teach} pickArea={pickArea} onChange={(trackers) => setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, trackers }))} />
            </section>
          </div>

          <section className="panel p-5">
            <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Discord live message</h2>
            <LivePanel macro={draft} settings={settings} onSaveSettings={app.saveSettings} onChange={(live) => setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, live }))} />
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
      {overlay}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/MakeMacros.tsx

import { useState } from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { Clapperboard, Library } from "lucide-react";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import type { LibraryActions } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { LibraryPage } from "./LibraryPage";
import { Recorder } from "./Recorder";

export type MakeTab = "studio" | "library";

/** "Make your own Macros": the studio (record, edit, play) and your saved macros. */
export function MakeMacros({
  app,
  selectedId,
  onSelect,
  actions,
  canCreate,
  initialTab = "studio",
}: {
  app: AppStateHook;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onSelect: (id: string | null) => void;
  actions: LibraryActions;
  canCreate: boolean;
  initialTab?: MakeTab;
}) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<MakeTab>(initialTab);
  const tabs = [
    { id: "studio" as const, label: "Studio", icon: Clapperboard },
    { id: "library" as const, label: "My macros", icon: Library },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex shrink-0 gap-1 border-b border-line px-7 pt-3">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={clsx(
              "-mb-px flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition",
              tab === t.id ? "border-accent text-text" : "border-transparent text-muted hover:text-text",
            )}
          >
            <t.icon className="size-4" /> {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
        {tab === "studio" ? (
          <Recorder app={app} selectedId={selectedId} onSelect={onSelect} library={actions} canCreate={canCreate} />
        ) : (
          <LibraryPage app={app} actions={actions} canCreate={canCreate} />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
