FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Owner.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Ban, Copy, Crown, KeyRound, Megaphone, RotateCcw, Send, ShieldOff } from "lucide-react";
import { useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { OwnerView } from "../lib/types";

const date = (s: number) => (s > 0 ? new Date(s * 1000).toLocaleString() : "—");

/**
 * Owner only: the owner list every app reads (owner.rs) — block keys on every PC, stop all old
 * keys, send one message — and making guest / lifetime keys. Signing needs the owner's key on this PC.
 */
export function Owner() {
  const { confirm } = useDialogs();
  const [view, setView] = useState<OwnerView | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [done, setDone] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [blockEmail, setBlockEmail] = useState("");
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [keyEmail, setKeyEmail] = useState("");
  const [keyPlan, setKeyPlan] = useState<"guest" | "lifetime">("guest");
  const [newKey, setNewKey] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .ownerGet()
      .then((v) => {
        setView(v);
        setNotice(v.list?.notice?.text ?? "");
      })
      .catch((e) => setError(errorMessage(e)));
  }, []);

  const list = view?.list ?? null;
  const blocked = list?.blocked ?? [];
  const ready = !!view?.can_sign && !!view?.can_publish;

  const publish = async (next: string[], keysFrom: number, text: string | null, what: string) => {
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    setDone(null);
    try {
      const v = await api.ownerPublish(next, keysFrom, text);
      setView(v);
      setDone(`${what} Every app picks it up within 15 minutes (on version 0.1.59 or newer).`);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  const keepNotice = list?.notice?.text ?? null;

  const block = async () => {
    const email = blockEmail.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!email) return;
    const ok = await confirm({ title: `Block ${email}?`, message: "Their key stops working on every PC. They get logged out and can only come back with a new key from you.", confirmText: "Block", danger: true });
    if (!ok) return;
    await publish([...blocked, email], 0, keepNotice, `${email} is blocked.`);
    setBlockEmail("");
  };

  const unblock = (email: string) => publish(blocked.filter((e) => e !== email), 0, keepNotice, `${email} is unblocked.`);

  const stopOld = async () => {
    const ok = await confirm({
      title: "Stop every key made before now?",
      message: "Every guest and lifetime key made until this moment stops working on every PC. Your owner key keeps working. People need a new key from you to get back in.",
      confirmText: "Stop old keys",
      danger: true,
      typeToConfirm: "STOP",
    });
    if (ok) await publish(blocked, -1, keepNotice, "Every older key is stopped.");
  };

  const sendNotice = (text: string | null) => publish(blocked, 0, text, text ? "Your message is out." : "The message is removed.");

  const makeKey = async () => {
    setError(null);
    setNewKey(null);
    try {
      setNewKey(await api.ownerMakeKey(keyEmail, keyPlan));
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-3xl space-y-8 p-10">
      <div>
        <p className="section-title">Owner</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-3 text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">
          <Crown className="size-8 text-amber-300" /> Owner tools
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-muted">
          What you change here goes to every copy of SLNC Macro through a list signed with your key. Nobody else can make that list, and nothing about other people is sent to you.
        </p>
      </div>

      {error && <p className="rounded-md border border-red-500/30 bg-red-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-200">{error}</p>}
      {done && <p className="rounded-md border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-emerald-200">{done}</p>}
      {view && !ready && (
        <p className="rounded-md border border-amber-400/30 bg-amber-400/10 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-amber-100">
          {!view.can_sign ? "Your signing key isn't on this PC (.slnc-keys), so you can look but not change anything here." : "The update repo (.slnc-macro-updates) isn't on this PC, so the list can't be published from here."}
        </p>
      )}

      <section className="panel space-y-4 p-6">
        <p className="section-title">The list now</p>
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-4 text-sm">
          <div>
            <dt className="label">Signed</dt>
            <dd className="mt-1 font-mono">{list ? date(list.iat) : "Nothing published yet"}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt className="label">Keys must be made after</dt>
            <dd className="mt-1 font-mono">{date(list?.keys_from ?? 0)}</dd>
          </div>
          <div>
            <dt className="label">Blocked</dt>
            <dd className="mt-1 font-mono">{blocked.length}</dd>
          </div>
        </dl>
      </section>

      <section className="panel space-y-4 p-6">
        <p className="section-title">Block a key</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <input className="field" placeholder="email of the key" value={blockEmail} onChange={(e) => setBlockEmail(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && block()} />
          <button className="btn-danger shrink-0" disabled={!ready || busy || !blockEmail.includes("@")} onClick={block}>
            <Ban className="size-4" /> Block
          </button>
        </div>
        {blocked.length > 0 ? (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-line rounded-md border border-line">
            {blocked.map((e) => (
              <li key={e} className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 py-2.5 text-sm">
                <span className="font-mono">{e}</span>
                <button className="btn-ghost py-1!" disabled={!ready || busy} onClick={() => unblock(e)}>
                  <RotateCcw className="size-4" /> Unblock
                </button>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nobody is blocked.</p>
        )}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-line pt-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">Start over: every guest and lifetime key made before now stops. Your owner key keeps working.</p>
          <button className="btn-danger shrink-0" disabled={!ready || busy} onClick={stopOld}>
            <ShieldOff className="size-4" /> Stop old keys
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="panel space-y-4 p-6">
        <p className="section-title">Message to everyone</p>
        <textarea className="field min-h-20" maxLength={300} placeholder="e.g. New version out — update from Help." value={notice} onChange={(e) => setNotice(e.target.value)} />
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <span className="text-xs text-muted">{notice.length}/300 · shows at the top of every app until they close it</span>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            {list?.notice && (
              <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!ready || busy} onClick={() => sendNotice(null)}>
                Remove
              </button>
            )}
            <button className="btn-primary" disabled={!ready || busy || !notice.trim() || notice.trim() === list?.notice?.text} onClick={() => sendNotice(notice.trim())}>
              <Megaphone className="size-4" /> Send
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="panel space-y-4 p-6">
        <p className="section-title">Make a key</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <input className="field" placeholder="their email" value={keyEmail} onChange={(e) => setKeyEmail(e.target.value)} />
          <select className="field w-36! shrink-0" value={keyPlan} onChange={(e) => setKeyPlan(e.target.value as "guest" | "lifetime")}>
            <option value="guest">Guest</option>
            <option value="lifetime">Lifetime</option>
          </select>
          <button className="btn-primary shrink-0" disabled={!view?.can_sign || !keyEmail.includes("@")} onClick={makeKey}>
            <KeyRound className="size-4" /> Make
          </button>
        </div>
        {newKey && (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <textarea readOnly className="field min-h-24 font-mono text-xs break-all" value={newKey} onFocus={(e) => e.target.select()} />
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(newKey).then(() => setDone("Key copied."))}>
                <Copy className="size-4" /> Copy
              </button>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted">
                <Send className="size-3.5" /> Send it to them yourself. It only works for {keyEmail.trim().toLowerCase()}.
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Profiles.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Check, Copy, Gem, Layers, Pencil, Plus, Trash2, UsersRound } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { macroIcon, SearchBox, useMacroSearch } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { ProfileApps } from "../components/ProfileApps";
import { Page, PageHeader, useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import type { View } from "../lib/nav";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

/**
 * Profiles group macros. Only hotkeys of macros in the ACTIVE profile are registered, so the same
 * hotkey can mean different things in different profiles. A macro can be in several profiles.
 */
export function Profiles({ app, onNavigate }: { app: AppStateHook; onNavigate: (v: string) => void }) {
  const { profiles, macros, conflicts, reportError, reloadProfiles, status } = app;
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState("");
  const [renaming, setRenaming] = useState<{ id: string; name: string } | null>(null);
  const { query, setQuery, filtered } = useMacroSearch(macros);
  const busy = status.state !== "idle";
  const dialogs = useDialogs();
  const maxProfiles = app.license?.entitlements.max_profiles === undefined ? 2 : app.license.entitlements.max_profiles;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profiles && (!selectedId || !profiles.profiles.some((p) => p.id === selectedId))) setSelectedId(profiles.active);
  }, [profiles, selectedId]);

  if (!profiles) return null;
  const atLimit = maxProfiles !== null && profiles.profiles.length >= maxProfiles;
  const selected = profiles.profiles.find((p) => p.id === selectedId) ?? profiles.profiles[0]!;
  const run = (fn: () => Promise<unknown>) =>
    fn()
      .then(reloadProfiles)
      .catch((e) => {
        reportError(e);
        reloadProfiles();
      });
  const hotkeyProblems = (problems: string[]) => problems.length && app.setError(`Some hotkeys could not be registered: ${problems.join("; ")}`);

  const create = () => {
    const name = newName.trim();
    if (!name) return;
    run(async () => {
      const next = await api.createProfile(name);
      setNewName("");
      setSelectedId(next.profiles[next.profiles.length - 1]!.id);
    });
  };

  return (
    <Page>
      <PageHeader
        icon={UsersRound}
        title="Profiles"
        subtitle={`${profiles.profiles.length}${maxProfiles !== null ? ` of ${maxProfiles}` : ""} profiles · active: ${profiles.profiles.find((p) => p.id === profiles.active)?.name ?? "—"}`}
      />
    <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[320px_1fr]">
      {/* Profile list */}
      <section className="space-y-4">
        <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-muted">
          Only hotkeys of macros in the <span className="text-accent-2">active</span> profile work. The same hotkey can be reused in different
          profiles. New macros are added to the active profile.
        </p>

        <form
          className="flex gap-2"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault();
            create();
          }}
        >
          <input className="field" placeholder="New profile name…" maxLength={40} value={newName} onChange={(e) => setNewName(e.target.value)} />
          <button className="btn-primary" type="submit" disabled={!newName.trim() || atLimit}>
            <Plus className="size-4" /> Add
          </button>
        </form>
        {atLimit && (
          <button className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-accent/25 bg-accent/10 px-3 py-2 text-left text-xs text-accent-2" onClick={() => onNavigate("store")}>
            <Gem className="size-4 shrink-0" /> Your edition allows {maxProfiles} profiles.
          </button>
        )}

        <ul className="space-y-2">
          {profiles.profiles.map((p) => {
            const isActive = p.id === profiles.active;
            const isSel = p.id === selected.id;
            return (
              <li
                key={p.id}
                className={clsx(
                  "panel cursor-pointer p-3 transition duration-200",
                  isSel ? "border-accent/60 shadow-[0_0_14px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.25*var(--fx)))]" : "hover:border-line-strong",
                )}
                onClick={() => setSelectedId(p.id)}
              >
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Layers className={clsx("size-4", isActive ? "text-accent-2" : "text-muted")} />
                  {renaming?.id === p.id ? (
                    <input
                      autoFocus
                      className="field py-0.5!"
                      value={renaming.name}
                      maxLength={40}
                      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
                      onChange={(e) => setRenaming({ id: p.id, name: e.target.value })}
                      onBlur={() => {
                        if (renaming.name.trim() && renaming.name !== p.name) run(() => api.renameProfile(p.id, renaming.name));
                        setRenaming(null);
                      }}
                      onKeyDown={(e) => {
                        if (e.key === "Enter") (e.target as HTMLInputElement).blur();
                        if (e.key === "Escape") setRenaming(null);
                      }}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <span className="flex-1 truncate font-medium">{p.name}</span>
                  )}
                  {isActive && <span className="rounded bg-accent/25 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold text-accent-2">ACTIVE</span>}
                </div>
                <div className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-muted">
                  <span className="flex-1">{p.macro_ids.length} macros</span>
                  {!isActive && (
                    <button
                      className="btn-ghost px-2 py-0.5 text-xs"
                      disabled={busy}
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        run(async () => hotkeyProblems(await api.setActiveProfile(p.id)));
                      }}
                    >
                      <Check className="size-3.5" /> Activate
                    </button>
                  )}
                  <button
                    className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-0.5"
                    title="Rename"
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      setRenaming({ id: p.id, name: p.name });
                    }}
                  >
                    <Pencil className="size-3.5" />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-0.5"
                    title={atLimit ? "Profile limit of your edition reached" : "Duplicate (same macros)"}
                    disabled={atLimit}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      run(async () => {
                        const next = await api.duplicateProfile(p.id);
                        setSelectedId(next.profiles[next.profiles.length - 1]!.id);
                      });
                    }}
                  >
                    <Copy className="size-3.5" />
                  </button>
                  <button
                    className="btn-ghost px-1.5 py-0.5 text-red-400"
                    title={profiles.profiles.length <= 1 ? "You need at least one profile" : "Delete profile (macros are kept)"}
                    disabled={profiles.profiles.length <= 1}
                    onClick={(e) => {
                      e.stopPropagation();
                      void dialogs
                        .confirm({ title: `Delete profile “${p.name}”?`, message: "Its macros are kept in your library and in other profiles.", confirmText: "Delete profile", danger: true })
                        .then((ok) => void (ok && run(() => api.deleteProfile(p.id))));
                    }}
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </div>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </section>

      {/* Membership */}
      <section className="panel flex min-h-[420px] flex-col p-5">
        <ProfileApps app={app} profile={selected} />
        <div className="h-4" />
        <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold">
            Macros in “{selected.name}”
            <span className="ml-2 text-sm font-normal text-muted">
              {selected.macro_ids.length} of {macros.length}
            </span>
          </h2>
          <div className="ml-auto w-60">
            <SearchBox value={query} onChange={setQuery} />
          </div>
        </div>

        {macros.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">No macros yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <ul className="divide-y divide-line">
            {filtered.map((m) => {
              const member = selected.macro_ids.includes(m.id);
              const Icon = macroIcon(m.id);
              const conflict = conflicts.find((c) => c.macro_ids.includes(m.id) && (c.profile === null || c.profile === selected.name));
              return (
                <li key={m.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-2.5">
                  <input
                    type="checkbox"
                    className="size-4 accent-[var(--accent)]"
                    checked={member}
                    disabled={busy}
                    onChange={(e) => run(async () => hotkeyProblems(await api.setProfileMember(selected.id, m.id, e.target.checked)))}
                    aria-label={`Include ${m.name} in ${selected.name}`}
                  />
                  <Icon className="size-4 text-accent-2" />
                  <span className={clsx("flex-1 truncate text-sm", !member && "text-muted")}>{m.name}</span>
                  {conflict && (
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-amber-500/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-amber-300" title={`Conflicts with “${conflict.first === m.name ? conflict.second : conflict.first}”`}>
                      <AlertTriangle className="size-3" /> Conflict
                    </span>
                  )}
                  <span className="w-28 text-right font-mono text-xs text-muted">{m.hotkey ?? "no hotkey"}</span>
                </li>
              );
            })}
          </ul>
        )}
        <p className="mt-auto pt-4 text-[11px] text-muted">
          Adding a macro is refused when its hotkey is already used by another macro in this profile. Removing a macro from a profile does not delete
          it.
        </p>
      </section>
    </div>
    </Page>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Recorder.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Circle, CircleDot, Clapperboard, Copy, Download, Eye, FilePlus2, Library, PanelRightClose, PanelRightOpen, Pause, Pencil, Play, Plus, Save, ScanSearch, Square, Trash2, Upload, X } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { ActionTable } from "../components/ActionTable";
import { Inspector, NEW_STEPS } from "../components/Inspector";
import { NEW_SCREEN_STEPS } from "../components/ScreenStep";
import type { MacroStep, SeenTarget } from "@macroapp/shared";
import { useTeach } from "../lib/use-teach";
import { LibraryPanel, type LibraryActions } from "../components/MacroLibrary";
import { PlaybackPanel } from "../components/PlaybackPanel";
import { VariablesPanel } from "../components/VariablesPanel";
import { formatMs, stepMeta, type StepKind } from "../lib/step-meta";
import {
  duplicateAt,
  flatten,
  getAt,
  insertAfter,
  moveAt,
  movedPath,
  removeAt,
  samePath,
  totalDuration,
  setWaitBefore,
  unwrapLoop,
  updateAt,
  waitBefore,
  wrapInLoop,
  type StepPath,
} from "../lib/step-tree";
import { api, onEvent } from "../lib/tauri";
import { Scanner } from "./Scanner";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { useMacroDraft, type SaveState } from "../lib/use-macro-draft";
import { clock, useElapsed, useRecording } from "../lib/use-recording";

type Tab = "timeline" | "keys" | "mouse" | "variables" | "scan";

const TABS: Array<{ id: Tab; label: string; soon?: boolean }> = [
  { id: "timeline", label: "Timeline" },
  { id: "keys", label: "Key Events" },
  { id: "mouse", label: "Mouse Events" },
  { id: "variables", label: "Variables" },
  { id: "scan", label: "Game scan" },
];

const FILTER: Record<Tab, StepKind | "all"> = { timeline: "all", keys: "key", mouse: "mouse", variables: "all", scan: "all" };

interface RecorderProps {
  app: AppStateHook;
  selectedId: string | null;
  onSelect: (id: string | null) => void;
  library: LibraryActions;
  canCreate: boolean;
}

export function Recorder({ app, selectedId, onSelect, library, canCreate }: RecorderProps) {
  const { status, settings, license, macros, reportError, reloadMacros } = app;
  const { draft, setDraft, saveState, problem, flush } = useMacroDraft(selectedId, reportError, reloadMacros);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("timeline");
  const [selectedPath, setSelectedPath] = useState<StepPath | null>(null);
  const [showSide, setShowSide] = useState(true);
  const [showLibrary, setShowLibrary] = useState(() => window.innerWidth >= 1340);
  const [addOpen, setAddOpen] = useState(false);
  const rec = useRecording(app, (id) => onSelect(id));
  const countdown = rec.countdown;
  const live = useLiveActions(status.state === "recording");
  // Screen steps: teaching a target = name it, then draw a box around it inside the game window.
  const { teach, overlay: teachOverlay } = useTeach(draft, (e) => app.setError(e), reportError);

  const isRecording = status.state === "recording";
  const isPlayingThis = status.state === "playing" && status.macro_id === draft?.id;
  const isPaused = (status.state === "recording" || status.state === "playing") && status.paused;
  const togglePause = () => api.togglePause().catch(reportError);
  const busy = status.state !== "idle" || countdown !== null;

  // Pick the first macro when nothing is selected.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedId && macros[0]) onSelect(macros[0].id);
  }, [selectedId, macros, onSelect]);

  useEffect(() => setSelectedPath(null), [selectedId]);

  const selectedStep = draft && selectedPath ? (getAt(draft.steps, selectedPath) ?? null) : null;

  // ----- Recording -----
  const startRecording = rec.start;
  const cancelCountdown = rec.cancel;
  const stopRecording = rec.stop;
  const playElapsed = useElapsed(isPlayingThis, isPaused);

  // ----- Playback -----
  const play = async () => {
    if (!draft) return;
    if (!(await flush())) return;
    api.playMacro(draft.id).catch(reportError);
  };

  // ----- Step editing -----
  const editSteps = (fn: (steps: NonNullable<typeof draft>["steps"]) => NonNullable<typeof draft>["steps"]) =>
    setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, steps: fn(m.steps) }));

  const addStep = (make: () => NonNullable<typeof selectedStep>) => {
    setAddOpen(false);
    const step = make();
    editSteps((s) => insertAfter(s, selectedPath, step));
    if (!draft) return;
    setSelectedPath(selectedPath ? movedPath(selectedPath, 1) : [draft.steps.length]);
  };

  const addScreenStep = async (item: (typeof NEW_SCREEN_STEPS)[number]) => {
    setAddOpen(false);
    if (!draft?.screen) {
      app.setError("Screen steps act in one game window: pick it in Macro settings → Game window first.");
      return;
    }
    let target: SeenTarget | null = null;
    if (item.teach) {
      target = await teach(item.teach.kind, item.teach.hint);
      if (!target) return;
    }
    const step: MacroStep = item.make(target);
    addStep(() => step);
  };

  const moveSelected = (dir: -1 | 1) => {
    if (!selectedPath || !draft) return;
    const siblings = selectedPath.length === 1 ? draft.steps.length : ((getAt(draft.steps, selectedPath.slice(0, -1)) as { steps?: unknown[] })?.steps?.length ?? 0);
    const target = selectedPath[selectedPath.length - 1]! + dir;
    if (target < 0 || target >= siblings) return;
    editSteps((steps) => moveAt(steps, selectedPath, dir));
    setSelectedPath(movedPath(selectedPath, dir));
  };
  const duplicateSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedPath) return;
    editSteps((steps) => duplicateAt(steps, selectedPath));
    setSelectedPath(movedPath(selectedPath, 1));
  };
  const deleteSelected = () => {
    if (!selectedPath) return;
    editSteps((steps) => removeAt(steps, selectedPath));
    setSelectedPath(null);
  };
  const changeWaitBefore = (ms: number) => {
    if (!selectedPath || !draft) return;
    const result = setWaitBefore(draft.steps, selectedPath, ms);
    setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, steps: result.steps }));
    setSelectedPath(result.path);
  };

  // Keyboard: ↑/↓ select, Ctrl+↑/↓ move, Ctrl+D duplicate, Delete remove (ignored while typing in a field).
  const visibleRows = draft ? flatten(draft.steps).filter((r) => FILTER[tab] === "all" || stepMeta(r.step).kind === FILTER[tab]) : [];
  const onEditorKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    const tag = (e.target as HTMLElement).tagName;
    if (tag === "INPUT" || tag === "TEXTAREA" || tag === "SELECT" || busy || !draft || tab === "variables" || tab === "scan") return;
    const index = visibleRows.findIndex((r) => samePath(r.path, selectedPath));
    if (e.key === "ArrowUp" || e.key === "ArrowDown") {
      e.preventDefault();
      const dir = e.key === "ArrowUp" ? -1 : 1;
      if (e.ctrlKey) return moveSelected(dir);
      const next = visibleRows[Math.max(0, Math.min(visibleRows.length - 1, index + dir))];
      if (next) setSelectedPath(next.path);
    } else if (e.key === "Delete") {
      e.preventDefault();
      deleteSelected();
    } else if (e.ctrlKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === "d") {
      e.preventDefault();
      duplicateSelected();
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-0 gap-4 p-4">
      {/* ===== Main column ===== */}
      <section className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col gap-4">
        {/* Header: recording / playback controls. Recording is red, playback is accent-coloured. */}
        <div
          className={clsx(
            "panel flex items-center gap-4 px-5 py-4 transition-colors",
            isRecording && "border-red-500/40 shadow-[0_0_30px_-8px_rgb(239_68_68/0.5)]",
            isPlayingThis && "border-accent/40",
          )}
        >
          <div className={clsx("raised grid size-11 place-items-center", isRecording ? "text-red-400" : "text-accent-2")}>
            <Clapperboard className="size-5" />
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0">
            <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold whitespace-nowrap">Macro Studio</h1>
            <p className="truncate text-xs text-muted">
              {isRecording ? (
                <span className="text-red-300">
                  {isPaused ? "Recording paused" : "● Recording"} · <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{clock(rec.elapsedMs)}</span> · {rec.events} events
                </span>
              ) : isPlayingThis ? (
                <span className="text-accent-2">
                  {isPaused ? "Playback paused" : "▶ Playing"} · <span className="font-mono tabular-nums">{clock(playElapsed)}</span>
                  {status.state === "playing" && ` · run ${status.run}${status.total_runs ? `/${status.total_runs}` : " (loop)"}`}
                </span>
              ) : draft ? (
                <>
                  {draft.steps.length} actions · {formatMs(totalDuration(draft.steps))}
                </>
              ) : (
                "Record your actions or build them step by step."
              )}
            </p>
          </div>
          <Equalizer active={isRecording && !isPaused} />
          <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">
            {isRecording ? (
              <>
                <button className="btn-secondary px-4 py-2" onClick={togglePause} title={`Hotkey: ${settings?.pause_hotkey ?? ""}`}>
                  {isPaused ? <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> : <Pause className="size-4 fill-current" />}
                  {isPaused ? "Resume" : "Pause"}
                </button>
                <button className="btn-danger px-4 py-2" onClick={stopRecording}>
                  <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop recording
                </button>
              </>
            ) : countdown !== null ? (
              <button className="btn-secondary px-4 py-2" onClick={cancelCountdown}>
                Recording in {countdown}… (cancel)
              </button>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span
                  className="hidden items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted 2xl:flex"
                  title="While you record in a game, SLNC Macro recognises what you click, which bar shows while you hold a key, and a click during a hold — and plays them back by finding them again on screen."
                >
                  <ScanSearch className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> Recognises what you click and hold
                </span>
                <button className="btn-primary px-4 py-2" onClick={startRecording} disabled={busy || !canCreate} title={`Hotkey: ${settings?.record_hotkey ?? ""}`}>
                  <Circle className="size-4 fill-current" /> Record
                </button>
              </>
            )}
            <button className="btn-ghost px-2" onClick={() => setShowSide((v) => !v)} title={showSide ? "Hide inspector" : "Show inspector"}>
              {showSide ? <PanelRightClose className="size-5" /> : <PanelRightOpen className="size-5" />}
            </button>
            <button className={clsx("btn-ghost px-2", showLibrary && "text-accent-2")} onClick={() => setShowLibrary((v) => !v)} title="Toggle library">
              <Library className="size-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Live: what the recorder sees you do (and what smart clicks found while playing) */}
        {(isRecording || live.lines.length > 0) && (
          <div className="panel px-4 py-3">
            <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">
              <Eye className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> {isRecording ? "What you're doing" : "What it saw"}
              {isRecording && <span className="rounded bg-accent/15 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] text-accent-2 normal-case">recognising clicks and holds</span>}
              {!isRecording && (
                <button className="btn-ghost ml-auto px-1" onClick={live.clear} title="Clear">
                  <X className="size-4" />
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
            {live.lines.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted">Do something: every click and key shows up here as you do it.</p>
            ) : (
              <ol className="space-y-1">
                {live.lines.map((l, i) => (
                  <li key={l.id} className={clsx("flex items-center gap-2 text-sm", i === 0 ? "text-text" : "text-muted")}>
                    <span className={clsx("size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", i === 0 ? "bg-accent-2 shadow-[0_0_8px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]" : "bg-line-strong")} />
                    {l.text}
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ol>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* File bar */}
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
          <FileButton icon={Save} label="Save" onClick={() => void flush()} disabled={!draft || saveState === "saved" || saveState === "invalid"} />
          <FileButton icon={FilePlus2} label="Save as…" onClick={() => draft && flush().then((ok) => ok && library.onSaveAs(draft.id))} disabled={!draft || !canCreate} />
          <FileButton icon={Pencil} label="Rename" onClick={() => draft && flush().then(() => library.onRename(draft.id))} disabled={!draft} />
          <FileButton icon={Copy} label="Duplicate" onClick={() => draft && flush().then((ok) => ok && library.onDuplicate(draft.id))} disabled={!draft || !canCreate} />
          <FileButton icon={Trash2} label="Delete" onClick={() => draft && library.onDelete(draft.id)} disabled={!draft || busy} danger />
          <span className="mx-1 h-5 w-px bg-line" />
          <FileButton icon={Upload} label="Import" onClick={library.onImport} disabled={!canCreate} />
          <FileButton icon={Download} label="Export" onClick={() => draft && flush().then((ok) => ok && library.onExport(draft.id))} disabled={!draft} />
          <span className="ml-auto hidden text-[11px] text-muted 2xl:inline">Changes save automatically · imported files are validated before they are added</span>
        </div>

        {!canCreate && (
          <p className="rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-amber-200">
            Macro limit of your edition reached ({license?.entitlements.max_macros} macros). Delete a macro to record more.
          </p>
        )}

        {/* Editor */}
        <div className="panel flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col outline-none" tabIndex={-1} onKeyDown={onEditorKeyDown}>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-1.5 gap-y-2 border-b border-line px-3 py-2.5">
            {TABS.map((t) => (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
                className={clsx(
                  "shrink-0 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-sm whitespace-nowrap transition",
                  tab === t.id ? "bg-gradient-to-r from-accent-deep to-accent text-white shadow-[0_0_14px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/calc(0.4*var(--fx)))]" : "text-muted hover:bg-panel-3 hover:text-text",
                )}
              >
                {t.label}
                {t.soon && <span className="ml-1.5 rounded bg-panel-3 px-1 text-[9px] text-muted">SOON</span>}
              </button>
            ))}
            <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-3">
              <SaveBadge state={saveState} problem={problem} />
              <div className="relative">
                <button className="btn-secondary py-1" onClick={() => setAddOpen((o) => !o)} disabled={!draft || busy}>
                  <Plus className="size-4" /> Add step
                </button>
                {addOpen && (
                  <div className="absolute top-full right-0 z-20 mt-1 w-60 rounded-lg border border-line-strong bg-panel-2 p-1 shadow-xl">
                    {NEW_STEPS.map((s) => (
                      <button key={s.label} className="block w-full rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-panel-3" onClick={() => addStep(s.make)}>
                        {s.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                    <p className="mt-1 border-t border-line px-2.5 pt-2 pb-1 text-[10px] font-semibold tracking-wide text-muted uppercase">Screen (game window)</p>
                    {NEW_SCREEN_STEPS.map((s) => (
                      <button key={s.label} className="block w-full rounded px-2.5 py-1.5 text-left text-sm hover:bg-panel-3" onClick={() => void addScreenStep(s)}>
                        {s.label}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                    <p className="px-2.5 pt-1 pb-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted">Inserted after the selected action.</p>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
            {tab === "scan" ? (
              <Scanner app={app} />
            ) : tab === "variables" ? (
              <VariablesPanel variables={app.variables} steps={draft?.steps ?? []} onSaved={app.setVariables} onError={reportError} />
            ) : draft ? (
              <ActionTable
                steps={draft.steps}
                filter={FILTER[tab]}
                selected={selectedPath}
                onSelect={setSelectedPath}
                activePath={isPlayingThis && app.progress?.macro_id === draft.id ? app.progress.path : null}
                speed={license?.entitlements.speed_control ? draft.settings.speed : 1}
                paused={isPaused}
              />
            ) : (
              <EmptyRecorder onRecord={startRecording} disabled={busy || !canCreate} />
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </section>

      {/* ===== Inspector column ===== */}
      {showSide && draft && (
        <aside className="flex w-[19rem] shrink-0 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <div className="panel p-4">
            <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Action inspector</h2>
            <Inspector
              step={selectedStep}
              canWrap={(selectedPath?.length ?? 0) <= 3}
              waitBefore={selectedStep && selectedStep.type !== "delay" && selectedPath ? waitBefore(draft.steps, selectedPath) : null}
              onWaitBefore={changeWaitBefore}
              onChange={(s) => selectedPath && editSteps((steps) => updateAt(steps, selectedPath, s))}
              onMove={moveSelected}
              onWrap={() => selectedPath && editSteps((steps) => wrapInLoop(steps, selectedPath))}
              onUnwrap={() => {
                if (!selectedPath) return;
                editSteps((steps) => unwrapLoop(steps, selectedPath));
                setSelectedPath(null);
              }}
              onDuplicate={duplicateSelected}
              onDelete={deleteSelected}
              variables={app.variables}
              screen={draft.screen}
              teach={teach}
            />
          </div>

          <div className="panel p-4">
            <h2 className="section-title mb-3">Macro playback</h2>
            <PlaybackPanel
              settings={draft.settings}
              entitlements={license?.entitlements}
              isPlaying={isPlayingThis}
              isPaused={isPlayingThis && isPaused}
              disabled={busy || draft.steps.length === 0 || saveState === "invalid"}
              onChange={(patch) => setDraft((m) => ({ ...m, settings: { ...m.settings, ...patch } }))}
              onPlay={play}
              onPause={togglePause}
              onStop={() => api.stopPlayback().catch(reportError)}
            />
            <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted">
              Play minimises SLNC Macro and starts after {((settings?.playback_start_delay_ms ?? 1000) / 1000).toFixed(1)}s in the window you used last.
              Pause: <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{settings?.pause_hotkey}</span> · Emergency stop:{" "}
              <span className="font-mono text-accent-2">{settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span> or mouse to the top-left corner.
            </p>
          </div>
        </aside>
      )}

      {teachOverlay}

      {/* ===== Library column ===== */}
      {showLibrary && (
        <aside className="panel flex w-64 shrink-0 flex-col p-4">
          <h2 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold">
            <Library className="size-4 text-accent-2" /> Macro Library
          </h2>
          <LibraryPanel macros={macros} broken={app.broken} selectedId={selectedId} canCreate={canCreate} actions={library} />
        </aside>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Equalizer({ active }: { active: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="ml-4 hidden h-9 min-w-0 items-end gap-[3px] overflow-hidden xl:flex" aria-hidden>
      {Array.from({ length: 28 }, (_, i) => (
        <span
          key={i}
          className={clsx("w-[3px] rounded-full", active ? "eq-bar bg-gradient-to-t from-red-500 to-fuchsia-400" : "bg-accent/30")}
          style={{ height: `${30 + ((i * 37) % 70)}%`, animationDelay: `${(i % 7) * 0.12}s` }}
        />
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

function FileButton({ icon: Icon, label, onClick, disabled, danger }: { icon: typeof Save; label: string; onClick: () => void; disabled?: boolean; danger?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <button className={clsx("btn-ghost px-2.5 py-1 text-xs", danger && "hover:text-red-300")} onClick={onClick} disabled={disabled}>
      <Icon className="size-3.5" /> {label}
    </button>
  );
}

function SaveBadge({ state, problem }: { state: SaveState; problem: string | null }) {
  const text = { saved: "Saved", saving: "Saving…", dirty: "Unsaved", invalid: problem ?? "Not saved" }[state];
  return (
    <span className={clsx("max-w-64 truncate text-xs", state === "invalid" ? "text-red-400" : state === "saved" ? "text-muted" : "text-accent-2")} title={problem ?? undefined}>
      {text}
    </span>
  );
}

function EmptyRecorder({ onRecord, disabled }: { onRecord: () => void; disabled: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-8 text-center">
      <CircleDot className="size-10 text-accent-2" />
      <p className="text-lg font-medium">Record your first macro</p>
      <p className="max-w-sm text-sm text-muted">Press Start Recording, do your actions in any window, then stop with your record hotkey.</p>
      <button className="btn-primary mt-2 px-5 py-2" onClick={onRecord} disabled={disabled}>
        <Circle className="size-4 fill-current" /> Start Recording
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Live plain-language lines from the engine ("Clicked “Shop”"), newest first. Cleared when a recording starts. */
function useLiveActions(recording: boolean) {
  const [lines, setLines] = useState<Array<{ id: number; text: string }>>([]);
  const next = useRef(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (recording) setLines([]);
  }, [recording]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const off = onEvent<string>("engine://action", (text) => setLines((ls) => [{ id: next.current++, text }, ...ls].slice(0, 8)));
    return () => void off.then((f) => f());
  }, []);
  return { lines, clear: () => setLines([]) };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Replay.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Circle, FolderOpen, HardDrive, Keyboard, Square, Video } from "lucide-react";
import { Toggle } from "../components/PlaybackPanel";
import { Badge, Page, PageHeader, Section, Segmented, StatTile } from "../components/ui";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { ReplaySettings } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

const fmt = (s: number) => (s >= 60 ? `${Math.floor(s / 60)} min ${String(s % 60).padStart(2, "0")} s` : `${s} s`);
const gb = (bytes: number) => `${(bytes / 1e9).toFixed(bytes < 1e9 ? 2 : 1)} GB`;

/**
 * Instant replay: a rolling screen buffer on disk; the hotkey opens a pop-up to save the last
 * 30 s … 1 h. Video only (no audio yet), primary monitor.
 */
export function Replay({ app, onOpenSettings }: { app: AppStateHook; onOpenSettings: (section: string) => void }) {
  const { settings, replay, reportError } = app;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<ReplaySettings | null>(settings?.replay ?? null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => setDraft(settings?.replay ?? null), [settings?.replay]);
  if (!settings || !draft) return null;

  const running = !!replay?.running;
  const dirty = JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(settings.replay);
  const estimate = (draft.bitrate_kbps * 1000) / 8 * (draft.max_minutes * 60 + 10);

  const save = () => app.saveSettings({ ...settings, replay: draft });
  const start = async () => {
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (dirty && !(await save())) return;
      await api.replayStart();
      app.setReplay(await api.getReplayStatus());
    } catch (e) {
      reportError(e);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader
        icon={Video}
        title="Replay"
        subtitle="Keep the last minutes of your screen in a buffer and save them when something happens"
        actions={
          running ? (
            <button className="btn-danger" onClick={() => api.replayStop().catch(reportError)}>
              <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop buffer
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={start} disabled={busy}>
              <Circle className="size-4 fill-current" /> {busy ? "Starting…" : "Start buffer"}
            </button>
          )
        }
      />

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StatTile icon={Video} label="Status" value={running ? "Buffering" : "Off"} hint={running && replay ? `${replay.width}×${replay.height} @ ${replay.fps} fps` : "Nothing is recorded while off"} />
        <StatTile icon={Circle} label="Can save now" value={running && replay ? fmt(replay.buffered_secs) : "—"} hint={`Up to ${draft.max_minutes} min`} />
        <StatTile icon={HardDrive} label="Buffer on disk" value={running && replay ? gb(replay.disk_bytes) : "—"} hint={`Full buffer ≈ ${gb(estimate)}`} />
        <StatTile icon={Keyboard} label="Save hotkey" value={settings.replay.hotkey || "none"} hint="Opens the save pop-up" />
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <Section title="How it works">
          <ol className="list-decimal space-y-2 pl-5 text-sm text-muted">
            <li>Start the buffer. SLNC Macro records your main screen into a rolling buffer on this PC (Windows shows a border around the screen while it records).</li>
            <li>Something happens worth keeping? Press <span className="kbd">{settings.replay.hotkey || "your hotkey"}</span>.</li>
            <li>Pick how far back: 30 s, 1, 5, 10, 30 or 60 min. That time — before you pressed the hotkey — is saved as an MP4.</li>
          </ol>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">
            Video only for now (no sound). Saves round up to whole 10-second pieces, so a clip can be a few seconds longer. The buffer is deleted when you stop it; saved clips stay in your
            folder. The pop-up appears over windowed and borderless-fullscreen apps; exclusive fullscreen may hide it.
          </p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.openReplayFolder().catch(reportError)}>
              <FolderOpen className="size-4" /> Open saved replays
            </button>
            <button className="btn-ghost" onClick={() => onOpenSettings("hotkeys")}>
              <Keyboard className="size-4" /> Change hotkey
            </button>
          </div>
        </Section>

        <Section
          title="Settings"
          description={running ? "Changes apply the next time you start the buffer." : undefined}
          actions={
            <Badge tone={dirty ? "warning" : "muted"}>{dirty ? "Unsaved" : "Saved"}</Badge>
          }
        >
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">Keep the last · {draft.max_minutes} min</span>
            <input type="range" min={1} max={60} value={draft.max_minutes} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, max_minutes: Number(e.target.value) })} className="w-full accent-[var(--accent)]" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
            <span>Frame rate</span>
            <Segmented value={String(draft.fps) as "30" | "60"} onChange={(v) => setDraft({ ...draft, fps: Number(v) as 30 | 60 })} options={[{ value: "30", label: "30 fps" }, { value: "60", label: "60 fps" }]} />
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
            <span>Quality</span>
            <Segmented
              value={String(draft.bitrate_kbps)}
              onChange={(v) => setDraft({ ...draft, bitrate_kbps: Number(v) })}
              options={[
                { value: "4000", label: "Small (4 Mbit/s)" },
                { value: "8000", label: "Good (8)" },
                { value: "16000", label: "High (16)" },
              ]}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <span className="label">Save replays to</span>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input className="field" readOnly value={draft.output_dir || "Videos\\SLNC Macro (default)"} />
              <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0" onClick={() => api.pickReplayFolder().then((d) => d && setDraft({ ...draft, output_dir: d })).catch(reportError)}>
                Choose…
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-sm">
            <span>Start the buffer when SLNC Macro starts</span>
            <Toggle checked={draft.start_with_app} onChange={(v) => setDraft({ ...draft, start_with_app: v })} />
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Full buffer needs about {gb(estimate)} of free space on the system drive. 60 fps uses more CPU.</p>
          <button className="btn-primary w-fit" onClick={() => void save()} disabled={!dirty}>
            Save settings
          </button>
        </Section>
      </div>
    </Page>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/RequestDev.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import { Check, Send } from "lucide-react";
import { api, errorMessage } from "../lib/tauri";

/** "Request dev": ask for a macro for a game. Goes to the developer's Discord; only what's typed here is sent. */
export function RequestDev() {
  const [game, setGame] = useState("");
  const [what, setWhat] = useState("");
  const [contact, setContact] = useState("");
  const [state, setState] = useState<"idle" | "sending" | "sent">("idle");
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const send = async () => {
    setState("sending");
    setError(null);
    try {
      await api.requestDev(game, what, contact);
      setState("sent");
    } catch (e) {
      setError(errorMessage(e));
      setState("idle");
    }
  };

  if (state === "sent") {
    return (
      <div className="page-enter mx-auto flex h-full max-w-xl flex-col items-center justify-center gap-5 p-10 text-center">
        <div className="grid size-16 place-items-center rounded-full border border-accent/50 bg-accent/10 text-accent">
          <Check className="size-8" />
        </div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Request sent</h1>
        <p className="text-muted">Your request for {game.trim()} is in. If you left a name, you'll hear back there.</p>
        <button
          className="btn-secondary"
          onClick={() => {
            setGame("");
            setWhat("");
            setState("idle");
          }}
        >
          Send another
        </button>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const ready = game.trim() && what.trim().length >= 10;
  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-2xl space-y-8 p-10">
      <div>
        <p className="section-title">02 · Request dev</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight">Need a macro for your game?</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-muted">Tell me which game and what it should do. The more detail (keys, what's on screen, when it should stop), the better.</p>
      </div>

      <div className="panel space-y-5 p-6">
        <label className="block space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Game</span>
          <input className="field" maxLength={100} placeholder="e.g. Steal An Egg" value={game} onChange={(e) => setGame(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        <label className="block space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">What should it do?</span>
          <textarea
            className="field min-h-36 resize-y"
            maxLength={1500}
            placeholder="e.g. Walk to the sell spot, sell everything, walk back, repeat every 5 minutes."
            value={what}
            onChange={(e) => setWhat(e.target.value)}
          />
          <span className="num block text-right text-[11px] text-muted">{what.length}/1500</span>
        </label>
        <label className="block space-y-1.5">
          <span className="label">Your Discord name (optional)</span>
          <input className="field" maxLength={100} placeholder="so I can reach you" value={contact} onChange={(e) => setContact(e.target.value)} />
        </label>
        {error && <p className="text-sm text-red-300">{error}</p>}
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 pt-1">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted">Only what you type here is sent — nothing from your PC or game.</p>
          <button className="btn-primary px-6 py-2.5" disabled={!ready || state === "sending"} onClick={send}>
            <Send className="size-4" /> {state === "sending" ? "Sending…" : "Send request"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
