import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { AlertTriangle, Check, Gauge, Play, Rocket, Save, Settings2, ShieldCheck, Square, Workflow, X, Zap } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { HotkeyInput } from "../components/HotkeyInput";
import { AutoRebirthPanel } from "../components/AutoRebirthPanel";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { RoutineKind, RoutineSettings } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import fish from "../assets/cards/fish.png";
import hook from "../assets/cards/hook.png";
import pumpkin from "../assets/cards/pumpkin.png";

const ROUTINES: Record<RoutineKind, { description: string; art: string; statArt: string; count: string; start: string; unit: string; steps: string[]; presets: [number, number, number]; max: number }> = {
  fishing: {
    description: "Automate your fishing routine",
    art: hook,
    statArt: fish,
    count: "Catches this session",
    start: "Start Fishing",
    unit: "clicks/sec",
    steps: [
      "Casts where you stand: go to the water yourself first",
      "Holds and lets go just as the fill reaches gold (x1.5): it times the bar on every picture and learns the game's delay from each cast",
      "Waits for a bite",
      "Clicks the Click Fast! bar until it is gone, then casts again",
      "Built for AFK: keeps the PC awake, never gives up (retries after a hiccup) and brings the game back to the front when you're away",
    ],
    presets: [15, 60, 100],
    max: 100,
  },
  pumpkins: {
    description: "Find, catch and return event pumpkins",
    art: pumpkin,
    statArt: pumpkin,
    count: "Pumpkins returned",
    start: "Start Pumpkins",
    unit: "E presses/sec",
    steps: ["Find a Lil Pump", "Chase it and press E", "Follow the arrows to the altar", "Find the next one"],
    presets: [4, 10, 20],
    max: 30,
  },
};

const rateOf = (kind: RoutineKind, s: RoutineSettings) => (kind === "fishing" ? s.tap_clicks_per_second : s.pumpkin_taps_per_second);

export function Routines({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { settings, routine, status, reportError } = app;
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState<RoutineSettings | null>(settings?.fishing ?? null);
  const [configuring, setConfiguring] = useState<RoutineKind | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => setDraft(settings?.fishing ?? null), [settings?.fishing]);
  if (!settings || !draft) return null;

  const saveRoutine = (next: RoutineSettings) => app.saveSettings({ ...settings, fishing: next });

  const dirty = JSON.stringify(draft) !== JSON.stringify(settings.fishing);
  const busy = status.state !== "idle" || routine.running || app.rebirth.running || app.autoclicker.running;
  const kinds: RoutineKind[] = ["fishing"];

  const start = async (kind: RoutineKind, next: RoutineSettings) => {
    setConfiguring(null);
    setDraft(next);
    if (JSON.stringify(next) !== JSON.stringify(settings.fishing) && !(await saveRoutine(next))) return;
    api.routineStart(kind).catch(reportError);
  };

  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-6 p-7">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-5">
        <div className="grid size-20 place-items-center rounded-2xl border border-accent/40 bg-[linear-gradient(145deg,rgb(var(--accent-deep-rgb)/0.75),rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.2))] text-white shadow-[0_0_32px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.35),inset_0_1px_0_rgb(255_255_255/0.2)]">
          <Workflow className="size-10" />
        </div>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white">Routines</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-text/70">Roblox routines, per game</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <GameHeading name="Steal An Egg" />
      <div className={clsx("grid gap-5", kinds.length > 1 && "xl:grid-cols-2")}>
        {kinds.map((kind) => {
          const r = ROUTINES[kind];
          const mine = routine.kind === kind;
          const running = routine.running && mine;
          const title = (kind === "fishing" ? settings.fishing.fishing_name : settings.fishing.pumpkin_name) || kind;
          const key = kind === "fishing" ? settings.fishing.hotkey : settings.fishing.pumpkin_hotkey;
          return (
            <section key={kind} className="panel space-y-5 p-6">
              <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
                <div className="raised grid size-[4.5rem] shrink-0 place-items-center">
                  <img src={r.art} alt="" className="size-14 object-contain" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <h2 className="truncate text-xl font-bold tracking-wide text-white uppercase">{title}</h2>
                  <p className="text-sm text-text/70">{r.description}</p>
                </div>
                {running ? (
                  <button className="btn-danger px-6 py-3" onClick={() => api.routineStop().catch(reportError)}>
                    <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <button className="btn-primary px-6 py-3 text-base" disabled={busy} title={busy ? "Stop what is running first" : undefined} onClick={() => setConfiguring(kind)}>
                    <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> {r.start}
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <span className={clsx("rounded-lg border px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase", running ? "border-accent/50 bg-accent/20 text-accent-2" : "border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300")}>
                  {running ? routine.phase || "Running" : "Ready"}
                </span>
                <span className="rounded-lg border border-white/10 bg-white/[0.04] px-3 py-1 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase">Hotkey: {key || "none"}</span>
              </div>

              <div className="card flex items-center gap-4 p-4">
                <div className="raised grid size-14 shrink-0 place-items-center">
                  <img src={r.statArt} alt="" className="size-10 object-contain" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-text/70">{r.count}</p>
                  <p className="text-3xl font-bold text-white tabular-nums">{mine ? routine.catches : 0}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {mine && routine.note && (
                <p className="flex items-start gap-2 text-sm text-amber-300">
                  <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" /> {routine.note}
                </p>
              )}

              <div>
                <p className="mb-3 font-semibold text-white">How it works:</p>
                <ol className="space-y-2.5">
                  {r.steps.map((s, i) => (
                    <li key={s} className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
                      <span className="grid size-6 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent/30 text-xs font-bold text-white ring-1 ring-accent/60">{i + 1}</span>
                      {s}
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ol>
              </div>
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      <GameHeading name="+1 Baseball" />
      <section className="panel space-y-4 p-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-wide text-white uppercase">Auto rebirth</h2>
          <p className="text-sm text-text/70">Opens Rebirth, waits until the REBIRTH button is green, clicks it, and repeats.</p>
        </div>
        <AutoRebirthPanel app={app} />
      </section>

      <section className="panel space-y-5 p-6">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <div className="raised grid size-14 place-items-center text-accent-2">
            <Settings2 className="size-7" />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Settings</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-text/70">Settings of the Steal An Egg fishing routine</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-6 border-t border-white/10 pt-5 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">Hotkey</h3>
            <HotkeyRow art={hook} label={draft.fishing_name} value={draft.hotkey} onChange={(hotkey) => void saveRoutine({ ...draft, hotkey })} />
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">A short sound plays when it starts; an error sound when it can't (the reason shows here and at the top).</p>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">Name</h3>
            <input className="field" aria-label="Name of the fishing routine" maxLength={40} value={draft.fishing_name} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, fishing_name: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">Let go at gold</h3>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <input type="range" className="flex-1" min={0} max={300} step={10} value={draft.release_early_ms} aria-label="Let go earlier (ms)" onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, release_early_ms: Number(e.target.value) })} />
              <span className="w-20 text-right text-sm tabular-nums text-accent-2">{draft.release_early_ms} ms</span>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">Where it starts: after every cast it checks where the bar ended and tunes this by itself, so after a few casts it lets go in the gold every time. Only change it if the first casts are far off.</p>
          </div>
          <div className="space-y-3">
            <h3 className="font-semibold text-white">Target program</h3>
            <input className="field font-mono" aria-label="Game program" value={draft.game_exe} onChange={(e) => setDraft({ ...draft, game_exe: e.target.value })} />
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">
              Input only goes to the game while it is active; otherwise a routine pauses. When nobody has touched the PC for a minute it brings the game back by itself. Stops with {settings.stop_playback_hotkey}, the top-left corner or Stop; after 15 failed attempts in a row it waits 15 seconds and carries on. Check that the
              game's rules allow automation: some games ban accounts for it.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button className="btn-primary px-6 py-3" disabled={!dirty} onClick={() => saveRoutine(draft)}>
          <Save className="size-4" /> Save Settings
        </button>
      </section>

      {configuring && <ConfigureDialog kind={configuring} draft={draft} onCancel={() => setConfiguring(null)} onStart={(next) => start(configuring, next)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

function GameHeading({ name }: { name: string }) {
  return (
    <h2 className="flex items-center gap-3 pt-2 text-sm font-semibold tracking-[0.18em] text-accent-2 uppercase">
      Routine – {name}
      <span className="h-px flex-1 bg-white/10" />
    </h2>
  );
}

function HotkeyRow({ art, label, value, onChange }: { art: string; label: string; value: string; onChange: (hotkey: string) => void }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
      <img src={art} alt="" className="size-8 shrink-0 object-contain" />
      <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-sm">{label}</span>
      <div className="w-32 shrink-0">
        <HotkeyInput value={value} placeholder="None" onChange={onChange} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Before a routine starts: how fast it should go. */
function ConfigureDialog({ kind, draft, onCancel, onStart }: { kind: RoutineKind; draft: RoutineSettings; onCancel: () => void; onStart: (next: RoutineSettings) => void }) {
  const r = ROUTINES[kind];
  const [rate, setRate] = useState(Math.min(r.max, rateOf(kind, draft)));
  const [safe, balanced, fast] = r.presets;
  const choices = [
    { value: safe, icon: ShieldCheck, title: "Safe / Slow", text: "Calmer input, closer to a person" },
    { value: balanced, icon: Zap, title: "Balanced", text: "A good mix of speed and stability" },
    { value: fast, icon: Rocket, title: "Fast", text: "Maximum speed" },
  ];
  const clamp = (n: number) => Math.min(r.max, Math.max(1, Math.round(n) || 1));
  const next = kind === "fishing" ? { ...draft, tap_clicks_per_second: rate } : { ...draft, pumpkin_taps_per_second: rate };

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 grid place-items-center bg-black/55 p-6 backdrop-blur-sm" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={`Configure ${r.start}`}>
      <div className="panel w-full max-w-xl space-y-5 border-accent/40 p-6 shadow-[0_0_60px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.4)]">
        <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
          <div className="raised grid size-14 shrink-0 place-items-center text-accent-2">
            <Gauge className="size-7" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Configure {kind === "fishing" ? "Fishing" : "Pumpkins"}</h2>
            <p className="text-sm text-text/70">Choose how fast the routine should run before starting.</p>
          </div>
          <button className="btn-ghost p-1.5" aria-label="Close" onClick={onCancel}>
            <X className="size-5" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3">
          {choices.map((c) => {
            const on = rate === c.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={c.title}
                onClick={() => setRate(c.value)}
                aria-pressed={on}
                className={clsx("relative rounded-xl border p-4 text-center transition", on ? "border-accent bg-accent/15 shadow-[0_0_20px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.4)]" : "border-white/10 bg-white/[0.03] hover:border-accent/50")}
              >
                {on && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 grid size-6 place-items-center rounded-full bg-accent text-white">
                    <Check className="size-4" />
                  </span>
                )}
                <c.icon className="mx-auto size-7 text-accent-2" />
                <span className="mt-2 block font-semibold text-white">{c.title}</span>
                <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-muted">{c.text}</span>
                <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-accent-2">{c.value}/sec</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        <div>
          <p className="mb-2 font-semibold text-white">
            Speed: <span className="text-accent-2">{rate}</span> {r.unit}
          </p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <input type="range" className="flex-1" min={1} max={r.max} value={rate} aria-label="Speed" onChange={(e) => setRate(clamp(Number(e.target.value)))} />
            <input type="number" className="field w-24" min={1} max={r.max} value={rate} aria-label={r.unit} onChange={(e) => setRate(clamp(Number(e.target.value)))} />
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-amber-200">
          <AlertTriangle className="size-4 shrink-0" /> Higher speeds may count fewer inputs, depending on your game and PC.
        </p>

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-3">
          <button className="btn-secondary px-6 py-2.5" onClick={onCancel}>
            Cancel
          </button>
          <button className="btn-primary px-8 py-2.5" onClick={() => onStart(next)}>
            {r.start} <Play className="size-4 fill-current" />
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Scanner.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { FolderOpen, Tag, Trash2, X } from "lucide-react";
import { Badge, Section, useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { ScanLabel, ScanSession } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

const size = (bytes: number) => (bytes >= 1e9 ? `${(bytes / 1e9).toFixed(1)} GB` : `${Math.round(bytes / 1e6)} MB`);

/**
 * Game scan, a tab of Macro Studio: while a macro is recorded, what happens in the game window is
 * recorded with it (see scan.rs).
 * Pictures can be labelled here: drag a box around something and name it.
 */
export function Scanner({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { settings, scan, reportError } = app;
  const { confirm } = useDialogs();
  const [sessions, setSessions] = useState<ScanSession[]>([]);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<ScanSession | null>(null);
  const [exe, setExe] = useState(settings?.routine.game_exe ?? "");
  useEffect(() => setExe(settings?.routine.game_exe ?? ""), [settings?.routine.game_exe]);

  const reload = useCallback(() => {
    api.listScans().then(setSessions).catch(reportError);
  }, [reportError]);
  useEffect(reload, [reload, scan.running]);

  if (!settings) return null;

  const remove = async (s: ScanSession) => {
    const ok = await confirm({ title: "Delete this scan?", message: `Deletes ${s.frames} pictures and everything recorded in ${s.name}.`, danger: true });
    if (!ok) return;
    await api.deleteScan(s.name).catch(reportError);
    if (open?.name === s.name) setOpen(null);
    reload();
  };

  return (
    <div className="h-full space-y-4 overflow-y-auto p-4">
      {scan.note && <p className="text-sm text-amber-300">{scan.note}</p>}

      {open ? (
        <Review session={open} onClose={() => { setOpen(null); reload(); }} reportError={reportError} />
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_1.4fr]">
          <Section title="Game scan">
            <p className="text-sm text-muted">{scan.running ? `Scanning the game: ${scan.frames} pictures so far.` : "When you record a macro with the game open, the game is recorded with it, including every key and mouse button you press. It stops with the recording, or after 10 minutes."}</p>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <input
                type="checkbox"
                checked={settings.routine.scan_with_macro}
                onChange={(e) => void app.saveSettings({ ...settings, routine: { ...settings.routine, scan_with_macro: e.target.checked } })}
              />
              Scan the game while recording a macro
            </label>
            <label className="block space-y-1.5">
              <span className="label">Game program</span>
              <input
                className="field font-mono"
                value={exe}
                disabled={scan.running}
                onChange={(e) => setExe(e.target.value)}
                onBlur={() => {
                  if (exe.trim() && exe !== settings.routine.game_exe) void app.saveSettings({ ...settings, routine: { ...settings.routine, game_exe: exe.trim() } });
                  else setExe(settings.routine.game_exe);
                }}
              />
            </label>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted">
              Saved on this PC only: 5 pictures per second of the game window, the text on them, and your keys and mouse buttons. Keys are only recorded while the game is the active window. Don't type
              private things in the game chat during a scan, and delete scans you don't need: they take about 1 GB per 10 minutes.
            </p>
          </Section>

          <Section
            title="Scans"
            actions={
              <button className="btn-ghost py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => api.openScanFolder(null).catch(reportError)}>
                <FolderOpen className="size-3.5" /> Open folder
              </button>
            }
          >
            {sessions.length === 0 && <p className="text-sm text-muted">No scans yet.</p>}
            <ul className="space-y-2">
              {sessions.map((s) => (
                <li key={s.name} className="card flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 p-3">
                  <span className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                    <span className="block truncate font-mono text-sm">{s.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted">
                      {s.frames} pictures · {size(s.bytes)} · {s.labels} labels
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <button className="btn-secondary py-1" disabled={s.frames === 0 || (scan.running && scan.session === s.name)} onClick={() => setOpen(s)}>
                    <Tag className="size-3.5" /> Label
                  </button>
                  <button className="btn-ghost py-1" title="Open folder" onClick={() => api.openScanFolder(s.name).catch(reportError)}>
                    <FolderOpen className="size-3.5" />
                  </button>
                  <button className="btn-ghost py-1" title="Delete" disabled={scan.running && scan.session === s.name} onClick={() => remove(s)}>
                    <Trash2 className="size-3.5" />
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </Section>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** One scan: walk through the pictures and put named boxes on them. */
function Review({ session, onClose, reportError }: { session: ScanSession; onClose: () => void; reportError: (e: unknown) => void }) {
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState(1);
  const [picture, setPicture] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [labels, setLabels] = useState<ScanLabel[]>([]);
  const [name, setName] = useState("");
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState<{ x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number } | null>(null);
  const [pictureSize, setPictureSize] = useState({ w: 1, h: 1 });
  const image = useRef<HTMLImageElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getScanLabels(session.name).then(setLabels).catch(reportError);
  }, [session.name, reportError]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    api
      .getScanFrame(session.name, frame)
      .then((p) => alive && setPicture(p))
      .catch(reportError);
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [session.name, frame, reportError]);

  const store = (next: ScanLabel[]) => {
    setLabels(next);
    api.saveScanLabels(session.name, next).catch(reportError);
  };

  /** Pointer position as a fraction of the shown picture. */
  const at = (e: React.PointerEvent) => {
    const r = image.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    return { x: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width)), y: Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height)) };
  };

  const commit = () => {
    const img = image.current;
    if (!drag || !img || !name.trim()) return setDrag(null);
    const pw = img.naturalWidth;
    const ph = img.naturalHeight;
    const x = Math.round(Math.min(drag.x0, drag.x1) * pw);
    const y = Math.round(Math.min(drag.y0, drag.y1) * ph);
    const w = Math.round(Math.abs(drag.x1 - drag.x0) * pw);
    const h = Math.round(Math.abs(drag.y1 - drag.y0) * ph);
    setDrag(null);
    if (w >= 4 && h >= 4) store([...labels, { frame, name: name.trim(), x, y, w, h }]);
  };

  const here = labels.filter((l) => l.frame === frame);
  const { w: pw, h: ph } = pictureSize;
  const usedNames = [...new Set(labels.map((l) => l.name))].slice(0, 12);
  const box = (x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number) => ({ left: `${x * 100}%`, top: `${y * 100}%`, width: `${w * 100}%`, height: `${h * 100}%` });

  return (
    <Section
      title={session.name}
      description={`Picture ${frame} of ${session.frames}. Drag a box around something to name it.`}
      actions={
        <button className="btn-ghost py-1" onClick={onClose}>
          <X className="size-4" /> Close
        </button>
      }
    >
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <input className="field w-56" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Name" aria-label="Name for the next box" />
        {usedNames.map((n) => (
          <button key={n} className={n === name ? "btn-primary py-1 text-xs" : "btn-secondary py-1 text-xs"} onClick={() => setName(n)}>
            {n}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div
        className="relative cursor-crosshair select-none overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-white/10"
        onPointerDown={(e) => {
          if (!image.current) return; // the picture is still loading
          const p = at(e);
          e.currentTarget.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
          setDrag({ x0: p.x, y0: p.y, x1: p.x, y1: p.y });
        }}
        onPointerMove={(e) => {
          if (!drag) return;
          const p = at(e);
          setDrag({ ...drag, x1: p.x, y1: p.y });
        }}
        onPointerUp={commit}
      >
        {picture && <img ref={image} src={picture} alt={`Scan picture ${frame}`} draggable={false} className="block h-auto w-full" onLoad={(e) => setPictureSize({ w: e.currentTarget.naturalWidth || 1, h: e.currentTarget.naturalHeight || 1 })} />}
        {here.map((l, i) => (
          <div key={i} className="pointer-events-none absolute border-2 border-accent-2 bg-accent/10" style={box(l.x / pw, l.y / ph, l.w / pw, l.h / ph)}>
            <span className="absolute -top-5 left-0 rounded bg-black/80 px-1 text-xs whitespace-nowrap text-white">{l.name}</span>
          </div>
        ))}
        {drag && <div className="pointer-events-none absolute border-2 border-white bg-white/10" style={box(Math.min(drag.x0, drag.x1), Math.min(drag.y0, drag.y1), Math.abs(drag.x1 - drag.x0), Math.abs(drag.y1 - drag.y0))} />}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <button className="btn-secondary py-1" disabled={frame <= 1} onClick={() => setFrame(frame - 1)}>
          Previous
        </button>
        <input className="min-w-48 flex-1" type="range" min={1} max={Math.max(1, session.frames)} value={frame} onChange={(e) => setFrame(Number(e.target.value))} aria-label="Picture" />
        <button className="btn-secondary py-1" disabled={frame >= session.frames} onClick={() => setFrame(frame + 1)}>
          Next
        </button>
        <Badge tone="muted">{labels.length} labels in this scan</Badge>
      </div>

      {here.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {here.map((l) => (
            <li key={`${l.name}-${l.x}-${l.y}`} className="chip flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {l.name}
              <button aria-label={`Remove ${l.name}`} onClick={() => store(labels.filter((x) => x !== l))}>
                <X className="size-3" />
              </button>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </Section>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Settings.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  Check,
  Database,
  ExternalLink,
  FolderOpen,
  HardDriveDownload,
  Info,
  Keyboard,
  MousePointerClick,
  Palette,
  Play,
  RefreshCw,
  Settings as SettingsIcon,
  SlidersHorizontal,
  Trash2,
  Upload,
} from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { AccountSecurity } from "../components/access/AccountSecurity";
import { Toggle } from "../components/PlaybackPanel";
import { Page, PageHeader, Section, Segmented, useDialogs } from "../components/ui";
import { editionLabel } from "../lib/edition";
import { NAV } from "../lib/nav";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import { ACCENTS } from "../lib/ui-prefs";
import type { AppSettings, DataInfo, SupportLinks, UiPrefs } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { Hotkeys } from "./Hotkeys";

export type SettingsSection = "general" | "recording" | "playback" | "hotkeys" | "appearance" | "privacy" | "about";

const SECTIONS: Array<{ id: SettingsSection; label: string; icon: typeof Info }> = [
  { id: "general", label: "General", icon: SlidersHorizontal },
  { id: "recording", label: "Recording", icon: MousePointerClick },
  { id: "playback", label: "Playback", icon: Play },
  { id: "hotkeys", label: "Hotkeys", icon: Keyboard },
  { id: "appearance", label: "Appearance", icon: Palette },
  { id: "privacy", label: "Privacy & data", icon: Database },
  { id: "about", label: "About", icon: Info },
];

/**
 * Settings center. Every change is saved automatically (numbers after a short pause) to
 * settings.json and applies immediately.
 */
export function Settings({ app, settings, initialSection = "general" }: { app: AppStateHook; settings: AppSettings; initialSection?: SettingsSection }) {
  const [section, setSection] = useState<SettingsSection>(initialSection);
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(settings);
  const [savedAt, setSavedAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => setDraft(settings), [settings]);
  useEffect(() => () => void (timer.current && window.clearTimeout(timer.current)), []);

  const commit = (next: AppSettings, delay: number) => {
    setDraft(next);
    if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
    timer.current = window.setTimeout(async () => {
      if (await app.saveSettings(next)) setSavedAt(Date.now());
    }, delay);
  };
  const set = <K extends keyof AppSettings>(key: K, value: AppSettings[K], delay = 0) => commit({ ...draft, [key]: value }, delay);
  const setUi = <K extends keyof UiPrefs>(key: K, value: UiPrefs[K], delay = 0) => commit({ ...draft, ui: { ...draft.ui, [key]: value } }, delay);

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader
        icon={SettingsIcon}
        title="Settings"
        subtitle="Changes are saved automatically"
        actions={savedAt && <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-emerald-300"><Check className="size-3.5" /> Saved</span>}
      />
      <div className="grid gap-5 lg:grid-cols-[220px_1fr]">
        <nav className="panel h-fit space-y-0.5 p-2" aria-label="Settings sections">
          {SECTIONS.map(({ id, label, icon: Icon }) => (
            <button
              key={id}
              onClick={() => setSection(id)}
              aria-current={section === id ? "page" : undefined}
              className={clsx("flex w-full items-center gap-2.5 rounded-lg px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm transition", section === id ? "bg-accent/15 text-text" : "text-muted hover:bg-white/5 hover:text-text")}
            >
              <Icon className={clsx("size-4", section === id && "text-accent-2")} />
              {label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </nav>

        <div key={section} className="page-enter min-w-0 space-y-5">
          {section === "general" && (
            <>
              <Section title="Startup">
                <ToggleRow label="Launch SLNC Macro when Windows starts (starts in the tray)" checked={draft.launch_at_startup} onChange={(v) => set("launch_at_startup", v)} />
                <ToggleRow label="Show the startup animation" checked={draft.ui.show_startup_animation} onChange={(v) => setUi("show_startup_animation", v)} />
                <Row label="Open on this page">
                  <select className="field w-48" value={draft.ui.start_page} onChange={(e) => setUi("start_page", e.target.value)}>
                    {NAV.map((n) => (
                      <option key={n.id} value={n.id}>
                        {n.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </Row>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">Closing the window keeps SLNC Macro running in the tray so hotkeys keep working. Quit from the tray icon menu.</p>
              </Section>
              <Section title="Interface">
                <ToggleRow label="Ask before deleting a macro" checked={draft.ui.confirm_delete} onChange={(v) => setUi("confirm_delete", v)} />
                <Row label="Language">
                  <select className="field w-48" value="en" disabled title="More languages may follow">
                    <option value="en">English</option>
                  </select>
                </Row>
                <Row label="Theme">
                  <select className="field w-48" value="dark" disabled title="SLNC Macro has one theme; change the accent colour under Appearance">
                    <option value="dark">Dark (ink)</option>
                  </select>
                </Row>
              </Section>
              <Section title="Updates">
                <Row label="Update channel">
                  <select className="field w-48" value={draft.update_channel} onChange={(e) => set("update_channel", e.target.value as AppSettings["update_channel"])}>
                    <option value="stable">Stable</option>
                    <option value="beta">Beta</option>
                  </select>
                </Row>
              </Section>
            </>
          )}

          {section === "recording" && (
            <>
              <Section title="Mouse & keyboard" description="SLNC Macro listens to your keyboard and mouse only while a recording is running (and during playback, to detect the stop hotkey).">
                <ToggleRow label="Record mouse movement (not only clicks and scrolling)" checked={draft.record_mouse_moves} onChange={(v) => set("record_mouse_moves", v)} />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                  Recording in a game recognises what you click and hold by itself (a small picture of each button, kept inside the encrypted macro). Nothing to switch on.
                </p>
                <ToggleRow label="Minimise SLNC Macro while recording" checked={draft.minimize_while_recording} onChange={(v) => set("minimize_while_recording", v)} />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                  Left, right and middle mouse buttons and the scroll wheel are recorded (side buttons are not supported yet). Keys held together stay held together on playback.
                </p>
              </Section>
              <Section title="Timing">
                <NumberRow label="Countdown before recording starts (seconds)" value={draft.countdown_secs} min={0} max={10} onChange={(v) => set("countdown_secs", v, 500)} />
                <NumberRow
                  label="Merge pauses shorter than (ms)"
                  hint="Very short gaps are added to the next pause instead of becoming their own step — total timing stays exact."
                  value={draft.min_recorded_delay_ms}
                  min={0}
                  max={1000}
                  onChange={(v) => set("min_recorded_delay_ms", v, 500)}
                />
              </Section>
              <Section title="Input filtering" description="Always on — these keep recordings clean.">
                <ul className="list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-xs text-muted">
                  <li>Clicks and keys inside SLNC Macro's own window are not recorded.</li>
                  <li>Input generated by software (including SLNC Macro's own playback) is ignored.</li>
                  <li>The record and pause hotkeys are removed from the recording.</li>
                  <li>Keyboard auto-repeat while holding a key is recorded as one long hold.</li>
                </ul>
              </Section>
            </>
          )}

          {section === "playback" && (
            <>
              <Section title="Starting playback">
                <ToggleRow label="Minimise SLNC Macro when I press Play (input goes to the window I used before)" checked={draft.minimize_while_playing} onChange={(v) => set("minimize_while_playing", v)} />
                <NumberRow label="Wait before the first action (ms)" value={draft.playback_start_delay_ms} min={0} max={10000} step={250} onChange={(v) => set("playback_start_delay_ms", v, 500)} />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">Repeat count, loop and speed are set per macro — in Macro Studio or on the Automation page. A macro started with its own hotkey plays immediately.</p>
              </Section>
              <Section title="Safety">
                <ToggleRow label="Stop when the mouse hits the top-left screen corner" checked={draft.corner_failsafe} onChange={(v) => set("corner_failsafe", v)} />
                <Row label="Emergency stop hotkey">
                  <button className="kbd cursor-pointer text-xs" onClick={() => setSection("hotkeys")}>
                    {settings.stop_playback_hotkey}
                  </button>
                </Row>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                  Limits: loops always stay stoppable, pausing releases all held keys, and the Free edition allows up to {app.license?.entitlements.max_repeat ?? "unlimited"} repeats per run.
                </p>
              </Section>
            </>
          )}

          {section === "hotkeys" && (
            <>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted">
                Global hotkeys are registered with Windows and work while SLNC Macro is in the background — no extra permissions needed. A hotkey that another program already
                uses can't be registered; SLNC Macro tells you when that happens. Programs running as administrator only receive SLNC Macro's input if SLNC Macro runs as administrator too.
              </p>
              <Hotkeys app={app} embedded />
            </>
          )}

          {section === "appearance" && (
            <>
              <Section title="Accent colour">
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  {ACCENTS.map((a) => (
                    <button
                      key={a.id}
                      onClick={() => setUi("accent", a.id)}
                      aria-pressed={draft.ui.accent === a.id}
                      className={clsx("flex flex-col items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border p-2.5 text-xs transition", draft.ui.accent === a.id ? "border-white/30 bg-white/5" : "border-transparent hover:bg-white/5")}
                    >
                      <span
                        className="size-9 rounded-full"
                        style={{ background: `radial-gradient(circle at 35% 30%, rgb(255 255 255 / 0.55), rgb(${a.rgb}) 45%, rgb(0 0 0 / 0.6))`, boxShadow: `0 0 16px rgb(${a.rgb} / 0.6)` }}
                      />
                      {a.label}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </Section>
              <Section title="Layout & motion">
                <Row label="Interface density">
                  <Segmented value={draft.ui.density} onChange={(v) => setUi("density", v)} options={[{ value: "comfortable", label: "Comfortable" }, { value: "compact", label: "Compact" }]} />
                </Row>
                <Row label="Animations">
                  <Segmented
                    value={draft.ui.motion}
                    onChange={(v) => setUi("motion", v)}
                    options={[
                      { value: "system", label: "Follow Windows" },
                      { value: "full", label: "On" },
                      { value: "reduced", label: "Reduced" },
                    ]}
                  />
                </Row>
                <Row label={`3D & glow effects · ${draft.ui.effects}%`}>
                  <input type="range" min={0} max={100} step={5} value={draft.ui.effects} onChange={(e) => setUi("effects", Number(e.target.value), 300)} className="w-56 accent-[var(--accent)]" aria-label="3D and glow effects intensity" />
                </Row>
                <ToggleRow label="Collapse the sidebar" checked={draft.ui.sidebar_collapsed} onChange={(v) => setUi("sidebar_collapsed", v)} />
                <p className="text-xs text-muted">“Follow Windows” uses Settings → Accessibility → Visual effects → Animation effects. Effects never run while nothing changes, so they don't use GPU time when idle.</p>
              </Section>
            </>
          )}

          {section === "privacy" && <PrivacySection app={app} />}
          {section === "about" && <AboutSection app={app} />}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Page>
  );
}

function PrivacySection({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<DataInfo | null>(null);
  const [backup, setBackup] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const dialogs = useDialogs();
  const load = () => api.getDataInfo().then(setInfo).catch(app.reportError);
  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  return (
    <>
      <Section title="What SLNC Macro collects" description="Short version: your data stays on this PC, in your own account.">
        <ul className="list-disc space-y-1 pl-5 text-sm text-muted">
          <li>Keyboard and mouse are captured only while you record (a red banner is shown the whole time).</li>
          <li>Screen rules look at the screen only while monitoring is on (banner shown); screenshots are never saved or sent.</li>
          <li>No telemetry, analytics or crash reports are sent. Statistics stay in your data folder.</li>
          <li>Your account (email, key and a protected password hash) stays on this PC, encrypted for your Windows user; the key is checked offline.</li>
          <li>Every account on this PC has its own macros, settings, history, logs and scans, encrypted on disk with that account's own key (XChaCha20-Poly1305). Only your password, your SLNC key or Windows Hello unlocks it; other accounts see unreadable files.</li>
          <li>Saved replay clips are normal video files in your Videos folder (per account), so you can share them.</li>
          <li>After a restart, an update or a PC start you log in again: without it your data can't be opened.</li>
          <li>Only what you use goes online: update checks, Discord messages and the Discord remote if you set them up, Request dev with what you type, and the payment page in your browser.</li>
        </ul>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Local data">
        <Row label="Data folder">
          <span className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-2">
            <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs text-muted" title={info?.data_dir}>
              {info?.data_dir ?? "…"}
            </span>
            <button className="btn-secondary shrink-0 px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => api.openFolder("data").catch(app.reportError)}>
              <FolderOpen className="size-3.5" /> Open
            </button>
          </span>
        </Row>
        <Row label="Log files">
          <button className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs" onClick={() => api.openFolder("logs").catch(app.reportError)}>
            <FolderOpen className="size-3.5" /> Open logs
          </button>
        </Row>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">
          {info ? `${info.macro_count} macros · ${(info.total_bytes / 1024).toFixed(0)} KB in total.` : ""} Macro files are plain JSON; invalid files are listed in the library and never overwritten.
        </p>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Backup, import & export">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.exportBackup().then((p) => p && setBackup(p)).catch(app.reportError)}>
            <HardDriveDownload className="size-4" /> Back up everything to a folder…
          </button>
          <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.importMacro().then(() => app.reloadMacros()).catch(app.reportError)}>
            <Upload className="size-4" /> Import a macro file…
          </button>
        </div>
        {backup && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-300">Backup saved to {backup}</p>}
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">A backup contains all macros, profiles, variables, screen rules, library data, statistics and settings. Single macros can be exported from the library.</p>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Account security" description="Your password and how you unlock this account on this PC.">
        <AccountSecurity />
      </Section>

      <Section title="License">
        <Row label="Plan">
          <span className="text-sm">
            {editionLabel(app.license)} · <span className="text-muted">{app.license?.status.replace("_", " ")}</span>
          </span>
        </Row>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted">Unlocked by the key from your email (owner, guest or lifetime). Log out with the button in the title bar.</p>
      </Section>

      <Section title="Delete local data" description="Permanently deletes all macros, profiles, variables, screen rules, scans, library data and statistics of this account. Other accounts on this PC, your settings and your sign-in are kept. Make a backup first if you might want them back.">
        <button
          className="btn-danger w-fit"
          onClick={async () => {
            const ok = await dialogs.confirm({
              title: "Delete all local data?",
              message: `This removes ${info?.macro_count ?? "all"} macros and all other SLNC Macro data on this PC. It cannot be undone.`,
              confirmText: "Delete everything",
              danger: true,
              typeToConfirm: "DELETE",
            });
            if (!ok) return;
            api
              .deleteLocalData("DELETE")
              .then(() => {
                app.reloadMacros();
                app.reloadLibrary();
                load();
              })
              .catch(app.reportError);
          }}
        >
          <Trash2 className="size-4" /> Delete local data…
        </button>
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

function AboutSection({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const [version, setVersion] = useState("");
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [links, setLinks] = useState<SupportLinks | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    api.appInfo().then((i) => setVersion(i.version)).catch(app.reportError);
    api.getSupportLinks().then(setLinks).catch(() => setLinks(null));
  }, [app.reportError]);

  return (
    <>
      <Section title="SLNC Macro">
        <Row label="Version">
          <span className="font-mono text-sm">{version}</span>
        </Row>
        <Row label="Updates">
          <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
            {result && <span className="text-xs text-muted">{result}</span>}
            <button
              className="btn-secondary px-2 py-1 text-xs"
              disabled={checking}
              onClick={() => {
                setChecking(true);
                setResult(null);
                api
                  .checkForUpdate()
                  .then((u) => {
                    if (u) app.setUpdate(u);
                    setResult(u ? `Version ${u.version} is available` : "You have the latest version");
                  })
                  .catch(() => setResult("Couldn't check for updates (offline or server not reachable)"))
                  .finally(() => setChecking(false));
              }}
            >
              <RefreshCw className={clsx("size-3.5", checking && "animate-spin")} /> Check now
            </button>
          </span>
        </Row>
        <Row label="Edition">
          <span className="text-sm">{editionLabel(app.license)}</span>
        </Row>
      </Section>
      <Section title="Legal & community">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.openWebsite("terms").catch(app.reportError)}>
            <ExternalLink className="size-4" /> Terms
          </button>
          <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.openWebsite("privacy").catch(app.reportError)}>
            <ExternalLink className="size-4" /> Privacy policy
          </button>
          <button className="btn-secondary" disabled={!links?.discord} title={links?.discord ? undefined : "No Discord link configured in this build"} onClick={() => api.openSupportLink("discord").catch(app.reportError)}>
            <ExternalLink className="size-4" /> Discord community
          </button>
        </div>
      </Section>
      <Section title="Credits">
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">
          Built with Tauri, Rust, React, Tailwind CSS and Lucide icons. Text recognition uses the Windows OCR engine built into Windows. Payments are processed by Stripe.
        </p>
      </Section>
    </>
  );
}

function Row({ label, hint, children }: { label: string; hint?: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-x-4 gap-y-2 text-sm">
      <span className="min-w-0">
        {label}
        {hint && <span className="block text-xs text-muted">{hint}</span>}
      </span>
      {children}
    </div>
  );
}

function ToggleRow({ label, checked, onChange, disabled }: { label: string; checked: boolean; onChange: (v: boolean) => void; disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <Row label={label}>
      <Toggle checked={checked} onChange={onChange} disabled={disabled} />
    </Row>
  );
}

function NumberRow({ label, hint, value, min, max, step = 1, onChange }: { label: string; hint?: string; value: number; min: number; max: number; step?: number; onChange: (v: number) => void }) {
  return (
    <Row label={label} hint={hint}>
      <input
        type="number"
        min={min}
        max={max}
        step={step}
        className="field w-28 font-mono"
        value={value}
        onChange={(e) => onChange(Math.max(min, Math.min(max, Math.round(Number(e.target.value) || 0))))}
      />
    </Row>
  );
}

FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/SlncMacros.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AppWindow, BarChart3, History, MessagesSquare, Play, Radio, Settings2, Sparkles, Square, Target, Timer } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { AutoRebirthPanel } from "../components/AutoRebirthPanel";
import { FishingHistory } from "../components/FishingHistory";
import { FishingSettings } from "../components/FishingSettings";
import { FishingStats } from "../components/FishingStats";
import { WrappedCard } from "../components/WrappedCard";
import { DiscordRemote } from "../components/DiscordRemote";
import { DiscordCards } from "../components/DiscordCards";
import { LiveBar } from "../components/LiveBar";
import { PopNumber } from "../components/fx/PopNumber";
import { Scramble } from "../components/fx/Scramble";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { useLive, type FeedLine } from "../lib/use-live";

/** "SLNC Macros": the routines made for specific games, with what they're doing live. */
export function SlncMacros({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  return (
    <div className="page-enter mx-auto max-w-[1500px] space-y-10 p-8">
      <div>
        <p className="section-title">03 · SLNC Macros</p>
        <h1 className="mt-2 text-5xl font-black tracking-tight">
          <Scramble text="Ready-made" speed={22} /> <span className="menu-shine"><Scramble text="routines" delay={200} speed={22} /></span>
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-muted">Made for one game each. Start one and watch it work.</p>
      </div>

      <Game name="Steal An Egg" sub="Fishing — go to the water in Alien Planet yourself, then start.">
        <Fishing app={app} />
      </Game>

      <Game name="+1 Baseball" sub="Auto rebirth — keeps the Rebirth window open and clicks the moment it turns green.">
        <section className="panel p-6">
          <AutoRebirthPanel app={app} />
        </section>
      </Game>
    </div>
  );
}

function Game({ name, sub, children }: { name: string; sub: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <section className="tile-in space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-baseline gap-4 border-b border-line pb-3">
        <h2 className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xl font-bold">
          <span className="h-5 w-1 rounded-full bg-accent shadow-[0_0_12px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb))]" />
          {name}
        </h2>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">{sub}</p>
      </div>
      {children}
    </section>
  );
}

function Fishing({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const { status, bar, feed, goldRate } = useLive();
  const { settings, reportError } = app;
  const running = status.running && status.kind === "fishing";
  const busy = !running && (app.status.state !== "idle" || app.rebirth.running || app.autoclicker.running);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"live" | "history" | "stats" | "discord" | "settings">("live");
  // Which account History and Stats show (null = all), and the accounts the history knows.
  const [account, setAccount] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState<string[]>([]);
  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .getFishingHistory(500)
      .then((list) => setAccounts([...new Set(list.map((s) => s.account ?? ""))].sort()))
      .catch(() => {});
  }, [tab]);
  // Wrapped: opens by itself when a session ends; also from the button and History.
  const [wrapped, setWrapped] = useState<number | null | undefined>(undefined);
  const wasRunning = useRef(running);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (wasRunning.current && !running) setWrapped(null);
    wasRunning.current = running;
  }, [running]);
  const goal = settings?.fishing.daily_goal ?? 0;
  const today = status.today_before + status.gold;
  const [now, setNow] = useState(Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    const id = window.setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
  }, [running]);
  const stopAt = running && settings?.fishing.stop_after_minutes ? status.started_at + settings.fishing.stop_after_minutes * 60_000 : null;

  const tabs = [
    { id: "live" as const, label: "Live", icon: Radio },
    { id: "history" as const, label: "History", icon: History },
    { id: "stats" as const, label: "Stats", icon: BarChart3 },
    { id: "discord" as const, label: "Discord", icon: MessagesSquare },
    { id: "settings" as const, label: "Settings", icon: Settings2 },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex gap-1 rounded-xl border border-line bg-panel/70 p-1 backdrop-blur" role="tablist">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            role="tab"
            aria-selected={tab === t.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={clsx(
              "relative flex flex-1 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold transition",
              tab === t.id ? "bg-accent/15 text-accent-2 shadow-[inset_0_0_0_1px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.4)]" : "text-muted hover:bg-white/[0.04] hover:text-text",
            )}
          >
            <t.icon className="size-4" /> {t.label}
            {t.id === "live" && running && <span className="pc-live size-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent" />}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {(tab === "history" || tab === "stats") && accounts.length > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
          <span className="text-muted">Account</span>
          {[null, ...accounts].map((a) => (
            <button key={a ?? "*"} onClick={() => setAccount(a)} className={clsx("rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold transition", account === a ? "border-accent bg-accent/15 text-accent-2" : "border-line text-muted hover:text-text")}>
              {a === null ? "All" : a || "no name"}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      {wrapped !== undefined && <WrappedCard start={wrapped} onClose={() => setWrapped(undefined)} />}
      {tab === "history" && <FishingHistory running={running} accountFilter={account} onWrapped={setWrapped} />}
      {tab === "stats" && <FishingStats running={running} goal={goal} accountFilter={account} />}
      {tab === "discord" && settings && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <DiscordRemote cfg={settings.fishing} onSave={(fishing) => app.saveSettings({ ...settings, fishing })} />
          <DiscordCards cfg={settings.fishing} />
        </div>
      )}
      {tab === "settings" && settings && <FishingSettings value={settings.fishing} onSave={(fishing) => app.saveSettings({ ...settings, fishing })} />}
      {tab === "live" && (
    <div className="grid gap-4 xl:grid-cols-[1.1fr_1fr]">
      {/* Live view */}
      <section className={clsx("panel fx-panel relative flex flex-col gap-6 overflow-hidden p-6", running && "fx-running")}>
        {running && <span className="fx-scanline fx-loop" aria-hidden />}
        <div className="relative flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <span className={clsx("flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-1 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide uppercase", running ? "border-accent/50 bg-accent/10 text-accent-2" : "border-line text-muted")}>
            <span className={clsx("size-1.5 rounded-full", running ? "pc-live bg-accent" : "bg-muted/60")} />
            {running ? status.phase || "Running" : "Stopped"}
          </span>
          <span className="kbd">{settings?.fishing.hotkey || "no hotkey"}</span>
          <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
            {!running && (
              <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => setWrapped(null)} title="Summary card of your last session, with achievements">
                <Sparkles className="size-4" /> Wrapped
              </button>
            )}
            <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => api.overlayShow().catch(reportError)} title="Small live window above the game">
              <AppWindow className="size-4" /> Overlay
            </button>
            {running ? (
              <button className="btn-danger px-5" onClick={() => api.routineStop().catch(reportError)}>
                <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button className={clsx("btn-primary px-5", !busy && "fx-cta")} disabled={busy} title={busy ? "Stop what is running first" : undefined} onClick={() => api.routineStart("fishing").catch(reportError)}>
                <Play className="size-4 fill-current" /> Start fishing
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="grid flex-1 items-center gap-6 sm:grid-cols-[auto_1fr]">
          <LiveBar bar={running ? bar : null} active={running} />
          <div className="relative grid grid-cols-2 gap-px overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-line">
            <Stat label="Perfect" value={status.gold} tone="text-amber-300 [text-shadow:0_0_18px_rgb(252_211_77/0.45)]" />
            <Stat label="No perfect" value={status.missed} tone="text-red-300" />
            <Stat label="Casts" value={status.casts} />
            <Stat label={`Streak · best ×${status.best_streak}`} value={status.streak} tone="text-orange-300" />
            <div className="col-span-2 bg-panel px-4 py-3">
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between">
                <span className="label">Perfect rate · {status.catches} caught</span>
                <span className="num text-xl font-black text-amber-300">{goldRate === null ? "—" : `${goldRate}%`}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-2 h-2 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/[0.06]">
                <div className="rate-fill h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-500" style={{ width: `${goldRate ?? 0}%` }} />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        {(goal > 0 || stopAt) && (
          <div className="relative flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-6 gap-y-2 rounded-xl border border-line bg-white/[0.02] px-4 py-3">
            {goal > 0 && (
              <div className="min-w-56 flex-1">
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between text-xs">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-muted">
                    <Target className="size-3.5" /> Daily goal
                  </span>
                  <span className="num font-bold text-amber-300">
                    {today.toLocaleString()} / {goal.toLocaleString()}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <div className="mt-1.5 h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-white/[0.06]">
                  <div className="rate-fill h-full rounded-full transition-[width] duration-500" style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (today / goal) * 100)}%` }} />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
            {stopAt && (
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-muted">
                <Timer className="size-3.5" /> stops in <b className="num text-text">{Math.max(0, Math.ceil((stopAt - now) / 60000))} min</b>
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
        {!running && status.note && <p className="text-sm text-amber-300">{status.note}</p>}
      </section>

      {/* Feed */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Feed lines={feed} running={running} />
      </div>
    </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Stat({ label, value, tone = "text-text" }: { label: string; value: number; tone?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="stat-cell bg-panel px-4 py-3">
      <p className="label">{label}</p>
      <p className={clsx("mt-0.5 text-4xl font-black", tone)}>
        <PopNumber value={value} />
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function Feed({ lines, running }: { lines: FeedLine[]; running: boolean }) {
  const tone = { info: "text-text/80", gold: "text-amber-300", miss: "text-red-300" } as const;
  const dot = { info: "bg-muted/60", gold: "bg-amber-300", miss: "bg-red-400" } as const;
  return (
    <section className="panel fx-panel flex min-h-0 flex-col">
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-line px-5 py-3">
        <h3 className="section-title">Live feed</h3>
        {running && (
          <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] font-bold tracking-wide text-accent-2 uppercase">
            <span className="pc-live size-1.5 rounded-full bg-accent" /> live
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <ol className="h-[26rem] space-y-px overflow-y-auto p-2">
        {lines.length === 0 && <li className="px-3 py-8 text-center text-sm text-muted">Start fishing and every cast shows up here.</li>}
        {lines.map((l) => (
          <li key={l.id} className={clsx("feed-row flex items-center gap-3 rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm hover:bg-white/[0.03]", l.tone === "gold" && "feed-gold")}>
            <span className="num w-16 shrink-0 text-[11px] text-muted">{l.at.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: "2-digit", minute: "2-digit", second: "2-digit" })}</span>
            <span className={clsx("size-1.5 shrink-0 rounded-full", dot[l.tone])} />
            <span className={clsx("truncate", tone[l.tone])}>{l.text}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
