import { useState } from "react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { Bug, LifeBuoy, Send } from "lucide-react";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";
import { Help } from "./Help";
import { RequestDev } from "./RequestDev";

/** Support / Report: answers and guides, reporting a problem, and asking for a macro. */
export function Support({ app, version, onNavigate }: { app: AppStateHook; version: string; onNavigate: (v: string) => void }) {
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<"help" | "request">("help");
  const tabs = [
    { id: "help" as const, label: "Help & report a problem", icon: LifeBuoy },
    { id: "request" as const, label: "Request a macro", icon: Send },
  ];
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col">
      <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border-b border-line px-7 pt-3">
        {tabs.map((t) => (
          <button
            key={t.id}
            onClick={() => setTab(t.id)}
            className={clsx("-mb-px flex items-center gap-2 border-b-2 px-4 py-2.5 text-sm font-medium transition", tab === t.id ? "border-accent text-text" : "border-transparent text-muted hover:text-text")}
          >
            <t.icon className="size-4" /> {t.label}
          </button>
        ))}
        <span className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 pb-2 text-xs text-muted">
          <Bug className="size-3.5" /> Found a bug? “Report a bug” is under Help.
        </span>
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">{tab === "help" ? <Help app={app} version={version} onNavigate={onNavigate} /> : <RequestDev />}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/routes/Trainer.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Crosshair, Play, RotateCcw, Square, Trophy } from "lucide-react";
import clsx from "clsx";
import { Page, PageHeader, Section, Segmented, StatTile } from "../components/ui";
import { api } from "../lib/tauri";
import type { TrainerResult } from "../lib/types";
import type { AppStateHook } from "../lib/use-app-state";

type Mode = "flick" | "tracking";
type Phase = "idle" | "countdown" | "running" | "done";

const SIZES = { small: 28, medium: 44, large: 64 } as const;
type Size = keyof typeof SIZES;

interface Live {
  hits: number;
  misses: number;
  reactions: number[];
  onTargetMs: number;
  totalMs: number;
}

const empty = (): Live => ({ hits: 0, misses: 0, reactions: [], onTargetMs: 0, totalMs: 0 });

/**
 * Aim trainer: an exercise area inside SLNC Macro's own window. Flick = click targets that appear at
 * random spots; Tracking = keep the mouse on a moving target. Results are stored locally.
 */
export function Trainer({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>("flick");
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState<"30" | "60">("30");
  const [size, setSize] = useState<Size>("medium");
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState<Phase>("idle");
  const [count, setCount] = useState(3);
  const [left, setLeft] = useState(0);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState({ x: 0.5, y: 0.5 });
  const [live, setLive] = useState<Live>(empty());
  const [result, setResult] = useState<TrainerResult | null>(null);
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<TrainerResult[]>([]);

  const arena = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const stats = useRef<Live>(empty());
  const spawnedAt = useRef(0);
  const mouse = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);
  const raf = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const endAt = useRef(0);
  const phaseRef = useRef<Phase>("idle");
  phaseRef.current = phase;

  useEffect(() => {
    api.getTrainerResults().then(setHistory).catch(app.reportError);
  }, [app.reportError]);

  const radius = SIZES[size] / 2;

  const spawn = useCallback(() => {
    setTarget({ x: 0.08 + Math.random() * 0.84, y: 0.1 + Math.random() * 0.8 });
    spawnedAt.current = performance.now();
  }, []);

  const stopLoop = () => {
    if (raf.current) cancelAnimationFrame(raf.current);
    raf.current = null;
  };

  const finish = useCallback(() => {
    stopLoop();
    setPhase("done");
    const s = stats.current;
    const secs = Number(duration);
    const r: TrainerResult =
      mode === "flick"
        ? {
            mode,
            at: "",
            duration_s: secs,
            hits: s.hits,
            misses: s.misses,
            avg_reaction_ms: s.reactions.length ? Math.round(s.reactions.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / s.reactions.length) : null,
            accuracy: s.hits + s.misses ? s.hits / (s.hits + s.misses) : 0,
            score: Math.max(0, Math.round(s.hits * 100 - s.misses * 25 + s.reactions.reduce((a, r) => a + Math.max(0, 700 - r) / 10, 0))),
          }
        : {
            mode,
            at: "",
            duration_s: secs,
            hits: Math.round(s.onTargetMs / 1000),
            misses: 0,
            avg_reaction_ms: null,
            accuracy: s.totalMs ? Math.min(1, s.onTargetMs / s.totalMs) : 0,
            score: Math.round((s.totalMs ? s.onTargetMs / s.totalMs : 0) * 1000),
          };
    setResult(r);
    api.saveTrainerResult(r).then(setHistory).catch(app.reportError);
  }, [duration, mode, app.reportError]);

  const tick = useCallback(
    (now: number, last: number) => {
      if (phaseRef.current !== "running") return;
      const dt = now - last;
      const remaining = endAt.current - now;
      if (remaining <= 0) return finish();
      setLeft(Math.ceil(remaining / 1000));

      if (mode === "tracking" && arena.current) {
        // Smooth, changing path (sum of sines) across the arena.
        const t = now / 1000;
        const x = 0.5 + 0.38 * Math.sin(t * 0.9) * Math.cos(t * 0.37);
        const y = 0.5 + 0.34 * Math.sin(t * 1.3 + 1.1);
        setTarget({ x, y });
        const r = arena.current.getBoundingClientRect();
        const m = mouse.current;
        const s = stats.current;
        s.totalMs += dt;
        if (m && Math.hypot(m.x - x * r.width, m.y - y * r.height) <= radius) s.onTargetMs += dt;
        setLive({ ...s });
      }
      raf.current = requestAnimationFrame((n) => tick(n, now));
    },
    [mode, radius, finish],
  );

  const start = () => {
    stats.current = empty();
    setLive(empty());
    setResult(null);
    setCount(3);
    setPhase("countdown");
  };

  // Countdown → run.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (phase !== "countdown") return;
    if (count <= 0) {
      endAt.current = performance.now() + Number(duration) * 1000;
      setLeft(Number(duration));
      setPhase("running");
      if (mode === "flick") spawn();
      const now = performance.now();
      raf.current = requestAnimationFrame((n) => tick(n, now));
      return;
    }
    const t = window.setTimeout(() => setCount((c) => c - 1), 700);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(t);
  }, [phase, count, duration, mode, spawn, tick]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === "Escape" && (phaseRef.current === "running" || phaseRef.current === "countdown")) {
        stopLoop();
        setPhase("idle");
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      stopLoop();
    };
  }, []);

  const onArenaDown = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    if (phase !== "running" || mode !== "flick" || e.button !== 0) return;
    const r = arena.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
    const hit = Math.hypot(e.clientX - r.left - target.x * r.width, e.clientY - r.top - target.y * r.height) <= radius;
    const s = stats.current;
    if (hit) {
      s.hits += 1;
      s.reactions.push(performance.now() - spawnedAt.current);
      spawn();
    } else {
      s.misses += 1;
    }
    setLive({ ...s });
  };

  const best = (m: Mode) => history.filter((h) => h.mode === m).reduce((a, h) => Math.max(a, h.score), 0);
  const recent = [...history].reverse().slice(0, 8);
  const running = phase === "running" || phase === "countdown";

  return (
    <Page wide>
      <PageHeader
        icon={Crosshair}
        title="Aim Trainer"
        subtitle="Practise in SLNC Macro's own exercise area — reaction time, accuracy and tracking"
        actions={
          running ? (
            <button className="btn-secondary" onClick={() => (stopLoop(), setPhase("idle"))}>
              <Square className="size-4 fill-current" /> Stop (Esc)
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button className="btn-primary" onClick={start}>
              {phase === "done" ? <RotateCcw className="size-4" /> : <Play className="size-4 fill-current" />} {phase === "done" ? "Again" : "Start"}
            </button>
          )
        }
      />

      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
        <Segmented<Mode> value={mode} onChange={setMode} disabled={running} options={[{ value: "flick", label: "Flick" }, { value: "tracking", label: "Tracking" }]} />
        <Segmented<"30" | "60"> value={duration} onChange={setDuration} disabled={running} options={[{ value: "30", label: "30 s" }, { value: "60", label: "60 s" }]} />
        <Segmented<Size>
          value={size}
          onChange={setSize}
          disabled={running}
          options={[
            { value: "small", label: "Small" },
            { value: "medium", label: "Medium" },
            { value: "large", label: "Large" },
          ]}
        />
        <span className="ml-auto text-sm text-muted tabular-nums">
          {running ? (phase === "countdown" ? "Get ready…" : `${left}s left`) : mode === "flick" ? "Click each target as fast as you can." : "Keep your mouse on the moving target."}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        ref={arena}
        onMouseDown={onArenaDown}
        onMouseMove={(e) => {
          const r = arena.current!.getBoundingClientRect();
          mouse.current = { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
        }}
        onMouseLeave={() => (mouse.current = null)}
        className={clsx("well relative h-[440px] overflow-hidden select-none", running && "cursor-crosshair")}
        style={{ backgroundImage: "radial-gradient(circle at center, rgb(var(--accent-rgb) / 0.06), transparent 70%)" }}
      >
        {phase === "countdown" && <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center text-6xl font-semibold text-accent-2">{count || "Go"}</div>}
        {phase === "running" && (
          <span
            className="pointer-events-none absolute rounded-full border-2 border-white/70 bg-[radial-gradient(circle,#fff_0_18%,rgb(var(--accent-rgb))_20%_55%,rgb(var(--accent-deep-rgb))_57%)] shadow-[0_0_18px_rgb(var(--accent-rgb)/0.8)]"
            style={{ width: SIZES[size], height: SIZES[size], left: `calc(${target.x * 100}% - ${radius}px)`, top: `calc(${target.y * 100}% - ${radius}px)` }}
          />
        )}
        {phase === "idle" && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center text-center text-sm text-muted">
            <div>
              <Crosshair className="mx-auto mb-2 size-8 text-accent-2" />
              Press Start. A 3-second countdown runs first; Esc stops.
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
        {phase === "done" && result && (
          <div className="absolute inset-0 grid place-items-center">
            <div className="panel space-y-1 p-6 text-center">
              <Trophy className="mx-auto size-7 text-amber-300" />
              <p className="text-3xl font-semibold tabular-nums">{result.score}</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted">
                {Math.round(result.accuracy * 100)}% {result.mode === "flick" ? "accuracy" : "on target"}
                {result.avg_reaction_ms != null && ` · ${result.avg_reaction_ms} ms average`}
                {result.mode === "flick" && ` · ${result.hits} hit${result.hits === 1 ? "" : "s"}, ${result.misses} miss${result.misses === 1 ? "" : "es"}`}
              </p>
              {result.score > 0 && result.score >= best(result.mode) && <p className="text-xs text-emerald-300">New personal best</p>}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StatTile icon={Crosshair} label={mode === "flick" ? "Hits" : "Seconds on target"} value={mode === "flick" ? String(live.hits) : (live.onTargetMs / 1000).toFixed(1)} />
        <StatTile
          icon={Crosshair}
          label="Accuracy"
          value={`${Math.round(mode === "flick" ? (live.hits + live.misses ? (live.hits / (live.hits + live.misses)) * 100 : 0) : live.totalMs ? (live.onTargetMs / live.totalMs) * 100 : 0)}%`}
        />
        <StatTile icon={Trophy} label="Best flick score" value={String(best("flick"))} />
        <StatTile icon={Trophy} label="Best tracking score" value={String(best("tracking"))} />
      </div>

      <Section title="Recent sessions" description="Stored on this PC (trainer.json).">
        {recent.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted">No sessions yet.</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <thead className="text-left text-xs text-muted">
              <tr>
                <th className="py-1 font-medium">When</th>
                <th className="py-1 font-medium">Mode</th>
                <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Score</th>
                <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Accuracy</th>
                <th className="py-1 text-right font-medium">Avg reaction</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-line">
              {recent.map((h, i) => (
                <tr key={`${h.at}-${i}`}>
                  <td className="py-1.5 text-muted">{h.at ? new Date(h.at).toLocaleString() : "—"}</td>
                  <td className="py-1.5 capitalize">{h.mode}</td>
                  <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{h.score}</td>
                  <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{Math.round(h.accuracy * 100)}%</td>
                  <td className="py-1.5 text-right tabular-nums">{h.avg_reaction_ms != null ? `${h.avg_reaction_ms} ms` : "—"}</td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </Section>
    </Page>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: apps/desktop/src/App.tsx
====================================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AccessFlow, OpeningAccount } from "./components/access/AccessFlow";
import { Banner } from "./components/Banner";
import { Backdrop } from "./components/shell/Backdrop";
import { LoadingScreen } from "./components/shell/LoadingScreen";
import { TitleBar } from "./components/shell/TitleBar";
import { DialogProvider } from "./components/ui";
import { toView, type View } from "./lib/nav";
import { api, isTauri, onEvent } from "./lib/tauri";
import { isMilestone, playPerfect, playStreak } from "./lib/sound";
import type { AccessStatus, LiveEvent, OwnerNotice } from "./lib/types";
import { applyUiPrefs, reducedMotion } from "./lib/ui-prefs";
import { useAppState, type AppStateHook } from "./lib/use-app-state";
import { useLibraryActions } from "./lib/use-library-actions";
import { Dock } from "./components/shell/Dock";
import { Hotkeys } from "./routes/Hotkeys";
import { MacroHistory } from "./routes/MacroHistory";
import { MacroSettings } from "./routes/MacroSettings";
import { MakeMacros, type MakeTab } from "./routes/MakeMacros";
import { Owner } from "./routes/Owner";
import { Settings, type SettingsSection } from "./routes/Settings";
import { Support } from "./routes/Support";


export default function App() {
  return (
    <DialogProvider>
      <Shell />
    </DialogProvider>
  );
}

/**
 * The app: a loading screen, then full-page sections switched from a dock at the bottom (Record,
 * Macro settings, History, Buttons, Settings, Support, Owner). The window opens maximized.
 */
// Browser preview only: ?loader=hold keeps the loading screen waiting (it shows "Still loading"
// after 10 s), ?loader=fail shows the startup error state. Never set in the app itself.
const loaderTest = isTauri ? null : new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("loader");

function Shell() {
  const app = useAppState();
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>("record");
  const [makeTab, setMakeTab] = useState<MakeTab>("studio");
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [version, setVersion] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [settingsSection, setSettingsSection] = useState<SettingsSection>("general");
  // Access: no key / logged out → only the start pages (access.rs). Unknown until it has loaded.
  const [access, setAccess] = useState<AccessStatus | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    api.accessStatus().then(setAccess).catch(() => setAccess({ has_access: false, plan: null, has_account: false, email: null, accounts: [], restarting: false, needs_key: null }));
  }, []);
  const granted = (s: AccessStatus) => {
    setAccess(s);
    api.getLicense().then(app.setLicense).catch(() => {});
    setView("record");
  };
  const logOut = () => api.accessLogOut().then(setAccess).catch(() => {});

  // Any page name, also from older buttons ("studio", "library", "routines" …).
  const navigate = useCallback((v: string) => {
    if (v === "library") setMakeTab("library");
    if (v === "studio") setMakeTab("studio");
    if (v === "settings") setSettingsSection("general");
    setView(toView(v));
  }, []);
  const openSettings = useCallback((section: string) => {
    setSettingsSection(section as SettingsSection);
    setView("settings");
  }, []);
  void openSettings;

  const openStudio = useCallback(() => navigate("studio"), [navigate]);
  const { actions, canCreate } = useLibraryActions(app, setSelectedId, openStudio);
  const ui = app.settings?.ui;

  // Appearance follows the saved preferences (accent, density, motion, effects).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ui) applyUiPrefs(ui);
  }, [ui]);

  // First settings load: open the chosen start page, and skip the intro if it's turned off
  // (Settings → General) or motion is reduced.
  const first = useRef(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ui || !first.current) return;
    first.current = false;
    const preview = !isTauri && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("view");
    if (ui.start_page && !preview) navigate(ui.start_page);
    if ((!ui.show_startup_animation || reducedMotion(ui)) && loaderTest === null) setLoading(false);
  }, [ui, navigate]);

  // Browser preview only (screenshots): ?view=macros opens that page without the loading screen.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isTauri) return;
    const preview = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("view");
    if (preview) {
      navigate(preview);
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [navigate]);

  // Fishing sounds: a chime on every PERFECT, a chord on streak milestones (Fishing → Settings).
  const fishing = app.settings?.fishing;
  const soundPrefs = useRef({ on: true, volume: 60 });
  soundPrefs.current = { on: fishing?.sound ?? true, volume: fishing?.sound_volume ?? 60 };
  useEffect(() => {
    const off = onEvent<LiveEvent>("routine://live", (e) => {
      if (e.kind !== "feed" || e.tone !== "gold" || !soundPrefs.current.on) return;
      const streak = Number(/×(\d+)/.exec(e.text)?.[1] ?? 0);
      if (isMilestone(streak)) playStreak(soundPrefs.current.volume, streak);
      else playPerfect(soundPrefs.current.volume);
    });
    return () => {
      off.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, []);

  // The "Play selected macro" hotkey plays whatever is selected here.
  useEffect(() => {
    api.setCurrentMacro(selectedId).catch(() => {});
  }, [selectedId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    api
      .appInfo()
      .then((i) => setVersion(i.version))
      .catch(() => setVersion("?"));
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="relative h-screen min-w-[1100px]">
      <Backdrop />
      {loading && (
        <LoadingScreen
          engine={app.license !== null}
          settings={!!ui && loaderTest === null}
          account={access !== null}
          error={loaderTest === "fail" ? "Could not read the settings file (browser preview test)." : app.error}
          version={version}
          onDone={() => setLoading(false)}
        />
      )}
      {access && (access.restarting || !access.has_access) ? (
        <div className="relative z-10 flex h-full flex-col">
          <TitleBar app={app} view={view} onNavigate={navigate} locked />
          <OwnerMessage />
          {access.restarting ? <OpeningAccount email={access.email} /> : <AccessFlow status={access} onDone={granted} />}
        </div>
      ) : (
      <div className="relative z-10 flex h-full flex-col">
        <TitleBar app={app} view={view} onNavigate={navigate} onLogOut={logOut} plan={access?.plan} />
        <StatusBanners app={app} />
        <OwnerMessage />
        <ScreenQuestion />
        {/* Every section is its own full page; the dock at the bottom switches between them. */}
        <main key={view} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto pb-24">
          {view === "record" && <MakeMacros app={app} selectedId={selectedId} onSelect={setSelectedId} actions={actions} canCreate={canCreate} initialTab={makeTab} />}
          {view === "macro" && <MacroSettings app={app} selectedId={selectedId} onSelect={setSelectedId} onNavigate={navigate} />}
          {view === "history" && <MacroHistory app={app} selectedId={selectedId} onSelect={setSelectedId} />}
          {view === "buttons" && (
            <div className="mx-auto max-w-5xl p-8">
              <Hotkeys app={app} />
            </div>
          )}
          {view === "settings" && app.settings && <Settings app={app} settings={app.settings} initialSection={settingsSection} />}
          {view === "support" && <Support app={app} version={version} onNavigate={navigate} />}
          {view === "owner" && (access?.plan === "owner" ? <Owner /> : null)}
        </main>
        <Dock view={view} onNavigate={navigate} owner={access?.plan === "owner"} busy={app.status.state === "recording" ? "recording" : app.status.state === "playing" ? "playing" : null} />
      </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** A running macro's screen step isn't sure what it sees: it waits for this answer, never guesses. */
function ScreenQuestion() {
  const [question, setQuestion] = useState<string | null>(() => (!isTauri && new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).has("ask") ? "Is this “Start”? I'm only 71% sure." : null));
  useEffect(() => {
    const offs = [onEvent<string>("screen://ask", setQuestion), onEvent<null>("screen://answered", () => setQuestion(null))];
    return () => offs.forEach((o) => void o.then((f) => f()));
  }, []);
  if (!question) return null;
  const answer = (a: "yes" | "skip" | "stop") => api.screenAnswer(a).catch(() => {}).finally(() => setQuestion(null));
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] grid place-items-center bg-black/75 p-6" role="dialog" aria-label="The macro needs you">
      <div className="panel w-full max-w-md space-y-4 p-6">
        <p className="section-title">The macro is waiting</p>
        <p className="text-lg font-semibold">{question}</p>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted">Nothing is pressed while it waits. “Yes” clicks what it found; “Skip” goes on without this step.</p>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className="btn-primary flex-1" onClick={() => answer("yes")}>
            Yes, that's it
          </button>
          <button className="btn-secondary flex-1" onClick={() => answer("skip")}>
            Skip step
          </button>
          <button className="btn-danger flex-1" onClick={() => answer("stop")}>
            Stop macro
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

/** The owner's message (owner list, owner.rs) until closed; a new message shows again. */
function OwnerMessage() {
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<OwnerNotice | null>(null);
  const [closed, setClosed] = useState<string | null>(() => {
    try {
      return localStorage.getItem("slnc.owner-notice-closed");
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  });
  useEffect(() => {
    api.ownerNotice().then(setNotice).catch(() => {});
    const off = onEvent<OwnerNotice | null>("owner://changed", setNotice);
    return () => {
      off.then((f) => f());
    };
  }, []);
  if (!notice || closed === notice.id) return null;
  const close = () => {
    setClosed(notice.id);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("slnc.owner-notice-closed", notice.id);
    } catch {
      /* fine: it shows again next start */
    }
  };
  return (
    <Banner tone="info" onClose={close}>
      <span>
        <span className="font-semibold text-amber-200">From the owner:</span> {notice.text}
      </span>
    </Banner>
  );
}

/** Always-visible status: recording/playing/monitoring are never hidden from the user. */
function StatusBanners({ app }: { app: AppStateHook }) {
  const [installing, setInstalling] = useState(false);
  const recording = app.status.state === "recording";
  const loopingForever = app.status.state === "playing" && app.status.total_runs === null;

  const installUpdate = () => {
    setInstalling(true);
    api.installUpdate().catch((e) => {
      setInstalling(false);
      app.reportError(e);
    });
  };

  return (
    <>
      {recording && (
        <Banner tone={app.status.state === "recording" && app.status.paused ? "info" : "recording"}>
          {app.status.state === "recording" && app.status.paused ? "❚❚ Recording paused — nothing is captured" : "● Recording keyboard & mouse"} — pause{" "}
          <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.pause_hotkey}</span> · stop <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.record_hotkey}</span>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.status.state === "playing" && app.status.paused && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          ❚❚ Playback paused — held keys were released. Resume with <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.pause_hotkey}</span>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {loopingForever && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          Macro is looping until stopped — emergency stop: <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.scan.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          Game scan is recording the game window and your keys in the game ({app.scan.frames} pictures), saved on this PC only. It stops with the macro recording.
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.routine.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          <span>
            Fishing: {app.routine.phase || "running"} · {app.routine.catches} catches · stop with <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.fishing.hotkey || app.settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span>
          </span>
          <button className="btn-secondary py-0.5!" onClick={() => api.routineStop().catch(app.reportError)}>
            Stop
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.rebirth.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          Auto rebirth: {app.rebirth.phase || "running"} · {app.rebirth.rebirths} rebirths · stop with <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.rebirth.start_hotkey || app.settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.autoclicker.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          <span>
            Auto clicker running — {app.autoclicker.clicks.toLocaleString()} clicks · stop with{" "}
            <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.autoclicker.hotkey || app.settings?.stop_playback_hotkey}</span>
          </span>
          <button className="btn-secondary py-0.5!" onClick={() => api.autoclickerStop().catch(app.reportError)}>
            Stop
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.replay?.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          <span>
            Replay buffer recording your screen (last {Math.round(app.replay.max_secs / 60)} min, kept on this PC only) · save with{" "}
            <span className="font-mono">{app.settings?.replay.hotkey || "the Replay page"}</span>
          </span>
          <button className="btn-secondary py-0.5!" onClick={() => api.replayStop().catch(app.reportError)}>
            Stop buffer
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.savedReplay && (
        <Banner tone="info" onClose={() => app.setSavedReplay(null)}>
          Replay saved ({Math.round(app.savedReplay.seconds)} s): <span className="truncate font-mono text-xs">{app.savedReplay.path}</span>
          <button className="btn-secondary py-0.5!" onClick={() => api.openReplayFolder().catch(app.reportError)}>
            Show folder
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.monitor?.running && (
        <Banner tone="info">
          <span>
            Screen monitoring {app.monitor.test_mode ? "in test mode (no actions)" : "active"}
            {app.monitor.rules.length > 0 && ` — ${app.monitor.rules.length} rule${app.monitor.rules.length === 1 ? "" : "s"}`} · checked locally, nothing is saved
          </span>
          <button
            className="btn-secondary py-0.5!"
            onClick={() =>
              api
                .stopMonitoring()
                .then(() => app.setMonitor(null))
                .catch(app.reportError)
            }
          >
            Stop monitoring
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.fired && (
        <Banner tone="info" onClose={() => app.setFired(null)}>
          Rule “{app.fired.name}” fired{app.fired.action !== "notify" && ` → ${app.fired.action}`}
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.update && (
        <Banner tone="info" onClose={app.update.mandatory ? undefined : () => app.setUpdate(null)}>
          <span>
            {app.update.mandatory ? "A required update is available" : "Update available"}: v{app.update.version}
          </span>
          <button className="btn-primary py-0.5!" onClick={installUpdate} disabled={installing || app.status.state !== "idle"}>
            {installing ? "Installing…" : "Update & restart"}
          </button>
        </Banner>
      )}
      {app.error && (
        <Banner tone="error" onClose={() => app.setError(null)}>
          {app.error}
        </Banner>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

    {window.location.hash === "#replay" ? <ReplayPicker /> : window.location.hash === "#overlay" ? <Overlay /> : <App />}

@import "tailwindcss";

/*
 * SLNC Macro design system — "graphite & signal".
 *
 * A quiet, built tool: near-black neutral background, flat matte graphite surfaces separated by
 * crisp hairlines, and ONE signal colour (emerald green by default) used sparingly for the live
 * state and the primary action — not as decoration. Depth is a single soft shadow, not glow.
 * Numbers are tabular so readouts line up. The accent is chosen in Settings → Appearance
 * (data-accent on <html>); the "3D & glow effects" slider (--fx, 0–1) only nudges shadow depth.
 * Density scales the root font size (everything is rem).
 */
:root {
  color-scheme: dark;
  font-family: "Segoe UI Variable", "Segoe UI", system-ui, sans-serif;
  font-size: 16px;

  /* "Ink & line": cool ink black, solid sheets, hairlines, one lit corner per panel. */
  --bg: #07080a;

/* ---------- 3D letters (brand wordmarks): <span class="t3d" data-text="SLNC">SLNC</span> ----------
   The span keeps the real text (layout, selection, screen readers) but is invisible; ::before is the
   extruded side (stacked shadows going down-right, scales with the font size), ::after the lit face.
   .t3d-accent = the mint face and green sides of the SM logo; .t3d-sm = for small text (title bar). */
.t3d {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-block;
  color: transparent;
}
.t3d::before,
.t3d::after {
  content: attr(data-text);
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  white-space: pre;
}
.t3d::before {
  color: #46525a;
  text-shadow:
    0.008em 0.01em 0 #3e4950,
    0.016em 0.02em 0 #353f45,
    0.024em 0.03em 0 #2c353a,
    0.032em 0.04em 0 #242b30,
    0.04em 0.05em 0 #1c2226,
    0.048em 0.06em 0 #15191c,
    0.1em 0.16em 0.22em rgb(0 0 0 / 0.6);
}
.t3d::after {
  background: linear-gradient(170deg, #ffffff 0%, #eef2f4 42%, #b9c4ca 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
  color: transparent;
  text-shadow: none;
}
.t3d-accent::before {
  color: #12684f;
  text-shadow:
    0.008em 0.01em 0 #0f5c46,
    0.016em 0.02em 0 #0d513e,
    0.024em 0.03em 0 #0b4636,
    0.032em 0.04em 0 #093b2d,
    0.04em 0.05em 0 #073125,
    0.048em 0.06em 0 #06281f,
    0.1em 0.16em 0.22em rgb(0 0 0 / 0.6);
}

  background: linear-gradient(160deg, #c4fadd 0%, #6ee7b0 45%, #4fd8b4 80%, #6ee9e0 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  background-clip: text;
}
/* 3D outline lettering (components/fx/Word3D.tsx, used for MACRO): front outline + glass face,
   outline copies stepping back down-right and darkening (--i = depth step), a dark core for body,
   and a glint sweeping over the front. Steps scale with the font size (em). */

}

import type { Entitlements, PlanId } from "./types";

export const APP_NAME = "SLNC Macro";

export const DEEP_LINK_SCHEME = "macroapp";
export const DEEP_LINK_AUTH_CALLBACK = `${DEEP_LINK_SCHEME}://auth/callback`;

export const DEFAULT_PLATFORM = "windows-x86_64" as const;

/** Statuses that grant paid entitlements. `past_due` gets a grace period (see entitlements.ts). */
export const ACTIVE_SUBSCRIPTION_STATUSES = ["trialing", "active", "past_due"] as const;
export const PAST_DUE_GRACE_DAYS = 7;

/** License JWT lifetime = offline grace period of the desktop app. */
export const LICENSE_TOKEN_TTL_SECONDS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60;
export const LICENSE_REFRESH_INTERVAL_SECONDS = 12 * 60 * 60;

export const DESKTOP_AUTH_CODE_TTL_SECONDS = 120;
export const DOWNLOAD_URL_TTL_SECONDS = 600;

export const MACRO_LIMITS = {
  maxSteps: 50_000,
  maxDelayMs: 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000,
  maxLoopCount: 1_000_000,
  maxTextLength: 10_000,
} as const;

/**
 * Fallback entitlements, used when the plans table cannot be read.
 * The plans table (supabase/seed.sql) is the source of truth.
 */
export const FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS: Record<PlanId, Entitlements> = {
  free: { max_macros: 5, max_repeat: 10, max_devices: 1, cloud_sync: false, speed_control: false, max_profiles: 2, max_active_rules: 1 },
  normal: { max_macros: 25, max_repeat: null, max_devices: 1, cloud_sync: false, speed_control: true, max_profiles: 5, max_active_rules: 3 },
  premium: { max_macros: null, max_repeat: null, max_devices: 3, cloud_sync: true, speed_control: true, max_profiles: null, max_active_rules: null },

FILE: packages/shared/src/editions.ts
====================================================================================================
import { FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS } from "./constants";
import type { Entitlements, PlanId } from "./types";

/**
 * Editions shown in the website pricing page and the desktop Premium Store.
 *
 * Display prices live here and in the `plans` table (price_cents). The amount actually charged is
 * always the Stripe Price in `plans.stripe_price_lifetime_id` — change all three together.
 * The desktop app prefers the server's list (GET /api/v1/plans) and only falls back to this.
 */
export type EditionId = "free" | "normal" | "premium";

export interface Edition {
  id: EditionId;
  name: string;
  tagline: string;
  /** One-time price in cents (0 = free). */
  price_cents: number;
  currency: string;
  entitlements: Entitlements;
}

export const EDITIONS: Edition[] = [
  { id: "free", name: "Free", tagline: "Core recording and playback", price_cents: 0, currency: "eur", entitlements: FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS.free },
  { id: "normal", name: "Normal Lifetime", tagline: "More macros, profiles and speed control", price_cents: 300, currency: "eur", entitlements: FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS.normal },
  { id: "premium", name: "Premium Lifetime", tagline: "Everything, without limits", price_cents: 1000, currency: "eur", entitlements: FALLBACK_ENTITLEMENTS.premium },
];

/** Higher rank wins when a user has several sources of access (purchase, subscription, override). */
export const PLAN_RANK: Record<PlanId, number> = { free: 0, normal: 1, premium: 2, pro: 2, team: 3 };

export const PAID_EDITIONS = ["normal", "premium"] as const;
export type PaidEdition = (typeof PAID_EDITIONS)[number];

export function formatPrice(cents: number, currency: string): string {
  if (cents === 0) return "Free";
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("en-IE", { style: "currency", currency: currency.toUpperCase(), minimumFractionDigits: cents % 100 ? 2 : 0 }).format(cents / 100);
}

/** Human-readable comparison rows (same order in the website and the app). */
export function featureRows(e: Entitlements): Array<{ label: string; value: string | boolean }> {
  // null = unlimited; undefined (older license tokens) = the Free value.
  const n = (v: number | null | undefined, fallback: number, one: string, many: string) => {
    const value = v === undefined ? fallback : v;
    return value === null ? `Unlimited ${many}` : `${value} ${value === 1 ? one : many}`;
  };
  return [
    { label: "Macros", value: n(e.max_macros, 5, "macro", "macros") },
    { label: "Repeat & loops", value: e.max_repeat == null ? "Unlimited + loop until stopped" : `Up to ${e.max_repeat}×` },
    { label: "Profiles", value: n(e.max_profiles, 2, "profile", "profiles") },
    { label: "Active screen rules", value: n(e.max_active_rules, 1, "active screen rule", "active screen rules") },
    { label: "Playback speed control", value: e.speed_control },
    { label: "Cloud backup & sync", value: e.cloud_sync },
    { label: "Devices", value: `${e.max_devices} device${e.max_devices === 1 ? "" : "s"}` },
  ];
}

FILE: packages/shared/src/schema.ts
====================================================================================================
import { z } from "zod";
import type { MacroStep, ScreenAction, ScreenExpect, ScreenSettings } from "./types";
import { MACRO_LIMITS } from "./constants";

/** Runtime validation for macro documents (import, cloud sync API). */

const mouseButton = z.enum(["left", "right", "middle", "back", "forward"]);
const modifier = z.enum(["ctrl", "alt", "shift", "win"]);
const normalised = z.number().min(0).max(1);
const vk = z.number().int().min(0).max(255);
const scan = z.number().int().min(0).max(0xffff);
/** Optional key/button hold time. Absent in files written before this field existed. */
const holdMs = z.number().int().min(0).max(60_000).optional();

/** Smart click: a small grayscale picture of what was clicked (base64), found again on playback. */
const clickTarget = z.object({
  width: z.number().int().min(1).max(512),
  height: z.number().int().min(1).max(512),
  gray_b64: z.string().max(400_000),
  click_x: z.number().int().min(0).max(512),
  click_y: z.number().int().min(0).max(512),
  label: z.string().max(200).optional(),
  find_ms: z.number().int().min(0).max(120_000).optional(),
});

export const macroStepSchema: z.ZodType<MacroStep> = z.lazy(() =>
  z.discriminatedUnion("type", [
    z.object({ type: z.literal("mouse_move"), x: normalised, y: normalised, monitor: z.number().int().min(0) }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("mouse_move_by"), dx: z.number().int().min(-100_000).max(100_000), dy: z.number().int().min(-100_000).max(100_000) }),
    z.object({
      type: z.literal("mouse_click"),
      button: mouseButton,
      x: normalised,
      y: normalised,
      monitor: z.number().int().min(0),
      hold_ms: holdMs,
      target: clickTarget.optional(),
    }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("mouse_down"), button: mouseButton }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("mouse_up"), button: mouseButton }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("key_press"), vk, scan, mods: z.array(modifier).optional(), hold_ms: holdMs }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("key_down"), vk, scan }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("key_up"), vk, scan }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("scroll"), delta: z.number().int().min(-12_000).max(12_000) }),
    z.object({ type: z.literal("text"), value: z.string().max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxTextLength) }),
    z.object({
      type: z.literal("delay"),
      ms: z.number().int().min(0).max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxDelayMs),
      var: z.string().regex(/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{0,31}$/).optional(),
    }),
    z.object({
      type: z.literal("loop"),
      count: z.number().int().min(1).max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxLoopCount),
      steps: z.array(macroStepSchema),
    }),
    // Screen steps are checked in full by the desktop app (screen_actions::validate_step).
    z.object({
      type: z.literal("screen"),
      action: z.custom<ScreenAction>((v) => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && typeof (v as { kind?: unknown }).kind === "string"),
      expect: z.custom<ScreenExpect>((v) => typeof v === "object" && v !== null).optional(),
      timeout_ms: z.number().int().min(100).max(600_000).optional(),
    }),
  ]),
);

export const macroSettingsSchema = z.object({
  repeat: z.number().int().min(0).max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxLoopCount),
  speed: z.number().min(0.1).max(10),
  delay_between_runs_ms: z.number().int().min(0).max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxDelayMs),
});

export const macroDocumentSchema = z.object({
  schema_version: z.literal(1),
  id: z.uuid(),
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
  hotkey: z.string().max(50).nullable(),
  settings: macroSettingsSchema,
  steps: z.array(macroStepSchema).max(MACRO_LIMITS.maxSteps),
  screen: z.custom<ScreenSettings>((v) => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && typeof (v as { exe?: unknown }).exe === "string").optional(),
});

/** Body for PUT /api/v1/macros (upsert one macro). */
export const macroUpsertSchema = z.object({
  content: macroDocumentSchema,
  /** Revision the client based its edit on; server rejects with 409 if stale. */
  base_revision: z.number().int().min(0),
  is_deleted: z.boolean().default(false),
});

export type MacroUpsertInput = z.infer<typeof macroUpsertSchema>;

export const desktopExchangeSchema = z.object({
  code: z.string().min(20).max(200),
  code_verifier: z.string().min(43).max(128),
  machine_hash: z.string().min(16).max(128),
  device_name: z.string().max(100).optional(),
  app_version: z.string().max(32).optional(),
});


====================================================================================================
FILE: packages/shared/src/types.ts
====================================================================================================
/**
 * Domain types shared by the website/backend (apps/web) and the desktop UI (apps/desktop).
 *
 * The Rust structs in apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/model.rs mirror the macro types below.
 * TODO: generate these macro types from Rust with `ts-rs` so there is one source of truth.
 */

// ---------- Plans & entitlements ----------

/**
 * "normal" and "premium" are one-time (lifetime) editions. "pro"/"team" are the older subscription
 * plans; they stay supported so existing subscribers keep their access.
 */
export type PlanId = "free" | "normal" | "premium" | "pro" | "team";

export interface Entitlements {
  max_macros: number | null; // null = unlimited
  max_repeat: number | null; // null = unlimited / infinite loop allowed
  max_devices: number;
  cloud_sync: boolean;
  speed_control: boolean;
  /** Profiles (null = unlimited). Missing in old license tokens → treated as the Free value. */
  max_profiles?: number | null;
  /** Screen rules that can be switched on at the same time (null = unlimited). */
  max_active_rules?: number | null;
}

export interface Plan {
  id: PlanId;
  name: string;
  entitlements: Entitlements;
  stripe_price_monthly_id: string | null;
  stripe_price_yearly_id: string | null;
  /** One-time price for lifetime editions. */
  stripe_price_lifetime_id?: string | null;
  price_cents?: number | null;
  currency?: string | null;
  is_active: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
}

export type BillingInterval = "monthly" | "yearly";

// ---------- Subscriptions ----------

/** Mirrors Stripe's subscription statuses. */
export type SubscriptionStatus =
  | "trialing"
  | "active"
  | "past_due"
  | "canceled"
  | "incomplete"
  | "incomplete_expired"
  | "unpaid"
  | "paused";

export interface Subscription {
  id: string; // Stripe sub_...
  user_id: string;
  plan_id: PlanId;
  stripe_price_id: string;
  status: SubscriptionStatus;
  quantity: number;
  current_period_end: string | null;
  cancel_at_period_end: boolean;
  trial_end: string | null;
}

// ---------- Devices ----------

export interface Device {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  device_name: string | null;
  app_version: string | null;
  last_seen_at: string;
  revoked_at: string | null;
  created_at: string;
}

// ---------- App versions / updates ----------

export type ReleaseChannel = "stable" | "beta";
export type Platform = "windows-x86_64";

export interface AppVersion {
  id: string;
  version: string;
  platform: Platform;
  channel: ReleaseChannel;
  release_notes: string | null;
  min_supported_version: string | null;
  file_size: number;
  /** SHA-256 of the installer (hex), shown on the download page for verification. */
  sha256?: string;
  published_at: string | null;
}

/** Response format expected by tauri-plugin-updater (dynamic server). */
export interface UpdateManifest {
  version: string;
  notes: string;
  pub_date: string;
  url: string;
  signature: string;
}

// ---------- Macros ----------

export type MouseButton = "left" | "right" | "middle" | "back" | "forward";
export type Modifier = "ctrl" | "alt" | "shift" | "win";

/** Coordinates are normalised (0..1) relative to the given monitor. */
/** Smart click: what was clicked while recording, so playback finds it anywhere on screen. */
export interface ClickTarget {
  width: number;
  height: number;
  /** Grayscale pixels, base64. */
  gray_b64: string;
  /** Where in the picture the click was. */
  click_x: number;
  click_y: number;
  /** Text read from it (e.g. "Shop"). */
  label?: string;
  /** How long playback looks for it before skipping the click (ms). */
  find_ms?: number;
}

export type MacroStep =
  | { type: "mouse_move"; x: number; y: number; monitor: number }
  | { type: "mouse_move_by"; dx: number; dy: number } // raw movement: turning a game camera with the right button held
  | { type: "mouse_click"; button: MouseButton; x: number; y: number; monitor: number; hold_ms?: number; target?: ClickTarget }
  | { type: "mouse_down"; button: MouseButton }
  | { type: "mouse_up"; button: MouseButton }
  | { type: "key_press"; vk: number; scan: number; mods?: Modifier[]; hold_ms?: number } // hold_ms optional: 0/absent = instant tap
  | { type: "key_down"; vk: number; scan: number }
  | { type: "key_up"; vk: number; scan: number }
  | { type: "scroll"; delta: number } // 120 = one wheel notch up
  | { type: "text"; value: string }
  | { type: "delay"; ms: number; var?: string } // var: optional numeric variable (ms) overriding `ms`
  | { type: "loop"; count: number; steps: MacroStep[] }
  /** Looks at the game window and acts on what it sees (needs MacroDocument.screen). */
  | { type: "screen"; action: ScreenAction; expect?: ScreenExpect; timeout_ms?: number };

// ---------- Screen steps (apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/screen_actions) ----------

/** A taught target: a grayscale picture of a button or indicator in the game window. */
export interface SeenTarget {
  name: string;
  kind: "button" | "indicator";
  width: number;
  height: number;
  gray_b64: string;
  taught_width: number;
  taught_height: number;
  taught_x: number;
  taught_y: number;
  search_everywhere?: boolean;
  /** Bars that change while they fill: their colour filling the box also counts. */
  color?: { r: number; g: number; b: number; tolerance: number; min_ratio: number };
  /** 0..1, overrides the macro's "sure" score. */
  sure?: number;
}

export type ScreenInput = { type: "key"; vk: number } | { type: "mouse"; button: MouseButton };

export type ScreenAction =
  | { kind: "click_target"; target: SeenTarget; button: MouseButton; clicks?: number }
  | { kind: "hold"; input: ScreenInput; until: { type: "time"; ms: number } | { type: "while_visible"; target: SeenTarget; max_ms?: number } }
  | {
      kind: "hold_and_click";
      hold: ScreenInput;
      click: { type: "input"; input: ScreenInput } | { type: "target"; target: SeenTarget; button: MouseButton } | { type: "point"; x: number; y: number; button: MouseButton };
      delay_ms: number;
      click_ms: number;
      release?: "click_first" | "hold_first";
    }
  | { kind: "click_point"; x: number; y: number; button: MouseButton }
  | { kind: "wait_for"; target: SeenTarget; gone?: boolean };

export type ScreenExpect = { type: "gone"; target: SeenTarget } | { type: "visible"; target: SeenTarget };

export interface ScreenLimits {
  timeout_ms: number;
  retries: number;
  unsure_retries: number;
  recheck_ms: number;
  max_clicks_per_second: number;
  sure: number;
  unsure_floor: number;
  max_hold_ms: number;
}

export const DEFAULT_SCREEN_LIMITS: ScreenLimits = {
  timeout_ms: 10_000,
  retries: 2,
  unsure_retries: 3,
  recheck_ms: 120,
  max_clicks_per_second: 4,
  sure: 0.85,
  unsure_floor: 0.6,
  max_hold_ms: 30_000,
};

/** The game window screen steps work in, and their limits. */
export interface ScreenSettings {
  exe: string;
  limits: ScreenLimits;
}

export interface MacroSettings {
  repeat: number; // 0 = infinite (Pro only)
  speed: number; // 1.0 = recorded speed
  delay_between_runs_ms: number;
}

export interface MacroDocument {
  schema_version: 1;
  id: string;
  name: string;
  hotkey: string | null;
  settings: MacroSettings;
  steps: MacroStep[];
  /** Game window + limits for screen steps (the macro pauses while it isn't in front). */
  screen?: ScreenSettings;
  /** What it keeps an eye on while playing: counts, read names, read numbers. */
  trackers?: Tracker[];
  /** Its live Discord message. */
  live?: LiveMessage;
}

/** An area of the game window as fractions of its size. */
export interface Area {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  w: number;
  h: number;
}

export type TrackKind =
  /** +1 each time this shows. hit = counts toward streak/rate, miss = breaks the streak. */
  | { type: "count"; target: SeenTarget; role?: "hit" | "miss" | "plain" }
  /** Reads text there: after a counter fires (`after`) or whenever it changes; optional rarity area. */
  | { type: "read_text"; area: Area; after?: string; rarity?: Area }
  /** Reads a number there (kick power, speed). */
  | { type: "read_number"; area: Area; unit?: string };

export interface Tracker {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  kind: TrackKind;
  show?: boolean;
}

export interface LiveMessage {
  enabled: boolean;
  title: string;
  footer?: string;
  predictions?: boolean;
  streaks?: boolean;
  /** Today, yesterday, all time, records and recent sessions. */
  totals?: boolean;
}

/** Row in public.macros (cloud copy). */
export interface MacroRecord {
  id: string;
  user_id: string;
  name: string;
  hotkey: string | null;
  schema_version: number;
  content: MacroDocument;
  content_hash: string;
  revision: number;
  is_deleted: boolean;
  created_at: string;
  updated_at: string;
}

// ---------- API contracts ----------

export interface LicenseResponse {
  token: string; // Ed25519-signed JWT
  plan: PlanId;
  entitlements: Entitlements;
  user: { email: string };
}

export interface LicenseClaims {
  sub: string; // user id
  device_id: string;
  plan: PlanId;
  entitlements: Entitlements;
  iat: number;
  exp: number;
}

export interface DesktopExchangeRequest {
  code: string;
  code_verifier: string;
  machine_hash: string;
  device_name?: string;
  app_version?: string;
}

export interface DesktopExchangeResponse {
  device_token: string;
  device_id: string;
}

export interface ApiError {
  error: { code: string; message: string };
}


====================================================================================================
FILE: packages/shared/src/utils.ts
====================================================================================================
import type { Entitlements, MacroStep } from "./types";

/**
 * Compare two semver strings (major.minor.patch, pre-release suffix ignored).
 * Returns -1 if a < b, 0 if equal, 1 if a > b.
 */
export function compareVersions(a: string, b: string): -1 | 0 | 1 {
  const parse = (v: string) =>
    v
      .replace(/^v/, "")
      .split("-")[0]!
      .split(".")
      .map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10) || 0);
  const pa = parse(a);
  const pb = parse(b);
  for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
    const diff = (pa[i] ?? 0) - (pb[i] ?? 0);
    if (diff !== 0) return diff > 0 ? 1 : -1;
  }
  return 0;
}

export function isValidSemver(v: string): boolean {
  return /^\d+\.\d+\.\d+(-[0-9A-Za-z.-]+)?$/.test(v);
}

/** Total step count including nested loop bodies (used for limits/UI). */
export function countSteps(steps: MacroStep[]): number {
  return steps.reduce((n, s) => n + 1 + (s.type === "loop" ? countSteps(s.steps) : 0), 0);
}

/** Estimated duration of one run in ms (delays + hold times, loops expanded). */
export function estimateDurationMs(steps: MacroStep[]): number {
  return steps.reduce((ms, s) => {
    if (s.type === "delay") return ms + s.ms;
    if (s.type === "key_press" || s.type === "mouse_click") return ms + (s.hold_ms ?? 0);
    if (s.type === "loop") return ms + s.count * estimateDurationMs(s.steps);
    return ms;
  }, 0);
}

export function canCreateMacro(entitlements: Entitlements, currentCount: number): boolean {
  return entitlements.max_macros === null || currentCount < entitlements.max_macros;
}

export function formatBytes(bytes: number): string {
  if (bytes < 1024) return `${bytes} B`;
  if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return `${(bytes / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`;
  return `${(bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`;
}
